/********************************************************************
 * PF2e AUTOMATIC SPELL RESOLUTION
 * Macro v8.40
 *
 * Run ONCE in the active GM's browser after each Foundry reload.
 * Players do not need to run it; keep that GM browser connected.
 *
 * v8.40
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Native PF2e rerolls retain their exact spell-flow entry through a
 *   context roll option, even when PF2e drops the custom message flags.
 * - Critical successes immediately stop ordered chains and mark later
 *   targets as skipped; rerolls and order changes recompute that stop.
 * - Already-applied results affected by a reroll require manual correction.
 *   Keeping the same save outcome does not invalidate applied results.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.39
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Phase areas recognize PF2e's inline emanation templates as well as
 *   plain text, including Incarnate Tempest of Shades in PF2e 8.5.0.
 * - An unrecognized Arrive/Depart spell cannot fall back to one ordinary
 *   save profile and mix its phase outcomes. Logs retain phase diagnostics.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.38
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Arrival/departure phase saves and distinct traits/outcomes.
 * - Manual movement/end-turn exposures, next-target-turn effects, native
 *   Escape checks and source-specific release/area cleanup.
 * - Ordered chain resolution: shared damage, range checks, critical-success
 *   stopping and durable reroll/application records.
 * - GM tracks area membership, line of effect, movement, MAP and action use.
 *   Spell-name overrides remain empty; all prior resolution paths retained.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.37
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Phantom Pain: native initial damage at cast rank; separate persistent
 *   mental damage and reducible Sickened on failed saves. Recovering from
 *   this Sickened, dismissal, or expiry removes only this spell's items.
 * - Vampiric Exsanguination: living targets; caster temporary HP from half
 *   the highest native damage taken, with a one-minute source effect.
 *   Tracks resolved targets, preserves spent temporary HP, and never stacks
 *   temporary HP pools. Uncertain interrupted applications require review.
 * - Description-based rules; SPELL_RULES remains empty. Warp Mind's first
 *   action restriction remains a manual GM step.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.36
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Never Mind: timed/permanent curse effects and permanent mental modifier
 *   overrides on critical failure. Records spellcasting/class/GM restrictions.
 * - Calm: attack penalty on success; tracks nonhostility/emotion suppression
 *   and provides a hostility-ended control only on failure.
 * - Laughing Fit: persistent Prone, a one-round action-restriction phase,
 *   then Slowed 1/no reactions; control ends this sustained cast on all targets.
 * - Cursed Metamorphosis: chosen animal-form tracker, one-minute failure
 *   with a voluntary Will recovery save, and unlimited critical failure.
 * - Form details, emotion suppression, action restrictions and sustaining
 *   remain explicit GM steps on their effect cards. No actor types/items
 *   or ownership are rewritten. Spell-name overrides remain empty.
 * - Bounded condition inference cannot leak mentions across full outcome
 *   sections; unrecognized fallback outcomes visibly require manual review.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.35
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Result descriptions and Apply buttons use PF2e's stored damage-roll
 *   formula, so resolved rank expressions display as ordinary typed damage.
 * - Creatures excluded from the cast area have a separate Not affected row
 *   with their reasons, instead of inheriting an inapplicable death heading.
 * - Death-only outcome headings omit the redundant No Damage prefix.
 * - Presentation changes only; retains all v8.34 resolution and kill tracking.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.34
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Fixes selected-area validation when unaffected undead/constructs have
 *   already rolled saves. Their rows are marked nonliving and skipped;
 *   they do not block the living targets or count as kills.
 * - Keeps recorded kills in the cast-wide result even if the killed token
 *   is no longer selected or has since been removed from the scene.
 * - Waits for an initial application already in progress before reading
 *   HP/kill records. Previously applied damage is retained and not repeated.
 * - Requires only relevant living saved targets in the selected area and
 *   identifies missing creatures by name. Scene-token identities stay exact.
 * - Retains every v8.33 spell, scroll/wand, and prismatic resolution path.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.33
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Keeps scroll/wand casting and the v8.30-v8.32 prismatic/table paths.
 * - Missing critical outcomes inherit their ordinary outcome, as PF2e
 *   specifies; they are not assumed to be basic saves or no effect.
 * - Beheading Buzz Saw: initial slashing + persistent bleed, followed by
 *   the critical-failure Fortitude save with death/incapacitation traits.
 *   Prompts for heads and lethal decapitation; canceled prompts can resume.
 * - Banishment: caster Stunned goes to the caster. Relocation is manual.
 * - Synesthesia: correctly scoped timed Clumsy 3 and speed penalty; tracks
 *   attack-target concealment. Concentrate and concealment flat checks
 *   remain explicit instructions on the effect/control card.
 * - Confusion, Dominate, Paralyze and Phantasmal Calamity: timed effects,
 *   native recovery saves, duration reduction, and conditional second saves.
 *   Dominate's order-triggered saves use the effect control card.
 * - Mariner's Curse: Sickened floor, reducible initial Sickened 2, and an
 *   environment control for conditional Slowed. Curse removal is manual.
 * - Sleep: base-rank no-Prone exception, Perception penalty, correct times;
 *   heightened expiry transitions to ordinary sleep. Wake through the
 *   effect control card; release of held items remains manual.
 * - Charm: chooses one attitude toward the caster only, with timed expiry.
 *   End daily-preparation/hostile-action effects through their control card.
 * - Massacre and analogous cast-wide riders: target EVERY living creature
 *   in the line, including those above the level limit, then click Resolve
 *   Selected Area on the spell/results card. Reuses recorded saves, rolls
 *   missing saves, resolves all initial effects, then checks for any kills.
 *   If none died, automatically applies the 30 void backlash to each living
 *   area creature AND the caster through PF2e damage/IWR. No extra save.
 * - Massacre: tracks actual HP changes and critical-failure deaths, uses
 *   rank 9/10 level limits and damage, and retains a per-cast ledger so
 *   deleting results or re-clicking cannot repeat recorded applications.
 *   A reroll after application or interrupted HP update requires review.
 * - Spell-name overrides remain empty. New mechanics are selected by their
 *   description structure, native condition catalog, and cast rank.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.32
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Recognizes PF2e's native scroll spell cards through message.item and
 *   flags.pf2e.casting.embeddedSpell, including after the scroll is consumed.
 * - Retains the embedded spell's native casting entry, rank, and variants.
 * - Adds Show Spell Card to identified scroll item cards for the GM. Uses
 *   PF2e's own eligible casting entry without consuming the scroll or slots.
 *   Normal Use/Trick Magic Item casting remains handled by PF2e.
 * - Recovers embedded spell origins from their original chat cards for saves.
 * - Keeps v8.31 behavior and the existing empty SPELL_RULES table.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.31
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Adds generic cast-once random damage tables for basic-save spells whose
 *   damage lives in their description, including Rainbow Fumarole.
 * - Rolls the table die once per cast and retains that result for subsequent
 *   exposures. A choose-for-each-creature result prompts for each exposure.
 * - Uses native PF2e DamageRoll/applyDamage for mixed and persistent damage,
 *   with normal basic-save scaling and IWR. Table condition riders apply on
 *   failure/critical failure; unsupported movement remains explicitly manual.
 * - Keeps one combined results card with separate, repeatable exposures.
 *   Rerolls keep their selected color and follow the existing correction rules.
 * - Adds GM Table Result and Resolve Exposure controls to the cast card.
 * - Connects shared, duration-scoped conditions to explicit save-result
 *   durations, including Uncontrollable Dance's timed Off-Guard. Keeps its
 *   dancing, reaction, and movement restrictions in the effect and chat card
 *   as manual instructions; does not replace compelled actions with Slowed.
 * - Retains v8.30's working Prismatic Wall changes; SPELL_RULES stays empty.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.30
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Fixes the Prismatic Wall / Modifiers Matter null-spellcasting error.
 *   Referenced spells use a temporary save item linked to the original
 *   caster and spellcasting entry; no spells are added to or edited on actors.
 *   Its UUID resolves to the actual cast spell for chat integrations, while
 *   its rules, traits, name, and rank come from the component being resolved.
 * - Excludes crossing saves from the ordinary save processor, preventing
 *   duplicate resolution and false "no matching spell card" warnings.
 * - Retains the caster's spell origin and the component rank on ongoing-save
 *   trackers, fixing the same error on later Petrify saves. Older trackers
 *   recover their origin from the original cast card when still available.
 * - Supplies extra save traits before native degree adjustments are computed,
 *   including incapacitation on ongoing saves.
 * - Keeps the two-phase crossing resolution and every v8.29 parsing behavior.
 *   SPELL_RULES remains empty.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.29
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Fixes PF2e 8.x rendered Chromatic Wall parsing for bracketed damage tags
 *   (for example, 20[fire] damage) and save labels that omit the visible
 *   word "basic" while retaining basic-save semantics.
 * - Keeps structured-description parsing for manual multi-save crossing spells.
 *   Table/list boundaries are preserved when reading referenced component
 *   spells, so table rows such as Chromatic Wall's Red-Green entries do not
 *   collapse into strings like "1RedThe wall..." and disappear from inference.
 * - Parses each fixed-damage color within its own bounded text window and uses
 *   flexible typed-damage/save grammar instead of one long sentence regex.
 * - Keeps the fifth automation entry path for persistent/manual-trigger spells
 *   that resolve multiple saving throws when a creature crosses/enters them.
 * - Detects the structural Prismatic Wall pattern without checking the spell
 *   name: seven colored component walls, each requiring its own save, with
 *   effects referenced from another wall spell.
 * - Adds a GM-only "Resolve Crossing" button to the original cast card. One
 *   click rolls the wall's light save (when not temporarily immune) plus all
 *   seven component saves against the original caster's spell DC.
 * - All component saves are rolled BEFORE any outcomes are applied, preserving
 *   Prismatic Wall's explicit simultaneous-resolution rule.
 * - Red/orange/yellow/green fixed typed damage uses native PF2e DamageRoll +
 *   actor.applyDamage(), including Basic Save scaling and IWR. Green also
 *   applies its degree-dependent Enfeebled rider.
 * - Blue/indigo resolve their referenced PF2e spells dynamically from the
 *   Chromatic Wall description/compendium links, reusing the existing generic
 *   condition/native-effect/ongoing-save/immunity engines at the parent DC.
 * - Violet automates Slowed on failure and records critical-failure plane shift
 *   as a manual relocation result rather than inventing a destination plane.
 * - The wall's separate light/blinding save receives a source-specific 1-hour
 *   temporary-immunity tracker, so repeat crossings do not repeat that save.
 * - The caster is recognized as able to pass through and ignore the wall.
 * - Keeps every working v8.25 behavior; SPELL_RULES remains empty.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.25
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Adds a second automation entry path for no-save spells whose outcome is
 *   determined by target level rather than a saving throw.
 * - Such spell cards receive a GM-only "Resolve Target" button. The macro
 *   resolves exactly one currently targeted creature directly from the cast
 *   card, so no artificial save button/message is required.
 * - Level bands such as "13th or Lower", "14th-15th", and "16th or
 *   Higher" are parsed generically from spell prose and shifted by textual
 *   heightening such as "Heightened (+1) ... levels ... increase by 2".
 * - Random condition durations such as "stunned for 1d6 rounds" are rolled
 *   once in chat and converted into a native timed PF2e condition effect.
 * - Generic "once targeted ... temporarily immune for X" wording creates a
 *   source-specific timed immunity tracker and blocks repeat resolution while
 *   it remains active. Spell-trait immunity checks still apply normally.
 * - No spell names are hard-coded; Power Word: Stun is handled by the generic
 *   no-save level-threshold grammar.
 * - Keeps every working v8.24 behavior.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.24
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Adds generic target-dependent damage-type substitution inferred from
 *   spell prose. Example: a creature with void healing takes vitality
 *   damage instead of the spell's void damage.
 * - Substitutions operate on a clone of the already-evaluated shared PF2e
 *   DamageRoll, so every target keeps the exact same dice results while only
 *   the relevant DamageInstance type changes before native PF2e IWR.
 * - Void-healing detection uses PF2e's prepared negativeHealing state and
 *   self:negative-healing roll option rather than creature names/traits.
 * - No spell names are hard-coded; the source and replacement damage types
 *   are read from "takes X damage instead of Y damage" wording.
 * - Keeps every working v8.23 behavior.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.23
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Adds a generic PF2e spell-immunity gate before any automatic spell
 *   outcome is prepared or applied. The gate projects the spell's actual
 *   traits into PF2e effect roll-options and tests the target's prepared
 *   Immunity objects with their native predicate engine.
 * - Trait/effect immunities such as mental, emotion, fear, auditory, visual,
 *   disease, magical, holy/unholy, and homebrew predicate-based immunities
 *   can therefore block the entire spell result without spell-name rules.
 * - Immunity exceptions remain PF2e-owned because each prepared Immunity's
 *   own `.test()` method decides whether the spell matches.
 * - Immune targets never pre-roll damage, receive conditions/native effects,
 *   enter secondary/ongoing saves, healing branches, or terminal riders.
 * - Master cards show immune targets explicitly, e.g. [Immune: Mental].
 * - Keeps every working v8.22 behavior.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * v8.21
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Detects native PF2e spell-effect links (Compendium.pf2e.spell-effects)
 *   and maps them to degree-of-success outcomes generically.
 * - Native spell-effect riders now inherit through outcome text such as
 *   "As Failure" / "As Success" just like conditions and damage do.
 * - Native-only outcomes are applied independently of ordinary conditions.
 * - Native Effect application now mirrors a normal PF2e compendium drag first,
 *   then adds origin/check metadata only after the item has been embedded.
 * - Removes the reserved `core.sourceId` mutation that could cause native
 *   Effect creation to be rejected by Foundry/PF2e.
 * - Adds direct spell-effects pack loading and a pristine-clone fallback, plus
 *   explicit diagnostics for every native Effect creation stage.
 * - Prefers applying the system's own Effect item for complex spell riders
 *   instead of trying to reconstruct its Rule Elements from prose.
 * - Outcome mapping uses the effect link's enclosing degree section first,
 *   then parenthetical/label degree names such as "(Success)".
 * - Native Effect items keep their PF2e Rule Elements and duration; creation
 *   is delegated to PF2e so start time/initiative are initialized natively.
 * - The effect's level is set to the actual cast rank when possible.
 * - Keeps v8.16 target-relative duration handling and every v8.13-v8.15 fix.
 * - Uses Foundry v13 `combatTurn` + `combatRound` as authoritative end-turn
 *   trigger. It fires after every turn-order progression and supplies explicit
 *   prior/current combat history, including last-turn -> next-round wraps.
 * - Resolves the actor from `prior.tokenId`, so end-of-turn saves belong to
 *   the combatant whose turn actually ended instead of whichever combatant is
 *   currently active after the update.
 * - Ignores reverse turn movement and guards each tracker with its existing
 *   per-turn stamp to avoid duplicate resolutions.
 * - Fixes ongoing-save terminal conditions being incorrectly applied as
 *   immediate conditions just because they are mentioned in the same outcome
 *   paragraph. Terminal conditions are now reserved for the state-machine
 *   threshold and are not added on the initial save.
 * - Adds generic ongoing end-of-turn save state machines for effects whose
 *   outcome text says to save again at the end of each turn.
 * - Ongoing effects remember the original spell DC and extra save traits.
 * - The changing valued condition is created as a source-specific condition
 *   item, so an unrelated permanent condition of the same name is untouched.
 * - Recognizes "As failure" outcome inheritance so a critical failure can
 *   inherit the ongoing state while overriding only its initial value.
 * - Can end the state when its tracked condition reaches 0 or is removed, and
 *   can transition to a permanent terminal condition when the text says the
 *   creature becomes unable to act.
 * - Keeps every working v8.7 behavior.
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------
 * - Keeps every working v8.6 behavior.
 * - No spell names are hard-coded into automatic inference.
 * - Linked-condition parsing now recognizes dependency-first wording such as:
 *      "while it is Sickened, it is also Slowed 1"
 *   in addition to source-first wording such as:
 *      "Slowed 1 for as long as it is Sickened".
 *   This keeps dependent riders attached to their prerequisite condition
 *   instead of applying them as unrelated standalone conditions.
 * - Adds generic target-mode healing: when spell text gives one target
 *   class healing while another class receives structured damage, matching
 *   healing targets use the same evaluated roll at full value and ignore an
 *   irrelevant save degree. Target classes are inferred from "living" or
 *   CONFIG.PF2E.creatureTraits rather than spell names.
 * - Linked "while CONDITION" riders now use ordinary granted conditions
 *   instead of in-memory restricted grants. Removing the dependency deletes
 *   the linked grant; manually deleting the linked condition is no longer
 *   blocked by Foundry.
 * - Adds generic target-conditional damage from spell descriptions.
 *   Example pattern: an additional typed damage roll against creatures
 *   with a particular PF2e creature trait. The target trait is read from
 *   CONFIG.PF2E.creatureTraits; no spell names or creature traits are
 *   hard-coded into the conditional-damage engine.
 * - Conditional damage is rolled once per cast/cycle and reused for every
 *   matching target, then scaled by that target's primary save outcome.
 * - Nonmatching targets never receive the conditional damage application.
 * - Conditional riders are parsed independently instead of causing
 *   the whole outcome to become manual.
 * - Supports linked conditions such as:
 *      "Fleeing for as long as it is Frightened"
 *   and retires the link when its dependency first ends, preventing
 *   unrelated future conditions from re-triggering the spell rider.
 * - Supports outcome-defined damage formulas and executable PF2e
 *   @Damage expressions, including rank-dependent formulas.
 * - Supports text-defined per-degree heightening.
 * - Supports secondary saves embedded inside a degree of success.
 * - Secondary-branch damage is rolled only AFTER the secondary save
 *   selects that branch; unused branch damage is no longer pre-rolled.
 * - Generic terminal-death text such as "the target dies" now applies
 *   Foundry/PF2e's configured Dead status automatically.
 * - Generic "reduced to 0 HP by this spell -> death" text is checked
 *   only AFTER native PF2e damage/IWR resolution; Dead is applied only
 *   when this particular damage application crosses the threshold.
 * - When Dead is applied, Dying and Unconscious are cleared on a
 *   best-effort basis so ordinary 0-HP state does not remain alongside
 *   an explicit instant-death result.
 * - Structured Basic Saves and structured custom-save damage still
 *   use PF2e's native spell.getDamage() path.
 * - Damage rolls remain serialized so deleting a damage chat card
 *   restores the SAME evaluated result.
 * - Deleting the master resolution card starts a fresh cycle.
 *
 * Explicit SPELL_RULES remain exception overrides.
 ********************************************************************/

const DEBUG = true;
const ACTIVE_CAST_WINDOW_MS = 15000;
const MAX_CACHED_CASTS = 200;

// =================================================================
// UTILITIES
// =================================================================

function normalizeSlug(value) {
    return String(value ?? "")
        .normalize("NFKD")
        .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .trim()
        .replace(/['’]/g, "")
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
        .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
}

function escapeHTML(value) {
    return String(value ?? "")
        .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
        .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
        .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
        .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
        .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
}

function escapeRegex(value) {
    return String(value ?? "").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}

function debug(...args) {
    if (!DEBUG) return;
    console.log(
        "%cPF2e Auto Spell Resolution |",
        "color:#8ac7ff;font-weight:bold",
        ...args
    );
}

function deepClone(value) {
    return foundry.utils.deepClone(value);
}

// EXPLICIT EXCEPTION RULES
// =================================================================

const SPELL_RULES = {
    // Intentionally empty.
    // Keep this object only as an emergency override hook for future spells
    // whose mechanics cannot be represented safely by generic inference.
};

if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_SPELL_CONDITIONS_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("createChatMessage", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_SPELL_CONDITIONS_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_RENDER_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("renderChatMessageHTML", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_RENDER_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_DAMAGE_BUTTON_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("renderChatMessageHTML", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_DAMAGE_BUTTON_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_DELETE_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("deleteChatMessage", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_DELETE_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_LINKED_CREATE_ITEM_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("createItem", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_LINKED_CREATE_ITEM_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_LINKED_UPDATE_ITEM_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("updateItem", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_LINKED_UPDATE_ITEM_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_LINKED_DELETE_ITEM_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("deleteItem", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_LINKED_DELETE_ITEM_HOOK);
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_TURN_HOOK) {
    // Remove historical/current turn hook names so rerunning/upgrading is safe.
    try { Hooks.off("combatTurnChange", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_TURN_HOOK); } catch (_) {}
    try { Hooks.off("combatTurn", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_TURN_HOOK); } catch (_) {}
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_ROUND_HOOK) {
    try { Hooks.off("combatRound", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_ROUND_HOOK); } catch (_) {}
}
if (globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_ITEM_HOOK) {
    Hooks.off("deleteItem", globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_ITEM_HOOK);
}

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_SPELL_STATE = {
    processed: new Set(),
    recentSpells: [],
    activeCastId: null,
    activeCastAt: 0,
    scrollCardJobs: new Map()
};

const STATE = globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_SPELL_STATE;

// =================================================================
// DEGREES / REROLLS
// =================================================================

function normalizeDegree(value) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) return null;

    if (typeof value === "number") {
        return ["criticalFailure", "failure", "success", "criticalSuccess"][value] ?? null;
    }

    const text = String(value).toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_-]/g, "");

    return {
        criticalfailure: "criticalFailure",
        critfailure: "criticalFailure",
        criticalfail: "criticalFailure",
        failure: "failure",
        fail: "failure",
        success: "success",
        criticalsuccess: "criticalSuccess",
        critsuccess: "criticalSuccess"
    }[text] ?? null;
}

function getDegree(message) {
    const pf2e = message.flags?.pf2e ?? {};
    const roll = message.rolls?.[0];

    for (const candidate of [
        pf2e.context?.outcome,
        pf2e.context?.degreeOfSuccess,
        pf2e.context?.degree,
        pf2e.outcome,
        pf2e.degreeOfSuccess,
        roll?.degreeOfSuccess,
        roll?.options?.degreeOfSuccess,
        message.system?.degreeOfSuccess
    ]) {
        const degree = normalizeDegree(candidate);
        if (degree) return degree;
    }

    return null;
}

function isRerollMessage(message) {
    const context = message.flags?.pf2e?.context ?? {};
    const options = Array.isArray(context.options) ? context.options : [];

    return context.isReroll === true
        || options.includes("check:reroll")
        || options.includes("check:reroll:hero-points");
}

function getSpellFlowReference(message) {
    const reference = message.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.spellFlow;
    if (typeof reference?.parentCastId === "string" && typeof reference?.entryId === "string") return reference;
    // PF2e 8.5 rerolls copy their system flags, not arbitrary module flags.
    // Context roll options are preserved for both GM and player rerolls.
    const options = message.flags?.pf2e?.context?.options ?? [];
    const references = Array.isArray(options) ? options.map(option => String(option).match(/^pf2e-auto-spell-flow:([^:]+):([^:]+)$/))
        .filter(Boolean) : [];
    if (references.length !== 1) return null;
    return { parentCastId: references[0][1], entryId: references[0][2] };
}

function readableDegree(degree) {
    return {
        criticalSuccess: "Critical Success",
        success: "Success",
        failure: "Failure",
        criticalFailure: "Critical Failure"
    }[degree] ?? degree;
}

// =================================================================
// STANDARD BASIC SAVE PROFILE
// =================================================================

function standardBasicOutcomes() {
    return {
        criticalSuccess: {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            damageMultiplier: 0,
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "Basic save: no damage"
        },
        success: {
            damage: { kind: "shared", multiplier: 0.5 },
            damageMultiplier: 0.5,
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "Basic save: half damage"
        },
        failure: {
            damage: { kind: "shared", multiplier: 1 },
            damageMultiplier: 1,
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "Basic save: full damage"
        },
        criticalFailure: {
            damage: { kind: "shared", multiplier: 2 },
            damageMultiplier: 2,
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "Basic save: double damage"
        }
    };
}

// =================================================================
// ACTOR / TOKEN RESOLUTION
// =================================================================

function getTokenDocumentFromSpeaker(message) {
    const speaker = message.speaker ?? {};

    if (speaker.scene && speaker.token) {
        const scene = game.scenes.get(speaker.scene);
        const token = scene?.tokens.get(speaker.token);
        if (token) return token;
    }

    if (speaker.token) {
        const token = canvas.tokens?.get(speaker.token);
        if (token?.document) return token.document;
    }

    return null;
}

function getActorFromSpeaker(message) {
    const token = getTokenDocumentFromSpeaker(message);

    if (token?.actor) {
        debug("Resolved actor from scene token:", token.actor.name);
        return token.actor;
    }

    try {
        if (message.actor) return message.actor;
    } catch (_) {}

    return message.speaker?.actor
        ? game.actors.get(message.speaker.actor) ?? null
        : null;
}

// =================================================================
// SPELL CARD DETECTION / CACHE
// =================================================================

function getEmbeddedSpellFromCard(message, actor) {
    const casting = message.flags?.pf2e?.casting;
    const source = casting?.embeddedSpell;
    if (!actor || source?.type !== "spell") return null;

    // PF2e reconstructs the item from its saved source and applies the card's
    // casting entry, heightening, and overlays. The consumed scroll need not
    // still exist in inventory, and no spell needs to be added to the actor.
    try {
        const item = message.item;
        if (item?.type === "spell" && item.actor?.uuid === actor.uuid) return item;
    } catch (error) {
        debug("Embedded spell card getter failed; trying its saved source:", error.message);
    }

    const ItemClass = CONFIG.Item?.documentClass;
    if (typeof ItemClass !== "function") return null;
    try {
        const spell = new ItemClass(deepClone(source), { parent: actor });
        const origin = message.flags?.pf2e?.origin;
        return spell.loadVariant?.({
            entryId: casting.id,
            castRank: origin?.castRank ?? spell.rank,
            overlayIds: origin?.variant?.overlays
        }) ?? spell;
    } catch (error) {
        debug("Embedded spell source could not be restored:", error.message);
        return null;
    }
}

function getEmbeddedSpellCardKey(message) {
    const pf2e = message?.flags?.pf2e;
    if (!pf2e?.casting?.embeddedSpell) return null;
    return JSON.stringify({ casting: pf2e.casting, origin: pf2e.origin, content: message.content });
}

function refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(castEntry) {
    if (castEntry?.flowParentCastId) {
        const parent = findCastById(castEntry.flowParentCastId);
        return !parent || refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(parent);
    }
    if (castEntry?.tableParentCastId) {
        const parent = findCastById(castEntry.tableParentCastId);
        return !parent || refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(parent);
    }
    if (!castEntry?.isEmbeddedSpell) return true;
    const message = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId);
    const key = getEmbeddedSpellCardKey(message);
    if (!key || key === castEntry.embeddedCardKey) return true;
    const hasResults = Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {}).some(group => group.targets?.length)
        || castEntry.randomTableState?.exposures?.length || castEntry.spellFlowState?.entries?.length;
    if (hasResults) {
        if (castEntry.embeddedMismatchKey !== key) {
            ui.notifications.warn(`${castEntry.spell.name}: this spell card changed after saving throws were recorded. Clear its resolution card and roll fresh saves for the new variant.`);
            castEntry.embeddedMismatchKey = key;
        }
        return false;
    }
    const refreshed = detectSpellCard(message);
    if (!refreshed) return false;
    for (const field of ["spell", "actor", "save", "dc", "castRank", "embeddedCardKey"]) {
        castEntry[field] = refreshed[field];
    }
    castEntry.resolvedSpell = null;
    castEntry.automationProfile = null;
    castEntry.embeddedMismatchKey = null;
    return true;
}

function getScrollFromCard(message) {
    const content = message.content ?? "";
    if (!content.includes("pf2e chat-card item-card")
        || message.flags?.pf2e?.casting?.embeddedSpell
        || /data-cast-rank=/.test(content)) return null;
    const itemId = content.match(/data-item-id="([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
    if (!itemId) return null;
    const actor = getActorFromSpeaker(message);
    const item = actor?.items.get(itemId);
    return item?.type === "consumable"
        && (item.category ?? item.system?.category) === "scroll"
        && item.system?.spell?.type === "spell"
        ? item : null;
}

async function showScrollSpellCard(message) {
    if (!game.user.isGM) return;
    const scroll = getScrollFromCard(message);
    if (!scroll) { ui.notifications.warn("That scroll is no longer available on its actor."); return; }
    if (scroll.isIdentified === false) { ui.notifications.warn("Identify the scroll before showing its spell card."); return; }
    const key = scroll.uuid;
    if (STATE.scrollCardJobs.has(key)) return await STATE.scrollCardJobs.get(key);

    const job = (async () => {
        if (!scroll.actor.spellcasting?.canCastConsumable?.(scroll)) {
            ui.notifications.warn(`${scroll.actor.name}: PF2e found no eligible spellcasting entry for this scroll. Use the normal Use button for Trick Magic Item, or check the actor's spellcasting setup.`);
            return;
        }
        if (typeof scroll.castEmbeddedSpell !== "function") {
            throw new Error("This PF2e version does not expose the scroll's native spell-card method.");
        }
        // This method selects PF2e's best eligible entry and calls its cast()
        // with consume:false. Inventory consumption only happens in consume().
        await scroll.castEmbeddedSpell();
        debug("SCROLL SPELL CARD POSTED:", { scroll: scroll.name, caster: scroll.actor.name });
    })();
    STATE.scrollCardJobs.set(key, job);
    try { return await job; }
    finally { STATE.scrollCardJobs.delete(key); }
}

function detectSpellCard(message) {
    const content = message.content ?? "";

    // Cache every actual PF2e spell card, not only cards containing a save
    // button. Save-based automation still enters through processSave(), while
    // no-save target-threshold spells are resolved directly from the cast card.
    if (!content.includes("pf2e chat-card item-card")) {
        return null;
    }

    const itemMatch = content.match(/data-item-id="([^"]+)"/);
    const actorMatch = content.match(/data-actor-id="([^"]+)"/);
    const tokenMatch = content.match(/data-token-id="([^"]+)"/);
    const saveMatch = content.match(/data-save="([^"]+)"/);
    const dcMatch = content.match(/data-dc="(\d+)"/);
    const rankMatch = content.match(/data-cast-rank="(\d+)"/);

    if (!itemMatch) return null;

    let actor = getActorFromSpeaker(message);

    if (!actor && tokenMatch) {
        const parts = tokenMatch[1].split(".");
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
            actor = game.scenes.get(parts[0])?.tokens.get(parts[1])?.actor ?? null;
        }
    }

    if (!actor && actorMatch) actor = game.actors.get(actorMatch[1]);
    if (!actor) return null;

    const embeddedSource = message.flags?.pf2e?.casting?.embeddedSpell;
    const spell = embeddedSource
        ? getEmbeddedSpellFromCard(message, actor)
        : actor.items.get(itemMatch[1]);
    if (!spell || spell.type !== "spell") return null;

    return {
        castId: message.id,
        spell,
        actor,
        isEmbeddedSpell: !!embeddedSource,
        embeddedCardKey: getEmbeddedSpellCardKey(message),
        save: saveMatch?.[1]?.toLowerCase() ?? null,
        dc: dcMatch ? Number(dcMatch[1]) : null,
        castRank: rankMatch ? Number(rankMatch[1])
            : embeddedSource ? Number(message.flags?.pf2e?.origin?.castRank ?? spell.rank) : null,

        resolvedSpell: null,
        automationProfile: null,

        sharedDamageRolled: false,
        sharedDamageRolling: false,
        sharedDamageMessageId: null,
        sharedDamageFormula: null,
        sharedDamageRollOptions: [],
        sharedDamageRollJSON: null,

        // Per-degree / branch rolls for spells whose damage formula
        // is written directly in outcome text instead of system.damage.
        customDamageStates: {},

        resolutionMessageId: null,
        resolutionQueue: Promise.resolve(),
        resultGroups: {},

        // Direct-resolution state for no-save target-threshold spells.
        directResolvedCycle: null,
        directResolution: null,

        cycle: 1,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    };
}

function findCastById(castId) {
    const direct = STATE.recentSpells.find(entry => entry.castId === castId);
    if (direct) return direct;
    for (const parent of STATE.recentSpells) {
        const exposure = parent.randomTableState?.exposures?.find(entry => entry.castId === castId);
        if (exposure) return exposure;
    }
    return null;
}

function pruneCastCache() {
    const seen = new Set();

    STATE.recentSpells = STATE.recentSpells
        .filter(entry => {
            if (!entry?.castId || seen.has(entry.castId)) return false;
            seen.add(entry.castId);
            return !!game.messages.get(entry.castId);
        })
        .slice(0, MAX_CACHED_CASTS);
}

function resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry) {
    if (castEntry.resolvedSpell) return castEntry.resolvedSpell;

    const spell = castEntry.spell;
    const castRank = castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank;
    let resolved = spell;

    if (castRank && typeof spell.loadVariant === "function") {
        try {
            resolved = spell.loadVariant({ castRank }) ?? spell;
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn(
                "PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not load heightened spell:",
                spell.name,
                error
            );
        }
    }

    castEntry.resolvedSpell = resolved;
    return resolved;
}

function inspectSpellItem(spell) {
    const save = spell.system?.defense?.save ?? null;
    const damage = spell.system?.damage ?? {};
    const damageEntries = Object.entries(damage);

    let damageKinds = [];
    try {
        if (spell.damageKinds) damageKinds = Array.from(spell.damageKinds);
    } catch (_) {}

    return {
        saveType: save?.statistic ?? null,
        basicSave: save?.basic === true,
        damageCount: damageEntries.length,
        damageKinds,
        damageComponents: damageEntries.map(([id, component]) => ({
            id,
            formula: component?.formula ?? null,
            type: component?.type ?? null,
            category: component?.category ?? null,
            applyMod: component?.applyMod ?? false
        })),
        heighteningType: spell.system?.heightening?.type ?? null,
        heightening: spell.system?.heightening ?? null
    };
}

function cacheSpellCast(message) {
    const existing = findCastById(message.id);
    if (existing) {
        existing.timestamp = Date.now();
        return existing;
    }

    const detected = detectSpellCard(message);
    if (!detected) return null;

    STATE.recentSpells.unshift(detected);
    STATE.activeCastId = detected.castId;
    STATE.activeCastAt = Date.now();
    pruneCastCache();

    const inspection = inspectSpellItem(resolveSpellAtCastRank(detected));

    debug("Cached spell cast:", {
        castId: detected.castId,
        spell: detected.spell.name,
        caster: detected.actor.name,
        embeddedSpell: detected.isEmbeddedSpell,
        save: detected.save,
        dc: detected.dc,
        castRank: detected.castRank,
        itemBasicSave: inspection.basicSave,
        structuredDamage: inspection.damageCount,
        heightening: inspection.heighteningType
    });

    return detected;
}

function activateSpellCard(message) {
    let castEntry = findCastById(message.id);

    if (!castEntry) {
        castEntry = detectSpellCard(message);
        if (castEntry) {
            STATE.recentSpells.unshift(castEntry);
            pruneCastCache();
        }
    }

    if (!castEntry) return;

    castEntry.timestamp = Date.now();
    STATE.activeCastId = castEntry.castId;
    STATE.activeCastAt = Date.now();

    debug("Activated spell card:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        castId: castEntry.castId,
        cycle: castEntry.cycle
    });
}

// =================================================================
// NO-SAVE TARGET-LEVEL SPELLS
// =================================================================
//
// Some PF2e spells do not call for a save at all. Their effect is selected
// from target-level bands written directly in the spell text. These spells
// need a direct cast-card resolver because no save chat message will ever be
// created to enter processSave().
// =================================================================

function getCreatureLevel(actor) {
    for (const candidate of [
        actor?.level,
        actor?.system?.details?.level?.value,
        actor?.system?.details?.level,
        actor?.system?.level?.value,
        actor?.system?.level
    ]) {
        const value = Number(candidate);
        if (Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
    }
    return null;
}

function isPotentialNoSaveLevelThresholdSpell(castEntry) {
    if (!castEntry) return false;
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const inspection = inspectSpellItem(spell);
    if (inspection.saveType) return false;

    const plain = htmlToPlain(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const hasBand = /\b\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)\s+or\s+(?:lower|higher)\b/i.test(plain)
        || /\b\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)\s*[\u2013\u2014-]\s*\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)\b/i.test(plain);

    return hasBand && /\btarget(?:'s)?\s+level\b|\beffect[^.]{0,80}\bdepends?\s+on\s+the\s+target(?:'s)?\s+level\b/i.test(plain);
}

function parseRandomDuration(text) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    const match = normalized.match(
        /\bfor\s+(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s+(rounds?|minutes?|hours?)\b/
    );
    if (!match) return null;

    const unit = match[2].startsWith("round")
        ? "rounds"
        : match[2].startsWith("minute")
            ? "minutes"
            : "hours";

    return {
        formula: match[1].replace(/\s+/g, ""),
        unit,
        expiry: unit === "rounds" ? "turn-start" : null,
        anchor: "effect-start"
    };
}

function extractLevelBandHeadings(plain) {
    const source = String(plain ?? "");
    const regex = /(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)\s+or\s+(lower|higher)\b|(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)\s*[\u2013\u2014-]\s*(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)\b/gi;
    const headings = [];

    for (const match of source.matchAll(regex)) {
        let min = null;
        let max = null;
        let kind = null;

        if (match[1]) {
            const level = Number(match[1]);
            kind = String(match[2]).toLowerCase();
            if (kind === "lower") max = level;
            else min = level;
        } else {
            min = Number(match[3]);
            max = Number(match[4]);
            kind = "range";
        }

        headings.push({
            start: match.index,
            end: match.index + match[0].length,
            heading: match[0],
            min,
            max,
            kind
        });
    }

    return headings;
}

function inferLevelBandHeightening(spell, castEntry, plain) {
    const match = String(plain ?? "").match(
        /Heightened\s*\(\s*\+(\d+)\s*\)[^.]*?levels?[^.]*?(?:increase|increases|increased)\s+by\s+(\d+)/i
    );

    if (!match) return null;

    const interval = Number(match[1]);
    const increasePerStep = Number(match[2]);
    const baseRank = getSpellBaseRank(spell);
    const castRank = Number(castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank ?? baseRank);

    if (!interval || !increasePerStep || !baseRank || !castRank) return null;

    const steps = Math.max(0, Math.floor((castRank - baseRank) / interval));
    return {
        interval,
        increasePerStep,
        baseRank,
        castRank,
        steps,
        levelShift: steps * increasePerStep
    };
}

function inferTemporaryTargetImmunity(plain) {
    const sentence = String(plain ?? "").match(
        /(?:^|[.!?]\s+)([^.!?]*\btemporarily\s+immune\b[^.!?]*)/i
    )?.[1] ?? null;

    if (!sentence) return null;
    if (!/\b(?:once|after|when)\b[^.]{0,80}\btarget(?:ed|ing)?\b/i.test(sentence)) return null;

    const duration = parseSimpleDuration(sentence);
    if (!duration) return null;

    return {
        duration,
        sourceText: sentence.trim()
    };
}

async function inferNoSaveLevelThresholdRule(castEntry) {
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const inspection = inspectSpellItem(spell);
    if (inspection.saveType) return { supported: false, reason: "Spell has a save" };

    const plain = htmlToPlain(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const headings = extractLevelBandHeadings(plain);
    if (!headings.length) return { supported: false, reason: "No target-level bands found" };

    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const heightening = inferLevelBandHeightening(spell, castEntry, plain);
    const shift = Number(heightening?.levelShift ?? 0);
    const bands = [];

    for (let index = 0; index < headings.length; index++) {
        const heading = headings[index];
        const nextStart = headings[index + 1]?.start ?? plain.length;
        let effectText = plain.slice(heading.end, nextStart).trim();
        effectText = effectText.split(/\bHeightened\s*\(/i)[0].trim();

        const conditions = await extractConditionsFromText(effectText, catalog);
        for (const condition of conditions) {
            const snippet = getConditionSnippet(effectText, condition.name);
            condition.duration ??= parseRandomDuration(snippet);
        }

        if (!conditions.length) continue;

        bands.push({
            heading: heading.heading,
            baseMin: heading.min,
            baseMax: heading.max,
            min: heading.min === null ? null : heading.min + shift,
            max: heading.max === null ? null : heading.max + shift,
            conditions,
            rawText: effectText
        });
    }

    if (!bands.length) {
        return {
            supported: false,
            reason: "Level bands were found, but no supported condition effects were recognized"
        };
    }

    return {
        supported: true,
        bands,
        heightening,
        temporaryImmunity: inferTemporaryTargetImmunity(plain),
        rawText: plain
    };
}

function findNoSaveLevelBand(rule, targetLevel) {
    return (rule?.bands ?? []).find(band => {
        if (band.min !== null && targetLevel < band.min) return false;
        if (band.max !== null && targetLevel > band.max) return false;
        return true;
    }) ?? null;
}

function getTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(actor, castEntry) {
    const spellSlug = normalizeSlug(castEntry?.spell?.slug ?? castEntry?.spell?.name);
    if (!actor || !spellSlug) return null;

    return actor.items.find(item =>
        item.type === "effect"
        && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.temporarySpellImmunity?.spellSlug === spellSlug
        && item.system?.expired !== true
        && item.active !== false
    ) ?? null;
}

async function applyTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(actor, castEntry, spec) {
    if (!actor || !spec?.duration) return null;

    const existing = getTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(actor, castEntry);
    if (existing) return existing;

    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const spellSlug = normalizeSlug(spell.slug ?? spell.name);
    const duration = spec.duration;

    const source = {
        name: `${spell.name}: Temporary Immunity`,
        type: "effect",
        img: spell.img ?? "systems/pf2e/icons/default-icons/effect.svg",
        flags: {
            pf2eAutoSpellResolution: {
                temporarySpellImmunity: {
                    spellSlug,
                    sourceSpell: spell.uuid ?? castEntry.spell.uuid ?? null,
                    castId: castEntry.castId
                }
            }
        },
        system: {
            description: {
                value: `<p>Temporarily immune to <strong>${escapeHTML(spell.name)}</strong>. Automatically inferred from the spell's targeting text.</p>`
            },
            duration: {
                value: Number(duration.value),
                unit: duration.unit,
                expiry: duration.expiry ?? null,
                sustained: false
            },
            rules: [],
            start: {
                value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0,
                initiative: game.combat?.combatant?.initiative ?? null
            },
            tokenIcon: { show: true },
            traits: { value: [] }
        }
    };

    const [created] = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
    return created ?? null;
}

async function rollNoSaveDuration(castEntry, targetActor, condition) {
    const formula = condition?.duration?.formula;
    if (!formula) return condition?.duration ?? null;

    const roll = await new Roll(formula).evaluate();
    await roll.toMessage({
        speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
        flavor: `${castEntry.spell.name} — ${condition.name ?? condition.condition} duration for ${targetActor.name}`
    });

    const total = Number(roll.total);
    if (!Number.isFinite(total)) {
        throw new Error(`Could not evaluate duration formula ${formula}.`);
    }

    debug("NO-SAVE RANDOM DURATION ROLLED:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        target: targetActor.name,
        condition: condition.condition,
        formula,
        total
    });

    return {
        value: total,
        unit: condition.duration.unit,
        expiry: condition.duration.expiry ?? null,
        anchor: condition.duration.anchor ?? "effect-start"
    };
}

function buildNoSaveResolutionCard(castEntry, resolution) {
    const spell = castEntry.spell;
    const target = resolution.target;

    let resultHTML = "";
    if (resolution.alreadyTemporarilyImmune) {
        resultHTML = `<strong>Unaffected</strong> — already temporarily immune to this spell.`;
    } else if (resolution.spellImmunity) {
        resultHTML = `<strong>Immune: ${escapeHTML(resolution.spellImmunity.label)}</strong> — spell effect blocked by PF2e immunity.`;
    } else {
        const effects = (resolution.appliedEffects ?? []).map(effect => {
            let text = escapeHTML(effect.name ?? effect.condition);
            if (effect.value !== null && effect.value !== undefined) text += ` ${effect.value}`;
            if (effect.durationRoll) text += ` for ${effect.durationRoll} ${escapeHTML(effect.durationUnit ?? "rounds")}`;
            else if (effect.duration?.value) text += ` for ${effect.duration.value} ${escapeHTML(effect.duration.unit)}`;
            return text;
        }).join(", ") || "No supported effect";
        resultHTML = `<strong>${effects}</strong>`;
    }

    const temp = resolution.temporaryImmunityApplied
        ? `<div style="margin-top:4px;opacity:0.8;">Temporary immunity applied: ${resolution.temporaryImmunityApplied}</div>`
        : "";

    return `
        <div class="pf2e-auto-direct-resolution-card" style="font-size:0.86em;line-height:1.2;">
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;">
                <strong>${escapeHTML(spell.name)} — Direct Resolution</strong>
                <span style="opacity:0.65;">Cycle ${castEntry.cycle}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-top:5px;border-top:1px solid rgba(128,128,128,0.25);padding-top:5px;">
                <div><strong>${escapeHTML(target.name)}</strong> — Level ${resolution.targetLevel ?? "?"}</div>
                ${resolution.bandHeading ? `<div style="opacity:0.8;">Matched: ${escapeHTML(resolution.bandHeading)}</div>` : ""}
                <div style="margin-top:3px;">${resultHTML}</div>
                ${temp}
            </div>
        </div>`;
}

async function resolveNoSaveTarget(castEntry, targetActor, targetToken = null) {
    const automation = await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);
    if (!automation || automation.kind !== "no-save-level-threshold") {
        throw new Error(`${castEntry.spell.name} is not a supported no-save level-threshold spell.`);
    }

    if (castEntry.directResolvedCycle === castEntry.cycle) {
        ui.notifications.warn(`${castEntry.spell.name}: this cast has already been resolved. Delete its resolution card to start a fresh cycle.`);
        return null;
    }

    const rule = automation.noSaveRule;
    const targetLevel = getCreatureLevel(targetActor);
    if (!Number.isFinite(targetLevel)) {
        throw new Error(`Could not determine ${targetActor.name}'s creature level.`);
    }

    const priorTemporaryImmunity = getTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(targetActor, castEntry);
    const band = findNoSaveLevelBand(rule, targetLevel);
    const resolution = {
        target: targetActor,
        targetLevel,
        bandHeading: band?.heading ?? null,
        alreadyTemporarilyImmune: !!priorTemporaryImmunity,
        temporaryImmunityApplied: null,
        spellImmunity: null,
        appliedEffects: []
    };

    // "Once targeted" occurs even if the target is otherwise immune to the
    // spell's traits, but an existing temporary immunity blocks repeat casts.
    if (!priorTemporaryImmunity && rule.temporaryImmunity) {
        await applyTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(targetActor, castEntry, rule.temporaryImmunity);
        const d = rule.temporaryImmunity.duration;
        resolution.temporaryImmunityApplied = `${d.value} ${d.unit}`;
    }

    if (!priorTemporaryImmunity) {
        resolution.spellImmunity = getSpellEffectImmunity(targetActor, castEntry);
    }

    if (!priorTemporaryImmunity && !resolution.spellImmunity) {
        if (!band) {
            throw new Error(`${castEntry.spell.name}: target level ${targetLevel} did not match any inferred level band.`);
        }

        for (const baseEffect of band.conditions ?? []) {
            const effect = deepClone(baseEffect);
            let duration = effect.duration ?? null;
            let durationRoll = null;

            if (duration?.formula) {
                duration = await rollNoSaveDuration(castEntry, targetActor, effect);
                durationRoll = duration.value;
            }

            if (duration) {
                await applyTimedCondition(targetActor, resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry), {
                    ...effect,
                    duration
                });
            } else {
                await applyCondition(targetActor, effect.condition, effect.value);
            }

            resolution.appliedEffects.push({
                ...effect,
                duration,
                durationRoll,
                durationUnit: duration?.unit ?? null
            });
        }
    }

    if (priorTemporaryImmunity) {
        debug("NO-SAVE SPELL BLOCKED BY TEMPORARY IMMUNITY:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            target: targetActor.name,
            targetLevel
        });
    } else if (resolution.spellImmunity) {
        debug("NO-SAVE SPELL BLOCKED BY IMMUNITY:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            target: targetActor.name,
            targetLevel,
            immunity: resolution.spellImmunity
        });
    } else {
        debug("NO-SAVE LEVEL-THRESHOLD RESULT APPLIED:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            target: targetActor.name,
            targetLevel,
            matchedBand: band,
            appliedEffects: resolution.appliedEffects
        });
    }

    const created = await ChatMessage.create({
        speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
        content: buildNoSaveResolutionCard(castEntry, resolution)
    });

    castEntry.resolutionMessageId = created?.id ?? null;
    castEntry.directResolvedCycle = castEntry.cycle;
    castEntry.directResolution = resolution;

    return resolution;
}


// =================================================================
// MANUAL-TRIGGER MULTI-SAVE CROSSING SPELLS
// =================================================================
//
// Some persistent spells do nothing when cast directly onto a creature, so
// PF2e correctly gives their cast cards no save button.  Their mechanics fire
// later when a creature voluntarily enters/crosses the effect.  The canonical
// structural pattern handled here is a layered wall whose prose says a creature
// must attempt a saving throw against each component wall.
//
// This path is deliberately description/reference driven rather than spell-name
// driven.  It resolves referenced PF2e spells dynamically so blue/indigo style
// component effects can reuse the same generic spell engine as normal casts.
// =================================================================

const PF2E_REFERENCED_SPELL_CACHE = new Map();

function getCastSpellDC(castEntry) {
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const actor = castEntry?.actor ?? spell?.actor ?? null;

    const candidates = [
        castEntry?.dc,
        spell?.spellcasting?.statistic?.dc?.value,
        spell?.spellcasting?.statistic?.dc,
        spell?.spellcasting?.dc?.value,
        actor?.getStatistic?.("spell-dc")?.dc?.value,
        actor?.system?.attributes?.spellDC?.value,
        actor?.system?.attributes?.classOrSpellDC?.value
    ];

    for (const candidate of candidates) {
        const value = Number(candidate);
        if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) return value;
    }

    return null;
}

function extractPF2eSpellLinks(html) {
    const source = String(html ?? "");
    const found = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    const add = (uuid, label = null, index = -1) => {
        if (!uuid || !/^Compendium\.pf2e\..+\.Item\./i.test(uuid)) return;
        const key = `${uuid}|${label ?? ""}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) return;
        seen.add(key);
        found.push({ uuid, label: String(label ?? "").trim() || null, index });
    };

    const raw = /@UUID\[(Compendium\.pf2e\.[^\]]+\.Item\.[^\]]+)\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi;
    for (const match of source.matchAll(raw)) {
        add(match[1], match[2] ?? null, match.index ?? -1);
    }

    const enriched = /<a\b[^>]*data-uuid=["'](Compendium\.pf2e\.[^"']+\.Item\.[^"']+)["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
    for (const match of source.matchAll(enriched)) {
        const label = String(match[2] ?? "")
            .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ")
            .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
            .trim();
        add(match[1], label, match.index ?? -1);
    }

    return found;
}

async function findPF2eSpellByName(name) {
    const normalized = normalizeSlug(name);
    if (!normalized) return null;
    if (PF2E_REFERENCED_SPELL_CACHE.has(normalized)) {
        return PF2E_REFERENCED_SPELL_CACHE.get(normalized);
    }

    // Prefer spells-srd if present, then search other PF2e Item packs.  This
    // also permits legacy spell names referenced by legacy wall content.
    const packs = Array.from(game.packs ?? [])
        .filter(pack => pack?.documentName === "Item" && pack?.metadata?.packageName === "pf2e")
        .sort((a, b) => {
            const aPreferred = a.collection === "pf2e.spells-srd" ? 1 : 0;
            const bPreferred = b.collection === "pf2e.spells-srd" ? 1 : 0;
            return bPreferred - aPreferred;
        });

    for (const pack of packs) {
        try {
            const index = pack.index?.size ? pack.index : await pack.getIndex();
            const match = Array.from(index ?? []).find(entry =>
                normalizeSlug(entry?.name) === normalized
            );
            if (!match) continue;

            const document = await pack.getDocument(match._id);
            if (document?.type !== "spell") continue;

            PF2E_REFERENCED_SPELL_CACHE.set(normalized, document);
            return document;
        } catch (_) {}
    }

    PF2E_REFERENCED_SPELL_CACHE.set(normalized, null);
    return null;
}

async function resolveReferencedSpell(sourceSpell, requestedName) {
    const wanted = normalizeSlug(requestedName);
    if (!wanted) return null;

    for (const link of extractPF2eSpellLinks(sourceSpell?.system?.description?.value ?? "")) {
        const labelSlug = normalizeSlug(link.label ?? "");
        if (!labelSlug) continue;
        if (labelSlug !== wanted && !labelSlug.includes(wanted) && !wanted.includes(labelSlug)) continue;

        try {
            const document = await fromUuid(link.uuid);
            if (document?.type === "spell") return document;
        } catch (_) {}
    }

    return await findPF2eSpellByName(requestedName);
}

function isPotentialManualMultiSaveCrossingSpell(castEntry) {
    if (!castEntry) return false;
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const inspection = inspectSpellItem(spell);
    if (inspection.saveType) return false;

    const plain = htmlToPlain(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    return /\bseven\s+different\s+layers\b/i.test(plain)
        && /\b(?:tries|attempts?)\s+to\s+pass\s+through\s+the\s+wall\b/i.test(plain)
        && /\bsaving\s+throw\s+against\s+each\s+component\s+wall\b/i.test(plain)
        && /\bred\b[^.]{0,80}\borange\b[^.]{0,80}\byellow\b[^.]{0,80}\bgreen\b/i.test(plain);
}

function parseReferencedWallName(parentPlain) {
    const match = String(parentPlain ?? "").match(
        /\beffect\s+of\s+a\s+\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)?[- ](?:rank|level)\s+([a-z][a-z '\u2019-]+?)\s+spell\s+of\s+that\s+color\b/i
    );
    return match?.[1]?.trim() ?? null;
}

function parseLayerRanks(parentPlain) {
    const text = String(parentPlain ?? "");
    const match = text.match(
        /\bred\s*,\s*orange\s*,\s*yellow\s*,\s*and\s*green\s+have\s+the\s+effect\s+of\s+a\s+(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)?[- ](?:rank|level)[\s\S]{0,180}?\bothers\s+have\s+the\s+effect\s+of\s+a\s+(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)?[- ](?:rank|level)/i
    );
    if (!match) return null;
    return { firstFourRank: Number(match[1]), lastThreeRank: Number(match[2]) };
}

function parseFixedBasicWallLayer(text, color, damageType, nextColor = null) {
    // Chromatic Wall's lower-rank colors are commonly stored in an HTML table.
    // Parse one color window at a time instead of relying on one long sentence,
    // because table-cell boundaries can otherwise collapse during textContent
    // extraction (for example, "1RedThe wall...").
    //
    // PF2e 8.x can render semantic damage tags into plain text as "20[fire]
    // damage" and can omit the visible word "basic" from a save link/label.
    // For a fixed-damage component whose prose explicitly pairs that damage
    // with one save, treat that save as the component's basic save even when
    // the rendered text only says "with a Reflex save" or "A Fortitude save".
    const source = getColorTextWindow(text, color, nextColor);
    if (!source) return null;

    const type = normalizeDamageTypeAlias(damageType);
    const amountPatterns = [
        new RegExp(`\\b(\\d+)\\s*\\[\\s*${escapeRegex(type)}\\s*\\]\\s*damage\\b`, "i"),
        new RegExp(`\\b(\\d+)\\s+${escapeRegex(type)}\\s+damage\\b`, "i"),
        new RegExp(`\\b(\\d+)\\s+(?:points?\\s+of\\s+)?${escapeRegex(type)}\\s+damage\\b`, "i"),
        new RegExp(`\\b(?:deals?|take(?:s)?)\\s+(\\d+)\\s*\\[?\\s*${escapeRegex(type)}\\s*\\]?\\b`, "i")
    ];

    let amount = null;
    for (const pattern of amountPatterns) {
        const match = source.match(pattern);
        if (match) {
            amount = Number(match[1]);
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(amount)) return null;

    const explicitBasicSave = source.match(/\bbasic\s+(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+save\b/i)
        ?? source.match(/\b(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+basic\s+save\b/i);

    const implicitBasicSave = source.match(/\bwith\s+(?:a\s+)?(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+save\b/i)
        ?? source.match(/\b(?:a|an)\s+(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+save\b/i)
        ?? source.match(/\b(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+save\b/i);

    const saveMatch = explicitBasicSave ?? implicitBasicSave;
    if (!saveMatch) return null;

    return {
        kind: "fixed-basic-damage",
        color: normalizeSlug(color),
        label: color,
        saveType: normalizeSlug(saveMatch[1]),
        damage: { amount, type },
        basicSaveSource: explicitBasicSave ? "explicit" : "implicit-fixed-damage",
        sourceText: source
    };
}

function getColorTextWindow(text, color, nextColor = null) {
    const source = String(text ?? "");
    const colorRegex = new RegExp(`\\b${escapeRegex(color)}\\b`, "i");
    const startMatch = colorRegex.exec(source);
    if (!startMatch) return "";

    const start = startMatch.index;
    let end = source.length;
    if (nextColor) {
        const remainder = source.slice(start + startMatch[0].length);
        const nextRegex = new RegExp(`\\b${escapeRegex(nextColor)}\\b`, "i");
        const nextMatch = nextRegex.exec(remainder);
        if (nextMatch) end = start + startMatch[0].length + nextMatch.index;
    }

    return source.slice(start, end).trim();
}

function parseReferencedSpellNameFromLayerText(layerText) {
    const match = String(layerText ?? "").match(
        /\b(?:subject(?:ed)?\s+to|affected\s+(?:as\s+if\s+)?by)[^.;]{0,80}?\b(?:the\s+)?effects?\s+of\s+(?:the\s+)?([a-z][a-z '\u2019-]+?)(?:\s+spell)?(?:[.;]|$)/i
    ) ?? String(layerText ?? "").match(
        /\b(?:subject(?:ed)?\s+to|affected\s+(?:as\s+if\s+)?by)\s+(?:the\s+effects?\s+of\s+)?(?:the\s+)?([a-z][a-z '\u2019-]+?)(?:\s+spell)?(?:[.;]|$)/i
    );

    return match?.[1]?.trim() ?? null;
}

function looseConditionFromPhrase(phrase, catalog) {
    const source = String(phrase ?? "");
    for (const entry of catalog.entries ?? []) {
        const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${escapeRegex(entry.name)}\\b(?:\\s+(\\d+))?`, "i");
        const match = regex.exec(source);
        if (!match) continue;
        return {
            condition: entry.slug,
            name: entry.name,
            value: match[1] ? Number(match[1]) : null,
            duration: parseSimpleDuration(source)
        };
    }
    return null;
}

async function inferCrossingLightSave(parentSpell) {
    const plain = htmlToPlain(parentSpell?.system?.description?.value ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const match = plain.match(
        /\bcome\s+into\s+the\s+wall(?:'|\u2019)?s\s+light\s+must\s+attempt\s+a\s+(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+save\s*;\s*(?:they(?:'re|\u2019re| are)|the\s+target\s+is)\s+(.+?)\s+on\s+a\s+success\s*,\s*(.+?)\s+on\s+a\s+failure\s*,\s*and\s+(.+?)\s+on\s+a\s+critical\s+failure\b/i
    );
    if (!match) return null;

    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const success = looseConditionFromPhrase(match[2], catalog);
    const failure = looseConditionFromPhrase(match[3], catalog);
    const criticalFailure = looseConditionFromPhrase(match[4], catalog);
    if (!success || !failure || !criticalFailure) return null;

    const immunitySentence = plain.match(/[^.!?]*\btemporarily\s+immune\s+to\s+the\s+blinding\s+effect\b[^.!?]*/i)?.[0] ?? "";
    const temporaryImmunity = parseSimpleDuration(immunitySentence);

    return {
        saveType: normalizeSlug(match[1]),
        outcomes: {
            criticalSuccess: null,
            success,
            failure,
            criticalFailure
        },
        temporaryImmunity,
        sourceText: match[0]
    };
}

function inferGenericTemporaryImmunityFromSpell(spell) {
    const plain = htmlToPlain(spell?.system?.description?.value ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    const sentence = plain.match(/[^.!?]*\btemporarily\s+immune\b[^.!?]*/i)?.[0] ?? null;
    if (!sentence) return null;
    const duration = parseSimpleDuration(sentence);
    return duration ? { duration, sourceText: sentence.trim() } : null;
}

function getManualTriggerTracker(actor, castEntry, key) {
    const spellSlug = normalizeSlug(castEntry?.spell?.slug ?? castEntry?.spell?.name);
    return actor?.items?.find?.(item =>
        item.type === "effect"
        && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.manualTriggerTracker?.spellSlug === spellSlug
        && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.manualTriggerTracker?.key === key
        && item.system?.expired !== true
        && item.active !== false
    ) ?? null;
}

async function applyManualTriggerTracker(actor, castEntry, key, label, duration) {
    if (!actor || !duration) return null;
    const existing = getManualTriggerTracker(actor, castEntry, key);
    if (existing) return existing;

    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const source = {
        name: `${spell.name}: ${label}`,
        type: "effect",
        img: spell.img ?? "systems/pf2e/icons/default-icons/effect.svg",
        flags: {
            pf2eAutoSpellResolution: {
                manualTriggerTracker: {
                    spellSlug: normalizeSlug(spell.slug ?? spell.name),
                    key,
                    sourceSpell: spell.uuid ?? castEntry.spell.uuid ?? null,
                    castId: castEntry.castId
                }
            }
        },
        system: {
            description: {
                value: `<p>Automatically tracked by <strong>${escapeHTML(spell.name)}</strong>.</p>`
            },
            duration: {
                value: Number(duration.value),
                unit: duration.unit,
                expiry: duration.expiry ?? null,
                sustained: false
            },
            rules: [],
            start: {
                value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0,
                initiative: game.combat?.combatant?.initiative ?? null
            },
            tokenIcon: { show: true },
            traits: { value: [] }
        }
    };

    const [created] = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
    return created ?? null;
}

async function inferManualMultiSaveCrossingRule(castEntry) {
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const parentPlain = htmlToPlain(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const ranks = parseLayerRanks(parentPlain);
    const referencedWallName = parseReferencedWallName(parentPlain);
    const dc = getCastSpellDC(castEntry);

    if (!ranks) return { supported: false, reason: "Could not infer component-wall ranks" };
    if (!referencedWallName) return { supported: false, reason: "Could not infer referenced component wall spell" };
    if (!dc) return { supported: false, reason: "Could not determine caster spell DC" };

    const wallSpell = await resolveReferencedSpell(spell, referencedWallName);
    if (!wallSpell) {
        return { supported: false, reason: `Could not resolve referenced spell: ${referencedWallName}` };
    }

    const wallPlain = htmlToStructuredPlain(wallSpell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const baseWallText = wallPlain.split(/\bHeightened\s*\(/i)[0];
    const heightenedWallText = wallPlain.match(/\bHeightened\s*\([\s\S]*/i)?.[0] ?? wallPlain;

    const red = parseFixedBasicWallLayer(baseWallText, "Red", "fire", "Orange");
    const orange = parseFixedBasicWallLayer(baseWallText, "Orange", "acid", "Yellow");
    const yellow = parseFixedBasicWallLayer(baseWallText, "Yellow", "electricity", "Green");
    const green = parseFixedBasicWallLayer(baseWallText, "Green", "poison");

    if (green) {
        const greenText = getColorTextWindow(baseWallText, "Green");
        const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
        const effect = looseConditionFromPhrase(greenText, catalog);
        if (effect && normalizeSlug(effect.condition) === "enfeebled") {
            green.failureCondition = effect;
        }
    }

    const blueText = getColorTextWindow(heightenedWallText, "Blue", "Indigo");
    const indigoText = getColorTextWindow(heightenedWallText, "Indigo", "Violet");
    const violetText = getColorTextWindow(heightenedWallText, "Violet");

    const blueSpellName = parseReferencedSpellNameFromLayerText(blueText);
    const indigoSpellName = parseReferencedSpellNameFromLayerText(indigoText);
    const blueSpell = blueSpellName ? await resolveReferencedSpell(wallSpell, blueSpellName) : null;
    const indigoSpell = indigoSpellName ? await resolveReferencedSpell(wallSpell, indigoSpellName) : null;

    const violetSave = violetText.match(/\b(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+save\b/i)?.[1] ?? "Will";
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const violetCondition = looseConditionFromPhrase(violetText, catalog);
    const violetPlaneShift = /\bcritical\s+failure\b[^.]{0,180}\b(?:sent|shifted|transported)\s+to\s+another\s+plane\b/i.test(violetText)
        || /\bon\s+a\s+critical\s+failure\b[^.]{0,180}\bplane\s+shift\b/i.test(violetText);

    const layers = [red, orange, yellow, green].filter(Boolean);
    if (blueSpell) {
        layers.push({
            kind: "referenced-spell",
            color: "blue",
            label: "Blue",
            saveType: inspectSpellItem(blueSpell).saveType ?? "fortitude",
            referencedSpell: blueSpell,
            referencedSpellName: blueSpell.name,
            castRank: getSpellBaseRank(blueSpell) ?? ranks.lastThreeRank
        });
    }
    if (indigoSpell) {
        layers.push({
            kind: "referenced-spell",
            color: "indigo",
            label: "Indigo",
            saveType: inspectSpellItem(indigoSpell).saveType ?? "will",
            referencedSpell: indigoSpell,
            referencedSpellName: indigoSpell.name,
            castRank: getSpellBaseRank(indigoSpell) ?? ranks.lastThreeRank
        });
    }
    layers.push({
        kind: "violet",
        color: "violet",
        label: "Violet",
        saveType: normalizeSlug(violetSave),
        failureCondition: violetCondition && normalizeSlug(violetCondition.condition) === "slowed"
            ? violetCondition
            : { condition: "slowed", name: "Slowed", value: 1, duration: { value: 1, unit: "minutes", expiry: null } },
        planeShiftOnCriticalFailure: violetPlaneShift
    });

    const expectedColors = new Set(["red", "orange", "yellow", "green", "blue", "indigo", "violet"]);
    const actualColors = new Set(layers.map(layer => layer.color));
    const missingColors = [...expectedColors].filter(color => !actualColors.has(color));
    if (missingColors.length) {
        return {
            supported: false,
            reason: `Could not infer all component walls: ${missingColors.join(", ")}`,
            dc,
            ranks,
            wallSpell,
            wallSpellName: wallSpell.name,
            lightSave: await inferCrossingLightSave(spell),
            layers,
            missingColors,
            wallTextPreview: wallPlain.slice(0, 1800)
        };
    }

    return {
        supported: true,
        dc,
        wallSpell,
        wallSpellName: wallSpell.name,
        ranks,
        lightSave: await inferCrossingLightSave(spell),
        layers,
        simultaneous: /\beffects?\s+take\s+place\s+simultaneously\b/i.test(parentPlain),
        casterIgnores: /\byou\s+can\s+pass\s+through\s+the\s+wall\s+and\s+ignore\s+its\s+effects\b/i.test(parentPlain)
    };
}

function createVirtualReferencedCast(parentCast, spell, castRank, label) {
    return {
        castId: `${parentCast.castId}:manual-trigger:${parentCast.cycle}:${normalizeSlug(label)}:${Date.now()}:${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        spell,
        actor: parentCast.actor,
        saveOriginSpell: parentCast.saveOriginSpell ?? resolveSpellAtCastRank(parentCast),
        save: inspectSpellItem(spell).saveType,
        dc: getCastSpellDC(parentCast),
        castRank: Number(castRank ?? spell.rank ?? getSpellBaseRank(spell)),
        resolvedSpell: null,
        automationProfile: null,
        sharedDamageRolled: false,
        sharedDamageRolling: false,
        sharedDamageMessageId: null,
        sharedDamageFormula: null,
        sharedDamageRollOptions: [],
        sharedDamageRollJSON: null,
        customDamageStates: {},
        resolutionMessageId: null,
        resolutionQueue: Promise.resolve(),
        resultGroups: {},
        cycle: parentCast.cycle,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    };
}

function createContextualSaveItem(effectSpell, originSpell, castRank = null) {
    // Compendium spells are templates, not owned casting items. Passing one
    // directly to a save gives chat integrations an origin with no spellcasting
    // entry. Use a save-only document with the caster's real spell UUID instead.
    // This is never embedded, updated, or saved, and the templates stay intact.
    if (!effectSpell || effectSpell.type !== "spell"
        || !originSpell?.actor || !originSpell?.spellcasting?.statistic) {
        throw new Error(`${effectSpell?.name ?? "Spell"}: the original caster's spellcasting entry could not be recovered.`);
    }

    const rank = Number(castRank ?? effectSpell.rank ?? getSpellBaseRank(effectSpell));
    if (!Number.isInteger(rank) || rank < 1 || rank > 10) {
        throw new Error(`${effectSpell.name}: the saving throw's spell rank could not be determined.`);
    }
    if (effectSpell === originSpell && Number(effectSpell.rank) === rank) return effectSpell;

    const source = deepClone(effectSpell.toObject());
    source._id = originSpell.id;
    source.system.location = {
        ...source.system.location,
        value: originSpell.system.location.value,
        heightenedLevel: rank
    };
    const saveItem = new originSpell.constructor(source, {
        parent: originSpell.actor,
        parentItem: originSpell.parentItem ?? null
    });
