    if (!saveItem.spellcasting?.statistic || saveItem.uuid !== originSpell.uuid) {
        throw new Error(`${effectSpell.name}: could not create a save item linked to the original cast.`);
    }
    return saveItem;
}

async function rollManualTriggerSave(actor, token, saveType, dc, item, label, extraTraits = [], { originSpell = item, castRank = null, onSaveMessage = null } = {}) {
    const statistic = actor?.saves?.[normalizeSlug(saveType)];
    if (!statistic || typeof statistic.roll !== "function") {
        throw new Error(`${actor?.name ?? "Target"}: PF2e ${saveType} save statistic could not be resolved.`);
    }

    const traits = new Set(extraTraits.map(normalizeSlug).filter(Boolean));
    for (const trait of item?.system?.traits?.value ?? []) traits.add(normalizeSlug(trait));
    const saveItem = createContextualSaveItem(item, originSpell, castRank);

    const roll = await statistic.roll({
        dc: Number(dc),
        token: token ?? undefined,
        item: saveItem,
        origin: originSpell.actor,
        label,
        title: label,
        extraRollOptions: [
            "pf2e-auto-manual-trigger-save",
            `pf2e-auto-manual-trigger-save:${normalizeSlug(label)}`,
            // PF2e calculates some adjustments before adding args.traits.
            ...[...traits].map(trait => `item:trait:${trait}`)
        ],
        traits: [...traits],
        callback: onSaveMessage ? (_roll, _outcome, message) => onSaveMessage(message) : undefined,
        skipDialog: true
    });

    const degree = normalizeDegree(roll?.degreeOfSuccess ?? roll?.options?.degreeOfSuccess);
    if (!degree) throw new Error(`${label}: PF2e save rolled but its degree of success could not be read.`);
    return { roll, degree };
}

function basicSaveMultiplierForDegree(degree) {
    return {
        criticalSuccess: 0,
        success: 0.5,
        failure: 1,
        criticalFailure: 2
    }[normalizeDegree(degree)] ?? 0;
}

async function applyFixedTypedDamageForCrossing(parentCast, actor, token, layer, degree) {
    const multiplier = basicSaveMultiplierForDegree(degree);
    if (multiplier <= 0) return { applied: false, multiplier, amount: 0 };

    const DamageRoll = getPF2eDamageRollClass();
    const roll = new DamageRoll(
        `(${Number(layer.damage.amount)})[${normalizeDamageTypeAlias(layer.damage.type)}]`,
        getOutcomeDamageRollData(parentCast)
    );
    await roll.evaluate();

    const result = await applyDamageRollToActor(
        actor,
        token,
        roll,
        multiplier,
        parentCast,
        `Applying ${layer.label} component damage:`
    );

    return {
        applied: !!result?.applied,
        multiplier,
        amount: Number(layer.damage.amount),
        type: layer.damage.type,
        beforeHP: result?.beforeHP ?? null,
        afterHP: result?.afterHP ?? null
    };
}

async function applyVirtualReferencedSpellOutcome(plan, actor, token) {
    const { childCast, degree } = plan;
    if (plan.blockedByTemporaryImmunity || plan.spellImmunity) return plan;

    const profile = await determineSpellAutomation(childCast);
    if (!profile?.outcomes) {
        plan.manual = true;
        plan.manualReason = `${childCast.spell.name}: generic referenced-spell outcome was not inferred.`;
        return plan;
    }

    const outcome = profile.outcomes[degree];
    if (!outcome) {
        plan.manual = true;
        plan.manualReason = `${childCast.spell.name}: no outcome for ${readableDegree(degree)}.`;
        return plan;
    }

    const damage = normalizeOutcomeDamage(outcome);
    if (damage.kind === "shared" && damage.multiplier > 0) {
        const roll = await getSharedDamageRoll(childCast);
        await applyDamageRollToActor(
            actor,
            token,
            roll,
            damage.multiplier,
            childCast,
            `Applying referenced ${childCast.spell.name} damage:`
        );
        plan.damageApplied = true;
    } else if (damage.kind === "formula") {
        const roll = await getCustomDamageRoll(
            childCast,
            `manual-trigger:${degree}`,
            damage,
            `${childCast.spell.name} — ${readableDegree(degree)} Damage`
        );
        await applyDamageRollToActor(
            actor,
            token,
            roll,
            null,
            childCast,
            `Applying referenced ${childCast.spell.name} damage:`
        );
        plan.damageApplied = true;
    }

    const conditions = deepClone(outcome.conditions ?? []);
    const linkedConditions = deepClone(outcome.linkedConditions ?? []);

    if (outcome.ongoingSave) {
        const trackedSlug = normalizeSlug(outcome.ongoingSave.trackedCondition);
        const trackedEffect = conditions.find(effect => normalizeSlug(effect.condition) === trackedSlug)
            ?? {
                condition: outcome.ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
                name: outcome.ongoingSave.trackedConditionName ?? outcome.ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
                value: 1,
                duration: null
            };

        await applyOutcomeConditions(
            actor,
            childCast.spell,
            conditions.filter(effect => normalizeSlug(effect.condition) !== trackedSlug),
            linkedConditions
        );

        await createOngoingSaveTracker(
            actor,
            childCast.spell,
            childCast,
            outcome.ongoingSave,
            trackedEffect
        );
        plan.effectsApplied = true;
    } else if (conditions.length || linkedConditions.length) {
        await applyOutcomeConditions(actor, childCast.spell, conditions, linkedConditions);
        plan.effectsApplied = true;
    }

    if ((outcome.nativeEffects?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
        plan.nativeEffectsApplied = await applyNativeSpellEffects(
            actor,
            childCast.spell,
            childCast,
            outcome.nativeEffects
        );
        plan.effectsApplied = true;
    }

    if (outcome.terminal) {
        const target = { terminalApplied: false, applied: false };
        await applyTerminalResult(childCast, target, actor, outcome.terminal);
        plan.terminalApplied = !!target.terminalApplied;
    }

    if (outcome.secondarySave) {
        plan.manual = true;
        plan.manualReason = `${childCast.spell.name}: nested secondary save requires manual follow-up.`;
    }

    const temp = inferGenericTemporaryImmunityFromSpell(childCast.spell);
    if (temp?.duration) {
        await applyTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(actor, childCast, temp);
        plan.temporaryImmunityApplied = `${temp.duration.value} ${temp.duration.unit}`;
    }

    plan.outcome = deepClone(outcome);
    return plan;
}

function buildManualCrossingSummaryCard(castEntry, targetActor, rule, lightResult, layerResults) {
    const rows = [];

    if (lightResult) {
        let effect = lightResult.skipped
            ? lightResult.reason
            : readableDegree(lightResult.degree);
        if (lightResult.appliedCondition) {
            effect += ` — ${lightResult.appliedCondition}`;
        }
        rows.push(`<div><strong>Light</strong> — ${escapeHTML(effect)}</div>`);
    }

    for (const result of layerResults) {
        let effect = result.degree ? readableDegree(result.degree) : "Skipped";
        if (result.spellImmunity) effect += ` — Immune: ${result.spellImmunity.label}`;
        else if (result.blockedByTemporaryImmunity) effect += " — Temporarily Immune";
        else if (result.summary) effect += ` — ${result.summary}`;
        if (result.manualReason) effect += ` — MANUAL: ${result.manualReason}`;
        rows.push(`<div><strong>${escapeHTML(result.label)}</strong> — ${escapeHTML(effect)}</div>`);
    }

    return `
        <div class="pf2e-auto-crossing-resolution-card" style="font-size:0.86em;line-height:1.25;">
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;">
                <strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)} — Crossing</strong>
                <span style="opacity:0.65;">DC ${rule.dc}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-top:5px;border-top:1px solid rgba(128,128,128,0.25);padding-top:5px;">
                <div style="margin-bottom:4px;"><strong>${escapeHTML(targetActor.name)}</strong></div>
                ${rows.join("")}
                <div style="margin-top:5px;opacity:0.72;">All component saves were rolled before effects were applied.</div>
            </div>
        </div>`;
}

async function resolveManualMultiSaveCrossing(castEntry, targetActor, targetToken = null) {
    const automation = await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);
    if (!automation || automation.kind !== "manual-trigger-multi-save") {
        throw new Error(`${castEntry.spell.name} is not a supported manual-trigger multi-save spell.`);
    }

    const rule = automation.crossingRule;
    const parentSpell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);

    if (rule.casterIgnores && castEntry.actor?.uuid && targetActor?.uuid === castEntry.actor.uuid) {
        const content = buildManualCrossingSummaryCard(castEntry, targetActor, rule, {
            skipped: true,
            reason: "Caster ignores the wall's effects"
        }, []);
        await ChatMessage.create({ speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }), content });
        debug("MANUAL CROSSING CASTER IGNORES EFFECTS:", { spell: parentSpell.name, target: targetActor.name });
        return { casterIgnored: true };
    }

    const plans = [];
    let lightResult = null;

    // Phase 1: roll everything first.  This deliberately occurs before any
    // condition/damage application because the source spell says the seven
    // layer effects happen simultaneously.
    if (rule.lightSave) {
        const lightTracker = getManualTriggerTracker(targetActor, castEntry, "wall-light-blinding-immunity");
        if (lightTracker) {
            lightResult = { skipped: true, reason: "Temporarily immune to the wall's blinding light" };
        } else {
            const rolled = await rollManualTriggerSave(
                targetActor,
                targetToken,
                rule.lightSave.saveType,
                rule.dc,
                parentSpell,
                `${parentSpell.name} — Light Save`
            );
            lightResult = { degree: rolled.degree, roll: rolled.roll };
        }
    }

    for (const layer of rule.layers) {
        if (layer.kind === "referenced-spell") {
            const childCast = createVirtualReferencedCast(
                castEntry,
                layer.referencedSpell,
                layer.castRank,
                `${layer.color}:${targetActor.id}`
            );

            const temp = getTemporarySpellImmunityTracker(targetActor, childCast);
            const immunity = temp ? null : getSpellEffectImmunity(targetActor, childCast);

            if (temp) {
                plans.push({
                    ...layer,
                    childCast,
                    blockedByTemporaryImmunity: true,
                    degree: null,
                    summary: `${layer.referencedSpellName}: temporarily immune`
                });
                continue;
            }

            if (immunity) {
                plans.push({
                    ...layer,
                    childCast,
                    spellImmunity: immunity,
                    degree: null,
                    summary: `${layer.referencedSpellName}: immune (${immunity.label})`
                });
                continue;
            }

            const item = resolveSpellAtCastRank(childCast);
            const rolled = await rollManualTriggerSave(
                targetActor,
                targetToken,
                layer.saveType,
                rule.dc,
                item,
                `${parentSpell.name} — ${layer.label} (${item.name}) Save`,
                [],
                { originSpell: parentSpell, castRank: childCast.castRank }
            );

            plans.push({ ...layer, childCast, degree: rolled.degree, roll: rolled.roll });
            continue;
        }

        const item = layer.color === "blue" || layer.color === "indigo"
            ? null
            : rule.wallSpell;
        const rolled = await rollManualTriggerSave(
            targetActor,
            targetToken,
            layer.saveType,
            rule.dc,
            item ?? parentSpell,
            `${parentSpell.name} — ${layer.label} Save`,
            layer.damage?.type ? [layer.damage.type] : [],
            {
                originSpell: parentSpell,
                castRank: layer.kind === "violet" ? rule.ranks.lastThreeRank : rule.ranks.firstFourRank
            }
        );
        plans.push({ ...layer, degree: rolled.degree, roll: rolled.roll });
    }

    // Phase 2: apply the already-known results.
    if (lightResult && !lightResult.skipped) {
        const effect = rule.lightSave.outcomes?.[lightResult.degree] ?? null;
        if (effect) {
            if (effect.duration) await applyTimedCondition(targetActor, parentSpell, effect);
            else await applyCondition(targetActor, effect.condition, effect.value);
            lightResult.appliedCondition = `${effect.name ?? effect.condition}${effect.value ? ` ${effect.value}` : ""}${effect.duration?.value ? ` for ${effect.duration.value} ${effect.duration.unit}` : ""}`;
        }

        if (rule.lightSave.temporaryImmunity) {
            await applyManualTriggerTracker(
                targetActor,
                castEntry,
                "wall-light-blinding-immunity",
                "Blinding Light Immunity",
                rule.lightSave.temporaryImmunity
            );
        }
    }

    for (const plan of plans) {
        if (plan.spellImmunity || plan.blockedByTemporaryImmunity) continue;

        if (plan.kind === "fixed-basic-damage") {
            const damageResult = await applyFixedTypedDamageForCrossing(
                castEntry,
                targetActor,
                targetToken,
                plan,
                plan.degree
            );
            const multiplierText = {
                0: "no damage",
                0.5: "half damage",
                1: "full damage",
                2: "double damage"
            }[damageResult.multiplier] ?? `${damageResult.multiplier}x damage`;
            plan.summary = `${multiplierText} (${plan.damage.amount} ${plan.damage.type} base)`;

            if (plan.color === "green" && ["failure", "criticalFailure"].includes(plan.degree) && plan.failureCondition) {
                if (plan.failureCondition.duration) {
                    await applyTimedCondition(targetActor, parentSpell, plan.failureCondition);
                } else {
                    await applyCondition(targetActor, plan.failureCondition.condition, plan.failureCondition.value);
                }
                plan.summary += ` + ${plan.failureCondition.name ?? plan.failureCondition.condition}${plan.failureCondition.value ? ` ${plan.failureCondition.value}` : ""}`;
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (plan.kind === "referenced-spell") {
            await applyVirtualReferencedSpellOutcome(plan, targetActor, targetToken);
            if (!plan.summary) {
                plan.summary = `${plan.referencedSpellName}: ${readableDegree(plan.degree)}`;
                if (plan.effectsApplied) plan.summary += " effect applied";
                if (plan.temporaryImmunityApplied) plan.summary += `; immunity ${plan.temporaryImmunityApplied}`;
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (plan.kind === "violet") {
            if (plan.degree === "failure") {
                const effect = deepClone(plan.failureCondition);
                if (effect.duration) await applyTimedCondition(targetActor, parentSpell, effect);
                else await applyCondition(targetActor, effect.condition, effect.value);
                plan.summary = `${effect.name ?? effect.condition}${effect.value ? ` ${effect.value}` : ""} for ${effect.duration?.value ?? 1} ${effect.duration?.unit ?? "minutes"}`;
            } else if (plan.degree === "criticalFailure" && plan.planeShiftOnCriticalFailure) {
                plan.summary = "sent to another plane";
                plan.manual = true;
                plan.manualReason = "Relocate the creature to the appropriate plane; destination cannot be chosen safely by automation.";
            } else {
                plan.summary = "no effect";
            }
        }
    }

    debug("MANUAL MULTI-SAVE CROSSING RESOLVED:", {
        spell: parentSpell.name,
        target: targetActor.name,
        dc: rule.dc,
        light: lightResult ? { degree: lightResult.degree ?? null, skipped: !!lightResult.skipped, reason: lightResult.reason ?? null } : null,
        layers: plans.map(plan => ({
            color: plan.color,
            degree: plan.degree ?? null,
            immunity: plan.spellImmunity?.label ?? null,
            temporaryImmunity: !!plan.blockedByTemporaryImmunity,
            summary: plan.summary ?? null,
            manual: !!plan.manual
        }))
    });

    await ChatMessage.create({
        speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
        content: buildManualCrossingSummaryCard(castEntry, targetActor, rule, lightResult, plans)
    });

    return { lightResult, layers: plans };
}

// =================================================================
// RANDOM DAMAGE TABLES (ONE TABLE ROLL PER CAST)
// =================================================================

function isPotentialRandomDamageTableSpell(castEntry) {
    if (!castEntry) return false;
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const inspection = inspectSpellItem(spell);
    const html = spell.system?.description?.value ?? "";
    if (!inspection.basicSave || inspection.damageCount > 0 || !/<table\b/i.test(html)) return false;
    const introduction = htmlToPlain(html.split(/<table\b/i)[0]);
    return /\bwhen\s+you\s+cast\b[^.]{0,100}\broll\s+1d\d+\s+on\s+the\s+table\b/i.test(introduction);
}

async function inferRandomDamageTableRule(castEntry) {
    if (!isPotentialRandomDamageTableSpell(castEntry)) return null;
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const html = spell.system.description.value;
    const introduction = htmlToPlain(html.split(/<table\b/i)[0]);
    const die = introduction.match(/\broll\s+(1d(\d+))\s+on\s+the\s+table\b/i);
    if (!die || !/\bon\s+a\s+failure\b[^.]{0,100}\badded\s+effect\b/i.test(introduction)) return null;

    const document = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, "text/html");
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    for (const table of document.querySelectorAll("table")) {
        const tableRows = [...table.querySelectorAll("tr")];
        const headings = [...(tableRows[0]?.querySelectorAll("th, td") ?? [])]
            .map(cell => htmlToPlain(cell.innerHTML).toLowerCase());
        const damageColumn = headings.findIndex(text => text === "damage");
        const labelColumn = headings.findIndex(text => /^(?:color|colour|result|type)$/.test(text));
        const effectColumn = headings.findIndex(text => /^(?:added|additional) effect$/.test(text));
        if (damageColumn < 0 || labelColumn < 0 || effectColumn < 0) continue;

        const rows = [];
        let unsupported = false;
        for (const tr of tableRows.slice(1)) {
            const cells = [...tr.querySelectorAll("td")];
            const number = cells[0]?.textContent.trim() ?? "";
            if (!/^\d+$/.test(number)) { unsupported = true; break; }
            const value = Number(number);
            const label = htmlToPlain(cells[labelColumn]?.innerHTML ?? "");
            const damageHTML = cells[damageColumn]?.innerHTML ?? "";
            const effectHTML = cells[effectColumn]?.innerHTML ?? "";
            const damageText = htmlToPlain(damageHTML);
            const choicePerCreature = /\bchoose\s+the\s+\w+\s+for\s+each\s+affected\s+creature\b/i.test(damageText);
            if (choicePerCreature) {
                rows.push({ value, label, choicePerCreature: true, damage: null, conditions: [] });
                continue;
            }

            // Keep every typed instance together, including persistent damage.
            const inline = collectBalancedInlines(damageHTML, "@Damage[");
            const damage = inline.length ? parseInlineDamageSpec(damageHTML, castEntry) : null;
            if (!damage || !label) { unsupported = true; break; }
            damage.inlineFormula = inline.map(entry => getInlineDamagePayload(entry.body)).join(",");
            const effectText = htmlToPlain(effectHTML);
            const conditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome({ html: effectHTML, text: effectText }, catalog);
            for (const condition of conditions) {
                // A table cell can consist solely of an unlabelled condition UUID
                // followed by a duration. Its whole cell is the duration scope.
                condition.duration ??= parseSimpleDuration(effectText);
            }
            let remaining = effectHTML
                .replace(/@UUID\[Compendium\.pf2e\.conditionitems\.Item\.[^\]]+\](?:\{[^}]*\})?/gi, "")
                .replace(/<a\b[^>]*data-uuid=["']Compendium\.pf2e\.conditionitems\.[\s\S]*?<\/a>/gi, "");
            remaining = htmlToPlain(remaining);
            for (const condition of conditions) {
                remaining = remaining.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${escapeRegex(condition.name)}\\b(?:\\s+\\d+)?`, "gi"), "");
            }
            remaining = remaining.replace(/\b(?:knocked|is|becomes?|for|rounds?|minutes?|hours?|one|two|three|\d+)\b/gi, "")
                .replace(/[\s.,;:—–-]+/g, "");
            rows.push({
                value, label, damage, conditions,
                damageLabel: inline.map(entry => entry.label ?? getInlineDamagePayload(entry.body)).join(" + "),
                manualText: remaining ? effectText : null
            });
        }
        const sides = Number(die[2]);
        if (unsupported || sides < 2 || rows.length !== sides
            || new Set(rows.map(row => row.value)).size !== sides
            || rows.some(row => row.value < 1 || row.value > sides)
            || !rows.some(row => row.damage)) continue;
        return { formula: die[1].toLowerCase(), rows, saveType: inspectSpellItem(spell).saveType };
    }
    return null;
}

function getRandomTableState(castEntry) {
    castEntry.randomTableState ??= {
        selection: null, selectionPromise: null, queue: Promise.resolve(), exposures: [], nextId: 0
    };
    return castEntry.randomTableState;
}

function queueRandomTableWork(castEntry, work) {
    const state = getRandomTableState(castEntry);
    const next = state.queue.then(work, work);
    state.queue = next.catch(() => {});
    return next;
}

async function storeRandomTableSelection(castEntry, rule, total) {
    const row = rule.rows.find(entry => entry.value === total);
    if (!row) throw new Error(`${castEntry.spell.name}: table result ${total} was not found.`);
    const selection = { formula: rule.formula, total, label: row.label };
    const message = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId);
    if (!message) throw new Error("The original spell card is no longer available.");
    await message.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.randomDamageTable": selection });
    getRandomTableState(castEntry).selection = selection;
    return selection;
}

async function ensureRandomTableSelection(castEntry) {
    const profile = await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);
    if (profile?.kind !== "random-table-basic-save") throw new Error("No supported random damage table was inferred.");
    const rule = profile.tableRule;
    const state = getRandomTableState(castEntry);
    if (state.selection) return state.selection;
    if (state.selectionPromise) return await state.selectionPromise;

    state.selectionPromise = (async () => {
        const saved = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId)?.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.randomDamageTable;
        if (saved?.formula === rule.formula && rule.rows.some(row => row.value === saved.total)) {
            state.selection = deepClone(saved);
            return state.selection;
        }
        const roll = await new Roll(rule.formula).evaluate();
        // Persist before posting the roll: a chat re-render must not reroll it.
        const selection = await storeRandomTableSelection(castEntry, rule, Number(roll.total));
        await roll.toMessage({
            speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
            flavor: `<strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)}</strong> — ${escapeHTML(selection.label)} (table result ${selection.total})`
        });
        debug("RANDOM DAMAGE TABLE RESULT:", { spell: castEntry.spell.name, ...selection });
        return selection;
    })();
    try { return await state.selectionPromise; }
    finally { state.selectionPromise = null; }
}

async function promptRandomTableChoice(castEntry, rows, label, selected = null) {
    return await foundry.applications.api.DialogV2.prompt({
        window: { title: `${castEntry.spell.name} — ${label}` },
        content: `<p>${escapeHTML(label)}</p><select name="tableChoice" style="width:100%;">${rows.map(row =>
            `<option value="${row.value}"${row.value === selected ? " selected" : ""}>${escapeHTML(`${row.value}: ${row.label}${row.damageLabel ? ` — ${row.damageLabel}` : ""}`)}</option>`
        ).join("")}</select>`,
        ok: { label: "Use Result", callback: (_event, button) => Number(button.form.elements.tableChoice.value) },
        rejectClose: false,
        modal: true
    });
}

async function setRandomTableResult(castEntry) {
    return await queueRandomTableWork(castEntry, async () => {
        const selection = await ensureRandomTableSelection(castEntry);
        const state = getRandomTableState(castEntry);
        if (state.exposures.length) {
            ui.notifications.warn(`${castEntry.spell.name}: its table result is fixed for this cast. Cast it again for a new table result.`);
            return;
        }
        const rule = castEntry.automationProfile.tableRule;
        const chosen = await promptRandomTableChoice(castEntry, rule.rows, "Use an existing table result", selection.total);
        if (chosen === null || chosen === undefined || chosen === false) return;
        await storeRandomTableSelection(castEntry, rule, Number(chosen));
    });
}

function configureRandomTableExposure(castEntry, exposure, row) {
    const outcomes = standardBasicOutcomes();
    for (const degree of ["failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
        outcomes[degree].conditions = deepClone(row.conditions);
        outcomes[degree].manualRider = !!row.manualText;
        outcomes[degree].manualRiderText = row.manualText ?? null;
    }
    exposure.tableRowValue = row.value;
    exposure.tableRowLabel = row.label;
    exposure.tableDamageLabel = row.damageLabel;
    exposure.automationProfile = {
        source: "random-table-inference", kind: "basic-save-damage",
        spell: exposure.spell, sharedDamageSpec: deepClone(row.damage), outcomes,
        conditionalDamageRules: [], targetHealingRules: [], targetDamageSubstitutionRules: []
    };
}

async function completeRandomTableExposure(castEntry, exposure) {
    const rule = castEntry.automationProfile.tableRule;
    const selection = await ensureRandomTableSelection(castEntry);
    const selectedRow = rule.rows.find(row => row.value === selection.total);
    let row = rule.rows.find(entry => entry.value === exposure.tableRowValue);
    if (!row && selectedRow.choicePerCreature) {
        const value = await promptRandomTableChoice(castEntry, rule.rows.filter(entry => entry.damage),
            `Choose a color for ${exposure.tableActorName}`);
        if (value === null || value === undefined || value === false) {
            await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry);
            return;
        }
        row = rule.rows.find(entry => entry.value === Number(value) && entry.damage);
    }
    row ??= selectedRow.damage ? selectedRow : null;
    if (!row) throw new Error("No damage row was selected for this exposure.");
    if (!getRandomTableState(castEntry).exposures.includes(exposure)) return;
    if (!exposure.automationProfile) configureRandomTableExposure(castEntry, exposure, row);
    const message = game.messages.get(exposure.tableMessageId);
    const { actor } = await resolveGroupedTarget({ actorUUID: exposure.tableActorUUID, tokenUUID: exposure.tableTokenUUID });
    if (!message || !actor) throw new Error("The exposure's saving throw or target could not be recovered.");
    exposure.tablePending = false;
    try {
        await processAutomaticSave(message, actor, exposure, exposure.tableDegree);
    } catch (error) {
        exposure.tablePending = true;
        throw error;
    }
}

async function processRandomTableSave(message, actor, castEntry, degree) {
    return await queueRandomTableWork(castEntry, async () => {
        await ensureRandomTableSelection(castEntry);
        const state = getRandomTableState(castEntry);
        if (state.exposures.some(entry => entry.tableMessageIds.includes(message.id))) return;
        const token = getTokenDocumentFromSpeaker(message);
        const key = getTargetKey(actor, token);
        const reference = message.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.randomTableExposure;
        let exposure = null;
        if (isRerollMessage(message)) {
            exposure = reference?.parentCastId === castEntry.castId
                ? state.exposures.find(entry => entry.castId === reference.exposureId)
                : [...state.exposures].reverse().find(entry => entry.tableTargetKey === key);
            if (!exposure) {
                ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: the original exposure for this reroll is unavailable. Adjust its result manually.`);
                return;
            }
        }
        if (!exposure) {
            exposure = createVirtualReferencedCast(castEntry, castEntry.spell, castEntry.castRank, "table-exposure");
            exposure.castId = `${castEntry.castId}:random-table:${castEntry.cycle}:${++state.nextId}`;
            exposure.tableParentCastId = castEntry.castId;
            exposure.tableTargetKey = key;
            exposure.tableActorName = actor.name;
            exposure.tableActorUUID = actor.uuid;
            exposure.tableTokenUUID = token?.uuid ?? null;
            exposure.tableMessageIds = [];
            exposure.tablePending = true;
            state.exposures.push(exposure);
        }
        exposure.tableMessageId = message.id;
        exposure.tableDegree = degree;
        exposure.tableMessageIds.push(message.id);
        // PF2e copies flags to its reroll message; this keeps an old reroll tied
        // to its own color/exposure even after the creature has been hit again.
        await message.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.randomTableExposure": {
            parentCastId: castEntry.castId, exposureId: exposure.castId
        } });
        await completeRandomTableExposure(castEntry, exposure);
    });
}

async function resolveRandomTableTargets(castEntry) {
    if (castEntry.tableTargetsBusy) return;
    const targets = [...(game.user.targets ?? [])];
    if (!targets.length) { ui.notifications.warn("Target at least one creature first."); return; }
    castEntry.tableTargetsBusy = true;
    try {
        await ensureRandomTableSelection(castEntry);
        const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
        const rule = castEntry.automationProfile.tableRule;
        for (const target of targets) {
            const token = target.document ?? target;
            const actor = token.actor ?? target.actor;
            if (!actor) continue;
            let saveMessage = null;
            const result = await rollManualTriggerSave(actor, token, rule.saveType, getCastSpellDC(castEntry),
                spell, `${spell.name} — Exposure Save`, [], {
                    originSpell: spell, castRank: castEntry.castRank,
                    onSaveMessage: message => { saveMessage = message; }
                });
            if (!saveMessage) throw new Error("PF2e did not return the exposure's save message.");
            await processRandomTableSave(saveMessage, actor, castEntry, result.degree);
        }
    } finally { castEntry.tableTargetsBusy = false; }
}

function buildRandomTableResolutionCard(castEntry) {
    const state = getRandomTableState(castEntry);
    const selection = state.selection;
    const manualCorrection = state.exposures.some(exposure => Object.values(exposure.resultGroups ?? {})
        .some(group => group.targets.some(target => target.manualCorrection)));
    const rows = state.exposures.map((exposure, index) => {
        if (exposure.tablePending) {
            return `<div style="padding:6px 0;border-top:1px solid #777;">
                <strong>${escapeHTML(exposure.tableActorName)}</strong> — ${escapeHTML(readableDegree(exposure.tableDegree))}
                <button type="button" class="pf2e-auto-table-choose" data-cast-id="${escapeHTML(castEntry.castId)}"
                    data-exposure-id="${escapeHTML(exposure.castId)}">Choose Color / Continue</button></div>`;
        }
        return `<div style="margin-top:6px;">
            <strong>Exposure ${index + 1}: ${escapeHTML(exposure.tableRowLabel)}</strong>
            <span style="opacity:0.8;"> — ${escapeHTML(exposure.tableDamageLabel)}</span>
            ${["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"].map(degree => buildResultRow(exposure, degree)).join("")}
        </div>`;
    }).join("");
    return `<div class="pf2e-auto-master-save-card" style="font-size:0.85em;line-height:1.3;">
        <strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)} — Exposure Results</strong>
        <div>${escapeHTML(selection ? `${selection.formula}: ${selection.total} — ${selection.label}` : "Table result pending")}</div>
        ${rows}
        ${manualCorrection ? "<p>⚠ A reroll occurred after results were applied. Correct the marked target manually.</p>" : ""}
    </div>`;
}

// =================================================================
// CONDITION CATALOG
// =================================================================

let CONDITION_CATALOG_PROMISE = null;

async function getConditionCatalog() {
    if (CONDITION_CATALOG_PROMISE) return CONDITION_CATALOG_PROMISE;

    CONDITION_CATALOG_PROMISE = (async () => {
        const pack = game.packs.get("pf2e.conditionitems");
        if (!pack) throw new Error('PF2e condition compendium "pf2e.conditionitems" was not found.');

        const index = await pack.getIndex({ fields: ["system.slug"] });
        const entries = index.map(entry => ({
            id: entry._id,
            name: entry.name,
            slug: normalizeSlug(entry.system?.slug ?? entry.name),
            uuid: `Compendium.pf2e.conditionitems.Item.${entry._id}`
        }));

        const byId = new Map(entries.map(entry => [entry.id, entry]));
        const bySlug = new Map(entries.map(entry => [entry.slug, entry]));
        const byName = new Map(entries.map(entry => [normalizeSlug(entry.name), entry]));

        return { entries, byId, bySlug, byName };
    })();

    return CONDITION_CATALOG_PROMISE;
}

function labelWithoutValue(label) {
    return String(label ?? "")
        .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim()
        .replace(/\s+\d+\s*$/g, "");
}

function getConditionValueFromLabel(label) {
    const match = String(label ?? "").match(/\b(\d+)\b/);
    return match ? Number(match[1]) : null;
}

function resolveConditionCatalogEntry(reference, label, catalog) {
    const rawRef = decodeURIComponent(String(reference ?? ""));
    const tail = rawRef.split(".").at(-1) ?? rawRef;

    return catalog.byId.get(tail)
        ?? catalog.bySlug.get(normalizeSlug(tail))
        ?? catalog.byName.get(normalizeSlug(tail))
        ?? catalog.bySlug.get(normalizeSlug(labelWithoutValue(label)))
        ?? catalog.byName.get(normalizeSlug(labelWithoutValue(label)))
        ?? null;
}

// =================================================================
// NON-BASIC OUTCOME PARSER
// =================================================================

function headingToDegree(text) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase();

    return {
        "critical success": "criticalSuccess",
        "success": "success",
        "failure": "failure",
        "critical failure": "criticalFailure"
    }[normalized] ?? null;
}

// =================================================================
// PF2e INLINE MARKUP NORMALIZATION
// =================================================================
//
// PF2e descriptions increasingly use inline automation instead of
// literal prose values, for example:
//
//   @Damage[4d6[mental]]
//   @Damage[2d6]
//   @Check[fortitude|against:spell]
//
// v8.1 treated these like ordinary text. That caused two problems:
//
// - @Damage remained opaque to parseDirectDamage().
// - an unlabeled @Check was replaced with an empty string, erasing
//   the secondary save before inferSecondarySave() could see it.
//
// v8.3 converts those inline constructs into semantic plain text
// before the generic outcome parser runs.
//
// This is intentionally NOT spell-specific.
// =================================================================

function replaceBalancedInline(source, marker, formatter) {
    source = String(source ?? "");

    let output = "";
    let cursor = 0;

    while (cursor < source.length) {
        const start = source.indexOf(marker, cursor);

        if (start < 0) {
            output += source.slice(cursor);
            break;
        }

        output += source.slice(cursor, start);

        const bodyStart = start + marker.length;
        let depth = 1;
        let index = bodyStart;

        for (; index < source.length; index++) {
            const char = source[index];

            if (char === "[") depth++;
            else if (char === "]") {
                depth--;
                if (depth === 0) break;
            }
        }

        if (depth !== 0) {
            // Malformed inline markup: preserve it rather than silently
            // destroying potentially useful spell text.
            output += source.slice(start);
            break;
        }

        const body = source.slice(bodyStart, index);
        let end = index + 1;
        let label = null;

        if (source[end] === "{") {
            const labelEnd = source.indexOf("}", end + 1);

            if (labelEnd >= 0) {
                label = source.slice(end + 1, labelEnd);
                end = labelEnd + 1;
            }
        }

        output += formatter(body, label);
        cursor = end;
    }

    return output;
}

function damageInlineToPlain(body, label = null) {
    const payload = String(body ?? "")
        .split("|")[0]
        .trim();

    // If a custom label actually contains a dice expression, it is often
    // already the best human-readable representation.
    if (label && /\d+d\d+/i.test(label)) {
        return String(label).trim();
    }

    // Most PF2e simple typed damage:
    //   4d6[mental]
    //   (2d8 + 4)[fire]
    //
    // v8.3's direct per-degree automation intentionally remains focused
    // on ordinary one-pool formulas, but normalization preserves more
    // complicated values for diagnostics instead of deleting them.
    const typed = payload.match(
        /^\(?\s*(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s*\)?\s*\[([^\]]+)\]\s*$/i
    );

    if (typed) {
        const formula = typed[1].replace(/\s+/g, "");
        const tags = typed[2]
            .split(",")
            .map(value => normalizeSlug(value))
            .filter(Boolean);

        // PF2e may put categories such as persistent/precision/splash in
        // the same bracket. For our simple direct-damage parser the last
        // non-category token is the most likely damage type.
        const categories = new Set([
            "persistent",
            "precision",
            "splash"
        ]);

        const type = [...tags]
            .reverse()
            .find(tag => !categories.has(tag));

        if (type) {
            return `${formula} ${type} damage`;
        }

        return formula;
    }

    // Untyped heightening increments such as @Damage[2d6].
    const untyped = payload.match(
        /^\(?\s*(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s*\)?$/i
    );

    if (untyped) {
        return untyped[1].replace(/\s+/g, "");
    }

    // Preserve an informative representation for diagnostics.
    return label?.trim() || payload;
}

function checkInlineToPlain(body, label = null) {
    const parts = String(body ?? "")
        .split("|")
        .map(part => part.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);

    let type = parts[0] ?? "";

    if (/^type:/i.test(type)) {
        type = type.replace(/^type:/i, "");
    }

    type = normalizeSlug(type);

    if (["fortitude", "reflex", "will"].includes(type)) {
        const name = type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1);
        return `${name} save`;
    }

    return label?.trim() || type;
}

function oldInlineRollToPlain(full, command, label = null) {
    if (label && /\d+d\d+/i.test(label)) {
        return label.trim();
    }

    const body = String(command ?? "").trim();

    // Legacy PF2e damage roll forms, e.g. /r 4d6[mental]
    const damageMatch = body.match(
        /^(?:r|roll|br|gmr)\s+\(?\s*(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s*\)?\s*\[([^\]]+)\]/i
    );

    if (damageMatch) {
        return `${damageMatch[1].replace(/\s+/g, "")} ${normalizeSlug(damageMatch[2])} damage`;
    }

    return label?.trim() || full;
}

function pf2eMarkupToPlain(text) {
    let plain = String(text ?? "");

    // @Damage has nested [] for damage types, so a normal [^]] regex is
    // insufficient. Use a balanced-bracket reader first.
    plain = replaceBalancedInline(
        plain,
        "@Damage[",
        damageInlineToPlain
    );

    // @Check itself normally has no nested square brackets.
    plain = plain.replace(
        /@Check\[([^\]]+)\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi,
        (_, body, label) => checkInlineToPlain(body, label)
    );

    // Preserve explicit UUID labels. Condition UUIDs without labels are
    // still recovered separately from raw HTML by extractConditionsFromOutcome().
    plain = plain.replace(
        /@UUID\[[^\]]+\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi,
        (_, label) => label ?? ""
    );

    // Legacy Foundry/PF2e inline rolls.
    plain = plain.replace(
        /\[\[\/([^\]]+)\]\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi,
        (full, command, label) => oldInlineRollToPlain(full, command, label)
    );

    return plain
        .replace(/\b(damage)\s+\1\b/gi, "$1")
        .replace(/\b(save)\s+\1\b/gi, "$1")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();
}

function htmlToPlain(html) {
    const document = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${html ?? ""}</div>`, "text/html");
    return pf2eMarkupToPlain(document.body.textContent ?? "");
}

function htmlToStructuredPlain(html) {
    // Preserve semantic boundaries that textContent normally erases between
    // table cells, rows, list items, and adjacent block elements. This is kept
    // separate from htmlToPlain() so the stable generic spell parsers retain
    // their existing behavior.
    const document = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${html ?? ""}</div>`, "text/html");
    const boundarySelector = [
        "p", "div", "section", "article", "li", "ul", "ol",
        "table", "thead", "tbody", "tfoot", "tr", "td", "th",
        "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6", "br"
    ].join(",");

    for (const element of document.body.querySelectorAll(boundarySelector)) {
        element.before(document.createTextNode(" "));
        element.after(document.createTextNode(" "));
    }

    return pf2eMarkupToPlain(document.body.textContent ?? "")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();
}

function extractOutcomeSections(descriptionHTML) {
    const document = new DOMParser().parseFromString(
        `<div id="pf2e-auto-root">${descriptionHTML ?? ""}</div>`,
        "text/html"
    );

    const sections = {};

    for (const strong of document.querySelectorAll("strong")) {
        const degree = headingToDegree(strong.textContent);
        if (!degree || sections[degree]) continue;

        const block = strong.closest("p, li, div") ?? strong.parentElement;
        if (!block) continue;

        const clone = block.cloneNode(true);
        const heading = clone.querySelector("strong");
        heading?.remove();

        sections[degree] = {
            html: clone.innerHTML.trim(),
            rawText: clone.textContent ?? "",
            text: pf2eMarkupToPlain(clone.textContent ?? "")
        };
    }

    // PF2e: an omitted critical result uses the corresponding ordinary
    // result. Do not invent a basic-save multiplier or assume "no effect".
    if (!sections.criticalSuccess && sections.success) sections.criticalSuccess = { ...deepClone(sections.success), omittedFrom: "success" };
    if (!sections.criticalFailure && sections.failure) sections.criticalFailure = { ...deepClone(sections.failure), omittedFrom: "failure" };
    return sections;
}

function extractHeightenedSections(descriptionHTML) {
    const document = new DOMParser().parseFromString(
        `<div id="pf2e-auto-height-root">${descriptionHTML ?? ""}</div>`,
        "text/html"
    );

    const sections = [];

    for (const strong of document.querySelectorAll("strong")) {
        const heading = String(strong.textContent ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
        const match = heading.match(/^Heightened\s*\(\+(\d+)\)/i);
        if (!match) continue;

        const block = strong.closest("p, li, div") ?? strong.parentElement;
        if (!block) continue;

        const clone = block.cloneNode(true);
        clone.querySelector("strong")?.remove();

        sections.push({
            interval: Number(match[1]),
            heading,
            html: clone.innerHTML.trim(),
            rawText: clone.textContent ?? "",
            text: pf2eMarkupToPlain(clone.textContent ?? "")
        });
    }

    return sections;
}

function inferDamageMultiplier(text) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[’]/g, "'")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

    if (/\bunaffected\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\btakes? no damage\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\bdoes(?:n't| not) take (?:any )?damage\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\bno damage\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return 0;
    }

    if (/\bhalf (?:the )?damage\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\bhalf damage\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return 0.5;
    }

    if (/\bdouble (?:the )?damage\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\btwice (?:the )?damage\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return 2;
    }

    if (/\bfull (?:the )?damage\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\bnormal damage\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\btakes? the spell'?s damage\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return 1;
    }

    return null;
}


// =================================================================
// PF2e INLINE @Damage AS AN EXECUTABLE DAMAGE SOURCE
// =================================================================
//
// v8.3:
// Some PF2e items intentionally store unusual/scaling damage only as
// an @Damage inline.  The rendered label may say something like
// "scaling d6 mental damage", which is not enough information to
// reconstruct the roll from prose.
//
// Keep the actual @Damage payload and let Foundry/PF2e resolve its
// @item.rank (and other @data references) against spell roll data.
//
// This is generic: no spell names are involved.
// =================================================================

function collectBalancedInlines(source, marker) {
    source = String(source ?? "");

    const entries = [];
    let cursor = 0;

    while (cursor < source.length) {
        const start = source.indexOf(marker, cursor);
        if (start < 0) break;

        const bodyStart = start + marker.length;
        let depth = 1;
        let index = bodyStart;

        for (; index < source.length; index++) {
            const char = source[index];

            if (char === "[") depth++;
            else if (char === "]") {
                depth--;
                if (depth === 0) break;
            }
        }

        if (depth !== 0) break;

        const body = source.slice(bodyStart, index);
        let end = index + 1;
        let label = null;

        if (source[end] === "{") {
            const labelEnd = source.indexOf("}", end + 1);
            if (labelEnd >= 0) {
                label = source.slice(end + 1, labelEnd);
                end = labelEnd + 1;
            }
        }

        entries.push({
            body,
            label,
            full: source.slice(start, end),
            start,
            end
        });

        cursor = end;
    }

    return entries;
}

function getInlineDamagePayload(body) {
    const source = String(body ?? "");
    let squareDepth = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i++) {
        const char = source[i];

        if (char === "[") squareDepth++;
        else if (char === "]") squareDepth = Math.max(0, squareDepth - 1);
        else if (char === "|" && squareDepth === 0) {
            return source.slice(0, i).trim();
        }
    }

    return source.trim();
}

function getOutcomeDamageRollData(castEntry) {
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);

    let data = {};

    try {
        data = foundry.utils.deepClone(
            spell.getRollData?.() ?? {}
        );
    } catch (_) {
        data = {};
    }

    data.item ??= {};

    const castRank = Number(
        castEntry.castRank
        ?? spell.rank
        ?? spell.baseRank
        ?? data.item.rank
        ?? data.item.level
    );

    if (Number.isFinite(castRank)) {
        // Current PF2e inline spell formulas use @item.rank.  Keeping
        // item.level in sync also helps older/custom content.
        data.item.rank = castRank;
        data.item.level = castRank;
    }

    return data;
}

function getDamageTypeFromTypedFormula(formula) {
    const match = String(formula ?? "").match(/\[([^\[\]]+)\]\s*$/);
    if (!match) return null;

    const categories = new Set([
        "persistent",
        "precision",
        "splash"
    ]);

    return match[1]
        .split(",")
        .map(value => normalizeSlug(value))
        .filter(Boolean)
        .reverse()
        .find(value => !categories.has(value))
        ?? null;
}

function simplifyResolvedInlineDamageFormula(typedFormula) {
    const source = String(typedFormula ?? "").trim();
    const typed = source.match(/^(.*)\[([^\[\]]+)\]\s*$/);

    if (!typed) {
        return {
            typedFormula: source,
            formula: source,
            type: null
        };
    }

    let formula = typed[1].trim();
    const type = getDamageTypeFromTypedFormula(source);

    // Common PF2e scaling form: an arithmetic expression determining
    // the number of dice, followed by dN.  Evaluate only the deterministic
    // dice-count expression; the dice themselves remain unevaluated.
    const diceMatch = formula.match(/^(.*)d(\d+)$/i);

    if (diceMatch) {
        let countExpression = diceMatch[1].trim();
        const faces = Number(diceMatch[2]);

        // Remove redundant outer parentheses only when balanced enough
        // for safeEval; safeEval itself remains the authority.
        try {
            const count = Number(Roll.safeEval(countExpression));

            if (Number.isFinite(count) && count >= 0 && Number.isInteger(count)) {
                formula = `${count}d${faces}`;
            }
        } catch (_) {}
    }

    return {
        typedFormula: source,
        formula,
        type
    };
}

function parseInlineDamageSpec(rawText, castEntry) {
    const entries = collectBalancedInlines(rawText, "@Damage[");
    if (!entries.length) return null;

    // For a degree-of-success line, the first inline damage expression is
    // normally the damage belonging to that outcome.  Secondary-save code
    // separately uses this fallback only when its success branch has a single
    // inline damage source.
    const entry = entries[0];
    const payload = getInlineDamagePayload(entry.body);

    if (!payload) return null;

    const DamageRoll = getPF2eDamageRollClass();
    const rollData = getOutcomeDamageRollData(castEntry);

    let resolvedPayload = payload;

    try {
        resolvedPayload = DamageRoll.replaceFormulaData(
            payload,
            rollData,
            {
                missing: "0",
                warn: false
            }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        debug("Could not pre-resolve inline @Damage formula; PF2e will try at roll time:", {
            payload,
            error
        });
    }

    const simplified = simplifyResolvedInlineDamageFormula(resolvedPayload);
    const dynamic = /@(?:item|actor|target)\./i.test(payload);

    return {
        kind: "formula",

        // Human-readable/concrete value where possible.
        formula: simplified.formula,
        type: simplified.type ?? getDamageTypeFromTypedFormula(payload) ?? "untyped",

        // Exact PF2e formula remains authoritative for the actual roll.
        inlineFormula: payload,
        resolvedInlineFormula: simplified.typedFormula,
        inlineLabel: entry.label ?? null,
        sourceText: entry.full,

        // If PF2e already made the @Damage expression rank-aware, do not
        // apply the prose Heightened section again or damage would double-scale.
        heighteningResolved: dynamic
    };
}

function parseDirectDamage(text) {
    const normalized = pf2eMarkupToPlain(text ?? "")
        .replace(/[–—]/g, "-")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

    // Generic ordinary typed damage. This now works whether the source
    // description used literal prose or PF2e @Damage inline markup.
    //
    // Examples after normalization:
    //   "takes 4d6 mental damage"
    //   "takes 2d8+4 fire damage"
    //   "deals 6d6 cold damage"
    const match = normalized.match(
        /\b(?:takes?|deals?|suffers?|receives?)\s+(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s+([a-z][a-z-]*)\s+damage\b/i
    );

    if (!match) return null;

    return {
        kind: "formula",
        formula: match[1].replace(/\s+/g, ""),
        type: normalizeSlug(match[2]),
        sourceText: match[0]
    };
}


// =================================================================
// TARGET-CONDITIONAL DAMAGE
// =================================================================
//
// v8.5:
// Some spells have a normal structured damage component plus an additional
// damage component that applies only to targets with a particular creature
// trait.  PF2e often stores that extra component as an inline @Damage entry
// in the description instead of system.damage.
//
// This parser discovers the target trait from CONFIG.PF2E.creatureTraits and
// associates the nearby @Damage expression with that trait.  Nothing here
// depends on a spell name or a specific creature trait.
// =================================================================

function getCreatureTraitCatalog() {
    const configured = CONFIG.PF2E?.creatureTraits ?? {};
    const catalog = [];

    for (const [slug, labelKey] of Object.entries(configured)) {
        const localized = game.i18n?.localize?.(labelKey) ?? labelKey ?? slug;
        const names = new Set([
            normalizeSlug(slug),
            normalizeSlug(localized),
            normalizeSlug(String(labelKey ?? ""))
        ]);

        catalog.push({
            slug: normalizeSlug(slug),
            label: String(localized ?? slug),
            names: Array.from(names).filter(Boolean)
        });
    }

    // Longest names first reduces accidental partial matches when one trait
    // label is contained inside another.
    return catalog.sort((a, b) =>
        Math.max(...b.names.map(name => name.length), 0)
        - Math.max(...a.names.map(name => name.length), 0)
    );
}

function findCreatureTraitInPhrase(phrase, catalog = getCreatureTraitCatalog()) {
    const normalizedPhrase = `-${normalizeSlug(phrase)}-`;

    for (const entry of catalog) {
        for (const name of entry.names) {
            if (normalizedPhrase.includes(`-${name}-`)) return entry;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function inferTargetTraitPredicate(contextText, catalog = getCreatureTraitCatalog()) {
    const plain = pf2eMarkupToPlain(contextText ?? "")
        .replace(/[–—]/g, "-")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

    const candidatePhrases = [];

    // "... damage to/against <trait> creatures"
    for (const match of plain.matchAll(
        /\b(?:to|against)\s+(?:all\s+|any\s+|an?\s+)?([^.;:,]{1,80}?)\s+creatures?\b/gi
    )) {
        candidatePhrases.push(match[1]);
    }

    // "creatures with the <trait> trait"
    for (const match of plain.matchAll(
        /\bcreatures?\s+with\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.;:,]{1,60}?)\s+trait\b/gi
    )) {
        candidatePhrases.push(match[1]);
    }

    // "if/when the target is <trait>"
    for (const match of plain.matchAll(
        /\b(?:if|when)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:target|creature)\s+(?:is|has)\s+(?:the\s+|an?\s+)?([^.;:,]{1,60}?)(?:\s+creature)?\b/gi
    )) {
        candidatePhrases.push(match[1]);
    }

    for (const phrase of candidatePhrases) {
        const trait = findCreatureTraitInPhrase(phrase, catalog);
        if (!trait) continue;

        return {
            kind: "creature-trait",
            trait: trait.slug,
            label: trait.label,
            sourcePhrase: String(phrase).trim()
        };
    }

    return null;
}

function actorHasCreatureTrait(actor, traitSlug) {
    const slug = normalizeSlug(traitSlug);
    if (!actor || !slug) return false;

    const traits = Array.from(actor.system?.traits?.value ?? [])
        .map(normalizeSlug);

    if (traits.includes(slug)) return true;

    // Some PF2e actor types expose prepared self roll options even when the
    // underlying sheet structure differs. Use them as a compatibility fallback.
    try {
        const options = actor.getRollOptions?.(["self"]) ?? [];
        if (options.includes(`self:trait:${slug}`)) return true;
    } catch (_) {}

    try {
        const options = actor.getRollOptions?.("self") ?? [];
        if (options.includes(`self:trait:${slug}`)) return true;
    } catch (_) {}

    return false;
}

function targetMatchesConditionalPredicate(actor, predicate) {
    if (!predicate) return false;

    if (predicate.kind === "creature-trait") {
        return actorHasCreatureTrait(actor, predicate.trait);
    }

    return false;
}

/** Whether PF2e currently considers this actor to have void/negative healing. */
function actorHasVoidHealing(actor) {
    if (!actor) return false;

    if (actor.hitPoints?.negativeHealing === true) return true;
    if (actor.system?.attributes?.hp?.negativeHealing === true) return true;

    const optionSets = [];
    try { optionSets.push(actor.getRollOptions?.(["self"]) ?? []); } catch (_) {}
    try { optionSets.push(actor.getRollOptions?.("self") ?? []); } catch (_) {}
    try { optionSets.push(actor.getRollOptions?.(["all"]) ?? []); } catch (_) {}

    return optionSets.some(options =>
        Array.from(options ?? []).some(option =>
            option === "self:negative-healing" || option === "self:void-healing"
        )
    );
}

function normalizeDamageTypeAlias(value) {
    const slug = normalizeSlug(value);
    if (slug === "negative") return "void";
    if (slug === "positive") return "vitality";
    return slug;
}

function targetMatchesDamageSubstitutionPredicate(actor, predicate) {
    if (!predicate) return false;

    if (predicate.kind === "void-healing") {
        return actorHasVoidHealing(actor);
    }

    if (predicate.kind === "creature-trait") {
        return actorHasCreatureTrait(actor, predicate.trait);
    }

    return false;
}

/**
 * Infer prose rules that replace one typed damage instance for a target class.
 *
 * Current generic pattern:
 *   "A creature ... with void healing ... takes vitality damage instead of
 *    void damage from this spell."
 *
 * Legacy positive/negative terminology is normalized to vitality/void.
 */
function inferTargetDamageSubstitutionRules(spell) {
    const raw = String(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    if (!raw) return [];

    const plain = htmlToPlain(raw).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const sentences = plain.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+/);
    const rules = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    for (let index = 0; index < sentences.length; index++) {
        const sentence = sentences[index];
        if (!/\b(?:void|negative)\s+healing\b/i.test(sentence)) continue;

        const replacement = sentence.match(
            /\btakes?\s+([a-z][a-z-]*)\s+damage\s+instead\s+of\s+([a-z][a-z-]*)\s+damage\b/i
        );
        if (!replacement) continue;

        const toType = normalizeDamageTypeAlias(replacement[1]);
        const fromType = normalizeDamageTypeAlias(replacement[2]);
        if (!toType || !fromType || toType === fromType) continue;

        const key = `void-healing:${fromType}:${toType}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);

        rules.push({
            id: `damage-substitution-${index}-${fromType}-to-${toType}`,
            predicate: {
                kind: "void-healing",
                label: "Void Healing",
                sourcePhrase: sentence
            },
            fromType,
            toType,
            sourceText: sentence
        });
    }

    return rules;
}

function getMatchingTargetDamageSubstitutionRules(actor, castEntry) {
    return (castEntry.automationProfile?.targetDamageSubstitutionRules ?? [])
        .filter(rule => targetMatchesDamageSubstitutionPredicate(actor, rule.predicate));
}

/**
 * Test whether PF2e considers this creature immune to the spell as an effect.
 *
 * PF2e's prepared Immunity objects own the real predicate logic, including
 * exceptions. Some important immunities (notably mental/emotion/fear) are
 * defined against an EFFECT carrying the trait rather than against a Spell
 * document directly, so we deliberately project the spell's native item roll
 * options into an effect-like statement set before asking Immunity#test().
 */
function getSpellEffectImmunity(actor, castEntry) {
    if (!actor || !castEntry) return null;

    // Mirror PF2e's own global IWR switch when it is available.
    try {
        if (game.pf2e?.settings?.iwr === false) return null;
    } catch (_) {}

    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    if (!spell) return null;

    const traits = Array.from(spell.system?.traits?.value ?? [])
        .map(normalizeSlug)
        .filter(Boolean);

    const statements = new Set([
        // Keep the actual spell identity for immunities such as magic/spells.
        "item:type:spell",
        "item:from-spell",
        // Also project it as the EFFECT produced by that spell. This is what
        // PF2e's mental/emotion/fear-style immunity predicates expect.
        "item:type:effect"
    ]);

    try {
        for (const option of spell.getRollOptions?.("item") ?? []) {
            statements.add(option);
        }
    } catch (_) {}

    for (const trait of traits) {
        statements.add(`item:trait:${trait}`);
        statements.add(`origin:action:trait:${trait}`);
    }

    const tradition = normalizeSlug(
        spell.spellcasting?.tradition
        ?? spell.system?.traditions?.value?.[0]
        ?? ""
    );

    if (tradition) {
        statements.add(`item:trait:${tradition}`);
        statements.add(`origin:action:trait:${tradition}`);
    }

    const immunities = Array.from(
        actor.attributes?.immunities
        ?? actor.system?.attributes?.immunities
        ?? []
    );

    for (const immunity of immunities) {
        if (typeof immunity?.test !== "function") continue;

        let matches = false;
        try {
            matches = immunity.test(statements) === true;
        } catch (error) {
            debug("SPELL IMMUNITY TEST ERROR:", {
                spell: spell.name,
                target: actor.name,
                immunity: immunity?.type ?? immunity?.label ?? null,
                message: error?.message ?? String(error)
            });
            continue;
        }

        if (!matches) continue;

        let data = null;
        try {
            data = immunity.toObject?.() ?? null;
        } catch (_) {}

        const type = normalizeSlug(data?.type ?? immunity.type ?? "immunity");
        const label = String(
            data?.label
            ?? immunity.label
            ?? immunity.typeLabel
            ?? CONFIG.PF2E?.immunityTypes?.[type]
            ?? type
            ?? "Immunity"
        );

        return {
            type,
            label,
            exceptions: deepClone(data?.exceptions ?? immunity.exceptions ?? []),
            source: data?.source ?? immunity.source ?? null,
            spellTraits: traits,
            tradition: tradition || null
        };
    }

    return null;
}

function heightenLiteralConditionalDamage(spell, castEntry, damageSpec, predicate) {
    if (!damageSpec || damageSpec.kind !== "formula" || damageSpec.heighteningResolved) {
        return damageSpec;
    }

    const baseRank = getSpellBaseRank(spell);
    const castRank = Number(castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank ?? baseRank);
    if (!baseRank || !castRank || castRank <= baseRank) return damageSpec;

    const sections = extractHeightenedSections(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    if (!sections.length) return damageSpec;

    const baseMatch = String(damageSpec.formula ?? "").match(/^(\d+)d(\d+)$/i);
    if (!baseMatch) return damageSpec;

    const baseDice = Number(baseMatch[1]);
    const faces = Number(baseMatch[2]);
    let addedDice = 0;

    for (const section of sections) {
        if (!section.interval || section.interval <= 0) continue;
        const steps = Math.floor((castRank - baseRank) / section.interval);
        if (steps <= 0) continue;

        const plain = section.text.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        const type = escapeRegex(normalizeSlug(damageSpec.type));
        const traitNames = [
            normalizeSlug(predicate?.trait),
            normalizeSlug(predicate?.label)
        ].filter(Boolean).map(escapeRegex);

        for (const traitName of traitNames) {
            // Generic wording such as:
            // "the vitality damage against <trait> increases by 1d10"
            const re = new RegExp(
                `(?:the\\s+)?${type}\\s+damage\\s+(?:against|to)\\s+[^.;]*${traitName}[^.;]*?(?:increases?|is increased)\\s+by\\s+(\\d+)d(\\d+)`,
                "i"
            );
            const match = plain.match(re);
            if (!match) continue;

            const dice = Number(match[1]);
            const dieFaces = Number(match[2]);
            if (dieFaces === faces) addedDice += dice * steps;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!addedDice) return damageSpec;

    return {
        ...damageSpec,
        baseFormula: damageSpec.formula,
        formula: `${baseDice + addedDice}d${faces}`,
        heighteningApplied: {
            addedDice,
            faces,
            source: "target-conditional-description"
        }
    };
}

function inferTargetConditionalDamageRules(spell, castEntry) {
    const raw = String(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    if (!raw) return [];

    const catalog = getCreatureTraitCatalog();
    const rules = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const inlineEntries = collectBalancedInlines(raw, "@Damage[");

    for (let index = 0; index < inlineEntries.length; index++) {
        const entry = inlineEntries[index];

        // Keep the window local to this inline. The target clause is normally
        // immediately after the damage link, while words like "additional"
        // or "plus" are normally immediately before it.
        const before = raw.slice(Math.max(0, entry.start - 220), entry.start);
        const after = raw.slice(entry.end, Math.min(raw.length, entry.end + 260));
        const context = htmlToPlain(`${before}${entry.full}${after}`);
        const predicate = inferTargetTraitPredicate(context, catalog);
        if (!predicate) continue;

        // Avoid treating an ordinary degree-of-success damage inline as a
        // global target conditional merely because a later sentence happens
        // to mention a creature trait. Require local target/damage language.
        if (!/\b(?:additional|extra|plus|damage\s+(?:to|against))\b/i.test(context)) {
            continue;
        }

        let damage = parseInlineDamageSpec(entry.full, castEntry);
        if (!damage) continue;

        damage = heightenLiteralConditionalDamage(spell, castEntry, damage, predicate);

        const key = [predicate.kind, predicate.trait, damage.type, damage.inlineFormula ?? damage.formula].join(":");
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);

        rules.push({
            id: `conditional-${index}-${predicate.trait}-${damage.type ?? "damage"}`,
            predicate,
            damage,
            saveScaling: "primary",
            sourceText: context
        });
    }

    // Fallback for custom/legacy descriptions that use literal dice rather
    // than PF2e @Damage markup.
    const plain = htmlToPlain(raw);
    const literal = /\b(?:plus\s+)?(?:an?\s+)?(?:additional|extra)\s+(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s+([a-z][a-z-]*)\s+damage\s+(?:to|against)\s+([^.;:,]{1,80}?)\s+creatures?\b/gi;

    for (const match of plain.matchAll(literal)) {
        const predicate = inferTargetTraitPredicate(match[0], catalog);
        if (!predicate) continue;

        let damage = {
            kind: "formula",
            formula: match[1].replace(/\s+/g, ""),
            type: normalizeSlug(match[2]),
            sourceText: match[0]
        };

        damage = heightenLiteralConditionalDamage(spell, castEntry, damage, predicate);

        const key = [predicate.kind, predicate.trait, damage.type, damage.formula].join(":");
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);

        rules.push({
            id: `conditional-literal-${rules.length}-${predicate.trait}-${damage.type ?? "damage"}`,
            predicate,
            damage,
            saveScaling: "primary",
            sourceText: match[0]
        });
    }

    return rules;
}


// =================================================================
// TARGET-MODE HEALING
// =================================================================
//
// Some PF2e spells use one evaluated amount in two mutually exclusive ways:
// one class of targets recovers HP while another class receives the spell's
// structured damage. The save on the item belongs to the damaging branch;
// the healing branch ignores that save degree.
//
// The parser recognizes "living" as a target class and otherwise discovers
// creature-trait classes from CONFIG.PF2E.creatureTraits. No spell names are
// used here.
// =================================================================

function actorIsLivingForTargetMode(actor) {
    if (!actor) return false;

    const negativeHealing = actor.system?.attributes?.hp?.negativeHealing;
    if (typeof negativeHealing === "boolean") return !negativeHealing;

    try {
        if (typeof actor.isAffectedBy === "function") {
            return !actor.isAffectedBy("vitality");
        }
    } catch (_) {}

    return true;
}

function inferHealingPredicateFromSentence(sentence, catalog = getCreatureTraitCatalog()) {
    const plain = pf2eMarkupToPlain(sentence ?? "")
        .replace(/[–—]/g, "-")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

    if (/\bliving\s+creatures?\b/i.test(plain)) {
        return {
            kind: "living",
            label: "Living",
            sourcePhrase: "living creature"
        };
    }

    const verbIndex = plain.search(/\b(?:recover|regain|restore)\b/i);
    const targetPhrase = verbIndex >= 0 ? plain.slice(0, verbIndex) : plain;
    const trait = findCreatureTraitInPhrase(targetPhrase, catalog);

    if (trait) {
        return {
            kind: "creature-trait",
            trait: trait.slug,
            label: trait.label,
            sourcePhrase: targetPhrase.trim()
        };
    }

    return null;
}

function targetMatchesHealingPredicate(actor, predicate) {
    if (!predicate) return false;

    if (predicate.kind === "living") {
        return actorIsLivingForTargetMode(actor);
    }

    if (predicate.kind === "creature-trait") {
        return actorHasCreatureTrait(actor, predicate.trait);
    }

    return false;
}

function heightenLiteralHealingFormula(spell, castEntry, formula) {
    const baseMatch = String(formula ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, "").match(/^(\d+)d(\d+)$/i);
    if (!baseMatch) return formula;

    const baseRank = getSpellBaseRank(spell);
    const castRank = Number(castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank ?? baseRank);
    if (!baseRank || !castRank || castRank <= baseRank) return formula;

    const sections = extractHeightenedSections(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    let addedDice = 0;
    const baseDice = Number(baseMatch[1]);
    const faces = Number(baseMatch[2]);

    for (const section of sections) {
        if (!section.interval || section.interval <= 0) continue;
        const steps = Math.floor((castRank - baseRank) / section.interval);
        if (steps <= 0) continue;

        const plain = String(section.text ?? "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        const match = plain.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?healing\s+(?:increases?|is increased)\s+by\s+(\d+)d(\d+)\b/i);
        if (!match) continue;

        const dice = Number(match[1]);
        const dieFaces = Number(match[2]);
        if (dieFaces === faces) addedDice += dice * steps;
    }

    return addedDice ? `${baseDice + addedDice}d${faces}` : formula;
}

function inferTargetHealingRules(spell, castEntry, inspection) {
    const raw = String(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    if (!raw) return [];

    const plain = htmlToPlain(raw)
        .replace(/[–—]/g, "-")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

    const catalog = getCreatureTraitCatalog();
    const rules = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    // Keep sentences reasonably local so a target phrase from one branch does
    // not attach itself to healing described in another branch.
    const sentences = plain
        .split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+/)
        .map(part => part.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);

    const baseDamageFormula = inspection?.damageComponents?.[0]?.formula
        ? String(inspection.damageComponents[0].formula).replace(/\s+/g, "")
        : null;

    for (const sentence of sentences) {
        if (!/\b(?:recover|regain|restore)\b/i.test(sentence)) continue;
        if (!/\b(?:hit points?|hp)\b/i.test(sentence)) continue;

        const predicate = inferHealingPredicateFromSentence(sentence, catalog);
        if (!predicate) continue;

        const verbTail = sentence.slice(Math.max(0, sentence.search(/\b(?:recover|regain|restore)\b/i)));
        const literalMatch = verbTail.match(
            /\b(?:recover|regain|restore)\s+(\d+d\d+(?:\s*[+-]\s*\d+)?)\s+(?:hit points?|hp)\b/i
        );
        const thatAmount = /\b(?:recover|regain|restore)\s+that\s+amount(?:\s+of)?\s+(?:hit points?|hp)\b/i.test(verbTail);

        let healing = null;

        if (thatAmount) {
            if ((inspection?.damageCount ?? 0) > 0) healing = { kind: "shared" };
        } else if (literalMatch) {
            const literalFormula = literalMatch[1].replace(/\s+/g, "");

            if ((inspection?.damageCount ?? 0) > 0 && baseDamageFormula === literalFormula) {
                // Reuse the structured roll so healing and damage from the same
                // spell event use the same evaluated dice and native heightening.
                healing = { kind: "shared" };
            } else {
                healing = {
                    kind: "formula",
                    formula: heightenLiteralHealingFormula(spell, castEntry, literalFormula),
                    type: "untyped"
                };
            }
        }

        if (!healing) continue;

        const key = [predicate.kind, predicate.trait ?? "", healing.kind, healing.formula ?? "shared"].join(":");
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);

        rules.push({
            id: `healing-${rules.length}-${predicate.kind}-${predicate.trait ?? "living"}`,
            predicate,
            healing,
            saveIndependent: true,
            sourceText: sentence
        });
    }

    return rules;
}

function findMatchingTargetHealingRule(actor, rules) {
    for (const rule of rules ?? []) {
        if (targetMatchesHealingPredicate(actor, rule.predicate)) return rule;
    }
    return null;
}

function conditionalDamageMultiplierForGroup(group) {
    if (!group) return null;

    if (group.damage?.kind === "shared" || group.damage?.kind === "none") {
        const multiplier = Number(group.damage?.multiplier ?? 0);
        return Number.isFinite(multiplier) ? multiplier : null;
    }

    // A formula-defined primary outcome might still explicitly say half/full/
    // double damage. Only reuse a conditional component when that relationship
    // can be inferred safely from the outcome text.
    const inferred = inferDamageMultiplier(group.rawText ?? "");
    return inferred === null ? null : inferred;
}

function groupHasConditionalDamage(group) {
    const multiplier = conditionalDamageMultiplierForGroup(group);
    return (group?.conditionalDamageRules?.length ?? 0) > 0
        && Number.isFinite(multiplier)
        && multiplier > 0;
}

function conditionalDamageSummary(rule) {
    const damage = rule?.damage ?? {};
    const formula = damage.formula ?? damage.inlineLabel ?? "Scaling";
    const type = damage.type ? ` ${damage.type}` : "";
    const target = rule?.predicate?.label ?? rule?.predicate?.trait ?? "matching targets";
    return `${formula}${type} vs ${target}`.trim();
}

async function applyConditionalDamageRules(castEntry, group, target, actor, token) {
    const rules = group?.conditionalDamageRules ?? [];
    if (!rules.length) return [];

    const multiplier = conditionalDamageMultiplierForGroup(group);
    if (!Number.isFinite(multiplier) || multiplier <= 0) return [];

    const applied = [];

    for (const rule of rules) {
        if (!targetMatchesConditionalPredicate(actor, rule.predicate)) {
            debug("Conditional damage skipped; target predicate did not match:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                target: actor.name,
                predicate: rule.predicate,
                damage: rule.damage
            });
            continue;
        }

        const roll = await getCustomDamageRoll(
            castEntry,
            `target-conditional:${rule.id}`,
            rule.damage,
            `Conditional Damage — ${rule.predicate?.label ?? rule.predicate?.trait ?? "Target"}`
        );

        if (!roll) continue;

        const result = await applyDamageRollToActor(
            actor,
            token,
            roll,
            multiplier,
            castEntry,
            "Applying target-conditional damage:"
        );

        if (result?.applied) {
            applied.push(rule.id);

            await handlePostDamageTerminal(
                castEntry,
                target,
                actor,
