                result.beforeHP,
                result.afterHP
            );
        }
    }

    target.conditionalDamageApplied = Array.from(new Set([
        ...(target.conditionalDamageApplied ?? []),
        ...applied
    ]));

    return applied;
}

function getSpellBaseRank(spell) {
    const candidates = [
        spell.baseRank,
        spell.system?.level?.value,
        spell.rank
    ];

    for (const candidate of candidates) {
        const value = Number(candidate);
        if (Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
    }

    return null;
}

function parseTextHeightening(spell, castRank) {
    const description = spell.system?.description?.value ?? "";
    const heightened = extractHeightenedSections(description);
    const baseRank = getSpellBaseRank(spell);

    if (!heightened.length || !baseRank || !castRank || castRank <= baseRank) {
        return {
            baseRank,
            castRank,
            increments: {},
            sections: heightened
        };
    }

    const increments = {};

    for (const section of heightened) {
        if (!section.interval || section.interval <= 0) continue;

        const steps = Math.floor((castRank - baseRank) / section.interval);
        if (steps <= 0) continue;

        const plain = section.text.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");

        // "increases by 2d6 on a failure"
        const afterAmount = /(?:increases?|increased)\s+by\s+(\d+)d(\d+)\s+on\s+(?:a\s+)?(critical success|success|critical failure|failure)/gi;
        for (const match of plain.matchAll(afterAmount)) {
            const degree = headingToDegree(match[3]);
            if (!degree) continue;
            increments[degree] = {
                dice: Number(match[1]) * steps,
                faces: Number(match[2]),
                steps,
                interval: section.interval
            };
        }

        // "damage on a failure increases by 2d6"
        const beforeAmount = /(?:damage\s+)?on\s+(?:a\s+)?(critical success|success|critical failure|failure)\s+(?:damage\s+)?(?:increases?|is increased)\s+by\s+(\d+)d(\d+)/gi;
        for (const match of plain.matchAll(beforeAmount)) {
            const degree = headingToDegree(match[1]);
            if (!degree) continue;
            increments[degree] = {
                dice: Number(match[2]) * steps,
                faces: Number(match[3]),
                steps,
                interval: section.interval
            };
        }

        // Common compact wording: "by 2d6 on a failure and by 3d6 on a critical failure"
        const compact = /(?:by\s+)?(\d+)d(\d+)\s+on\s+(?:a\s+)?(critical success|success|critical failure|failure)/gi;
        for (const match of plain.matchAll(compact)) {
            const degree = headingToDegree(match[3]);
            if (!degree || increments[degree]) continue;
            increments[degree] = {
                dice: Number(match[1]) * steps,
                faces: Number(match[2]),
                steps,
                interval: section.interval
            };
        }
    }

    return {
        baseRank,
        castRank,
        increments,
        sections: heightened
    };
}

function applyTextHeighteningToDamage(damageSpec, degree, heightening) {
    if (!damageSpec || damageSpec.kind !== "formula") return damageSpec;
    if (damageSpec.heighteningResolved) return damageSpec;

    const increment = heightening?.increments?.[degree];
    if (!increment?.dice) return damageSpec;

    const match = damageSpec.formula.match(/^(\d+)d(\d+)$/i);
    if (!match) {
        return {
            ...damageSpec,
            manualHeightening: true
        };
    }

    const baseDice = Number(match[1]);
    const faces = Number(match[2]);

    if (faces !== increment.faces) {
        return {
            ...damageSpec,
            manualHeightening: true
        };
    }

    return {
        ...damageSpec,
        baseFormula: damageSpec.formula,
        formula: `${baseDice + increment.dice}d${faces}`,
        heighteningApplied: {
            addedDice: increment.dice,
            faces,
            steps: increment.steps,
            interval: increment.interval
        }
    };
}

function parseSimpleDuration(text) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    const numberWords = {
        one: 1, two: 2, three: 3, four: 4, five: 5,
        six: 6, seven: 7, eight: 8, nine: 9, ten: 10
    };

    let match = normalized.match(
        /\bfor\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(rounds?|minutes?|hours?)\b/
    );

    if (match) {
        const value = Number(match[1]) || numberWords[match[1]];
        const rawUnit = match[2];
        const unit = rawUnit.startsWith("round")
            ? "rounds"
            : rawUnit.startsWith("minute")
                ? "minutes"
                : "hours";

        return {
            value,
            unit,
            expiry: unit === "rounds" ? "turn-start" : null,
            anchor: "effect-start"
        };
    }

    // Creature-relative turn durations appear in several equivalent PF2e
    // phrasings.  In particular, remastered Blindness uses
    // "until its next turn begins" rather than "for 1 round".
    // These must expire on the AFFECTED creature's turn, not the caster's.
    const subject = "(?:its|their|the target'?s|the creature'?s)";

    match = normalized.match(new RegExp(
        `\\buntil\\s+(?:the\\s+)?(start|beginning|end)\\s+of\\s+${subject}\\s+next\\s+turn\\b`
    ));
    if (match) {
        return {
            value: 1,
            unit: "rounds",
            expiry: match[1] === "end" ? "turn-end" : "turn-start",
            anchor: "target-turn"
        };
    }

    match = normalized.match(new RegExp(
        `\\buntil\\s+${subject}\\s+next\\s+turn\\s+(begins?|starts?|ends?)\\b`
    ));
    if (match) {
        return {
            value: 1,
            unit: "rounds",
            expiry: /^ends?$/i.test(match[1]) ? "turn-end" : "turn-start",
            anchor: "target-turn"
        };
    }

    return null;
}

function getConditionSnippet(plain, conditionName) {
    const normalized = String(plain ?? "");
    const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${escapeRegex(conditionName)}\\b`, "i");
    const match = regex.exec(normalized);
    if (!match) return normalized;

    const start = Math.max(0, match.index - 45);
    const end = Math.min(normalized.length, match.index + match[0].length + 120);
    return normalized.slice(start, end);
}

function detectLinkedCondition(effect, plain, catalog) {
    const sourceName = effect.name ?? effect.condition;
    const source = escapeRegex(sourceName);

    // PF2e descriptions commonly express linked conditions in either order:
    //   source first:     "Slowed 1 for as long as it's Sickened"
    //   dependency first: "while it's Sickened, it's also Slowed 1"
    // Treat both as the same relationship: SOURCE exists only while DEPENDENCY
    // remains present. This is intentionally generic and condition-name driven.
    for (const dependency of catalog.entries) {
        if (dependency.slug === effect.condition) continue;

        const dep = escapeRegex(dependency.name);
        const subject = `(?:it(?:'s| is| remains)?|the target(?: is| remains)?|the creature(?: is| remains)?|they(?:'re| are| remain)?)?`;
        const sourceLead = `(?:it(?:'s| is)\\s+(?:also\\s+)?|the target(?: is| becomes)?\\s+(?:also\\s+)?|the creature(?: is| becomes)?\\s+(?:also\\s+)?|they(?:'re| are| become)\\s+(?:also\\s+)?)?`;

        const patterns = [
            // SOURCE ... for as long as / while ... DEPENDENCY
            new RegExp(`\\b${source}\\b[^.;]{0,120}\\b(?:for\\s+)?as long as\\s+${subject}\\s*${dep}\\b`, "i"),
            new RegExp(`\\b${source}\\b[^.;]{0,120}\\bwhile\\s+${subject}\\s*${dep}\\b`, "i"),

            // while / as long as ... DEPENDENCY ... SOURCE
            // Example: "while it's Sickened, it's also Slowed 1"
            new RegExp(`\\bwhile\\s+${subject}\\s*${dep}\\b[^.;]{0,120}\\b${sourceLead}${source}\\b`, "i"),
            new RegExp(`\\b(?:for\\s+)?as long as\\s+${subject}\\s*${dep}\\b[^.;]{0,120}\\b${sourceLead}${source}\\b`, "i"),

            // Common shortened forms.
            new RegExp(`\\b${source}\\b[^.;]{0,120}\\buntil\\s+${subject}\\s*(?:is\\s+)?no\\s+longer\\s+${dep}\\b`, "i"),
            new RegExp(`\\b${source}\\b[^.;]{0,120}\\buntil\\s+${dep}\\b[^.;]{0,40}\\bends?\\b`, "i")
        ];

        if (patterns.some(regex => regex.test(plain))) {
            debug("LINKED CONDITION INFERENCE:", {
                condition: effect.condition,
                value: effect.value ?? null,
                dependency: dependency.slug
            });

            return {
                ...effect,
                whileCondition: dependency.slug,
                whileConditionName: dependency.name,
                duration: null
            };
        }
    }

    return null;
}

async function extractConditionsFromText(text, catalog) {
    const found = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const plain = pf2eMarkupToPlain(text ?? "");

    for (const entry of catalog.entries) {
        const name = escapeRegex(entry.name);
        const regex = new RegExp(
            `\\b(?:is|becomes?|become|gains?|be)\\s+(?:permanently\\s+)?${name}(?:\\s+(\\d+))?\\b`,
            "i"
        );
        const match = plain.match(regex);
        if (!match) continue;

        const key = `${entry.slug}:${match[1] ?? ""}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);

        const snippet = getConditionSnippet(plain, entry.name);

        found.push({
            condition: entry.slug,
            value: match[1] ? Number(match[1]) : null,
            name: entry.name,
            duration: parseSimpleDuration(snippet)
        });
    }

    return found;
}

async function extractConditionsFromOutcome(section, catalog) {
    const found = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const raw = section.html ?? "";
    const plain = section.text ?? "";

    function addCondition(entry, label) {
        if (!entry) return;

        const value = getConditionValueFromLabel(label);
        const key = `${entry.slug}:${value ?? ""}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) return;
        seen.add(key);

        const snippet = getConditionSnippet(plain, entry.name);

        found.push({
            condition: entry.slug,
            value,
            name: entry.name,
            duration: parseSimpleDuration(snippet)
        });
    }

    // Raw PF2e @UUID condition links.
    const rawUUID = /@UUID\[(Compendium\.pf2e\.conditionitems\.Item\.([^\]]+))\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi;
    for (const match of raw.matchAll(rawUUID)) {
        const entry = resolveConditionCatalogEntry(match[2], match[3], catalog);
        addCondition(entry, match[3] ?? match[2]);
    }

    // Enriched Foundry content links.
    const enrichedUUID = /data-uuid=["']Compendium\.pf2e\.conditionitems\.Item\.([^"']+)["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
    for (const match of raw.matchAll(enrichedUUID)) {
        const label = match[2].replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
        const entry = resolveConditionCatalogEntry(match[1], label, catalog);
        addCondition(entry, label);
    }

    // Text fallback also runs even when links were found, because a single
    // outcome can contain a mix of linked and unlinked conditions.
    for (const condition of await extractConditionsFromText(plain, catalog)) {
        const key = `${condition.condition}:${condition.value ?? ""}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);
        found.push(condition);
    }

    return found;
}

function splitLinkedConditions(conditions, plain, catalog) {
    const normal = [];
    const linked = [];

    for (const effect of conditions ?? []) {
        const linkedEffect = detectLinkedCondition(effect, plain, catalog);
        if (linkedEffect) linked.push(linkedEffect);
        else normal.push(effect);
    }

    return { normal, linked };
}


function inferOutcomeInheritance(text) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "").replace(/[’]/g, "'").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const match = normalized.match(/^as\s+(?:a\s+)?(critical success|success|critical failure|failure)\b/i);
    return match ? headingToDegree(match[1]) : null;
}

function mergeConditionsForInheritedOutcome(base = [], own = []) {
    const bySlug = new Map();
    for (const effect of base) bySlug.set(normalizeSlug(effect.condition), deepClone(effect));
    for (const effect of own) bySlug.set(normalizeSlug(effect.condition), deepClone(effect));
    return [...bySlug.values()];
}

function mergeInheritedOutcome(base, own, { ownHasExplicitDamage = false } = {}) {
    if (!base) return own;
    return {
        ...deepClone(base),
        ...own,
        damage: ownHasExplicitDamage ? own.damage : deepClone(base.damage),
        conditions: mergeConditionsForInheritedOutcome(base.conditions, own.conditions),
        linkedConditions: mergeConditionsForInheritedOutcome(base.linkedConditions, own.linkedConditions),
        recognizedConditions: mergeConditionsForInheritedOutcome(base.recognizedConditions, own.recognizedConditions),
        secondarySave: own.secondarySave ?? deepClone(base.secondarySave ?? null),
        ongoingSave: own.ongoingSave ?? deepClone(base.ongoingSave ?? null),
        terminal: own.terminal ?? deepClone(base.terminal ?? null),
        manualRider: base.manualRider === true || own.manualRider === true,
        rawText: own.rawText ?? base.rawText ?? ""
    };
}

function inferOngoingSave(section, catalog) {
    const text = String(section?.text ?? "").replace(/[’]/g, "'").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const saveMatch = text.match(/\b(?:must|has to)\s+(?:attempt|make)\s+(?:another\s+)?(?:a\s+)?(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+(?:save|saving throw)\s+at\s+the\s+end\s+of\s+each\s+of\s+(?:its|their|the target'?s|the creature'?s)\s+turns?\b/i);
    if (!saveMatch) return null;

    const saveType = saveMatch[1].toLowerCase();
    let tracked = null;
    for (const entry of catalog.entries) {
        const name = escapeRegex(entry.name);
        const patterns = [
            new RegExp(`\\b(?:the\\s+)?${name}\\s+condition\\s+increases?\\b`, "i"),
            new RegExp(`\\breduces?\\s+(?:the\\s+)?${name}\\s+condition\\b`, "i"),
            new RegExp(`\\b${name}\\s+\\d+\\b`, "i")
        ];
        if (patterns.some(regex => regex.test(text))) {
            tracked = entry;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!tracked) return null;

    const name = escapeRegex(tracked.name);
    const failure = text.match(new RegExp(`\\b(?:on\\s+)?a\\s+failed\\s+save[^.;]{0,120}?(?:the\\s+)?${name}\\s+condition\\s+increases?\\s+by\\s+(\\d+)`, "i"));
    const critFailure = text.match(/\bor\s+(\d+)\s+on\s+a\s+critical\s+failure\b/i)
        ?? text.match(new RegExp(`\\bcritical\\s+failure[^.;]{0,120}?(?:the\\s+)?${name}\\s+condition\\s+increases?\\s+by\\s+(\\d+)`, "i"));
    const success = text.match(new RegExp(`\\b(?:a\\s+)?successful\\s+save[^.;]{0,120}?reduces?\\s+(?:the\\s+)?${name}\\s+condition\\s+by\\s+(\\d+)`, "i"));

    const failureDelta = failure ? Number(failure[1]) : 0;
    const criticalFailureDelta = critFailure ? Number(critFailure[1]) : failureDelta;
    const successDelta = success ? -Number(success[1]) : 0;
    const criticalSuccessDelta = successDelta;
    if (!failureDelta && !criticalFailureDelta && !successDelta) return null;

    const traits = [];
    for (const match of text.matchAll(/\b(?:this|the)\s+ongoing\s+save\s+has\s+the\s+([a-z][a-z-]*)\s+trait\b/gi)) {
        const slug = normalizeSlug(match[1]);
        if (slug && !traits.includes(slug)) traits.push(slug);
    }

    let terminal = null;
    const terminalStart = text.search(/\b(?:fully\s+unable\s+to\s+act|becomes?\s+fully\s+unable\s+to\s+act|actions?\s+(?:are\s+)?reduced\s+to\s+0)\b/i);
    if (terminalStart >= 0) {
        const tail = text.slice(terminalStart);
        for (const entry of catalog.entries) {
            if (entry.slug === tracked.slug) continue;
            const terminalName = escapeRegex(entry.name);
            if (new RegExp(`\\b${terminalName}\\b`, "i").test(tail)) {
                terminal = {
                    kind: "condition",
                    condition: entry.slug,
                    name: entry.name,
                    threshold: "unable-to-act",
                    permanent: /\bpermanent(?:ly)?\b/i.test(tail)
                };
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    debug("ONGOING SAVE INFERENCE:", {
        saveType,
        trackedCondition: tracked.slug,
        deltas: {
            criticalSuccess: criticalSuccessDelta,
            success: successDelta,
            failure: failureDelta,
            criticalFailure: criticalFailureDelta
        },
        traits,
        terminal
    });

    return {
        timing: "turn-end",
        saveType,
        dcSource: "spell",
        traits,
        trackedCondition: tracked.slug,
        trackedConditionName: tracked.name,
        deltas: {
            criticalSuccess: criticalSuccessDelta,
            success: successDelta,
            failure: failureDelta,
            criticalFailure: criticalFailureDelta
        },
        endWhenTrackedConditionRemoved: new RegExp(`\\b(?:spell|effect)\\s+(?:also\\s+)?ends?\\s+if\\s+(?:the\\s+)?${name}\\s+condition\\s+is\\s+removed\\b`, "i").test(text),
        terminal,
        rawText: text
    };
}

function inferTerminalResult(text, { branchContext = false } = {}) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");

    // Primary outcome text can contain a conditional death clause whose
    // condition is not itself understood (for example, "if reduced to 0 HP").
    // Do not turn such wording into unconditional death. Secondary branch
    // text is already scoped to a known branch, so "if it fails, it dies"
    // can safely be treated as that branch's terminal result.
    if (!branchContext) {
        const conditionalDeath = /\b(?:if|unless|when|whenever)\b[^.!?;]*\b(?:dies|killed)\b/i;
        if (conditionalDeath.test(normalized)) return null;
    }

    if (/\b(?:it|the target|target|creature)\s+dies\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\bkilled instantly\b/.test(normalized)
        || /\bdies instantly\b/.test(normalized)) {
        return {
            kind: "death",
            label: "Dies",
            automatic: true
        };
    }

    return null;
}

function inferPostDamageTerminal(spell) {
    const plain = htmlToPlain(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ");

    // Generic death-effect wording where this spell's damage reducing the
    // target to 0 HP causes immediate death rather than ordinary dying.
    const reducedToZero = /\bif\s+(?:the\s+)?target\s+is\s+reduced\s+to\s+0\s+(?:hp|hit points)\s+by\s+(?:this|the)\s+spell\b/i;
    const terminal = /\b(?:kills?\s+(?:it|the target)\s+instantly|(?:it|the target)\s+dies(?:\s+instantly)?)\b/i;

    const sourceDamageKills = /\bif the damage from [^.!?]+ reduces a creature to 0 (?:hp|hit points),? that creature dies instantly\b/i.test(plain);
    if (sourceDamageKills || (reducedToZero.test(plain) && terminal.test(plain))) {
        return {
            kind: "death",
            label: "Dies at 0 HP",
            threshold: 0,
            requiresReductionBySpell: true,
            automatic: true
        };
    }

    return null;
}

function splitSecondarySaveText(text, saveType) {
    const normalized = String(text ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const saveName = saveType.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase());

    const successRegex = new RegExp(
        `\\b(?:on|with)\\s+a\\s+successful\\s+${escapeRegex(saveName)}\\s+(?:save|saving throw)\\s*,?\\s*`,
        "i"
    );

    const successMatch = successRegex.exec(normalized);

    if (!successMatch) {
        return {
            beforeSuccess: normalized,
            successText: null
        };
    }

    return {
        beforeSuccess: normalized.slice(0, successMatch.index).trim(),
        successText: normalized.slice(successMatch.index + successMatch[0].length).trim()
    };
}

async function inferSecondarySave(section, catalog, heightening, degree, castEntry) {
    const text = section.text ?? "";
    const saveMatch = text.match(/\b(?:must|has to)\s+(?:attempt|make)\s+a\s+(Fortitude|Reflex|Will)\s+(?:save|saving throw)\b/i);
    if (!saveMatch) return null;

    const saveType = saveMatch[1].toLowerCase();
    const split = splitSecondarySaveText(text, saveType);

    const failureText = split.beforeSuccess;
    const successText = split.successText;

    const failureBranch = {
        terminal: inferTerminalResult(failureText, { branchContext: true }),
        damage: null,
        conditions: [],
        linkedConditions: [],
        manualRider: false,
        rawText: failureText
    };

    let successBranch = null;

    if (successText) {
        let damage = parseDirectDamage(successText);

        // If PF2e rendered a dynamic inline as vague prose such as
        // "scaling d6 ...", recover the actual @Damage expression from
        // the raw outcome section.  This branch is deliberately used only
        // when there is exactly one @Damage inline in the entire secondary
        // outcome, which avoids guessing between multiple branch damages.
        if (!damage) {
            const inlineEntries = collectBalancedInlines(section.rawText ?? "", "@Damage[");

            if (inlineEntries.length === 1) {
                damage = parseInlineDamageSpec(section.rawText, castEntry);
            }
        }

        damage = applyTextHeighteningToDamage(damage, degree, heightening);

        const successConditions = await extractConditionsFromText(successText, catalog);
        const splitConditions = splitLinkedConditions(successConditions, successText, catalog);

        successBranch = {
            terminal: inferTerminalResult(successText),
            damage,
            conditions: splitConditions.normal,
            linkedConditions: splitConditions.linked,
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: successText
        };
    }

    const incapacitation = /\bincapacitation trait\b/i.test(text);

    return {
        saveType,
        dcSource: "spell",
        traits: incapacitation ? ["incapacitation"] : [],
        successBranch,
        failureBranch,
        rawText: text
    };
}

function outcomeDamageRecognized(outcome, text) {
    if (outcome.secondarySave) return true;
    if (outcome.terminal) return true;
    if (outcome.damage?.kind === "formula") return true;
    if (outcome.damage?.kind === "shared") return true;
    if (outcome.damage?.kind === "none") return true;

    // Conditions-only outcome with no mention of damage.
    if ((outcome.conditions?.length || outcome.linkedConditions?.length)
        && !/\bdamage\b/i.test(text ?? "")) {
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}


// =================================================================
// GENERIC CONDITION-ONLY / CONDITION-RIDER INFERENCE
// =================================================================
//
// Conditions are mechanically independent from damage.  Earlier versions
// only reached condition parsing through the non-basic four-outcome parser,
// which meant two common patterns were missed:
//
//   1) save spells with no damage at all; and
//   2) compact prose such as
//        "becomes Sickened 1 if it fails ... (Sickened 2 on a critical failure)"
//      instead of separate Critical Success / Success / Failure / Critical
//      Failure paragraphs.
//
// v8.14 extracts degree-conditioned condition mentions across the entire
// description and can overlay them onto either standard Basic Save outcomes
// or a no-damage condition-only profile.  No spell names are involved.
// =================================================================

function emptyNoDamageOutcomes() {
    return {
        criticalSuccess: {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "No inferred effect"
        },
        success: {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "No inferred effect"
        },
        failure: {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "No inferred effect"
        },
        criticalFailure: {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "No inferred effect"
        }
    };
}

function mergeConditionEffectList(base = [], extra = []) {
    const byKey = new Map();

    // For the same condition slug in the same degree, prefer the last parsed
    // value. This matters for compact parentheticals such as Sickened 1 on a
    // failure / Sickened 2 on a critical failure, while still allowing two
    // different conditions in one degree.
    for (const effect of [...base, ...extra]) {
        if (!effect?.condition) continue;
        byKey.set(normalizeSlug(effect.condition), deepClone(effect));
    }

    return [...byKey.values()];
}

function getDegreeCueMatches(text) {
    const source = String(text ?? "");
    const cues = [];

    const patterns = [
        ["criticalFailure", /\bcritical failure\b|\bcritically\s+fails?\b/gi],
        ["criticalSuccess", /\bcritical success\b|\bcritically\s+succeeds?\b/gi],
        ["failure", /\bfailure\b|\bfailed\s+(?:save|saving throw)\b|\bfails?\s+(?:(?:a|the|its|their)\s+)?(?:(?:fortitude|reflex|will)\s+)?(?:save|saving throw)\b/gi],
        ["success", /\bsuccess\b|\bsuccessful\s+(?:save|saving throw)\b|\bsucceeds?\s+(?:(?:on|at)\s+)?(?:(?:a|the|its|their)\s+)?(?:(?:fortitude|reflex|will)\s+)?(?:save|saving throw)\b/gi]
    ];

    for (const [degree, regex] of patterns) {
        for (const match of source.matchAll(regex)) {
            cues.push({
                degree,
                index: match.index ?? 0,
                end: (match.index ?? 0) + match[0].length,
                text: match[0]
            });
        }
    }

    // A literal "critical failure" also contains the word "failure", and a
    // literal "critical success" also contains "success".  Do not retain the
    // nested base-degree cue: it creates two contradictory interpretations of
    // the same phrase and can steal nearby condition mentions.
    const criticalCues = cues.filter(cue => cue.degree.startsWith("critical"));
    return cues.filter(cue => {
        if (cue.degree !== "failure" && cue.degree !== "success") return true;

        const matchingCritical = cue.degree === "failure"
            ? "criticalFailure"
            : "criticalSuccess";

        const nestedInCritical = criticalCues.some(critical =>
            critical.degree === matchingCritical
            && cue.index >= critical.index
            && cue.end <= critical.end
        );
        if (nestedInCritical) return false;

        // In an outcome such as "Critical Failure: As failure, and ...", the
        // word "failure" after "As" describes INHERITANCE. It is not a new
        // degree cue for nearby rider text. Keeping it as a cue can leak a
        // Critical Failure-only condition backward into Failure.
        const prefix = source.slice(Math.max(0, cue.index - 14), cue.index);
        if (/\bas\s+(?:a\s+)?$/i.test(prefix)) return false;

        return true;
    });
}

function distanceBetweenRanges(aStart, aEnd, bStart, bEnd) {
    if (aEnd < bStart) return bStart - aEnd;
    if (bEnd < aStart) return aStart - bEnd;
    return 0;
}

function isForwardDegreeBridge(text) {
    const bridge = String(text ?? "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[\u2013\u2014]/g, "-")
        .trim();

    if (!bridge || bridge.length > 34) return false;

    // These are grammatical connectors that make the degree cue belong to the
    // condition immediately before it: "Sickened 2 on a critical failure" or
    // "Sickened 1 if it fails a Will save".  Prefer this relationship over a
    // slightly closer degree phrase that happens to precede the condition.
    return /^(?:[()\[\],:;\s-]*)(?:(?:on|upon|with|after)\s+(?:(?:a|an|the)\s*)?|(?:if|when)\s+(?:it|they|the target|the creature|target|creature)\s*)[()\[\],:;\s-]*$/i.test(bridge);
}

function inferNearestDegreeForMention(text, mentionStart, mentionEnd, cues) {
    const source = String(text ?? "");

    // First honor an explicit grammatical cue immediately following the
    // condition. Evil Eye is the canonical compact form:
    //   "Sickened 1 if it fails a Will save (or Sickened 2 on a critical failure)"
    // The prior Failure cue is one character closer to "Sickened 2" than the
    // following Critical Failure cue, so pure distance gives the wrong answer.
    const forwardCandidates = cues
        .filter(cue => cue.index >= mentionEnd)
        .map(cue => ({
            cue,
            distance: cue.index - mentionEnd,
            bridge: source.slice(mentionEnd, cue.index)
        }))
        .filter(candidate =>
            candidate.distance <= 34
            && isForwardDegreeBridge(candidate.bridge)
        )
        .sort((a, b) => {
            if (a.distance !== b.distance) return a.distance - b.distance;
            const aSpecific = a.cue.degree.startsWith("critical") ? 1 : 0;
            const bSpecific = b.cue.degree.startsWith("critical") ? 1 : 0;
            return bSpecific - aSpecific;
        });

    if (forwardCandidates.length) {
        return forwardCandidates[0].cue.degree;
    }

    let best = null;

    for (const cue of cues) {
        const distance = distanceBetweenRanges(
            mentionStart,
            mentionEnd,
            cue.index,
            cue.end
        );

        // Keep compact prose inference deliberately local.  This avoids
        // treating conditions mentioned in the general spell description as
        // save outcomes merely because a degree word appears much later.
        if (distance > 105) continue;

        // Prefer the closest cue. On ties, prefer the more specific critical
        // degree because "failure ... critical failure" clauses often sit in
        // the same parenthetical sentence.
        const specificity = cue.degree.startsWith("critical") ? 1 : 0;

        if (!best
            || distance < best.distance
            || (distance === best.distance && specificity > best.specificity)) {
            best = {
                degree: cue.degree,
                distance,
                specificity,
                cue
            };
        }
    }

    return best?.degree ?? null;
}

function conditionMentionWindow(text, start, end) {
    const source = String(text ?? "");
    const leftBoundary = Math.max(
        source.lastIndexOf(".", start - 1),
        source.lastIndexOf(";", start - 1),
        source.lastIndexOf("\n", start - 1)
    );

    const nextCandidates = [
        source.indexOf(".", end),
        source.indexOf(";", end),
        source.indexOf("\n", end)
    ].filter(value => value >= 0);

    const rightBoundary = nextCandidates.length
        ? Math.min(...nextCandidates)
        : source.length;

    const from = Math.max(0, Math.max(leftBoundary + 1, start - 100));
    const to = Math.min(source.length, Math.min(rightBoundary + 1, end + 150));
    return source.slice(from, to).trim();
}

function collectPlainConditionMentions(text, catalog) {
    const source = String(text ?? "");
    const mentions = [];

    for (const entry of catalog.entries) {
        const regex = new RegExp(
            `\\b${escapeRegex(entry.name)}\\b(?:\\s+(\\d+))?`,
            "gi"
        );

        for (const match of source.matchAll(regex)) {
            mentions.push({
                entry,
                value: match[1] ? Number(match[1]) : null,
                index: match.index ?? 0,
                end: (match.index ?? 0) + match[0].length,
                text: match[0]
            });
        }
    }

    return mentions.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);
}

async function inferConditionRidersAcrossDescription(spell) {
    const description = stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const plain = htmlToPlain(description);
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(description);
    const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
    let recognizedAny = false;
    const sectionConditionSignatures = new Set();
    const signatureOf = effect => `${normalizeSlug(effect?.condition)}:${effect?.value ?? ""}`;

    // First consume conventional degree sections whenever they exist. This
    // path handles normal condition-only spells as well as condition riders
    // attached to otherwise ordinary damage spells.
    for (const [degree, section] of Object.entries(sections)) {
        const conditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome(section, catalog);
        const split = splitLinkedConditions(conditions, section.text, catalog);

        // Explicit degree sections are a stronger signal than the later
        // compact-prose heuristic. Remember every condition/value they own so
        // the whole-description scan cannot remap the same rider to a nearby
        // word such as "As failure".
        for (const effect of [...split.normal, ...split.linked]) {
            sectionConditionSignatures.add(signatureOf(effect));
        }

        if (split.normal.length || split.linked.length) {
            recognizedAny = true;
            outcomes[degree].conditions = mergeConditionEffectList(
                outcomes[degree].conditions,
                split.normal
            );
            outcomes[degree].linkedConditions = mergeConditionEffectList(
                outcomes[degree].linkedConditions,
                split.linked
            );
            outcomes[degree].rawText = section.text;
        }
    }

    // Then scan compact prose. This intentionally searches condition names
    // directly rather than requiring a verb before every mention, because
    // PF2e commonly writes parentheticals such as "(or Sickened 2 on a
    // critical failure)".
    // With full degree sections, their boundaries own every mention inside
    // them. Only the preamble remains eligible for compact-prose inference.
    // In particular, "recovers from Sickened" in Success must not migrate
    // into Failure simply because its heading happens to be closer.
    const fullSections = ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"].every(degree => sections[degree]);
    const heading = description.search(/<strong\b[^>]*>\s*(?:Critical\s+Success|Success|Failure|Critical\s+Failure)/i);
    const compactPlain = fullSections && heading >= 0 ? htmlToPlain(description.slice(0, heading)) : plain;
    const cues = getDegreeCueMatches(compactPlain);
    const mentions = collectPlainConditionMentions(compactPlain, catalog);

    for (const mention of mentions) {
        const mentionSignature = `${normalizeSlug(mention.entry.slug)}:${mention.value ?? ""}`;
        if (sectionConditionSignatures.has(mentionSignature)) continue;

        const degree = inferNearestDegreeForMention(
            compactPlain,
            mention.index,
            mention.end,
            cues
        );
        if (!degree) continue;

        const context = conditionMentionWindow(compactPlain, mention.index, mention.end);
        // A named condition can also be an ordinary adjective or a reference
        // to recovery, rather than an instruction to apply that condition.
        const before = compactPlain.slice(Math.max(0, mention.index - 65), mention.index);
        const after = compactPlain.slice(mention.end, mention.end + 35);
        if (/^\s+(?:actions?|creatures?|targets?|objects?|terrain)\b/i.test(after)
            || /\b(?:recovers? from|remove|removing|reduce|reducing|no longer|isn't|is not|while|when)\s+(?:being\s+|the\s+)?$/i.test(before)) continue;
        const effect = {
            condition: mention.entry.slug,
            value: mention.value,
            name: mention.entry.name,
            duration: parseSimpleDuration(context)
        };

        const linked = detectLinkedCondition(effect, context, catalog);
        if (linked) {
            outcomes[degree].linkedConditions = mergeConditionEffectList(
                outcomes[degree].linkedConditions,
                [linked]
            );
        } else {
            outcomes[degree].conditions = mergeConditionEffectList(
                outcomes[degree].conditions,
                [effect]
            );
        }

        outcomes[degree].rawText = context;
        recognizedAny = true;
    }

    // Some condition-only spells impose additional stateful restrictions on
    // the condition (for example a minimum value while the spell remains
    // active). We can safely apply the condition itself, but until a generic
    // sustain/lifecycle rule is inferred, flag the extra rider for the GM.
    const hasConditionFloor = /\b(?:can(?:not|'t)|cannot)\s+be\s+reduced\s+below\s+\d+\b/i.test(plain);
    if (hasConditionFloor && recognizedAny) {
        for (const outcome of Object.values(outcomes)) {
            if (outcome.conditions.length || outcome.linkedConditions.length) {
                outcome.manualRider = true;
            }
        }
    }

    return {
        supported: recognizedAny,
        outcomes,
        sections,
        plain,
        hasConditionFloor
    };
}

function overlayConditionRiders(baseOutcomes, riderOutcomes) {
    const merged = deepClone(baseOutcomes);

    for (const degree of ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
        merged[degree] ??= {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: ""
        };

        const rider = riderOutcomes?.[degree];
        if (!rider) continue;

        merged[degree].conditions = mergeConditionEffectList(
            merged[degree].conditions,
            rider.conditions
        );
        merged[degree].linkedConditions = mergeConditionEffectList(
            merged[degree].linkedConditions,
            rider.linkedConditions
        );
        merged[degree].manualRider = merged[degree].manualRider === true
            || rider.manualRider === true;
        if (rider.manualRiderText) merged[degree].manualRiderText = [merged[degree].manualRiderText, rider.manualRiderText].filter(Boolean).join(" ");

        if (rider.rawText && rider.rawText !== "No inferred effect") {
            merged[degree].rawText = rider.rawText;
        }
    }

    return merged;
}

// Some condition-only spells define their effect before the save headings;
// each affected outcome then supplies the spell's duration. Require that
// explicit structure rather than applying every preamble condition to every
// outcome. Unrecognized restrictions remain visible for manual resolution.
async function inferSharedDurationOutcomes(spell, inspection, sections, catalog) {
    if (inspection.basicSave || inspection.damageCount > 0) return null;
    const description = String(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    if (/\bdamage\b|@Damage\[/i.test(description)
        || extractNativeSpellEffectLinks(description).length) return null;

    const headingIndex = description.search(/<strong\b[^>]*>\s*(?:Critical\s+Success|Success|Failure|Critical\s+Failure)\s*<\/strong>/i);
    if (headingIndex < 0) return null;

    // Bare @UUID links lose their labels in ordinary plain-text conversion.
    // Recover condition names from the actual installed compendium first.
    const preamble = description.slice(0, headingIndex).replace(
        /@UUID\[Compendium\.pf2e\.conditionitems\.Item\.([^\]]+)\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi,
        (full, id, label) => {
            const entry = resolveConditionCatalogEntry(id, label, catalog);
            return entry ? escapeHTML(label ?? entry.name) : full;
        }
    );
    const plain = htmlToStructuredPlain(preamble).replace(/[’]/g, "'");
    const scope = /\bFor the duration of (?:the|this) spell,?\s+((?:the target|the creature|it) (?:is|becomes)\s+[^.!?]+[.!?]?)/i.exec(plain);
    if (!scope || /\b(?:if|unless|against|while)\b/i.test(scope[1])) return null;

    const sharedConditions = await extractConditionsFromText(scope[1], catalog);
    if (!sharedConditions.length || sharedConditions.some(effect => effect.duration)) return null;

    const sharedText = plain.slice(scope.index).trim();
    let sharedManual = sharedText.replace(/^For the duration of (?:the|this) spell,?\s+/i, "");
    for (const effect of sharedConditions) {
        const valueSuffix = effect.value == null ? "" : `\\s+${effect.value}`;
        sharedManual = sharedManual.replace(new RegExp(
            `^(?:the target|the creature|it) (?:is|becomes)\\s+${escapeRegex(effect.name)}${valueSuffix}\\b`, "i"
        ), "");
    }
    const cleanRemainder = text => String(text).replace(/^[\s,.;]+/, "").replace(/^and\s+/i, "").trim()
        .replace(/^\w/, letter => letter.toUpperCase());
    sharedManual = cleanRemainder(sharedManual);

    const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
    let affected = 0;
    for (const degree of ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
        const section = sections[degree];
        if (!section) return null;
        const text = section.text.replace(/[’]/g, "'").trim();
        outcomes[degree].rawText = text;
        if (/^(?:(?:the target|the creature|it) is unaffected|(?:the spell has )?no effect)[.!]?$/i.test(text)) continue;

        const durationStatement = /\b(?:the|this) spell's duration is (\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(rounds?|minutes?|hours?)\b/i.exec(text);
        if (!durationStatement) return null;
        const duration = parseSimpleDuration(`for ${durationStatement[1]} ${durationStatement[2]}`);
        if (!duration || !Number.isFinite(duration.value) || duration.value <= 0) return null;

        // Leave more complex, degree-specific condition/state transitions to
        // the ordinary parser. This path only carries the shared conditions.
        const ownConditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome(section, catalog);
        if (ownConditions.length || /\b(?:saving throw|save|counteract)\b/i.test(text)) return null;

        const degreeManual = cleanRemainder(text.replace(durationStatement[0], ""));
        const manualText = [degreeManual, sharedManual].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
        outcomes[degree].conditions = sharedConditions.map(effect => ({
            ...deepClone(effect), duration: deepClone(duration),
            descriptionText: `${sharedText}\n${text}`
        }));
        outcomes[degree].manualRider = !!manualText;
        outcomes[degree].manualRiderText = manualText || null;
        affected++;
    }
    return affected ? { supported: true, outcomes, sections, sharedDuration: true } : null;
}

// v8.33: bounded descriptions, recipients, and continuing effects. These
// grammars describe mechanics; no spell names or compendium spell IDs select
// a rule. Conditions still come from the installed PF2e condition catalog.
function stripHeightenedDescription(html) {
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${html ?? ""}</div>`, "text/html");
    for (const strong of [...doc.querySelectorAll("strong")]) {
        if (/^Heightened\b/i.test(strong.textContent.trim())) {
            (strong.closest("p, li") ?? strong.parentElement)?.remove();
        }
    }
    return doc.body.innerHTML;
}

function readableConditionHTML(html, catalog) {
    return String(html ?? "").replace(/@Check\[flat\|dc:(\d+)[^\]]*\](?:\{[^}]*\})?/gi, "DC $1 flat check").replace(
        /@UUID\[Compendium\.pf2e\.conditionitems\.Item\.([^\]]+)\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi,
        (full, id, label) => {
            const entry = resolveConditionCatalogEntry(id, label, catalog);
            return entry ? escapeHTML(label ?? entry.name) : full;
        }
    );
}

function scopedPlain(html, catalog) {
    return htmlToStructuredPlain(readableConditionHTML(html, catalog)).replace(/[’]/g, "'");
}

function activeFixedHeightening(spell, rank, catalog) {
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${spell.system?.description?.value ?? ""}</div>`, "text/html");
    const parts = [];
    for (const strong of doc.querySelectorAll("strong")) {
        const match = strong.textContent.trim().match(/^Heightened\s*\((\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)\)/i);
        if (match && Number(match[1]) <= Number(rank)) {
            parts.push(scopedPlain((strong.closest("p, li") ?? strong.parentElement).innerHTML, catalog));
        }
    }
    return parts.join(" ");
}

function scopedDuration(text, fallback = null) {
    const duration = parseSimpleDuration(text)
        ?? parseSimpleDuration(String(text).replace(/\bafter\s+/i, "for "));
    return duration ?? deepClone(fallback);
}

function applyFixedOutcomeDamage(outcomes, fixedText) {
    // Fixed heightening replaces an amount; interval heightening adds dice.
    // Retain the degree's known damage type, and leave executable @Damage
    // expressions alone because PF2e already evaluates their cast rank.
    for (const match of String(fixedText).matchAll(/\b(?:damage to|and to) (\d+d\d+(?:[+-]\d+)?) on (?:a )?(critical success|success|critical failure|failure)\b/gi)) {
        const outcome = outcomes[headingToDegree(match[2])];
        if (outcome?.damage?.kind === "formula" && !outcome.damage.heighteningResolved && outcome.damage.type) {
            outcome.damage = { ...outcome.damage, baseFormula: outcome.damage.formula, formula: match[1], heighteningResolved: true };
        }
    }
}

function inferRecoveryRule(text, defaultSave) {
    const plain = String(text ?? "");
    if (/\bno save to end early\b/i.test(plain)) return null;
    const save = plain.match(/\b(?:new\s+)?(Will|Reflex|Fortitude)\s+save\b/i)?.[1]?.toLowerCase() ?? defaultSave;
    if (!save) return null;
    if (/\bnew save only if\b/i.test(plain)) {
        return { saveType: save, timing: "manual", mode: "end", trigger: plain.match(/\bonly if\s+(.+?)(?:\.$|$)/i)?.[1] ?? plain };
    }
    if (!/\bat the end of each of (?:its|their) turns\b/i.test(plain)
        || !/\b(?:can|may) attempt\b/i.test(plain)) return null;
    const reduction = plain.match(/\breduce the remaining duration by (\d+) rounds?\b/i);
    if (reduction && /\bcritical success\b/i.test(plain)) {
        return { saveType: save, timing: "turn-end", mode: "shorten", rounds: Number(reduction[1]), criticalSuccessEnds: true };
    }
    if (/\b(?:to end|spell ends|recovers from|on a success)\b/i.test(plain)) {
        return { saveType: save, timing: "turn-end", mode: "end" };
    }
    return null;
}

function scopedConditionMentions(text, catalog) {
    return collectPlainConditionMentions(text, catalog).map(mention => ({
        condition: mention.entry.slug, name: mention.entry.name, value: mention.value,
        index: mention.index, end: mention.end
    }));
}

function scopedConditionSpec(mention) {
    return { condition: mention.condition, name: mention.name, value: mention.value ?? null };
}

function managedOutcomeEffect(outcome, spec) {
    outcome.managedEffects ??= [];
    outcome.managedEffects.push(spec);
}

function addManualText(outcome, text) {
    if (!text?.trim()) return;
    outcome.manualRider = true;
    outcome.manualRiderText = [outcome.manualRiderText, text.trim()].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

// Bounded grammars for restrictions, transformations, and attribute overrides.
// Selection depends on the spell's text, never its name or compendium ID.
// Repeated exposures, distinct arrival/departure saves, and ordered chains.
// These grammars use the item's instructions, never spell names or IDs.
function hasArrivalDeparturePhases(html) {
    return /<strong[^>]*>\s*Arrive\s*<\/strong>/i.test(html)
        && /<strong[^>]*>\s*Depart\s*<\/strong>/i.test(html);
}

function potentialSpellFlow(spell) {
    const text = String(spell?.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    return hasArrivalDeparturePhases(text)
        || /\bchain ends if[^.]+critically succeeds/i.test(text)
        || /\bbegins to use a move action\b/i.test(text)
        || /\bends its turn in the area must also attempt this save\b/i.test(text);
}

function inferPhaseEmanation(html) {
    // Current PF2e descriptions store the distance in @Template, not its
    // visible label. Read only literal distances; never guess an expression.
    const distances = new Set([...htmlToStructuredPlain(html).matchAll(/\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)-foot emanation\b/gi)]
        .map(match => Number(match[1])));
    for (const inline of collectBalancedInlines(html, "@Template[")) {
        const parts = inline.body.split("|").map(part => part.trim());
        const type = parts.find(part => /^type\s*:/i.test(part))?.replace(/^type\s*:\s*/i, "")
            ?? parts.find(part => !part.includes(":"));
        if (type?.toLowerCase() !== "emanation") continue;
        const distance = parts.find(part => /^distance\s*:/i.test(part))?.replace(/^distance\s*:\s*/i, "");
        if (!/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(distance ?? "") || Number(distance) <= 0) return null;
        distances.add(Number(distance));
    }
    return distances.size === 1 && [...distances][0] > 0 ? [...distances][0] : null;
}

async function inferSpellFlow(spell, castEntry, inspection) {
    if (!potentialSpellFlow(spell)) return null;
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const html = stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const plain = scopedPlain(html, catalog).replace(/[’]/g, "'");
    const degrees = ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"];
    const phaseProfile = (outcomes, saveType, extras = {}) => ({ outcomes, saveType, traits: [], ...extras });
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(html);
    for (const section of Object.values(sections)) section.text = scopedPlain(section.html, catalog);
    const duration = scopedDuration(`for ${spell.system?.duration?.value ?? ""}`);

    if (inspection.basicSave && inspection.damageCount > 0
        && /\bchain ends if[^.]+critically succeeds/i.test(plain)
        && /\bRoll the damage only once\b/i.test(plain)
        && /\bcan't target the same creature more than once\b/i.test(plain)) {
        const hop = plain.match(/\banother creature within (\d+) feet of the first target/i);
        if (!hop) return null;
        return { mode: "chain", hopDistance: Number(hop[1]), range: Number.parseFloat(spell.system?.range?.value) || null,
            phases: { chain: phaseProfile(standardBasicOutcomes(), inspection.saveType, { label: "Chain" }) },
            notes: `Check the displayed target order and the caster's line of effect to every target. Jumps must be within ${hop[1]} feet. A critical success stops all later targets. You can also resolve one selected target at a time.` };
    }

    const headings = [...html.matchAll(/<strong\b[^>]*>\s*(Arrive|Depart)\s*<\/strong>/gi)];
    if (headings.length === 2 && headings[0][1].toLowerCase() === "arrive" && headings[1][1].toLowerCase() === "depart") {
        const phases = {};
        for (let index = 0; index < headings.length; index++) {
            const name = headings[index][1].toLowerCase();
            const block = html.slice(headings[index].index, headings[index + 1]?.index ?? html.length);
            const text = scopedPlain(block, catalog);
            const saveType = block.match(/@Check\[(fortitude|reflex|will)(?:\||\])/i)?.[1]?.toLowerCase()
                ?? text.match(/\b(Fortitude|Reflex|Will) sav/i)?.[1]?.toLowerCase();
            const area = inferPhaseEmanation(block);
            const parts = extractOutcomeSections(block);
            if (!saveType || !area || degrees.some(degree => !parts[degree])) return null;
            const traits = text.match(/^(?:Arrive|Depart)\s*\(([^)]+)\)/i)?.[1]?.split(",")
                .map(value => normalizeSlug(value.trim())).map(value => value === "negative" ? "void" : value) ?? [];
            const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
            for (const degree of degrees) {
                const text = scopedPlain(parts[degree].html, catalog);
                const outcome = outcomes[degree]; outcome.rawText = text;
                if (/\bunaffected\b/i.test(text)) continue;
                const mentions = scopedConditionMentions(text, catalog);
                if (!mentions.length) return null;
                const link = text.match(/\bfor (\d+) rounds? or until it's no longer (\w+), whichever comes first/i);
                const linked = link ? mentions.find(mention => mention.value == null) : null;
                outcome.conditions = mentions.filter(mention => mention !== linked && mention.value != null).map(scopedConditionSpec);
                if (linked) managedOutcomeEffect(outcome, { label: linked.name, conditions: [scopedConditionSpec(linked)],
                    duration: { value: Number(link[1]), unit: "rounds", expiry: "turn-start" }, durationConditions: true,
                    durationAnchor: "caster", endsWithCondition: normalizeSlug(link[2]),
                    notes: `${linked.name} ends after ${link[1]} round or when ${link[2]} ends, whichever comes first.` });
                else if (mentions.some(mention => mention.value == null)) return null;
            }
            phases[name] = phaseProfile(outcomes, saveType, { label: name === "arrive" ? "Arrive" : "Depart", area: Number(area), traits });
        }
        return { mode: "phases", phases, notes: "Select the enemies in the appropriate emanation and resolve that phase when it occurs. The incarnate's placement, movement, and departure timing are GM steps." };
    }

    if (!duration || degrees.some(degree => !sections[degree])) return null;
    if (/\bbegins to use a move action\b/i.test(plain) && /@Check\[athletics\b/i.test(html)) {
        const penalty = sections.failure.text.match(/[–−-](\d+)-foot circumstance penalty to its Speeds until the start of its next turn/i);
        const phase = sections.criticalFailure.text.match(/\bimmobilized until the start of its next turn, after which it takes a [–−-](\d+)-foot circumstance penalty to its Speeds for (\d+) rounds?/i);
        if (!penalty || !phase || Number(phase[2]) !== 1) return null;
        const speed = value => ({ label: `−${value}-foot speed penalty`, untilTargetNextTurn: true,
            rules: [{ key: "FlatModifier", selector: "all-speeds", type: "circumstance", value: -Number(value) }],
            notes: "Ends at the start of this creature's next turn, or when it leaves this area." });
        const immobilized = catalog.bySlug.get("immobilized");
        if (!immobilized) return null;
        const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.failure, speed(penalty[1]));
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.criticalFailure, { label: "Immobilized", untilTargetNextTurn: true,
            conditions: [{ condition: immobilized.slug, name: immobilized.name, value: null }], durationConditions: true,
            escape: { mode: "condition", condition: immobilized.slug }, transition: speed(phase[1]),
            notes: "Immobilized until the start of this creature's next turn, then a speed penalty until its following turn. Escape releases immobilization; the scheduled penalty still applies. Clear this area's effects when the creature leaves." });
        for (const degree of degrees) outcomes[degree].rawText = sections[degree].text;
        return { mode: "movement", duration, turnImmunityOnCriticalSuccess: true,
            phases: { exposure: phaseProfile(outcomes, inspection.saveType, { label: "Move Exposure" }) },
            notes: "Trigger an exposure when a creature begins a move action inside the area or enters during movement. Choose Reflex or Athletics. On Athletics success/critical success, clear the squares it leaves manually. Difficult terrain and damage to the area are GM steps." };
    }

    if (inspection.damageCount > 0 && /\bends its turn in the area must also attempt this save\b/i.test(plain)
        && /\bEscape\b/i.test(html) && /\bgrabbed\b/i.test(sections.failure.text) && /\brestrained\b/i.test(sections.criticalFailure.text)) {
        const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
        for (const degree of degrees) {
            const out = outcomes[degree];out.rawText = sections[degree].text;
            const multiplier = inferDamageMultiplier(out.rawText);
            if (multiplier === null) return null;
            out.damage = { kind: multiplier ? "shared" : "none", multiplier };
            if (!multiplier) continue;
            const condition = scopedConditionMentions(out.rawText, catalog)
                .find(mention => mention.condition === (degree === "criticalFailure" ? "restrained" : "grabbed"));
            if (!condition) return null;
            managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: condition.name, conditions: [scopedConditionSpec(condition)],
                durationConditions: true, exposureBinding: degree === "criticalFailure" ? 2 : 1,
                escape: { mode: "binding" }, endLabel: "Release Capture",
                notes: "Escape or destroying the capturing creature releases this spell's capture. Existing persistent damage uses normal recovery and is not removed by Escape, release, or spell expiry." });
        }
        const threshold = plain.match(/destroyed if it takes (\d+) or more damage at once/i)?.[1];
        const heightened = extractHeightenedSections(spell.system.description.value).find(section => /HP increases by (\d+)/i.test(section.text));
        const increase = Number(heightened?.text.match(/HP increases by (\d+)/i)?.[1] ?? 0);
        const increments = heightened ? Math.floor(Math.max(0, Number(castEntry.castRank) - getSpellBaseRank(spell)) / heightened.interval) : 0;
        return { mode: "recurring", duration,
            phases: { initial: phaseProfile(outcomes, inspection.saveType, { label: "Initial Area" }),
                exposure: phaseProfile(deepClone(outcomes), inspection.saveType, { label: "End-Turn Exposure" }) },
            notes: `Select affected creatures for initial resolution, or those ending their turns in the area for another exposure. Escape DC and capturing creature AC equal the spell DC.${threshold ? ` Destroying one requires ${Number(threshold) + increments * increase} damage at once.` : ""} Use Release Capture after destruction. Persistent damage recovers normally.` };
    }
    return null;
}

async function inferInitialPersistentOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection) {
    if (inspection.basicSave || inspection.damageCount !== 1) return null;
    const components = Object.values(spell.system?.damage ?? {});
    if (components.some(component => component.category === "persistent")) return null;
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const html = stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(html);
    const degrees = ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"];
    if (degrees.some(degree => !sections[degree])) return null;
    for (const section of Object.values(sections)) section.text = scopedPlain(section.html, catalog);
    if (!/\bunaffected\b/i.test(sections.criticalSuccess.text)
        || !/\bfull initial damage but no persistent damage\b/i.test(sections.success.text)
        || !/\bfull initial and persistent damage\b/i.test(sections.failure.text)
        || inferOutcomeInheritance(sections.criticalFailure.text) !== "failure") return null;
    const recovery = sections.failure.text.match(/\brecovers from being (\w+),? the persistent damage ends and the spell ends\b/i);
    const dependency = recovery ? normalizeSlug(recovery[1]) : null;
    const entries = collectBalancedInlines(html, "@Damage[").filter(entry => /\bpersistent\b/.test(getInlineDamagePayload(entry.body)));
    const duration = scopedDuration(`for ${spell.system?.duration?.value ?? ""}`);
    if (!dependency || entries.length !== 1 || !duration) return null;
    const persistentDamage = parseInlineDamageSpec(entries[0].full, castEntry);
    // The inline must already scale with rank, or there must be no damage
    // heightening. Never apply prose heightening twice to an @item formula.
    if (!persistentDamage || (!persistentDamage.heighteningResolved && spell.system?.heightening)) return null;
    const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
    for (const degree of degrees) {
        const out = outcomes[degree];
        out.rawText = sections[degree].text;
        if (degree === "criticalSuccess") continue;
        out.damage = { kind: "shared", multiplier: 1 };
        if (degree === "success") continue;
        const condition = scopedConditionMentions(sections[degree].text, catalog)
            .find(mention => mention.condition === dependency);
        if (!condition) return null;
        managedOutcomeEffect(out, {
            label: `${persistentDamage.formula} persistent ${persistentDamage.type}; ${condition.name} ${condition.value}`,
            conditions: [scopedConditionSpec(condition)], duration, durationAnchor: "caster",
            durationConditions: true, endsWithOwnCondition: dependency, persistentDamage,
            notes: `Recovering from this spell's ${condition.name} ends its persistent damage and the spell. Normal persistent-damage recovery also applies. Both conditions expire with this spell.`
        });
    }
    return { supported: true, outcomes, sections };
}

function inferCasterTemporaryHPRule(spell) {
    const text = htmlToPlain(stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? ""));
    if (!/\bYou gain temporary Hit Points equal to half the damage a single creature takes from this spell\b/i.test(text)
        || !/\bcreature that took the most damage\b/i.test(text)) return null;
    const expiration = text.match(/\blose any remaining temporary Hit Points after (\d+) (rounds?|minutes?|hours?)\b/i);
    const duration = expiration ? scopedDuration(`for ${expiration[1]} ${expiration[2]}`) : null;
    if (!duration) return null;
    return { fraction: 0.5, duration, livingOnly: /\bdamage to living creatures in the area\b/i.test(text) };
}

async function inferRestrictedConditionOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection) {
    if (inspection.basicSave || inspection.damageCount > 0) return null;
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const html = stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(html);
    const degrees = ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"];
    if (degrees.some(degree => !sections[degree])) return null;
    for (const section of Object.values(sections)) section.text = scopedPlain(section.html, catalog);
    const text = scopedPlain(html, catalog);
    const critical = sections.criticalFailure.text;
    const failure = sections.failure.text;
    const sustained = spell.system?.duration?.sustained === true;
    const duration = scopedDuration(`for ${spell.system?.duration?.value ?? ""}`);
    const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
    const finish = grammar => {
        for (const outcome of Object.values(outcomes)) for (const spec of outcome.managedEffects ?? []) spec.durationAnchor = "caster";
        return { supported: true, outcomes, sections, grammar };
    };
    for (const degree of degrees) outcomes[degree].rawText = sections[degree].text;

    const attributes = { Strength: "str", Dexterity: "dex", Constitution: "con", Intelligence: "int", Wisdom: "wis", Charisma: "cha" };
    const attributeClause = critical.match(/\btreats its ([^.]+?) modifiers as\s*[–−-](\d+)\b/i);
    if (attributeClause && /\bpermanently\b/i.test(critical)) {
        const names = Object.keys(attributes).filter(name => new RegExp(`\\b${name}\\b`, "i").test(attributeClause[1]));
        if (!names.length) return null;
        for (const degree of ["success", "failure"]) {
            const conditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome(sections[degree], catalog);
            if (!conditions.length) return null;
            managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes[degree], {
                label: conditions.map(formatConditionSummary).join(", "),
                conditions: conditions.map(condition => ({ ...condition, duration: null })),
                duration: scopedDuration(sections[degree].text), curse: /\bcurse\b/i.test(text),
                notes: /\bonly through effects that target curses\b/i.test(text)
                    ? "This curse can be removed only by an effect that targets curses. End Curse after a successful removal." : sections[degree].text
            });
        }
        const notes = "Mental modifiers are overridden by this effect. Resolve the loss of mental class abilities and all spellcasting at the table. A PC is under GM control while this result remains; changing actor ownership or actor type is a GM decision. Remove this curse only through an effect that targets curses.";
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.criticalFailure, {
            label: `${names.join(", ")} −${attributeClause[2]}`,
            attributeOverrides: Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [attributes[name], -Number(attributeClause[2])])),
            curse: /\bcurse\b/i.test(text), notes
        });
        addManualText(outcomes.criticalFailure, notes);
        return finish("permanent-attribute-override");
    }

    if (/\bharmless animal\b/i.test(text) && /\bkeeps its mind\b/i.test(failure)
        && /\bbody and mind\b/i.test(critical)) {
        const conditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome(sections.success, catalog);
        if (!conditions.length) return null;
        outcomes.success.conditions = conditions.map(condition => ({ ...condition, duration: null }));
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.success, { label: "Minor animal features",
            endsWithCondition: conditions[0].condition,
            notes: "Minor animal features remain until the associated condition is gone." });
        const recoveryType = sections.failure.html.match(/@Check\[(will|fortitude|reflex)\b/i)?.[1]
            ?? failure.match(/\b(Will|Fortitude|Reflex)\s+(?:save|saving throw)\b/i)?.[1]?.toLowerCase();
        const conditionalRecovery = /\bspends all its actions on its turn concentrating\b/i.test(failure);
        if (!recoveryType || !conditionalRecovery) return null;
        const formNotes = "Choose a harmless animal appropriate to the area. The transformation is tracked here; adjudicate its movement, available actions, statistics, and token appearance for the chosen animal.";
        for (const degree of ["failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
            const keepsMind = degree === "failure";
            managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes[degree], {
                label: keepsMind ? "Animal body; original mind" : "Animal body and mind",
                formChoice: true, curse: /\bcurse\b/i.test(spell.system.traits.value.join(" ")),
                duration: keepsMind ? scopedDuration(failure) : null,
                recovery: keepsMind ? { saveType: recoveryType, timing: "manual", mode: "end",
                    trigger: "the creature has spent all its actions this turn concentrating on its original form",
                    confirmTrigger: true, oncePerTurn: true } : null,
                notes: `${formNotes} ${keepsMind ? "The creature keeps its mind. Recovery requires all its actions on its own turn." : "Both body and mind are transformed for an unlimited duration; this outcome grants no recovery save."}`
            });
            addManualText(outcomes[degree], formNotes);
        }
        return finish("harmless-animal-transformation");
    }

    if (/\bemotion effects\b[^.]*\bsuppressed\b/i.test(failure)
        && /\bcan(?:not|'t) use hostile actions\b/i.test(failure)) {
        const penalty = sections.success.text.match(/[–−-](\d+) status penalty to (?:the creature's|its|the target's) attack rolls/i);
        if (!penalty) return null;
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.success, { label: `−${penalty[1]} to attack rolls`, duration, sustained,
            rules: [{ key: "FlatModifier", selector: "attack", type: "status", value: -Number(penalty[1]) }] });
        for (const degree of ["failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
            const endsOnHostility = degree === "failure";
            const notes = `The creature cannot use hostile actions. Suppress emotion effects affecting it at the table. ${endsOnHostility ? "Hostility from another creature ends this effect on this target; use Hostility / End Target." : "Hostility does not end this critical-failure effect."}`;
            managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes[degree], { label: "No hostile actions; emotion effects suppressed",
                duration, sustained, endsOnHostility, notes });
            addManualText(outcomes[degree], notes);
        }
        return finish("nonhostile-emotion-suppression");
    }

    if (sustained && /\bcan(?:not|'t) use reactions\b/i.test(sections.success.text)
        && /\bcan(?:not|'t) use actions or reactions for 1 round\b/i.test(critical)
        && /\bthen takes the effects of a failure\b/i.test(critical)) {
        const conditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome(sections.failure, catalog);
        const prone = scopedConditionMentions(critical, catalog).find(condition => condition.condition === "prone");
        if (!conditions.length || !prone) return null;
        const notes = "The creature cannot use reactions while this effect lasts. Enforce this restriction at the table.";
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.success, { label: "No reactions", duration, sustained, notes });
        const later = { label: `${conditions.map(formatConditionSummary).join(", ")}; no reactions`, duration, sustained,
            conditions: conditions.map(condition => ({ ...condition, duration: null })), notes };
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.failure, later);
        // Falling prone is an immediate event: standing up, not a timer,
        // removes Prone. Only the action restriction has a one-round phase.
        outcomes.criticalFailure.conditions = [{ ...scopedConditionSpec(prone), duration: null }];
        managedOutcomeEffect(outcomes.criticalFailure, {
            label: "No actions or reactions (first round)", duration, sustained,
            phaseDuration: { value: 1, unit: "rounds", expiry: "turn-start" }, transition: later,
            notes: "The creature cannot use actions or reactions for 1 round. The effect then changes to the failure result automatically. Prone ends normally when the creature stands. Enforce the action restriction at the table."
        });
        for (const degree of ["success", "failure", "criticalFailure"]) addManualText(outcomes[degree],
            "Enforce the listed action/reaction restrictions. Sustain normally; use End Sustained Spell when the caster stops sustaining.");
        return finish("sustained-delayed-restriction");
    }
    return null;
}

function markUnresolvedConditionOutcomes(outcomes, sections) {
    for (const [degree, section] of Object.entries(sections ?? {})) {
        const outcome = outcomes[degree];
        if (!outcome || /^(?:(?:the target|the creature|it) is unaffected|(?:the spell has )?no effect)[.!]?$/i.test(section.text.trim())) continue;
        const recognized = (outcome.conditions?.length ?? 0) + (outcome.linkedConditions?.length ?? 0)
            + (outcome.nativeEffects?.length ?? 0) + (outcome.managedEffects?.length ?? 0);
        if (!recognized && !outcome.secondarySave && !outcome.ongoingSave && !outcome.terminal
            && (!outcome.damage || outcome.damage.kind === "none")) {
            addManualText(outcome, `Unresolved outcome: ${section.text}`);
            outcome.rawText = section.text;
        }
    }
    return outcomes;
}


async function inferScopedSpellOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection) {
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const html = stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const plain = scopedPlain(html, catalog);
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(html);
    for (const section of Object.values(sections)) section.text = scopedPlain(section.html, catalog);
    const degrees = ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"];

    // Initial damage is never replaced by a subsequent save. The follow-up
    // carries its own scope, traits, conditions, and anatomy questions.
    const followUps = {};
    for (const degree of degrees) {
        let text = sections[degree]?.text;
        if (!text && degree === "criticalFailure") {
            const start = plain.search(/\bOn a critical failure\b/i);
            if (start >= 0) text = plain.slice(start);
        }
        if (!text) continue;
        const next = /\bmust (?:also )?succeed at a (Reflex|Fortitude|Will)(?: save)?\b/i.exec(text);
        if (!next || !/\b(?:second save|decapitated)\b/i.test(text)) continue;
        const failed = text.match(/\bIf it fails the second save,?\s+(.+)/i)?.[1] ?? "";
        const conditions = scopedConditionMentions(failed.split(/\bIt can attempt\b/i)[0], catalog)
            .map(scopedConditionSpec);
        const duration = scopedDuration(failed);
        if (conditions.length && duration) {
            followUps[degree] = {
                saveType: next[1].toLowerCase(), traits: [],
                failureEffect: { label: conditions.map(c => c.name).join(", "), conditions, duration,
                    durationConditions: true, recovery: inferRecoveryRule(failed, inspection.saveType), notes: failed }
            };
        } else if (/\bIf the creature has a head\b/i.test(text) && /\bdecapitated\b/i.test(text)) {
            const inline = String(spell.system.description.value).match(/@Check\[fortitude\|[^\]]*traits:([^\]|]+)/i);
            followUps[degree] = {
                saveType: next[1].toLowerCase(), traits: inline?.[1]?.split(",").map(normalizeSlug) ?? [],
                requiresHead: true, decapitation: true,
                notes: "A failed second save severs one head. Whether this kills the creature depends on its anatomy and remaining heads."
            };
        }
    }
    if (inspection.damageCount > 0 && Object.keys(followUps).length) {
        const outcomes = inspection.basicSave ? standardBasicOutcomes() : emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
        for (const degree of degrees) {
            if (!inspection.basicSave) {
                if (!sections[degree]) return null;
                const multiplier = inferDamageMultiplier(sections[degree].text);
                if (multiplier === null) return null;
                outcomes[degree].damage = { kind: multiplier > 0 ? "shared" : "none", multiplier };
            }
            outcomes[degree].followUpSave = followUps[degree] ?? null;
            outcomes[degree].rawText = sections[degree]?.text ?? plain;
        }
        return { supported: true, outcomes, sections };
    }
    if (inspection.damageCount > 0 || inspection.basicSave || degrees.some(d => !sections[d])) return null;

    const heightening = activeFixedHeightening(spell, castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank, catalog);
    const heading = html.search(/<strong\b[^>]*>\s*(?:Critical\s+Success|Success|Failure|Critical\s+Failure)/i);
    const preamble = scopedPlain(html.slice(0, Math.max(0, heading)), catalog);
    const shared = /\b(?:target|creature) is affected for\b/i.test(sections.success.text)
        && /\beffects\b/i.test(preamble);
    const sleep = /\bfalls unconscious\b/i.test(sections.failure.text)
        && /\bwakes up automatically\b/i.test(sections.failure.text);
    const attitude = /\battitude becomes\b/i.test(sections.failure.text);
    const floor = /\b(?:can't|cannot) reduce its \w+ condition below \d+\b/i.test(plain);
    const recipient = /\byou are\b/i.test(sections.criticalSuccess.text)
        && scopedConditionMentions(sections.criticalSuccess.text, catalog).length > 0;
    const recovery = degrees.some(d => inferRecoveryRule(sections[d].text, inspection.saveType));
    if (!shared && !sleep && !attitude && !floor && !recipient && !recovery) return null;

    let defaultDuration = scopedDuration(`for ${spell.system?.duration?.value ?? ""}`);
    let dailyPrep = /\bdaily preparations\b/i.test(spell.system?.duration?.value ?? "");
    if (/\bdaily preparations\b/i.test(heightening)) { dailyPrep = true; defaultDuration = null; }
    if (/\bduration is unlimited\b/i.test(heightening)) { dailyPrep = false; defaultDuration = null; }
    const outcomes = emptyNoDamageOutcomes();
    for (const degree of degrees) {
        const ownText = sections[degree].text;
        const inheritance = inferOutcomeInheritance(ownText);
        const baseText = inheritance ? sections[inheritance]?.text ?? "" : "";
        const text = `${baseText} ${ownText}`.trim();
        const out = outcomes[degree];
        out.rawText = ownText;
        if (/\bunaffected\b/i.test(ownText)) {
            if (attitude) addManualText(out, ownText);
            continue;
        }

        if (recipient) {
            // Subject is attached to each occurrence, never inferred from a
            // different sentence or from proximity to a degree heading.
            for (const mention of scopedConditionMentions(ownText, catalog)) {
                const lead = ownText.slice(Math.max(0, mention.index - 80), mention.index);
                out.conditions.push({ ...scopedConditionSpec(mention), recipient: /\byou are\s*$/i.test(lead) ? "caster" : "target" });
            }
            addManualText(out, ownText);
            addManualText(out, "Check the caster's home plane and any optional anathema cost/save modifier before resolving. Plane relocation remains a GM action.");
            continue;
        }

        if (sleep) {
            if (degree === "success") {
                const penalty = ownText.match(/[–−-](\d+) status penalty to Perception checks/i);
                if (!penalty) return null;
                managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: "Perception penalty", duration: scopedDuration(ownText),
                    rules: [{ key: "FlatModifier", selector: "perception", type: "status", value: -Number(penalty[1]) }] });
                continue;
            }
            const condition = scopedConditionMentions(ownText, catalog).find(c => c.condition === "unconscious");
            if (!condition) return null;
            const heightenedSleep = /\bsleeping normally instead of automatically waking up\b/i.test(heightening);
            let duration = scopedDuration(ownText);
            if (heightenedSleep) {
                const pattern = degree === "criticalFailure"
                    ? /(\d+ (?:rounds?|minutes?|hours?)) on a critical failure/i
                    : /for (\d+ (?:rounds?|minutes?|hours?)) on a failure/i;
                const match = heightening.match(pattern);
                if (!match) return null;
                duration = scopedDuration(`for ${match[1]}`);
            }
            if (!duration) return null;
            const notes = heightenedSleep
                ? "Release held items. No Perception checks to wake during the magical duration. At expiry this becomes ordinary sleep; other valid ways of waking still apply. Use Wake / End Effect when awakened."
