                : "Do not fall prone or release held items because of this spell. Normal waking rules, including Perception checks, apply. Use Wake / End Effect when awakened; automatic expiry wakes this sleeper.";
            managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: "Sleep", conditions: [scopedConditionSpec(condition)], duration,
                independent: true, suppressProne: !heightenedSleep,
                transitionToSleep: heightenedSleep, notes });
            addManualText(out, notes);
            continue;
        }

        if (attitude) {
            const mentions = scopedConditionMentions(ownText, catalog);
            const initial = mentions[0];
            if (!initial) { addManualText(out, ownText); continue; }
            const conditional = ownText.match(/\bIf it was (\w+), it becomes (\w+)/i);
            managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: "Attitude toward caster", relationship: {
                initial: initial.name, ifAlready: conditional?.[1] ?? null, then: conditional?.[2] ?? null
            }, duration: defaultDuration, dailyPrep,
                notes: "This attitude applies toward the caster only. No hostile actions against the caster. Dismiss or end this effect if the caster acts hostilely toward the target." });
            addManualText(out, "Attitude is tracked toward the caster only. Apply the spell's conditional +4 save bonus before resolving; dismiss or end the effect when its hostile-action trigger occurs.");
            continue;
        }

        const immediateText = [baseText, ownText].map(part => part.split(/\b(?:It can attempt|At the end of|Whenever)\b/i)[0]).join(" ");
        const mentions = scopedConditionMentions(immediateText, catalog);
        const bySlug = new Map();
        for (const mention of mentions) {
            if (!bySlug.has(mention.condition) || mention.value != null) bySlug.set(mention.condition, scopedConditionSpec(mention));
        }
        if (shared && !/\bis affected for\b/i.test(text)) return null;
        if (shared) {
            const duration = scopedDuration(text);
            const sharedConditions = scopedConditionMentions(preamble, catalog)
                .filter(c => !/\ball creatures and objects\s*$/i.test(preamble.slice(Math.max(0, c.index - 45), c.index)))
                .map(scopedConditionSpec);
            if (!duration || !sharedConditions.length) return null;
            const rules = [];
            const speed = preamble.match(/[–−-](\d+)-foot status penalty to (?:its |their )?Speeds/i);
            if (speed) rules.push({ key: "FlatModifier", selector: "all-speeds", type: "status", value: -Number(speed[1]) });
            const concealment = /\ball creatures and objects Concealed from it\b/i.test(preamble);
            if (concealment) rules.push({ key: "EphemeralEffect", affects: "target", selectors: ["attack-roll"], uuid: await getConditionUUID("concealed") });
            const notes = preamble;
            managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: "Sensory effects", conditions: sharedConditions, duration, rules, notes });
            for (const effect of bySlug.values()) out.conditions.push(effect);
            addManualText(out, preamble);
            continue;
        }

        if (floor) {
            const floorMatch = text.match(/\b(?:can't|cannot) reduce its (\w+) condition below (\d+)/i);
            const guard = text.match(/\bWhenever the target is (\w+) and (.+?), it is also (\w+)(?: (\d+))?/i);
            if (floorMatch) {
                const condition = bySlug.get(normalizeSlug(floorMatch[1]));
                if (!condition) return null;
                const floorValue = Number(floorMatch[2]);
                managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: "Curse", conditions: [{ ...condition, value: floorValue }],
                    floor: true, extraCondition: condition.value > floorValue ? condition : null,
                    environment: guard ? { question: guard[2], condition: normalizeSlug(guard[3]), value: Number(guard[4] ?? 1), dependency: condition.condition } : null,
                    notes: text });
                addManualText(out, "The condition has a minimum value while this curse remains. Use Environment on the effect's control card when the stated location requirement changes; end the effect when the curse is lifted.");
            } else {
                for (const effect of bySlug.values()) out.conditions.push(effect);
                addManualText(out, ownText);
            }
            continue;
        }

        const ownRecovery = inferRecoveryRule(ownText, inspection.saveType);
        const recoveryRule = ownRecovery ?? (inheritance && !/\bno save to end early\b/i.test(ownText)
            ? inferRecoveryRule(baseText, inspection.saveType) : null);
        for (const effect of bySlug.values()) {
            // A valued Stunned N consumes actions; it does not last for the
            // spell's general duration. Duration-based Stunned has no value.
            if (effect.condition === "stunned" && effect.value != null) { out.conditions.push(effect); continue; }
            const duration = scopedDuration(text, defaultDuration);
            if (!duration && !recoveryRule && !dailyPrep) out.conditions.push(effect);
            else managedOutcomeEffect(out, { label: effect.name, conditions: [effect], duration,
                recovery: recoveryRule, dailyPrep, notes: text,
                durationConditions: effect.condition === "stunned" && effect.value == null });
        }
        if (!bySlug.size) return null;
        if (/\bControlled\b/i.test(text)) addManualText(out, "Resolve orders and self-destructive instructions using the spell text. A save triggered by an order is available on the effect's control card.");
    }
    debug("SCOPED SPELL INFERENCE:", { spell: spell.name, outcomes, fixedHeightening: heightening });
    return { supported: true, outcomes, sections };
}


async function inferNonBasicSaveOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection) {
    const description = spell.system?.description?.value ?? "";
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(description);
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    const required = ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"];
    const missing = required.filter(degree => !sections[degree]);

    if (missing.length) {
        return {
            supported: false,
            reason: `Missing outcome sections: ${missing.join(", ")}`,
            sections
        };
    }

    const sharedDuration = await inferSharedDurationOutcomes(spell, inspection, sections, catalog);
    if (sharedDuration) {
        debug("SHARED DURATION CONDITION INFERENCE:", { spell: spell.name, outcomes: sharedDuration.outcomes });
        return sharedDuration;
    }

    const heightening = parseTextHeightening(spell, castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank);
    const outcomes = {};
    const failures = [];

    for (const degree of required) {
        const section = sections[degree];
        const inheritance = inferOutcomeInheritance(section.text);
        const conditions = await extractConditionsFromOutcome(section, catalog);
        const ongoingSave = inferOngoingSave(section, catalog);

        // A long-running outcome can mention the condition that occurs only
        // when its state machine reaches a terminal threshold.  That mention
        // is explanatory, not an immediate condition application.  Keep the
        // terminal condition exclusively on ongoingSave.terminal so it is
        // applied only by resolveOngoingSaveTracker() at the proper time.
        let immediateConditions = conditions;
        if (ongoingSave?.terminal?.condition) {
            const terminalSlug = normalizeSlug(ongoingSave.terminal.condition);
            const removed = conditions.filter(effect =>
                normalizeSlug(effect.condition) === terminalSlug
            );

            immediateConditions = conditions.filter(effect =>
                normalizeSlug(effect.condition) !== terminalSlug
            );

            if (removed.length) {
                debug("ONGOING TERMINAL CONDITION RESERVED:", {
                    spell: spell.name,
                    degree,
                    terminalCondition: ongoingSave.terminal.condition,
                    removedFromImmediateConditions: removed.map(effect => ({
                        condition: effect.condition,
                        value: effect.value ?? null
                    }))
                });
            }
        }

        const splitConditions = splitLinkedConditions(immediateConditions, section.text, catalog);
        const secondarySave = ongoingSave
            ? null
            : await inferSecondarySave(section, catalog, heightening, degree, castEntry);

        let damage = null;
        let ownHasExplicitDamage = false;
        const terminal = secondarySave ? null : inferTerminalResult(section.text);

        if (secondarySave || ongoingSave) {
            damage = { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 };
        } else {
            const parsedDirectDamage = parseDirectDamage(section.text)
                ?? parseInlineDamageSpec(section.rawText ?? section.html ?? "", castEntry);
            const direct = applyTextHeighteningToDamage(parsedDirectDamage, degree, heightening);

            if (direct) {
                damage = direct;
                ownHasExplicitDamage = true;
            } else {
                const multiplier = inferDamageMultiplier(section.text);
                if (multiplier !== null) {
                    damage = inspection.damageCount > 0
                        ? { kind: multiplier > 0 ? "shared" : "none", multiplier }
                        : { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 };
                    ownHasExplicitDamage = true;
                } else if ((splitConditions.normal.length || splitConditions.linked.length || terminal)
                    && !/\bdamage\b/i.test(section.text)) {
                    damage = { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 };
                }
            }
        }

        let outcome = {
            damage,
            conditions: secondarySave ? [] : splitConditions.normal,
            linkedConditions: secondarySave ? [] : splitConditions.linked,
            secondarySave,
            ongoingSave,
            terminal,
            recognizedConditions: conditions,
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: section.text
        };

        if (inheritance && outcomes[inheritance]) {
            outcome = mergeInheritedOutcome(outcomes[inheritance], outcome, { ownHasExplicitDamage });
        }

        if (!outcomeDamageRecognized(outcome, section.text) && !outcome.ongoingSave) {
            failures.push(`${readableDegree(degree)} damage/result was not recognized`);
            continue;
        }

        if (damage?.manualHeightening) outcome.manualRider = true;
        outcomes[degree] = outcome;
    }

    if (failures.length) {
        debug("NON-BASIC PARSER FAILURE DETAILS:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            normalizedSections: Object.fromEntries(
                Object.entries(sections).map(([degree, section]) => [degree, {
                    text: section.text,
                    rawText: section.rawText,
                    normalized: pf2eMarkupToPlain(section.text),
                    inlineDamage: collectBalancedInlines(section.rawText ?? "", "@Damage[")
                        .map(entry => getInlineDamagePayload(entry.body)),
                    html: section.html
                }])
            ),
            heightening
        });
        return { supported: false, reason: failures.join("; "), sections, outcomes, heightening };
    }

    applyFixedOutcomeDamage(outcomes, activeFixedHeightening(spell, castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank, catalog));
    return { supported: true, outcomes, sections, heightening };
}


// =================================================================
// NATIVE PF2E SPELL-EFFECT INFERENCE
// =================================================================
//
// PF2e spells can link prebuilt Effect items from the spell-effects
// compendium. Those effects often contain Rule Elements that are much safer
// to reuse than to reconstruct from prose (status penalties, concealment,
// action restrictions, etc.).  Different degree outcomes frequently use
// different Effect items when their durations differ.
//
// This parser is intentionally generic: it only looks at PF2e spell-effect
// UUID links, the degree section containing the link, and degree words in the
// link label/name. It never checks a spell name.
// =================================================================

function emptyNativeEffectOutcomes() {
    return {
        criticalSuccess: [],
        success: [],
        failure: [],
        criticalFailure: []
    };
}

function degreeFromEffectLabel(label) {
    const text = String(label ?? "")
        .replace(/[’]/g, "'")
        .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
        .trim();

    // Critical degrees must be checked before their contained base words.
    if (/\bcritical\s+success\b/i.test(text)) return "criticalSuccess";
    if (/\bcritical\s+failure\b/i.test(text)) return "criticalFailure";
    if (/\bsuccess\b/i.test(text)) return "success";
    if (/\bfailure\b/i.test(text)) return "failure";
    return null;
}

function extractNativeSpellEffectLinks(html) {
    const source = String(html ?? "");
    const found = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    function add(uuid, label = null, index = -1) {
        if (!uuid || !/^Compendium\.pf2e\.spell-effects\.Item\./i.test(uuid)) return;
        const key = `${uuid}|${label ?? ""}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) return;
        seen.add(key);
        found.push({ uuid, label: String(label ?? "").trim() || null, index });
    }

    // Unenriched PF2e content links in item source data.
    const rawUUID = /@UUID\[(Compendium\.pf2e\.spell-effects\.Item\.[^\]]+)\](?:\{([^}]+)\})?/gi;
    for (const match of source.matchAll(rawUUID)) {
        add(match[1], match[2] ?? null, match.index ?? -1);
    }

    // Enriched links, useful if a module/system has already enriched the HTML.
    const enriched = /<a\b[^>]*data-uuid=["'](Compendium\.pf2e\.spell-effects\.Item\.[^"']+)["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
    for (const match of source.matchAll(enriched)) {
        const label = String(match[2] ?? "")
            .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ")
            .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
            .trim();
        add(match[1], label, match.index ?? -1);
    }

    return found;
}

function mergeNativeEffectList(base = [], extra = []) {
    const byKey = new Map();
    for (const effect of [...base, ...extra]) {
        if (!effect?.uuid) continue;
        byKey.set(effect.uuid, deepClone(effect));
    }
    return [...byKey.values()];
}

function inferNativeSpellEffectRiders(spell) {
    const descriptionHTML = spell?.system?.description?.value ?? "";
    const outcomes = emptyNativeEffectOutcomes();
    const assignedUUIDs = new Set();

    // Strongest signal: the effect link physically lives in a degree section.
    const sections = extractOutcomeSections(descriptionHTML);
    for (const degree of ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
        const section = sections[degree];
        if (!section) continue;

        for (const link of extractNativeSpellEffectLinks(section.html ?? "")) {
            outcomes[degree].push({
                uuid: link.uuid,
                label: link.label,
                degree,
                mapping: "outcome-section"
            });
            assignedUUIDs.add(link.uuid);
        }
    }

    // PF2e's data style also permits effect links below the outcome text. When
    // multiple duration variants exist, their names/labels include the degree.
    for (const link of extractNativeSpellEffectLinks(descriptionHTML)) {
        if (assignedUUIDs.has(link.uuid)) continue;

        const degree = degreeFromEffectLabel(link.label);
        if (!degree) continue;

        outcomes[degree].push({
            uuid: link.uuid,
            label: link.label,
            degree,
            mapping: "effect-label"
        });
        assignedUUIDs.add(link.uuid);
    }

    // Degree prose can inherit another outcome: "As Failure", "As Success",
    // etc. Native Effect riders must inherit too. Do this after both direct
    // section and label mapping are complete so inherited effects can come
    // from either PF2e data-entry style. The order also allows transitive
    // inheritance (e.g. Critical Failure -> Failure -> Success).
    for (const degree of ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
        const section = sections[degree];
        const inheritedFrom = inferOutcomeInheritance(section?.text ?? "");
        if (!inheritedFrom || inheritedFrom === degree) continue;

        const inherited = (outcomes[inheritedFrom] ?? []).map(effect => ({
            ...deepClone(effect),
            degree,
            inheritedFrom,
            mapping: "outcome-inheritance"
        }));

        if (inherited.length > 0) {
            outcomes[degree] = mergeNativeEffectList(inherited, outcomes[degree]);
            debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT INHERITANCE:", {
                spell: spell.name,
                degree,
                inheritedFrom,
                effects: inherited.map(effect => ({ uuid: effect.uuid, label: effect.label }))
            });
        }
    }

    const count = Object.values(outcomes).reduce((sum, list) => sum + list.length, 0);
    return {
        supported: count > 0,
        count,
        outcomes,
        unmapped: extractNativeSpellEffectLinks(descriptionHTML)
            .filter(link => !assignedUUIDs.has(link.uuid))
    };
}

function overlayNativeSpellEffects(baseOutcomes, nativeOutcomes) {
    const merged = deepClone(baseOutcomes ?? emptyNoDamageOutcomes());

    for (const degree of ["criticalSuccess", "success", "failure", "criticalFailure"]) {
        merged[degree] ??= {
            damage: { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 },
            conditions: [],
            linkedConditions: [],
            manualRider: false,
            rawText: "No inferred effect"
        };
        merged[degree].nativeEffects = mergeNativeEffectList(
            merged[degree].nativeEffects ?? [],
            nativeOutcomes?.[degree] ?? []
        );
    }

    return merged;
}

// =================================================================
// AUTOMATION PROFILE
// =================================================================

async function determineSpellAutomation(castEntry) {
    if (!refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(castEntry)) return null;
    if (castEntry.automationProfile) return castEntry.automationProfile;

    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const slug = normalizeSlug(spell.slug ?? spell.name);
    const explicitRule = SPELL_RULES[slug];

    if (explicitRule) {
        castEntry.automationProfile = {
            source: "explicit",
            kind: "outcomes",
            spell,
            rule: explicitRule
        };
        return castEntry.automationProfile;
    }

    const inspection = inspectSpellItem(spell);

    debug("SPELL ITEM INSPECTION:", {
        spell: spell.name,
        castRank: castEntry.castRank,
        saveType: inspection.saveType,
        basicSave: inspection.basicSave,
        damageCount: inspection.damageCount,
        damageKinds: inspection.damageKinds,
        damageComponents: inspection.damageComponents,
        heighteningType: inspection.heighteningType
    });

    const flow = await inferSpellFlow(spell, castEntry, inspection);
    const hasPhases = hasArrivalDeparturePhases(spell.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    if (hasPhases) debug("ARRIVAL/DEPARTURE INFERENCE:", {
        spell: spell.name, supported: !!flow,
        phases: flow ? Object.entries(flow.phases).map(([id, phase]) => ({ id, save: phase.saveType, area: phase.area, traits: phase.traits })) : null,
        reason: flow ? null : "Could not safely read both phases' saves, emanations, and outcomes. Resolve this spell manually."
    });
    if (flow) {
        castEntry.automationProfile = { source: "scoped-description-inference", kind: "interactive-spell", spell, inspection,
            flow, outcomes: Object.values(flow.phases)[0].outcomes, resolutionLabel: "Spell Resolution" };
        return castEntry.automationProfile;
    }
    // A whole-description parser would keep the first set of degree headings
    // and lose the other phase. Keep unsupported phases out of that fallback.
    if (hasPhases) return null;

    // Table rows are alternatives, so they must be parsed before the ordinary
    // whole-description rider inference can see conditions from every row.
    if (isPotentialRandomDamageTableSpell(castEntry)) {
        const tableRule = await inferRandomDamageTableRule(castEntry);
        debug("RANDOM DAMAGE TABLE INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name, supported: !!tableRule, formula: tableRule?.formula,
            rows: tableRule?.rows.map(row => ({ value: row.value, label: row.label,
                formula: row.damage?.inlineFormula, conditions: row.conditions,
                choicePerCreature: !!row.choicePerCreature, manual: row.manualText ?? null }))
        });
        if (!tableRule) return null;
        castEntry.automationProfile = { source: "random-table-inference", kind: "random-table-basic-save", spell, tableRule };
        return castEntry.automationProfile;
    }

    // Persistent/manual-trigger spells can require multiple saves only when a
    // creature later crosses/enters the effect. Their cast card has no native
    // save button, so expose a direct crossing resolver instead.
    if (!inspection.saveType && isPotentialManualMultiSaveCrossingSpell(castEntry)) {
        const crossingRule = await inferManualMultiSaveCrossingRule(castEntry);
        debug("MANUAL MULTI-SAVE CROSSING INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            supported: crossingRule.supported,
            reason: crossingRule.reason ?? null,
            dc: crossingRule.dc ?? null,
            wallSpell: crossingRule.wallSpellName ?? crossingRule.wallSpell?.name ?? null,
            ranks: crossingRule.ranks ?? null,
            lightSave: crossingRule.lightSave ?? null,
            layers: crossingRule.layers?.map(layer => ({
                color: layer.color,
                kind: layer.kind,
                saveType: layer.saveType,
                damage: layer.damage ?? null,
                referencedSpell: layer.referencedSpellName ?? null
            })) ?? [],
            missingColors: crossingRule.missingColors ?? [],
            wallTextPreview: crossingRule.supported ? null : crossingRule.wallTextPreview ?? null
        });

        if (crossingRule.supported) {
            castEntry.automationProfile = {
                source: "automatic",
                kind: "manual-trigger-multi-save",
                spell,
                crossingRule,
                resolutionLabel: "Crossing Resolution"
            };
            return castEntry.automationProfile;
        }
    }

    // No-save level-threshold spells never create a save chat message. Build a
    // direct-resolution profile that the cast-card button can execute instead.
    if (!inspection.saveType && isPotentialNoSaveLevelThresholdSpell(castEntry)) {
        const noSaveRule = await inferNoSaveLevelThresholdRule(castEntry);
        debug("NO-SAVE LEVEL-THRESHOLD INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            supported: noSaveRule.supported,
            reason: noSaveRule.reason ?? null,
            bands: noSaveRule.bands ?? [],
            heightening: noSaveRule.heightening ?? null,
            temporaryImmunity: noSaveRule.temporaryImmunity ?? null
        });

        if (noSaveRule.supported) {
            castEntry.automationProfile = {
                source: "automatic",
                kind: "no-save-level-threshold",
                spell,
                noSaveRule,
                resolutionLabel: "Direct Resolution"
            };
            return castEntry.automationProfile;
        }
    }

    const nativeEffectRiders = inferNativeSpellEffectRiders(spell);
    if (nativeEffectRiders.supported || nativeEffectRiders.unmapped.length) {
        debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            supported: nativeEffectRiders.supported,
            count: nativeEffectRiders.count,
            outcomes: nativeEffectRiders.outcomes,
            unmapped: nativeEffectRiders.unmapped
        });
    }

    const scoped = await inferInitialPersistentOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection)
        ?? await inferRestrictedConditionOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection)
        ?? await inferScopedSpellOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection);
    if (scoped?.supported) {
        castEntry.automationProfile = {
            source: "scoped-description-inference", kind: inspection.basicSave ? "basic-save-damage" : "degree-save-damage",
            spell, inspection, resolutionLabel: "Save Resolution", outcomes: scoped.outcomes,
            conditionalDamageRules: [], targetHealingRules: [], targetDamageSubstitutionRules: [],
            postDamageTerminal: inferPostDamageTerminal(spell)
        };
        return castEntry.automationProfile;
    }

    const targetDamageSubstitutionRules = inferTargetDamageSubstitutionRules(spell);
    if (targetDamageSubstitutionRules.length) {
        debug("TARGET DAMAGE-TYPE SUBSTITUTION INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            rules: targetDamageSubstitutionRules
        });
    }

    if (inspection.basicSave) {
        const conditionalDamageRules = inferTargetConditionalDamageRules(spell, castEntry);
        const targetHealingRules = inferTargetHealingRules(spell, castEntry, inspection);
        const conditionRiders = await inferConditionRidersAcrossDescription(spell);

        if (conditionRiders.supported) {
            debug("CONDITION RIDER INFERENCE:", {
                spell: spell.name,
                basicSave: true,
                outcomes: conditionRiders.outcomes,
                conditionFloorManual: conditionRiders.hasConditionFloor
            });
        }

        if (inspection.damageCount <= 0
            && conditionalDamageRules.length === 0
            && targetHealingRules.length === 0
            && !conditionRiders.supported
            && !nativeEffectRiders.supported) {
            return null;
        }

        if (targetHealingRules.length) {
            debug("TARGET-MODE HEALING INFERENCE:", {
                spell: spell.name,
                rules: targetHealingRules
            });
        }

        // With structured damage, preserve ordinary Basic Save multipliers and
        // overlay any degree-specific condition riders. With no damage at all,
        // use the same inferred condition outcomes but leave every damage
        // result at 0 so the master card becomes an effects-only resolver.
        const baseOutcomes = inspection.damageCount > 0
            ? standardBasicOutcomes()
            : emptyNoDamageOutcomes();

        castEntry.automationProfile = {
            source: conditionRiders.supported && inspection.damageCount <= 0
                ? "condition-inference"
                : "spell-item",
            kind: inspection.damageCount > 0 ? "basic-save-damage" : "degree-save-damage",
            spell,
            inspection,
            resolutionLabel: targetHealingRules.length
                ? "Target Resolution"
                : inspection.damageCount > 0
                    ? "Basic Save"
                    : "Condition Resolution",
            outcomes: overlayNativeSpellEffects(
                overlayConditionRiders(baseOutcomes, conditionRiders.outcomes),
                nativeEffectRiders.outcomes
            ),
            conditionalDamageRules,
            targetHealingRules,
            targetDamageSubstitutionRules,
            casterTemporaryHP: inferCasterTemporaryHPRule(spell),
            postDamageTerminal: inferPostDamageTerminal(spell)
        };
        return castEntry.automationProfile;
    }

    // v8.4: non-basic saves are allowed to have ZERO structured damage.
    // Their damage can instead be defined independently in each outcome.
    const inferred = await inferNonBasicSaveOutcomes(spell, castEntry, inspection);

    debug("NON-BASIC SAVE INFERENCE:", {
        spell: spell.name,
        supported: inferred.supported,
        reason: inferred.reason ?? null,
        structuredDamage: inspection.damageCount,
        outcomes: inferred.outcomes ?? null,
        heightening: inferred.heightening ?? null,
        sections: inferred.sections
    });

    if (!inferred.supported) {
        // Fallback for condition-only save spells written as compact prose
        // rather than four separate degree-of-success paragraphs.
        if (inspection.damageCount <= 0) {
            const conditionOnly = await inferConditionRidersAcrossDescription(spell);

            debug("CONDITION-ONLY SAVE INFERENCE:", {
                spell: spell.name,
                supported: conditionOnly.supported,
                outcomes: conditionOnly.outcomes,
                conditionFloorManual: conditionOnly.hasConditionFloor,
                originalReason: inferred.reason ?? null
            });

            if (conditionOnly.supported || nativeEffectRiders.supported) {
                const fallbackOutcomes = conditionOnly.supported
                    ? conditionOnly.outcomes
                    : emptyNoDamageOutcomes();

                castEntry.automationProfile = {
                    source: nativeEffectRiders.supported ? "native-effect-inference" : "condition-inference",
                    kind: "degree-save-damage",
                    spell,
                    inspection,
                    resolutionLabel: nativeEffectRiders.supported ? "Effect Resolution" : "Condition Resolution",
                    outcomes: markUnresolvedConditionOutcomes(overlayNativeSpellEffects(fallbackOutcomes, nativeEffectRiders.outcomes), inferred.sections),
                    conditionalDamageRules: [],
                    targetHealingRules: [],
                    targetDamageSubstitutionRules,
                    postDamageTerminal: inferPostDamageTerminal(spell)
                };
                return castEntry.automationProfile;
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    const conditionalDamageRules = inferTargetConditionalDamageRules(spell, castEntry);
    const targetHealingRules = inferTargetHealingRules(spell, castEntry, inspection);

    if (conditionalDamageRules.length) {
        debug("TARGET-CONDITIONAL DAMAGE INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            rules: conditionalDamageRules
        });
    }

    if (targetHealingRules.length) {
        debug("TARGET-MODE HEALING INFERENCE:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            rules: targetHealingRules
        });
    }

    castEntry.automationProfile = {
        source: "description-inference",
        kind: "degree-save-damage",
        spell,
        inspection,
        resolutionLabel: targetHealingRules.length ? "Target Resolution" : "Save Resolution",
        outcomes: overlayNativeSpellEffects(inferred.outcomes, nativeEffectRiders.outcomes),
        conditionalDamageRules,
        targetHealingRules,
        targetDamageSubstitutionRules,
        textHeightening: inferred.heightening ?? null,
        castBacklash: inferCastBacklashRule(spell, castEntry),
        postDamageTerminal: inferPostDamageTerminal(spell)
    };

    return castEntry.automationProfile;
}

// =================================================================
// SAVE ORIGIN / MATCHING / CHAT RECOVERY
// =================================================================

async function getSpellDirectlyFromSave(message) {
    const pf2e = message.flags?.pf2e ?? {};

    const candidates = [
        pf2e.origin?.uuid,
        pf2e.origin?.item,
        pf2e.origin?.itemUuid,
        pf2e.context?.origin?.uuid,
        pf2e.context?.origin?.item,
        pf2e.context?.origin?.itemUuid
    ].filter(Boolean);

    for (const uuid of candidates) {
        try {
            const document = await fromUuid(uuid);
            if (document?.type === "spell") return document;
            if (document?.item?.type === "spell") return document.item;
        } catch (_) {}
    }

    // A scroll spell's Actor...Item UUID usually cannot resolve through the
    // actor's ordinary items collection. Its original native card retains the
    // embedded source even after PF2e deletes the consumed scroll.
    for (const entry of STATE.recentSpells) {
        if (entry.isEmbeddedSpell && candidates.includes(entry.spell?.uuid)) return entry.spell;
    }
    if (candidates.length) {
        const cards = Array.from(game.messages?.contents ?? game.messages ?? []).reverse();
        for (const card of cards) {
            if (!card.flags?.pf2e?.casting?.embeddedSpell
                || !candidates.includes(card.flags?.pf2e?.origin?.uuid)) continue;
            const cast = detectSpellCard(card);
            if (cast) return cast.spell;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function getSaveType(message) {
    const pf2e = message.flags?.pf2e ?? {};
    const text = [
        pf2e.context?.identifier,
        pf2e.context?.statistic,
        pf2e.context?.type,
        pf2e.modifierName,
        message.flavor
    ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase();

    if (text.includes("will")) return "will";
    if (text.includes("fortitude")) return "fortitude";
    if (text.includes("reflex")) return "reflex";
    return null;
}

function getDC(message) {
    const pf2e = message.flags?.pf2e ?? {};

    for (const candidate of [
        pf2e.context?.dc?.value,
        pf2e.context?.dc,
        pf2e.dc?.value,
        pf2e.dc
    ]) {
        const value = Number(candidate);
        if (Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
    }

    return null;
}

function castMatchesSave(castEntry, saveType, dc) {
    if (!castEntry) return false;

    const phaseSave = Object.values(castEntry.automationProfile?.flow?.phases ?? {}).some(phase => phase.saveType === saveType);
    const phaseText = String(castEntry.spell?.system?.description?.value ?? "");
    const uncachedPhaseSave = /<strong[^>]*>\s*Depart\s*<\/strong>/i.test(phaseText)
        && new RegExp(`@Check\\[${escapeRegex(saveType ?? "")}\\b`, "i").test(phaseText);
    const saveMatches = !saveType || !castEntry.save || castEntry.save === saveType || phaseSave || uncachedPhaseSave;
    const dcMatches = dc === null || castEntry.dc === null || castEntry.dc === dc;
    return saveMatches && dcMatches;
}

function castMatchesDirectSpell(castEntry, directSpell) {
    if (!directSpell) return true;

    const castSlug = normalizeSlug(castEntry.spell?.slug ?? castEntry.spell?.name);
    const directSlug = normalizeSlug(directSpell.slug ?? directSpell.name);

    if (castSlug && directSlug && castSlug !== directSlug) return false;

    if (castEntry.spell?.id && directSpell.id && castEntry.spell.id === directSpell.id) {
        return true;
    }

    if (castEntry.actor?.id && directSpell.actor?.id && castEntry.actor.id !== directSpell.actor.id) {
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

function recoverCastEntryFromChat(saveMessage, directSpell = null) {
    const saveType = getSaveType(saveMessage);
    const dc = getDC(saveMessage);

    const messages = Array.from(game.messages?.contents ?? [])
        .slice()
        .sort((a, b) => (b.timestamp ?? 0) - (a.timestamp ?? 0));

    for (const chatMessage of messages) {
        if (chatMessage.id === saveMessage.id) continue;

        let candidate = findCastById(chatMessage.id);
        let newlyRecovered = false;

        if (!candidate) {
            candidate = detectSpellCard(chatMessage);
            newlyRecovered = !!candidate;
        }

        if (!candidate) continue;
        if (!castMatchesSave(candidate, saveType, dc)) continue;
        if (!castMatchesDirectSpell(candidate, directSpell)) continue;

        if (newlyRecovered) {
            STATE.recentSpells.unshift(candidate);
            pruneCastCache();
        }

        candidate.timestamp = Date.now();
        STATE.activeCastId = candidate.castId;
        STATE.activeCastAt = Date.now();

        debug("RECOVERED CAST FROM EXISTING CHAT:", {
            spell: candidate.spell.name,
            castId: candidate.castId,
            castRank: candidate.castRank,
            save: candidate.save,
            dc: candidate.dc
        });

        return candidate;
    }

    return null;
}

function findCachedSpellEntry(message, directSpell = null) {
    pruneCastCache();

    const saveType = getSaveType(message);
    const dc = getDC(message);

    if (STATE.activeCastId && Date.now() - STATE.activeCastAt <= ACTIVE_CAST_WINDOW_MS) {
        const active = findCastById(STATE.activeCastId);
        if (active
            && castMatchesSave(active, saveType, dc)
            && castMatchesDirectSpell(active, directSpell)) {
            return active;
        }
    }

    return STATE.recentSpells.find(entry =>
        castMatchesSave(entry, saveType, dc)
        && castMatchesDirectSpell(entry, directSpell)
    ) ?? null;
}

// =================================================================
// CONDITIONS
// =================================================================

function getExistingCondition(actor, slug) {
    try {
        const matches = actor.conditions?.bySlug?.(slug);
        if (matches?.length) return matches.find(condition => condition.active) ?? matches[0];
    } catch (_) {}

    return actor.items.find(item =>
        item.type === "condition"
        && item.slug === slug
        && item.active !== false
    ) ?? null;
}

function getConditionValue(condition) {
    if (!condition) return 0;

    for (const candidate of [
        condition.value,
        condition.system?.value?.value,
        condition.system?.badge?.value
    ]) {
        const value = Number(candidate);
        if (Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
    }

    return 0;
}

async function applyCondition(actor, slug, desiredValue = null) {
    if (!actor) return;
    if (typeof actor.increaseCondition !== "function") {
        throw new Error(`${actor.name} does not expose increaseCondition().`);
    }

    let existing = getExistingCondition(actor, slug);

    if (desiredValue === null || desiredValue === undefined) {
        if (!existing) await actor.increaseCondition(slug);
        return;
    }

    let currentValue = getConditionValue(existing);
    if (currentValue >= desiredValue) return;

    let safety = 0;
    while (currentValue < desiredValue && safety < 10) {
        safety++;
        await actor.increaseCondition(slug);
        existing = getExistingCondition(actor, slug);
        const newValue = getConditionValue(existing);
        currentValue = newValue <= currentValue ? currentValue + 1 : newValue;
    }
}

async function getConditionUUID(slug) {
    const catalog = await getConditionCatalog();
    return catalog.bySlug.get(normalizeSlug(slug))?.uuid ?? null;
}

function getCombatantForActor(actor) {
    const combat = game.combat;
    if (!combat || !actor) return null;

    const actorUUID = actor.uuid ?? null;
    const actorID = actor.id ?? null;

    return combat.combatants.find(combatant => {
        const combatActor = combatant.actor;
        if (!combatActor) return false;
        if (actorUUID && combatActor.uuid === actorUUID) return true;
        return actorID && combatActor.id === actorID;
    }) ?? null;
}

// Continuing effects are native Effect items. Their flags retain the source
// cast so recovery saves also work for a consumed scroll after a reload.
// A cast-wide "kills nobody" rider cannot be resolved after the first save.
// The GM declares the complete area once; every initial result is recorded
// before the rider is evaluated. This is independent of the spell's name.
function inferCastBacklashRule(spell, castEntry) {
    const plain = htmlToPlain(stripHeightenedDescription(spell.system?.description?.value ?? ""))
        .replace(/[’]/g, "'").replace(/\b(\d+)\[([a-z-]+)\]/gi, "$1 $2");
    const trigger = plain.match(/\bIf [^.!?]+ (?:doesn't|does not) kill (?:even )?(?:a single|one|any) creature,\s*(.+)/i);
    if (!trigger) return null;
    const damage = trigger[1].match(/\bdealing an additional (\d+) (\w+) damage to every living creature in the line[^.!?]*?and (\d+) (\w+) damage to you\b/i);
    const cap = plain.match(/\bliving creature of (\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th) level or lower\b/i);
    if (!damage || !cap) return null;
    let levelCap = Number(cap[1]);
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${spell.system.description.value}</div>`, "text/html");
    for (const strong of doc.querySelectorAll("strong")) {
        const rank = strong.textContent.match(/^Heightened\s*\((\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th)\)/i)?.[1];
        if (!rank || Number(rank) > Number(castEntry.castRank)) continue;
        const text = htmlToPlain((strong.closest("p, li") ?? strong.parentElement).innerHTML);
        const higherCap = text.match(/\baffect living creatures up to (\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th) level/i);
        if (higherCap) levelCap = Number(higherCap[1]);
    }
    return { levelCap, areaDamage: Number(damage[1]), areaType: normalizeDamageTypeAlias(damage[2]),
        casterDamage: Number(damage[3]), casterType: normalizeDamageTypeAlias(damage[4]), trigger: "kills-none" };
}

function getCastBacklashState(castEntry) {
    if (!castEntry.automationProfile?.castBacklash) return null;
    if (!castEntry.castBacklashState) {
        const stored = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId)?.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.castBacklash;
        castEntry.castBacklashState = stored ? deepClone(stored) : {
            version: 1, roster: null, initial: {}, pendingInitial: {}, backlashApplied: [], pendingApplication: null,
            finished: false, killed: false, result: null
        };
    }
    return castEntry.castBacklashState;
}

async function persistCastBacklash(castEntry) {
    const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
    if (!state) return;
    const message = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId);
    if (!message) throw new Error("The original cast card is missing; cast-wide results cannot be saved.");
    await message.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.castBacklash": deepClone(state) });
}

function isLivingAreaCreature(actor) {
    if (actor?.modeOfBeing) return actor.modeOfBeing === "living";
    const traits = new Set(actor?.system?.traits?.value ?? []);
    return !traits.has("undead") && !traits.has("construct");
}

function backlashTargetEligibility(actor, rule) {
    const level = getCreatureLevel(actor);
    return { living: isLivingAreaCreature(actor), level,
        eligible: isLivingAreaCreature(actor) && Number.isFinite(level) && level <= rule.levelCap };
}

async function recordCastInitialResult(castEntry, target, actor, beforeHP, wasDead) {
    const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
    if (!state) return;
    const afterHP = getActorHPValue(actor);
    const reducedToZero = Number.isFinite(beforeHP) && Number.isFinite(afterHP) && beforeHP > 0 && afterHP <= 0;
    const killed = !wasDead && (target.terminalApplied || (actor.statuses?.has?.("dead") === true));
    state.initial[target.key] = {
        actorUUID: actor.uuid, tokenUUID: target.tokenUUID, actorName: actor.name, beforeHP, afterHP,
        reducedToZero, killed, complete: !target.manualCorrection,
        degree: target.degree, immune: !!target.spellImmunity
    };
    if (state.pendingInitial) delete state.pendingInitial[target.key];
    state.killed ||= killed;
    await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
}

function castBacklashIsReady(castEntry) {
    const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
    if (!state?.roster || state.finished || state.pendingApplication || state.reviewRequired) return false;
    return state.roster.every(row => !row.eligible || state.initial[row.key]?.complete);
}

async function maybeApplyCastBacklash(castEntry) {
    if (!castBacklashIsReady(castEntry)) return;
    return await runManagedEffectJob(`${castEntry.castId}:backlash`, async () => {
        if (!castBacklashIsReady(castEntry)) return;
        const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
        const rule = castEntry.automationProfile.castBacklash;
        // Count only kills of declared area creatures, never unrelated HP
        // changes, a creature already at zero, or the later backlash itself.
        const killed = state.roster.some(row => state.initial[row.key]?.killed);
        if (killed) {
            state.finished = true; state.result = "At least one creature was killed; no backlash.";
            await persistCastBacklash(castEntry); return;
        }
        const applications = [
            ...state.roster.map(row => ({ ...row, amount: rule.areaDamage, damageType: rule.areaType })),
            { key: "caster-backlash", actorUUID: castEntry.actor.uuid, tokenUUID: getActorTokenUUID(castEntry.actor),
                actorName: castEntry.actor.name, amount: rule.casterDamage, damageType: rule.casterType }
        ];
        for (const row of applications) {
            if (state.backlashApplied.includes(row.key)) continue;
            const { actor, token } = await resolveGroupedTarget(row);
            if (!actor) throw new Error(`Could not resolve ${row.actorName}; backlash remains pending.`);
            if (getSpellEffectImmunity(actor, castEntry)) {
                state.backlashApplied.push(row.key);
                await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
                continue;
            }
            // Persist an intent before changing HP. A connection loss in the
            // middle is reported for review instead of blindly dealing twice.
            state.pendingApplication = { key: row.key, actorName: actor.name, beforeHP: getActorHPValue(actor) };
            await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
            const DamageRoll = getPF2eDamageRollClass();
            const roll = new DamageRoll(`{${row.amount}[${row.damageType}]}`, getOutcomeDamageRollData(castEntry));
            await roll.evaluate();
            const result = await applyDamageRollToActor(actor, token, roll, null, castEntry, "Applying cast-wide backlash:");
            if (!result?.applied) throw new Error(`Backlash application to ${actor.name} was not confirmed.`);
            // The spell's zero-HP death rider also applies to this spell's
            // backlash damage. It never cancels an already-triggered backlash.
            const terminalTarget = { actorName: actor.name, key: row.key };
            await handlePostDamageTerminal(castEntry, terminalTarget, actor, result.beforeHP, result.afterHP);
            state.backlashApplied.push(row.key); state.pendingApplication = null;
            await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
        }
        state.finished = true;
        state.result = `No creature was killed by the initial effect. Backlash applied: ${rule.areaDamage} ${rule.areaType} to each living area creature and ${rule.casterDamage} ${rule.casterType} to the caster.`;
        await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
        await ChatMessage.create({ speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
            content: `<p><strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)}</strong>: ${escapeHTML(state.result)}</p>` });
    });
}

async function prepareCastBacklashRoster(castEntry, selected) {
    const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
    const rule = castEntry.automationProfile.castBacklash;
    const currentTargets = Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {}).flatMap(group => group.targets);
    // The persistent record survives a result-card deletion or a GM reload.
    // A creature killed earlier by this cast still counts, even if its token
    // has been deselected or deleted. Its present HP is not a new kill test.
    const prior = new Map(Object.entries(state.initial ?? {}).map(([key, record]) => [key, { ...record, key }]));
    for (const target of currentTargets) prior.set(target.key, { ...prior.get(target.key), ...target });
    for (const key of Object.keys(state.pendingInitial ?? {})) {
        if (!prior.has(key)) {
            throw new Error("An earlier initial application was interrupted. Review that creature's HP and death status before resolving this cast.");
        }
    }

    const selectedByKey = new Map();
    const roster = new Map();
    const excluded = [];
    for (const selectedToken of selected) {
        const token = selectedToken.document ?? selectedToken;
        const actor = selectedToken.actor ?? token.actor;
        if (!actor) throw new Error("A selected token has no actor.");
        const key = getTargetKey(actor, token);
        const eligibility = backlashTargetEligibility(actor, rule);
        selectedByKey.set(key, { actor, token });
        if (!eligibility.living || actor.isDead || actor.statuses?.has?.("dead")) continue;
        if (!Number.isFinite(eligibility.level)) throw new Error(`${actor.name}: level could not be read.`);
        roster.set(key, { key, actorUUID: actor.uuid, tokenUUID: token.uuid ?? "",
            actorName: actor.name, eligible: eligibility.eligible, level: eligibility.level });
    }

    const missing = [];
    for (const [key, target] of prior) {
        const record = state.initial?.[key];
        if (record?.complete && record.killed) {
            roster.set(key, { key, actorUUID: record.actorUUID ?? target.actorUUID,
                tokenUUID: record.tokenUUID ?? target.tokenUUID ?? "",
                actorName: target.actorName ?? record.actorName ?? "Previously resolved creature",
                eligible: true, killedByThisCast: true });
            continue;
        }

        const { actor } = selectedByKey.get(key) ?? await resolveGroupedTarget(target);
        if (!actor) throw new Error(`${target.actorName ?? "A saved creature"}: its actor could not be recovered. Restore that creature before resolving the area.`);
        const nonliving = !isLivingAreaCreature(actor);
        const dead = actor.isDead || actor.statuses?.has?.("dead") === true;
        if (nonliving || dead) {
            if (state.pendingInitial?.[key]) {
                throw new Error(`${actor.name}: an initial application is incomplete. Review its HP and death status before continuing.`);
            }
            excluded.push({ key, actor, target, reason: nonliving ? "Not affected: nonliving" : "Not affected: already dead" });
            continue;
        }
        // Compare identities before discarding unaffected selections. Never
        // require a nonliving saved creature in a living-only roster, and do
        // not equate different scene tokens just because they share an actor.
        if (!selectedByKey.has(key)) missing.push({ name: actor.name, key });
    }
    if (missing.length) {
        debug("CAST AREA MISSING LIVING TARGETS:", { spell: castEntry.spell.name, missing,
            selected: [...selectedByKey.keys()] });
        throw new Error(`Select these living creatures already saved or resolved on this card: ${missing.map(row => row.name).join(", ")}. Keep all other living creatures in the line selected too.`);
    }

    for (const { key, actor, target, reason } of excluded) {
        for (const current of currentTargets.filter(row => row.key === key)) {
            current.applied = true;
            current.followUpNote = reason;
        }
        state.initial[key] = { ...state.initial[key], actorUUID: actor.uuid,
            tokenUUID: target.tokenUUID ?? "", actorName: actor.name,
            complete: true, killed: false, excluded: reason };
    }
    debug("CAST AREA RECONCILED:", { spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        included: [...roster.values()].map(row => ({ name: row.actorName, key: row.key, killedByThisCast: !!row.killedByThisCast })),
        excluded: excluded.map(row => ({ name: row.actor.name, reason: row.reason })) });
    return [...roster.values()];
}

async function resolveCastBacklashArea(castEntry) {
    return await runManagedEffectJob(`${castEntry.castId}:area`, async () => {
        if (!refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(castEntry)) return;
        await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);
        const rule = castEntry.automationProfile?.castBacklash;
        const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
        if (!rule || !state || state.finished) return;
        if (state.pendingApplication) throw new Error(`A prior backlash application to ${state.pendingApplication.actorName} was interrupted. Check its HP before making a manual correction; this cast will not repeat that damage automatically.`);
        // Apply Results may still be finishing when this button is clicked.
        // Read its final kill record, rather than excluding a newly dead
        // creature in the gap between its HP update and record persistence.
        await Promise.all(Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {}).map(group => group._applyInFlight).filter(Boolean));
        if (!state.roster) {
            const selected = [...(game.user.targets ?? [])];
            if (!selected.length) {
                const answer = await promptSpellDecision(castEntry.spell.name,
                    "No tokens are selected. Is the line empty of living creatures?",
                    [{ value: "select", label: "I need to select the affected creatures first" },
                        { value: "empty", label: "Yes, the area is empty — resolve backlash" }]);
                if (answer !== "empty") return;
            }
            state.roster = await prepareCastBacklashRoster(castEntry, selected);
            await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
        }
        for (const row of state.roster) {
            if (!row.eligible || state.initial[row.key]?.complete) continue;
            const { actor, token } = await resolveGroupedTarget(row);
            if (!actor) throw new Error(`${row.actorName}: target is unavailable.`);
            if (findTargetResult(castEntry, row.key)) continue;
            const immunity = getSpellEffectImmunity(actor, castEntry);
            if (immunity) {
                state.initial[row.key] = { complete: true, killed: false, immune: true, actorUUID: actor.uuid };
                await persistCastBacklash(castEntry); continue;
            }
            const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
            const result = await rollManualTriggerSave(actor, token, castEntry.save, castEntry.dc, spell,
                `${spell.name} — Area ${castEntry.save} Save`, [],
                { originSpell: castEntry.saveOriginSpell ?? spell, castRank: castEntry.castRank });
            addTargetToResultGroup(getOrCreateResultGroup(castEntry, result.degree), actor, token);
        }
        for (const group of Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {})) {
            for (const target of group.targets) {
                if (target.applied && !target.manualCorrection && !state.initial[target.key]
                    && (!groupNeedsAutomatedAction(group) || target.spellImmunity)) {
                    const { actor } = await resolveGroupedTarget(target);
                    if (actor) await recordCastInitialResult(castEntry, target, actor, getActorHPValue(actor), !!actor.isDead);
                }
            }
            await applyResultGroup(castEntry, group, { disabled: false, innerHTML: "Resolve Area" });
        }
        await maybeApplyCastBacklash(castEntry);
        await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry);
    });
}

function buildCastBacklashControls(castEntry) {
    const state = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
    if (!state) return "";
    const pending = state.pendingApplication;
    const status = state.result ?? (pending ? `Interrupted damage to ${pending.actorName}; review HP manually before proceeding.`
        : state.roster ? "Area recorded. Waiting for every initial result before checking backlash."
        : "Target every living creature in the line, including creatures above the spell's level limit. This control records the area, rolls missing saves, and resolves the cast.");
    return `<p>${escapeHTML(status)}</p>` + (!state.finished && !pending
        ? `<button type="button" class="pf2e-auto-backlash-area" data-cast-id="${escapeHTML(castEntry.castId)}">${state.roster ? "Continue Area Resolution" : "Resolve Selected Area"}</button>` : "");
}


const MANAGED_EFFECT_JOBS = new Map();

async function promptSpellDecision(title, question, choices) {
    return await foundry.applications.api.DialogV2.prompt({
        window: { title },
        content: `<p>${escapeHTML(question)}</p><select name="decision" style="width:100%">${choices.map(choice =>
            `<option value="${escapeHTML(choice.value)}">${escapeHTML(choice.label)}</option>`).join("")}</select>`,
        ok: { label: "Continue", callback: (_event, button) => button.form.elements.decision.value },
        rejectClose: false, modal: true
    });
}

function managedEffectsOnActor(actor) {
    return actor?.items?.filter?.(item => item.type === "effect" && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect) ?? [];
}

// A cast's maximum-damage benefit is durable across result-card deletion and
// reload. Each target contributes once; later targets increase only the pool
// ceiling, preserving points already spent and the original expiration.
function getCasterTemporaryHPState(castEntry) {
    if (!castEntry.automationProfile?.casterTemporaryHP) return null;
    if (!castEntry.casterTemporaryHPState) {
        const stored = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId)?.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.casterTemporaryHP;
        castEntry.casterTemporaryHPState = stored ? deepClone(stored) : {
            version: 1, records: {}, pending: null, pendingGrant: null,
            peakDamage: 0, offered: 0, effectId: null, started: false, claimed: false, result: null
        };
    }
    return castEntry.casterTemporaryHPState;
}

async function persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry) {
    const state = getCasterTemporaryHPState(castEntry);
    const card = game.messages.get(castEntry.castId);
    if (!state || !card) throw new Error("The original cast card is missing; caster temporary HP cannot be tracked.");
    await card.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.casterTemporaryHP": deepClone(state) });
}

async function updateCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry) {
    const state = getCasterTemporaryHPState(castEntry);
    if (!state || state.reviewRequired) return;
    if (state.pendingGrant) throw new Error("A temporary HP grant was interrupted. Review the caster's temporary HP manually before continuing this cast.");
    const rule = castEntry.automationProfile.casterTemporaryHP;
    const award = Math.floor(state.peakDamage * rule.fraction);
    if (award <= state.offered) return;
    const caster = castEntry.actor;
    let effect = state.effectId ? caster.items.get(state.effectId) : null;
    if (state.started && (!effect || effect.isExpired || effect.remainingDuration?.expired)) {
        state.result = "Caster temporary HP effect has ended; this cast will not renew it.";
        await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry); return;
    }
    const previousOffer = state.offered;
    state.pendingGrant = { award, effectId: state.effectId };
    await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
    if (!effect) {
        effect = await createManagedSpellEffect(caster, castEntry, {
            label: "Temporary HP", duration: rule.duration, durationAnchor: "caster",
            rules: [{ key: "TempHP", value: award }],
            notes: "Temporary HP from the greatest damage taken by one creature in this cast. Temporary HP do not stack. Spending them does not cause this spell to refill them."
        }, `${castEntry.castId}:caster-temporary-hp`);
        state.effectId = effect.id;
        state.started = true;
    } else {
        const hp = caster.system.attributes.hp;
        const ownsPool = hp.tempsource === effect.id;
        // A different source replacing an already-granted pool ends this
        // cast's claim. Deleting the source likewise never grants it again.
        if (state.claimed && !ownsPool) state.displaced = true;
        if (!state.displaced) {
            if (ownsPool) {
                await caster.update({ "system.attributes.hp.temp": Math.max(0, Number(hp.temp) || 0) + award - previousOffer });
            } else if (award > (Number(hp.temp) || 0)) {
                await caster.update({ "system.attributes.hp.temp": award, "system.attributes.hp.tempsource": effect.id });
            }
        }
        await effect.update({ "system.rules": [{ key: "TempHP", value: award }] });
    }
    state.claimed ||= caster.system.attributes.hp.tempsource === effect.id;
    state.offered = award;
    state.pendingGrant = null;
    state.result = state.displaced ? "Caster temporary HP were replaced by another source; this cast will not refill them."
        : caster.system.attributes.hp.tempsource === effect.id
            ? `Caster temporary HP: ${Number(caster.system.attributes.hp.temp) || 0} remaining; ${award} granted from ${state.peakDamage} damage. Expires after ${rule.duration.value} ${rule.duration.unit}.`
            : `Caster keeps their existing temporary HP (${Number(caster.system.attributes.hp.temp) || 0}); this cast offers ${award}.`;
    await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
    debug("CASTER TEMPORARY HP:", { spell: castEntry.spell.name, caster: caster.name,
        peakDamage: state.peakDamage, award, remaining: caster.system.attributes.hp.temp, effectId: effect.id });
}

function buildCasterTemporaryHPSummary(castEntry) {
    const state = getCasterTemporaryHPState(castEntry);
    if (!state) return "";
    const effect = state.effectId ? castEntry.actor.items.get(state.effectId) : null;
    const hp = castEntry.actor.system.attributes.hp;
    const current = effect && hp.tempsource === effect.id && !effect.isExpired && !effect.remainingDuration?.expired
        ? `Caster temporary HP: ${Number(hp.temp) || 0} remaining; ${state.offered} granted from ${state.peakDamage} damage.` : null;
    const text = state.reviewRequired ? state.reviewReason ?? "Caster temporary HP require manual review."
        : state.pending || state.pendingGrant ? "An application was interrupted; review this cast before continuing."
        : current ?? state.result ?? "Caster gains temporary HP equal to half the greatest damage taken by one target as results are applied.";
    return `<p>${escapeHTML(text)}</p>`;
}

function managedAutomationGM() {
    const activeGM = game.users?.activeGM;
    return game.user.isGM && (!activeGM || activeGM.id === game.user.id);
}

function runManagedEffectJob(key, work) {
    if (MANAGED_EFFECT_JOBS.has(key)) return MANAGED_EFFECT_JOBS.get(key);
    const promise = Promise.resolve().then(work).finally(() => MANAGED_EFFECT_JOBS.delete(key));
    MANAGED_EFFECT_JOBS.set(key, promise);
    return promise;
}

async function managedGrantRule(condition, extras = {}) {
    const uuid = await getConditionUUID(condition.condition);
    if (!uuid) throw new Error(`Could not resolve ${condition.condition}.`);
    const rule = { key: "GrantItem", uuid, inMemoryOnly: true, ...extras };
    if (condition.value != null) rule.alterations = [{ mode: "override", property: "badge-value", value: condition.value }];
    return rule;
}

async function createManagedCondition(actor, parent, spec, condition) {
    const uuid = await getConditionUUID(condition.condition);
    const template = uuid ? await fromUuid(uuid) : null;
    if (!template) throw new Error(`Could not load ${condition.name ?? condition.condition}.`);
    const source = deepClone(template.toObject());
    for (const key of ["_id", "folder", "sort", "ownership"]) delete source[key];
    const seconds = { rounds: 6, minutes: 60, hours: 3600 }[spec.duration?.unit];
    source.flags = { pf2eAutoSpellResolution: { managedParent: parent.id,
        managedExpiresAt: seconds ? Number(parent.system.start.value) + seconds * spec.duration.value : null,
        managedSleepTransition: spec.transitionToSleep === true } };
    source.system.references = { parent: null, children: [], overriddenBy: [], overrides: [] };
    if (condition.value != null) source.system.value = { isValued: true, value: condition.value };
    if (spec.durationConditions && condition.value == null) source.system.value = { isValued: false, value: null };
    if (spec.suppressProne) {
        const proneUUID = await getConditionUUID("prone");
        source.system.rules = (source.system.rules ?? []).filter(rule =>
            !(rule.key === "GrantItem" && (rule.uuid === proneUUID || /\.Prone$/i.test(rule.uuid ?? ""))));
    }
    const [created] = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
    if (!created) throw new Error(`Could not create ${condition.name ?? condition.condition}.`);
    return created;
}

async function promptManagedAnimalForm(actor, spellName) {
    return await foundry.applications.api.DialogV2.prompt({
        window: { title: spellName },
        content: `<p>Choose a harmless animal appropriate to the area for ${escapeHTML(actor.name)}.</p><label>Animal form <input name="animalForm" type="text" value="Harmless animal" maxlength="100"></label>`,
        ok: { label: "Apply Transformation", callback: (_event, button) =>
            String(button.form.elements.animalForm.value ?? "").trim() || "Harmless animal" },
        rejectClose: false, modal: true
    });
}

function managedAttributeRules(actor, overrides) {
    const rules = Object.entries(overrides ?? {}).map(([attribute, value]) => ({
        key: "ActiveEffectLike", mode: "override", phase: "beforeDerived",
        path: `system.abilities.${attribute}.mod`, value
    }));
    if (actor.type !== "npc") return rules;
    // NPCs store explicit base statistics rather than constructing them from
    // attribute + proficiency. Adjust those bases by the attribute change.
    // Override from the unchanged source avoids stacking the same curse twice.
    const source = actor._source?.system ?? actor.toObject?.()?.system;
    if (!source) return rules;
    const addBase = (selector, base, attribute) => {
        const value = overrides?.[attribute];
        const original = Number(source.abilities?.[attribute]?.mod);
        if (value == null || !Number.isFinite(original) || !Number.isFinite(Number(base))) return;
        rules.push({ key: "AdjustModifier", selector, slug: "base", mode: "override", value: Number(base) + value - original });
    };
    addBase("perception", source.perception?.mod, "wis");
    for (const [save, data] of Object.entries(source.saves ?? {})) {
        addBase(save, data.value, data.attribute ?? ({ fortitude: "con", reflex: "dex", will: "wis" })[save]);
    }
    for (const [slug, skill] of Object.entries(source.skills ?? {})) {
        addBase(slug, skill.base, CONFIG.PF2E.skills?.[slug]?.attribute);
    }
    for (const item of actor.items ?? []) {
        if (item.type === "lore") addBase(item.slug, item.system.mod?.value, "int");
    }
    return rules;
}

function managedEffectDescription(castEntry, spec, formName = null) {
    const notes = [
        spec.notes,
        formName ? `Chosen animal: ${formName}.` : null,
        spec.sustained ? "Sustain this spell normally. When the caster stops sustaining, use End Sustained Spell to remove this cast's effects from every target." : null,
        spec.dailyPrep ? `Ends at ${castEntry.actor.name}'s next daily preparations. Use End Effect then.` : null
    ].filter(Boolean);
    return `<p>From <strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)}</strong>, cast by ${escapeHTML(castEntry.actor.name)}.</p>`
        + notes.map(note => `<p>${escapeHTML(note)}</p>`).join("");
}

function nextSpellTargetTurn(actor) {
    const combat = game.combat;
    const fallback = Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) + 6;
    if (!combat?.started) return { fallback };
    const turns = Array.from(combat.turns ?? combat.combatants?.contents ?? []);
    const matches = turns.map((turn, index) => ({ turn, index })).filter(row => row.turn.actor?.uuid === actor.uuid);
    const next = matches.find(row => row.index > Number(combat.turn)) ?? matches[0];
    if (!next) return { fallback };
    return { combatId: combat.id, round: Number(combat.round) + (next.index <= Number(combat.turn) ? 1 : 0),
        turn: next.index, combatantId: next.turn.id, fallback, createdStamp: spellFlowTurnStamp() };
}

function spellTargetTurnReached(boundary) {
    if (!boundary) return false;
    const combat = game.combat;
    if (boundary.combatId && combat?.started && combat.id === boundary.combatId) {
        if (spellFlowTurnStamp() === boundary.createdStamp) return false;
        const turns = Array.from(combat.turns ?? combat.combatants?.contents ?? []);
        const liveIndex = turns.findIndex(turn => turn.id === boundary.combatantId);
        const index = liveIndex < 0 ? boundary.turn : liveIndex;
        return Number(combat.round) > boundary.round || Number(combat.round) === boundary.round && Number(combat.turn) >= index;
    }
    return Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) >= boundary.fallback;
}

async function advanceTurnBoundSpellEffect(actor, effect) {
    return await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:phase`, async () => {
        if (!actor.items.get(effect.id)) return;
        const state = effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
        const next = state.spec.transition;
        if (!next) { await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect);return; }
        const spec = { ...deepClone(next), flowAreaCastId: state.spec.flowAreaCastId, areaExpiresAt: state.spec.areaExpiresAt };
        for (const id of state.independentIds ?? []) { const child = actor.items.get(id);if (child) await child.delete(); }
        await effect.update({ name: `${state.sourceSpellName}: ${spec.label}`, "system.rules": deepClone(spec.rules ?? []),
            "system.description.value": managedEffectDescription({ spell: { name: state.sourceSpellName }, actor: { name: state.sourceActorName } }, spec),
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.spec": spec,
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.independentIds": [],
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.turnBoundary": nextSpellTargetTurn(actor),
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.escapeReleased": true });
        await postManagedEffectControls(actor, effect);
    });
}

async function releaseSpellCapture(actor, effect) {
    const state = effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
    if (!state?.spec.escape) return;
    const related = managedEffectsOnActor(actor).filter(candidate => {
        const other = candidate.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
        return other.spec.flowAreaCastId === state.spec.flowAreaCastId && other.spec.escape;
    });
    for (const parent of related) {
        const data = parent.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
        if (data.spec.escape.mode === "binding") await finishManagedEffect(actor, parent);
        else {
            const kept = [];
            for (const id of data.independentIds ?? []) {
                const child = actor.items.get(id);
                if (child && normalizeSlug(child.slug ?? child.name) === data.spec.escape.condition) await child.delete();
                else if (child) kept.push(id);
            }
            await parent.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.independentIds": kept,
                "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.escapeReleased": true });
            await postManagedEffectControls(actor, parent);
        }
    }
}

async function resolveSpellEscape(actor, effect) {
    return await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:escape`, async () => {
        const state = effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
        if (!actor.items.get(effect.id) || !state?.spec.escape || state.escapeReleased) return;
        const lock = actor.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.escapeLock;
        if (lock && !spellTargetTurnReached(lock)) {
            ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: a critical failure prevents further Escape attempts until its next turn.`);return;
        }
        const options = await foundry.applications.api.DialogV2.prompt({ window: { title: `${actor.name} — Escape (DC ${state.dc})` },
            content: '<p>Spend an Escape action and choose its current multiple attack penalty.</p><label>Statistic <select name="statistic"><option value="">Best available</option><option value="unarmed">Unarmed</option><option value="athletics">Athletics</option><option value="acrobatics">Acrobatics</option></select></label><label>Penalty <select name="map"><option value="0">0</option><option value="-5">−5</option><option value="-10">−10</option></select></label>',
            ok: { label: "Roll Escape", callback: (_event, button) => ({ statistic: button.form.elements.statistic.value, map: Number(button.form.elements.map.value) }) },
            rejectClose: false, modal: true });
        if (!options) return;
        const action = game.pf2e?.actions?.get?.("escape");
        if (typeof action?.use !== "function") throw new Error("PF2e's Escape action is unavailable. Roll Escape normally, then use Release Capture on success.");
        const results = await action.use({ actors: [actor], statistic: options.statistic, multipleAttackPenalty: options.map,
            difficultyClass: { value: state.dc }, rollOptions: ["pf2e-auto-manual-trigger-save"], target: null });
        const result = results?.find(row => !row.actor || row.actor.uuid === actor.uuid);
        const degree = normalizeDegree(result?.outcome ?? result?.roll?.degreeOfSuccess ?? result?.roll?.options?.degreeOfSuccess);
        if (!degree) return;
        if (["success", "criticalSuccess"].includes(degree)) await releaseSpellCapture(actor, effect);
        if (degree === "criticalFailure") await actor.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.escapeLock": nextSpellTargetTurn(actor) });
        if (degree === "criticalSuccess" || degree === "criticalFailure") await ChatMessage.create({ speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor }),
            content: `<p>${escapeHTML(actor.name)}: ${degree === "criticalSuccess" ? "released; may Stride up to 5 feet (move the token manually)." : "cannot attempt to Escape again until its next turn."}</p>` });
    });
}

function managedPhaseExpired(effect) {
    const state = effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
    if (!state?.spec.phaseDuration || !state.spec.transition) return false;
    // Ask PF2e's own duration getter, preserving initiative tie handling and
    // the original effect start. The temporary document is never embedded.
    const source = effect.toObject();
    source.system.duration = { ...state.spec.phaseDuration, sustained: false };
    const timer = new effect.constructor(source, { parent: effect.actor });
    if (timer.remainingDuration && typeof timer.remainingDuration.expired === "boolean") return timer.remainingDuration.expired;
    // A non-native document boundary can still resolve time outside combat.
    if (!game.combat?.started) {
        return Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) >= Number(effect.system.start.value) + Number(state.spec.phaseDuration.value) * 6;
    }
    return false;
}

async function advanceManagedPhase(actor, effect) {
    return await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:phase`, async () => {
        if (!actor.items.get(effect.id)) return;
        const state = effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
        if (!state.spec.transition) return;
        const spec = deepClone(state.spec.transition);
        const rules = [...deepClone(spec.rules ?? []), ...managedAttributeRules(actor, spec.attributeOverrides)];
        for (const condition of spec.conditions ?? []) rules.push(await managedGrantRule(condition));
        const caster = { name: state.sourceActorName ?? "the caster" };
        await effect.update({ name: `${state.sourceSpellName}: ${spec.label}`,
            "system.rules": rules,
            "system.description.value": managedEffectDescription({ spell: { name: state.sourceSpellName }, actor: caster }, spec, state.formName),
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.spec": spec });
        // Keep the parent's duration/start and source key. A reload, repeated
        // button click, or a second expiry hook cannot duplicate the next phase.
        await postManagedEffectControls(actor, effect);
    });
}

async function endManagedSustainedCast(actor, effect) {
    const state = effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
    if (!state?.spec.sustained) return;
    const actors = [actor, ...(game.actors?.contents ?? [...(game.actors ?? [])]),
        ...(canvas.tokens?.placeables ?? []).map(token => token.actor).filter(Boolean)];
    for (const target of new Map(actors.map(candidate => [candidate.uuid, candidate])).values()) {
        for (const item of managedEffectsOnActor(target)) {
            const data = item.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
            if (data.sourceCastId === state.sourceCastId && data.sourceActor === state.sourceActor && data.spec.sustained) {
                await finishManagedEffect(target, item);
            }
        }
    }
}

async function confirmManagedRecoveryTrigger(actor, effect) {
    const state = effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
    const recovery = state.spec.recovery;
    if (!recovery?.confirmTrigger) return true;
    const combat = game.combat;
    const inCombat = combat?.started === true;
    if (inCombat && combat.combatant?.actor?.uuid !== actor.uuid) {
        ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: this recovery attempt requires its own turn.`);
        return false;
    }
    const turn = inCombat ? `${combat.id}:${combat.round}:${combat.combatant.id}` : null;
    if (turn && recovery.oncePerTurn && state.recoveryAttemptTurn === turn) {
        ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: this turn's recovery save has already been attempted.`);
        return false;
    }
    const choice = await promptSpellDecision(state.sourceSpellName,
        `Confirm that ${recovery.trigger}.`,
        [{ value: "no", label: "Not yet" }, { value: "yes", label: "Yes — attempt recovery" }]);
    if (choice !== "yes") return false;
    // The native manual-trigger roller skips its dialog, so a successful
    // attempt consumes this turn's opportunity even if the save fails.
    if (turn) await effect.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.recoveryAttemptTurn": turn });
    return true;
}


async function createManagedSpellEffect(actor, castEntry, originalSpec, sourceKey) {
    const previousBindings = originalSpec.exposureBinding ? managedEffectsOnActor(actor).filter(item => {
        const spec = item.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.spec;
        return spec.flowAreaCastId === originalSpec.flowAreaCastId && spec.exposureBinding && !item.isExpired;
    }) : [];
    const stronger = previousBindings.find(item => item.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.spec.exposureBinding >= originalSpec.exposureBinding);
    if (stronger) return stronger;
    const existing = managedEffectsOnActor(actor).find(item => item.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.sourceKey === sourceKey);
    if (existing) {
