        if (existing.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.initializing) {
            throw new Error(`${existing.name}: a persistent-damage application was interrupted. Review this effect manually; uncertain damage will not be repeated.`);
        }
        return existing;
    }
    const spec = deepClone(originalSpec);
    let formName = null;
    if (spec.formChoice) {
        formName = await promptManagedAnimalForm(actor, castEntry.spell.name);
        if (formName == null || formName === false) return null;
    }
    let relation = null;
    if (spec.relationship) {
        relation = spec.relationship.initial;
        if (spec.relationship.ifAlready) {
            const choice = await promptSpellDecision(castEntry.spell.name,
                `Was ${actor.name} already ${spec.relationship.ifAlready} toward ${castEntry.actor.name}?`,
                [{ value: "no", label: `No — becomes ${relation}` }, { value: "yes", label: `Yes — becomes ${spec.relationship.then}` }]);
            if (choice == null || choice === false) return null;
            if (choice === "yes") relation = spec.relationship.then;
        }
    }
    let environmentActive = false;
    if (spec.environment) {
        const choice = await promptSpellDecision(castEntry.spell.name,
            `Is ${actor.name} ${spec.environment.question}?`,
            [{ value: "no", label: "No" }, { value: "yes", label: "Yes" }]);
        if (choice == null || choice === false) return null;
        environmentActive = choice === "yes";
    }
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const rules = [...deepClone(spec.rules ?? []), ...managedAttributeRules(actor, spec.attributeOverrides)];
    const environmentOption = `spell-environment:${normalizeSlug(sourceKey)}`;
    const independent = spec.independent || spec.durationConditions;
    if (!independent) for (const condition of spec.conditions ?? []) rules.push(await managedGrantRule(condition));
    if (spec.environment) {
        rules.push({ key: "RollOption", domain: "all", option: environmentOption, value: environmentActive });
        rules.push(await managedGrantRule(spec.environment, { predicate: [environmentOption, `self:condition:${spec.environment.dependency}`] }));
    }
    const duration = spec.duration ?? { value: -1, unit: "unlimited", expiry: null };
    const label = formName ? `${spec.label}: ${formName}` : relation ? `${relation} toward ${castEntry.actor.name}` : spec.label;
    const source = {
        name: `${spell.name}: ${label}`, type: "effect", img: spell.img,
        flags: { pf2eAutoSpellResolution: { managedSpellEffect: {
            version: 1, sourceKey, sourceCastId: castEntry.castId, sourceSpell: spell.uuid,
            sourceSpellName: spell.name, sourceActor: castEntry.actor.uuid, sourceActorName: castEntry.actor.name, spellSource: deepClone(spell.toObject()),
            saveOriginSpell: (castEntry.saveOriginSpell ?? spell).uuid,
            saveItemRank: Number(castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank), dc: Number(castEntry.dc),
            spec, relation, formName, environmentActive, environmentOption, independentIds: [], lastTurnStamp: null,
            watchedConditionSeen: !!(spec.endsWithCondition && getExistingCondition(actor, spec.endsWithCondition)),
            initializing: !!spec.persistentDamage,
            turnBoundary: spec.untilTargetNextTurn ? nextSpellTargetTurn(actor) : null
        } } },
        system: {
            description: { value: managedEffectDescription(castEntry, spec, formName) },
            ...(spec.durationAnchor === "caster" ? { fromSpell: true, context: {
                origin: { actor: castEntry.actor.uuid, token: castEntry.actor.token?.uuid ?? null,
                    item: spell.uuid, spellcasting: null, rollOptions: [] },
                target: { actor: actor.uuid, token: actor.token?.uuid ?? null }, roll: null
            } } : {}),
            duration: { value: duration.value, unit: duration.unit,
                expiry: duration.expiry ?? (duration.unit === "unlimited" ? null : "turn-start"), sustained: spec.sustained === true },
            start: { value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0, initiative: game.combat?.combatant?.initiative ?? null },
            level: { value: Number(castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank) }, rules, tokenIcon: { show: true }, traits: { value: [] }
        }
    };
    const [parent] = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
    if (!parent) throw new Error(`Could not create ${source.name}.`);
    const children = [];
    try {
        // PF2e's preCreate can reset start initiative to the effect owner.
        // These spell-duration effects explicitly anchor to their caster.
        if (spec.durationAnchor === "caster") await parent.update({ "system.start": {
            value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0,
            initiative: getCombatantForActor(castEntry.actor)?.initiative ?? game.combat?.combatant?.initiative ?? null
        } });
        if (independent) for (const condition of spec.conditions ?? []) children.push(await createManagedCondition(actor, parent, spec, condition));
        // A floor is immutable, but a higher initial value can still be
        // reduced normally. Removing the curse removes only its own items.
        if (spec.extraCondition) children.push(await createManagedCondition(actor, parent, {}, spec.extraCondition));
        if (children.length) await parent.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.independentIds": children.map(item => item.id) });
        if (spec.persistentDamage) {
            const roll = await getCustomDamageRoll(castEntry, `persistent:${sourceKey}`, spec.persistentDamage, "Persistent Damage");
            const token = actor.token ?? actor.getActiveTokens?.()?.[0]?.document ?? null;
            const receipt = await applyDamageRollToActor(actor, token, roll, null, castEntry, "Applying linked persistent damage:", true);
            if (!receipt.receiptConfirmed) throw new Error("PF2e did not provide a matching persistent-damage receipt.");
            for (const id of receipt.persistentIds) {
                const child = actor.items.get(id);
                if (!child || child.type !== "condition" || !child.system.persistent) continue;
                await child.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedParent": parent.id });
                children.push(child);
            }
            await parent.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.independentIds": children.map(item => item.id),
                "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.initializing": false });
        }
    } catch (error) {
        // Retain evidence of uncertain native applications and the explicit
        // retry guard instead of erasing the parent and duplicating damage.
        if (spec.persistentDamage) throw error;
        for (const child of children) { try { await child.delete(); } catch (_) {} }
        try { await parent.delete(); } catch (_) {}
        throw error;
    }
    for (const previous of previousBindings) await finishManagedEffect(actor, previous);
    await postManagedEffectControls(actor, parent);
    return parent;
}

async function applyManagedSpellEffects(actor, castEntry, specs, keyPrefix) {
    for (let index = 0; index < (specs ?? []).length; index++) {
        const effect = await createManagedSpellEffect(actor, castEntry, specs[index], `${keyPrefix}:${index}`);
        if (!effect) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

async function postManagedEffectControls(actor, effect) {
    const state = effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
    const spec = state.spec;
    const button = (action, label) => `<button type="button" class="pf2e-auto-managed-control" data-actor-uuid="${escapeHTML(actor.uuid)}" data-effect-id="${escapeHTML(effect.id)}" data-effect-action="${action}">${escapeHTML(label)}</button>`;
    const recovery = spec.recovery;
    const content = `<div><strong>${escapeHTML(actor.name)} — ${escapeHTML(effect.name)}</strong>`
        + (recovery ? `<p>${escapeHTML(recovery.timing === "turn-end" ? `A ${recovery.saveType} save is offered at the end of each turn.` : `New save only when ${recovery.trigger}.`)}</p>`
            + button("save", recovery.timing === "manual" ? "Triggered Recovery Save" : "Recovery Save / Retry") : "")
        + (spec.environment ? `<p>Environment: ${escapeHTML(spec.environment.question)}. Initial answer: ${state.environmentActive ? "Yes" : "No"}.</p>` + button("environment", "Update Environment") : "")
        + (state.formName ? `<p>Chosen animal: ${escapeHTML(state.formName)}.</p>` : "")
        + (spec.transition ? button("phase", spec.untilTargetNextTurn ? "Apply Next Phase Now" : "Finish First Round / Apply Next Phase") : "")
        + (spec.escape && !state.escapeReleased ? button("escape", "Escape") + button("release", "Release Capture") : "")
        + (spec.endsOnHostility ? button("hostility", "Hostility / End Target") : "")
        + (spec.sustained ? `<p>Sustain normally. When the caster stops, end this cast on every affected target.</p>` + button("end-cast", "End Sustained Spell") : "")
        + (spec.dailyPrep ? `<p>End at the caster's next daily preparations.</p>` : "")
        + (spec.notes ? `<p>${escapeHTML(spec.notes)}</p>` : "")
        + button("end", spec.endLabel ?? (spec.curse ? "End Curse / Remove Effect" : spec.independent ? "Wake / End Effect" : "End Effect / Dismiss")) + "</div>";
    const existing = state.controlMessageId ? game.messages.get(state.controlMessageId) : null;
    if (existing) await existing.update({ content });
    else {
        const message = await ChatMessage.create({ speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor }), content });
        if (message?.id) await effect.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.controlMessageId": message.id });
    }
}

async function recoverManagedCast(state) {
    const card = game.messages.get(String(state.sourceCastId).split(":manual-trigger:")[0]);
    const recovered = findCastById(state.sourceCastId) ?? (card ? detectSpellCard(card) : null);
    let originSpell = recovered?.saveOriginSpell ?? (recovered ? resolveSpellAtCastRank(recovered) : null);
    let spell = null;
    try { spell = await fromUuid(state.sourceSpell); } catch (_) {}
    if (!originSpell?.spellcasting?.statistic) {
        try { originSpell = await fromUuid(state.saveOriginSpell); } catch (_) {}
    }
    if (!spell && originSpell?.uuid === state.sourceSpell) spell = originSpell;
    if (state.spellSource && originSpell?.actor) {
        spell = new originSpell.constructor(deepClone(state.spellSource), {
            parent: originSpell.actor, parentItem: originSpell.parentItem ?? null
        });
    }
    const saveItem = createContextualSaveItem(spell, originSpell, state.saveItemRank);
    return { spell: saveItem, actor: originSpell.actor, castRank: state.saveItemRank,
        dc: state.dc, saveOriginSpell: originSpell, castId: state.sourceCastId };
}

async function finishManagedEffect(actor, effect, { expired = false, alreadyDeleted = false } = {}) {
    const state = effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
    if (!state) return;
    const preserveSleep = expired && state.spec.transitionToSleep;
    for (const id of state.independentIds ?? []) {
        const child = actor.items.get(id);
        if (!child) continue;
        if (preserveSleep) {
            // Keep the *same* unconscious item and its ordinary condition
            // rules. Magical expiry does not wake a heightened sleeper.
            await child.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.-=managedParent": null,
                "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.-=managedExpiresAt": null,
                "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.-=managedSleepTransition": null });
        } else await child.delete();
    }
    if (!alreadyDeleted && actor.items.get(effect.id)) {
        await effect.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.independentIds": [],
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.endedByMacro": true });
        await effect.delete();
    }
    if (preserveSleep) await ChatMessage.create({ speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor }),
        content: `<p>${escapeHTML(actor.name)}: ${escapeHTML(state.sourceSpellName)} has ended. The creature is now sleeping normally and remains unconscious until awakened.</p>` });
}

async function reconcileManagedEffects(actor) {
    for (const effect of managedEffectsOnActor(actor)) {
        const state = effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
        if (effect.isExpired || effect.remainingDuration?.expired
            || (state.spec.areaExpiresAt != null && Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) >= state.spec.areaExpiresAt)) {
            await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:end`, () => finishManagedEffect(actor, effect, { expired: true }));
        } else if (state.initializing) {
            continue;
        } else if (state.spec.untilTargetNextTurn && spellTargetTurnReached(state.turnBoundary)) {
            await advanceTurnBoundSpellEffect(actor, effect);
        } else if (state.spec.exposureBinding && state.independentIds.length && state.independentIds.every(id => !actor.items.get(id))) {
            await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:end`, () => finishManagedEffect(actor, effect));
        } else if (state.spec.endsWithOwnCondition) {
            const present = (state.independentIds ?? []).some(id => {
                const child = actor.items.get(id);
                return child && normalizeSlug(child.slug ?? child.name) === state.spec.endsWithOwnCondition
                    && (child.system.value?.value == null || child.system.value.value > 0);
            });
            if (!present) await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:end`, () => finishManagedEffect(actor, effect));
        } else if (state.spec.endsWithCondition) {
            const present = !!getExistingCondition(actor, state.spec.endsWithCondition);
            if (present && !state.watchedConditionSeen) await effect.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.watchedConditionSeen": true });
            else if (!present && state.watchedConditionSeen) await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:end`, () => finishManagedEffect(actor, effect));
        } else if (state.spec.transition && managedPhaseExpired(effect)) {
            await advanceManagedPhase(actor, effect);
        } else if (state.spec.independent && state.independentIds.length
            && state.independentIds.every(id => !actor.items.get(id))) {
            await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:end`, () => finishManagedEffect(actor, effect));
        }
    }
    // Native expiry may delete a parent while the macro is not running.
    // Recover its own orphaned condition using the saved expiry, never by
    // deleting every condition of that type on the actor.
    for (const child of actor?.items?.filter?.(item => item.type === "condition" && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedParent) ?? []) {
        const flags = child.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution;
        if (actor.items.get(flags.managedParent)) continue;
        if (flags.managedSleepTransition && Number.isFinite(flags.managedExpiresAt)
            && Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) >= flags.managedExpiresAt) {
            await child.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.-=managedParent": null,
                "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.-=managedExpiresAt": null,
                "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.-=managedSleepTransition": null });
        } else await child.delete();
    }
}

async function resolveManagedRecovery(actor, token, effect, stamp = null) {
    return await runManagedEffectJob(`${actor.uuid}:${effect.id}:save`, async () => {
        if (!actor.items.get(effect.id)) return;
        const state = effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect;
        const recovery = state.spec.recovery;
        if (!recovery || (stamp && state.lastTurnStamp === stamp)) return;
        if (effect.isExpired || effect.remainingDuration?.expired) {
            await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect, { expired: true }); return;
        }
        const cast = await recoverManagedCast(state);
        if (!await confirmManagedRecoveryTrigger(actor, effect)) return;
        // Mark the offered turn before opening a roll dialog. The persistent
        // control card permits a retry if the roll is canceled or fails.
        if (stamp) await effect.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.lastTurnStamp": stamp });
        const result = await rollManualTriggerSave(actor, token, recovery.saveType, state.dc, cast.spell,
            `${state.sourceSpellName} — Recovery ${recovery.saveType} Save`, [],
            { originSpell: cast.saveOriginSpell, castRank: state.saveItemRank });
        if (!actor.items.get(effect.id)) return;
        const success = ["success", "criticalSuccess"].includes(result.degree);
        if (!success) return;
        if (recovery.mode === "end" || (result.degree === "criticalSuccess" && recovery.criticalSuccessEnds)) {
            await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect); return;
        }
        if (recovery.mode === "shorten") {
            const duration = effect.system.duration;
            if (duration.unit !== "rounds") throw new Error("Recovery requires a duration measured in rounds.");
            const next = Math.max(0, Number(duration.value) - Number(recovery.rounds));
            // Retain system.start; resetting it would incorrectly extend the
            // spell every time a successful save shortens it.
            await effect.update({ "system.duration.value": next });
            if (next <= 0 || effect.isExpired || effect.remainingDuration?.expired) await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect);
        }
    });
}

function queueManagedRecoveryAtTurnEnd(context) {
    if (!managedAutomationGM()) return;
    const { actor, token, stamp } = context;
    if (!actor) return;
    setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
            await reconcileManagedEffects(actor);
            for (const effect of managedEffectsOnActor(actor)) {
                if (effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.spec.recovery?.timing === "turn-end") {
                    await resolveManagedRecovery(actor, token, effect, stamp);
                }
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Recovery save failed:", error);
            ui.notifications.error(`${actor.name}: recovery save failed. Use Recovery Save / Retry on the effect's control card.`);
        }
    }, 0);
}

async function resolveAfterDamageSave(castEntry, group, target, actor, token) {
    const rule = group.followUpSave;
    if (!rule || target.followUpComplete) return true;
    if (rule.requiresHead && target.hasHead === undefined) {
        const answer = await promptSpellDecision(castEntry.spell.name, `Does ${actor.name} have a head?`,
            [{ value: "yes", label: "Yes — roll the second save" }, { value: "no", label: "No — no second save" }]);
        if (answer == null || answer === false) return false;
        target.hasHead = answer === "yes";
    }
    if (rule.requiresHead && !target.hasHead) { target.followUpComplete = true; target.followUpNote = "No head; second save does not apply"; return true; }
    if (!target.followUpDegree) {
        const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
        // Added save traits must be present for both immunity predicates and
        // degree adjustments; they are not retroactive traits on the damage.
        const data = deepClone(spell.toObject());
        data.system.traits.value = [...new Set([...(spell.system.traits.value ?? []), ...(rule.traits ?? [])])];
        const saveSpell = new spell.constructor(data, { parent: spell.actor, parentItem: spell.parentItem ?? null });
        const checkCast = { ...castEntry, spell: saveSpell, resolvedSpell: saveSpell, automationProfile: null };
        const immunity = getSpellEffectImmunity(actor, checkCast);
        if (immunity) { target.followUpComplete = true; target.followUpNote = `Second effect immune: ${immunity.label ?? immunity.type}`; return true; }
        const result = await rollManualTriggerSave(actor, token, rule.saveType, castEntry.dc, saveSpell,
            `${spell.name} — Second ${rule.saveType} Save`, rule.traits ?? [],
            { originSpell: castEntry.saveOriginSpell ?? spell, castRank: castEntry.castRank });
        target.followUpDegree = result.degree;
    }
    const failed = ["failure", "criticalFailure"].includes(target.followUpDegree);
    if (failed && rule.failureEffect) {
        const applied = await applyManagedSpellEffects(actor, castEntry, [rule.failureEffect],
            `${castEntry.castId}:${castEntry.cycle}:${target.key}:follow-up`);
        if (!applied) return false;
        target.effectsApplied = true;
    }
    if (failed && rule.decapitation && !target.decapitationResolved) {
        const answer = await promptSpellDecision(castEntry.spell.name,
            `${actor.name} loses one head. Does losing this head kill it? A creature that does not need a head survives; a creature with multiple heads usually survives unless this was its last head.`,
            [{ value: "lethal", label: "Lethal — apply death" }, { value: "survives", label: "Survives losing this head" }, { value: "manual", label: "Resolve anatomy manually" }]);
        if (answer == null || answer === false) return false;
        if (answer === "lethal") await applyTerminalResult(castEntry, target, actor, { kind: "death", label: "Decapitated", automatic: true });
        target.followUpNote = answer === "lethal" ? "Decapitated" : answer === "survives" ? "One head severed; survives" : "Decapitation requires GM resolution";
        target.decapitationResolved = true;
    }
    target.followUpComplete = true;
    return true;
}


async function applyTimedCondition(actor, spell, effect) {
    const conditionUUID = await getConditionUUID(effect.condition);
    if (!conditionUUID) {
        throw new Error(`Could not resolve PF2e condition "${effect.condition}".`);
    }

    const grantRule = {
        key: "GrantItem",
        uuid: conditionUUID,
        onDeleteActions: { grantee: "restrict" }
    };

    if (effect.value !== null && effect.value !== undefined) {
        grantRule.alterations = [{
            mode: "override",
            property: "badge-value",
            value: effect.value
        }];
    }

    const duration = effect.duration;
    const expiry = duration.expiry ?? (duration.unit === "rounds" ? "turn-start" : null);
    const targetCombatant = duration.anchor === "target-turn"
        ? getCombatantForActor(actor)
        : null;
    const startInitiative = targetCombatant?.initiative
        ?? game.combat?.combatant?.initiative
        ?? null;

    if (duration.anchor === "target-turn") {
        debug("TARGET-TURN DURATION INFERENCE:", {
            actor: actor.name,
            spell: spell.name,
            condition: effect.condition,
            duration,
            targetCombatant: targetCombatant?.name ?? null,
            startInitiative
        });
    }

    const source = {
        name: `${spell.name}: ${effect.name ?? effect.condition}`,
        type: "effect",
        img: spell.img ?? "systems/pf2e/icons/default-icons/effect.svg",
        system: {
            description: {
                value: `<p>Automatically inferred from <strong>${escapeHTML(spell.name)}</strong>.</p>`
                    + (effect.descriptionText ? String(effect.descriptionText).split(/\n+/)
                        .map(text => `<p>${escapeHTML(text)}</p>`).join("") : "")
            },
            duration: {
                value: Number(duration.value),
                unit: duration.unit,
                expiry,
                sustained: false
            },
            rules: [grantRule],
            start: {
                value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0,
                initiative: startInitiative
            },
            tokenIcon: { show: true },
            traits: { value: [] }
        }
    };

    await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
}

async function applyLinkedCondition(actor, spell, effect) {
    const conditionUUID = await getConditionUUID(effect.condition);
    if (!conditionUUID) {
        throw new Error(`Could not resolve PF2e condition "${effect.condition}".`);
    }

    const dependentOn = normalizeSlug(effect.whileCondition);
    if (!dependentOn) {
        throw new Error(`Linked condition ${effect.condition} has no dependency.`);
    }

    // The ordinary condition is applied before its linked rider. If the
    // dependency somehow failed to apply, do not create an orphaned grant.
    if (!getExistingCondition(actor, dependentOn)) {
        debug("Linked condition skipped; dependency is not present:", {
            actor: actor.name,
            linkedCondition: effect.condition,
            dependency: dependentOn
        });
        return;
    }

    const grantRule = {
        key: "GrantItem",
        uuid: conditionUUID,
        allowDuplicate: false,
        // The parent tracking effect is created only after the dependency has
        // already been applied. A normal grant keeps the condition removable
        // in the UI. Cascade ensures deleting either side also removes the
        // other, while our actor hooks remove the parent when the dependency
        // condition ends.
        onDeleteActions: { grantee: "cascade" }
    };

    if (effect.value !== null && effect.value !== undefined) {
        grantRule.alterations = [{
            mode: "override",
            property: "badge-value",
            value: effect.value
        }];
    }

    const source = {
        name: `${spell.name}: ${effect.name ?? effect.condition} while ${effect.whileConditionName ?? dependentOn}`,
        type: "effect",
        img: spell.img ?? "systems/pf2e/icons/default-icons/effect.svg",
        flags: {
            pf2eAutoSpellResolution: {
                linkedCondition: true,
                dependency: dependentOn,
                grantedCondition: effect.condition,
                sourceSpell: spell.uuid ?? null
            }
        },
        system: {
            description: {
                value:
                    `<p>Automatically inferred from <strong>${escapeHTML(spell.name)}</strong>. `
                    + `${escapeHTML(effect.name ?? effect.condition)} remains while `
                    + `${escapeHTML(effect.whileConditionName ?? dependentOn)} is present.</p>`
            },
            duration: {
                value: -1,
                unit: "unlimited",
                expiry: null,
                sustained: false
            },
            rules: [grantRule],
            start: {
                value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0,
                initiative: game.combat?.combatant?.initiative ?? null
            },
            tokenIcon: { show: true },
            traits: { value: [] }
        }
    };

    await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
}

const LINKED_EFFECT_CLEANUP_TIMERS = new Map();

async function cleanupExpiredLinkedConditionEffects(actor) {
    if (!actor) return;

    const linkedEffects = actor.items.filter(item =>
        item.type === "effect"
        && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.linkedCondition === true
    );

    for (const effect of linkedEffects) {
        const dependency = normalizeSlug(
            effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.dependency
        );

        if (!dependency) continue;

        // As soon as the dependency actually disappears, remove the parent
        // effect too. This prevents the linked condition from reappearing if
        // the actor becomes Frightened (or otherwise satisfies the predicate)
        // again later from an unrelated source.
        if (!getExistingCondition(actor, dependency)) {
            try {
                await effect.delete();
                debug("Removed completed linked-condition effect:", {
                    actor: actor.name,
                    effect: effect.name,
                    dependency
                });
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn(
                    "PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not remove completed linked-condition effect:",
                    effect,
                    error
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

function scheduleLinkedConditionCleanup(actor) {
    if (!actor) return;

    const key = actor.uuid ?? actor.id;
    if (!key) return;

    const existing = LINKED_EFFECT_CLEANUP_TIMERS.get(key);
    if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);

    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
        LINKED_EFFECT_CLEANUP_TIMERS.delete(key);
        await cleanupExpiredLinkedConditionEffects(actor);
    }, 75);

    LINKED_EFFECT_CLEANUP_TIMERS.set(key, timer);
}

async function applyOutcomeConditions(actor, spell, conditions, linkedConditions = []) {
    // Apply ordinary conditions first so linked predicates can immediately see them.
    for (const effect of conditions ?? []) {
        const recipient = effect.recipient === "caster" ? spell.actor : actor;
        if (!recipient) throw new Error("The condition's caster could not be resolved.");
        if (effect.duration) {
            await applyTimedCondition(recipient, spell, effect);
        } else {
            await applyCondition(recipient, effect.condition, effect.value);
        }
    }

    for (const effect of linkedConditions ?? []) {
        await applyLinkedCondition(actor, spell, effect);
    }
}

function degreeToNumber(degree) {
    return {
        criticalFailure: 0,
        failure: 1,
        success: 2,
        criticalSuccess: 3
    }[normalizeDegree(degree)] ?? null;
}

function getActorTokenUUID(actor) {
    try {
        const active = actor?.getActiveTokens?.(true, true)?.[0] ?? actor?.getActiveTokens?.()?.[0] ?? null;
        return active?.document?.uuid ?? active?.uuid ?? null;
    } catch (_) {
        return null;
    }
}

async function loadNativeSpellEffectTemplate(effectSpec) {
    const uuid = effectSpec?.uuid ?? null;
    if (!uuid) return { template: null, method: null };

    let template = null;
    let method = null;
    let fromUuidError = null;

    try {
        template = await fromUuid(uuid);
        if (template) method = "fromUuid";
    } catch (error) {
        fromUuidError = error;
    }

    // Direct pack fallback. This mirrors the UUID but avoids depending on the
    // global UUID resolver if a module/version combination interferes with it.
    if (!template && /^Compendium\./i.test(uuid)) {
        try {
            const withoutPrefix = uuid.replace(/^Compendium\./i, "");
            const marker = ".Item.";
            const markerIndex = withoutPrefix.lastIndexOf(marker);
            if (markerIndex > 0) {
                const packId = withoutPrefix.slice(0, markerIndex);
                const documentId = withoutPrefix.slice(markerIndex + marker.length);
                const pack = game.packs.get(packId);
                if (pack) {
                    template = await pack.getDocument(documentId);
                    if (template) method = "pack.getDocument";
                }
            }
        } catch (error) {
            debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT PACK FALLBACK ERROR:", {
                uuid,
                message: error?.message ?? String(error)
            });
        }
    }

    if (!template && fromUuidError) {
        debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT UUID LOAD ERROR:", {
            uuid,
            message: fromUuidError?.message ?? String(fromUuidError)
        });
    }

    return { template, method };
}

function buildNativeEffectOriginContext(actor, spell, castEntry, effectSpec) {
    const numericDegree = degreeToNumber(effectSpec?.degree);
    const originActor = castEntry?.actor ?? spell?.actor ?? null;

    return {
        target: actor?.uuid ?? null,
        origin: {
            actor: originActor?.uuid ?? null,
            token: getActorTokenUUID(originActor),
            item: spell?.uuid ?? null,
            spellcasting: null,
            rollOptions: []
        },
        roll: numericDegree === null
            ? null
            : { degreeOfSuccess: numericDegree, total: null }
    };
}


function degreeFromNativeChoice(choice) {
    const texts = [];
    const value = choice?.value;
    const label = choice?.label;

    if (typeof label === "string") {
        texts.push(label);
        try {
            const localized = game.i18n?.localize?.(label);
            if (localized && localized !== label) texts.push(localized);
        } catch (_) {}
    }

    if (typeof value === "string") {
        texts.push(value);
        const normalized = normalizeDegree(value);
        if (normalized) return normalized;
        try {
            const localized = game.i18n?.localize?.(value);
            if (localized && localized !== value) texts.push(localized);
        } catch (_) {}
    } else if (typeof value === "number") {
        const normalized = normalizeDegree(value);
        if (normalized) return normalized;
    } else if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {
        try { texts.push(JSON.stringify(value)); } catch (_) {}
    }

    for (const text of texts) {
        const byLabel = degreeFromEffectLabel(text);
        if (byLabel) return byLabel;

        const normalized = normalizeDegree(text);
        if (normalized) return normalized;
    }

    return null;
}

function defaultChoiceSetFlag(source, rule) {
    if (rule?.flag) return String(rule.flag);

    // PF2e derives an omitted ChoiceSet flag from the item's slug/name. This
    // fallback mirrors that shape closely enough for effect items, while most
    // outcome ChoiceSets use an explicit flag anyway.
    const base = normalizeSlug(source?.system?.slug ?? source?.name ?? "choice");
    return base.replace(/-([a-z0-9])/g, (_, c) => c.toUpperCase());
}

function preselectNativeEffectDegreeChoices(source, degree, { spellName = null } = {}) {
    const wanted = normalizeDegree(degree);
    const rules = Array.isArray(source?.system?.rules) ? source.system.rules : [];
    const selections = [];
    if (!wanted || !rules.length) return selections;

    for (const rule of rules) {
        if (String(rule?.key ?? "").toLowerCase() !== "choiceset") continue;
        if (!Array.isArray(rule.choices)) continue;

        const inspectedChoices = rule.choices
            .map(choice => ({ choice, degree: degreeFromNativeChoice(choice) }));
        const candidates = inspectedChoices.filter(entry => entry.degree === wanted);

        if (candidates.length !== 1) {
            debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT CHOICESET NOT AUTO-RESOLVED:", {
                spell: spellName,
                effect: source?.name ?? null,
                degree: wanted,
                flag: rule?.flag ?? null,
                choices: inspectedChoices.map(entry => ({
                    label: entry.choice?.label ?? null,
                    value: entry.choice?.value ?? null,
                    inferredDegree: entry.degree
                }))
            });
            continue;
        }

        const flag = defaultChoiceSetFlag(source, rule);
        const selected = foundry.utils.deepClone(candidates[0].choice.value);

        // PF2e ChoiceSet preCreate does NOT use the eventual rulesSelections
        // flag as its preselection input. It reads the ChoiceSet rule's own
        // `selection` field, validates that value against the inflated choices,
        // and only then writes the rulesSelections flag itself. Setting the
        // rule source directly therefore suppresses the prompt in the same way
        // a preselected GrantItem does, while still letting PF2e perform all of
        // its normal ChoiceSet initialization.
        rule.selection = foundry.utils.deepClone(selected);

        selections.push({
            flag,
            value: selected,
            degree: wanted,
            label: candidates[0].choice.label ?? null,
            ruleSelectionSet: true
        });
    }

    if (selections.length) {
        debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT CHOICESET PRESELECTED:", {
            spell: spellName,
            effect: source?.name ?? null,
            degree: wanted,
            selections
        });
    }

    return selections;
}

async function applyNativeSpellEffect(actor, spell, castEntry, effectSpec) {
    if (!actor || !effectSpec?.uuid) return false;

    debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT APPLICATION ATTEMPT:", {
        spell: spell?.name ?? null,
        target: actor.name,
        degree: effectSpec.degree ?? null,
        inheritedFrom: effectSpec.inheritedFrom ?? null,
        uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
        label: effectSpec.label ?? null
    });

    const { template, method } = await loadNativeSpellEffectTemplate(effectSpec);

    debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT TEMPLATE RESOLVED:", {
        spell: spell?.name ?? null,
        target: actor.name,
        uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
        method,
        found: !!template,
        name: template?.name ?? null,
        type: template?.type ?? null,
        documentName: template?.documentName ?? null,
        actorAllowsEffects: actor?.allowedItemTypes?.includes?.("effect") ?? null
    });

    const isEffect = template
        && (template.type === "effect" || template.isOfType?.("effect"));

    if (!isEffect) {
        debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT TEMPLATE INVALID:", {
            spell: spell?.name ?? null,
            target: actor.name,
            uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
            resolvedType: template?.type ?? null
        });
        return false;
    }

    // v8.21 intentionally mirrors a normal PF2e compendium drag as closely as
    // possible. Create the native Effect first with its compendium source data
    // intact. Do NOT inject core flags or a custom spell context before
    // creation: PF2e's own _preCreate lifecycle gets first control.
    let source = foundry.utils.deepClone(template.toObject());
    delete source._id;
    delete source.folder;
    delete source.sort;
    delete source.ownership;

    source._stats ??= {};
    source._stats.compendiumSource ??= template?._stats?.compendiumSource ?? effectSpec.uuid;
    source._stats.duplicateSource = template.uuid ?? effectSpec.uuid;

    if (source.system?.level && Number.isFinite(Number(castEntry?.castRank))) {
        source.system.level.value = Number(castEntry.castRank);
    }

    const preselectedChoices = preselectNativeEffectDegreeChoices(
        source,
        effectSpec.degree,
        { spellName: spell?.name ?? null }
    );

    // Our own namespace has already been accepted by the other tracker/effect
    // items in this macro. Leave Foundry's reserved `core` flags untouched.
    source.flags ??= {};
    source.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution = {
        ...(source.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution ?? {}),
        nativeSpellEffect: true,
        sourceSpell: spell?.uuid ?? null,
        castId: castEntry?.castId ?? null,
        degree: effectSpec.degree ?? null,
        inheritedFrom: effectSpec.inheritedFrom ?? null
    };

    let effect = null;
    let firstError = null;

    try {
        const created = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source], { keepId: false });
        effect = created?.[0] ?? null;
    } catch (error) {
        firstError = error;
        debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT CREATE ERROR:", {
            spell: spell?.name ?? null,
            target: actor.name,
            uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
            stage: "drag-style-clone",
            name: error?.name ?? null,
            message: error?.message ?? String(error),
            stack: error?.stack ?? null
        });
    }

    // Absolute-minimum fallback: if our metadata happened to upset a module or
    // system migration, try the pristine compendium document just as a simple
    // manual macro would. This is deliberately boring because boring is good
    // here: PF2e already knows how to initialize its own Effect items.
    if (!effect) {
        try {
            const pristine = foundry.utils.deepClone(template.toObject());
            delete pristine._id;
            delete pristine.folder;
            delete pristine.sort;
            delete pristine.ownership;

            preselectNativeEffectDegreeChoices(
                pristine,
                effectSpec.degree,
                { spellName: spell?.name ?? null }
            );

            const created = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [pristine], { keepId: false });
            effect = created?.[0] ?? null;

            debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT PRISTINE FALLBACK:", {
                spell: spell?.name ?? null,
                target: actor.name,
                uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
                created: !!effect,
                originalError: firstError?.message ?? null
            });
        } catch (error) {
            debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT PRISTINE CREATE ERROR:", {
                spell: spell?.name ?? null,
                target: actor.name,
                uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
                name: error?.name ?? null,
                message: error?.message ?? String(error),
                stack: error?.stack ?? null
            });
            return false;
        }
    }

    if (!effect) {
        debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT CREATE RETURNED NO DOCUMENT:", {
            spell: spell?.name ?? null,
            target: actor.name,
            uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
            existingItemsWithName: actor.items?.filter?.(i => i.name === template.name)?.map?.(i => ({
                id: i.id,
                type: i.type,
                sourceId: i.sourceId ?? null
            })) ?? []
        });
        return false;
    }

    // v8.22: Do not update a newly embedded native PF2e Effect merely to add
    // resolver metadata/context. PF2e 8.5.0 can accept the compendium Effect
    // during creation while retaining a legacy/null context.target.actor, but
    // a subsequent Item#update revalidates the entire Effect model and rejects
    // that pre-existing null field. The normal drag-style path already received
    // our resolver flag and cast-rank adjustment in `source` before creation, so
    // no post-create mutation is necessary. The pristine fallback intentionally
    // remains pristine: a working native Effect is more important than optional
    // provenance metadata.
    debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT POST-CREATE UPDATE SKIPPED:", {
        spell: spell?.name ?? null,
        target: actor.name,
        effect: effect.name,
        uuid: effectSpec.uuid,
        reason: "avoid PF2e Effect context revalidation"
    });

    debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT APPLIED:", {
        spell: spell?.name ?? null,
        target: actor.name,
        degree: effectSpec.degree ?? null,
        inheritedFrom: effectSpec.inheritedFrom ?? null,
        sourceUUID: effectSpec.uuid,
        effect: effect.name,
        embeddedId: effect.id,
        duration: effect.system?.duration ?? null,
        rules: effect.system?.rules?.length ?? 0,
        sourceId: effect.sourceId ?? null,
        preselectedChoices
    });

    return true;
}

async function applyNativeSpellEffects(actor, spell, castEntry, nativeEffects = []) {
    let applied = 0;
    for (const effectSpec of nativeEffects ?? []) {
        if (await applyNativeSpellEffect(actor, spell, castEntry, effectSpec)) applied++;
    }
    return applied;
}


// =================================================================
// ONGOING SAVE STATE
// =================================================================

async function createIndependentCondition(actor, spell, effect, sourceTag) {
    const conditionUUID = await getConditionUUID(effect.condition);
    if (!conditionUUID) throw new Error(`Could not resolve PF2e condition "${effect.condition}".`);

    const template = await fromUuid(conditionUUID);
    if (!template) throw new Error(`Could not load PF2e condition "${effect.condition}".`);

    const source = template.toObject();
    delete source._id;
    source.flags ??= {};
    source.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution = {
        ...(source.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution ?? {}),
        independentCondition: true,
        sourceTag,
        sourceSpell: spell.uuid ?? null
    };

    if (effect.value !== null && effect.value !== undefined) {
        source.system ??= {};
        source.system.value ??= {};
        source.system.value.isValued = true;
        source.system.value.value = Number(effect.value);
    }

    const [created] = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
    return created ?? null;
}

async function setIndependentConditionValue(condition, value) {
    if (!condition) return null;
    const next = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(next) || next <= 0) {
        await condition.delete();
        return null;
    }
    await condition.update({
        "system.value.isValued": true,
        "system.value.value": next
    });
    return condition;
}

async function createOngoingSaveTracker(actor, spell, castEntry, ongoingSave, initialEffect) {
    if (!ongoingSave?.trackedCondition) return null;

    const existing = actor.items.find(item =>
        item.type === "effect"
        && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave?.sourceCastId === castEntry.castId
        && item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave?.trackedCondition === ongoingSave.trackedCondition
    );
    if (existing) return existing;

    const initialValue = Number(initialEffect?.value ?? 1) || 1;
    const tracked = await createIndependentCondition(
        actor,
        spell,
        { condition: ongoingSave.trackedCondition, value: initialValue },
        `${castEntry.castId}:${ongoingSave.trackedCondition}`
    );
    if (!tracked) throw new Error(`Could not create tracked ${ongoingSave.trackedCondition} condition.`);

    const source = {
        name: `${spell.name}: Ongoing ${ongoingSave.saveType.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase())} Save`,
        type: "effect",
        img: spell.img ?? "systems/pf2e/icons/default-icons/effect.svg",
        flags: {
            pf2eAutoSpellResolution: {
                ongoingSave: {
                    version: 1,
                    sourceCastId: castEntry.castId,
                    sourceSpell: spell.uuid ?? null,
                    sourceSpellName: spell.name,
                    saveOriginSpell: (castEntry.saveOriginSpell ?? spell).uuid ?? null,
                    saveItemRank: Number(castEntry.castRank ?? spell.rank ?? getSpellBaseRank(spell)),
                    dc: Number(castEntry.dc),
                    saveType: ongoingSave.saveType,
                    traits: [...(ongoingSave.traits ?? [])],
                    trackedCondition: ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
                    trackedConditionName: ongoingSave.trackedConditionName ?? ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
                    trackedConditionId: tracked.id,
                    deltas: deepClone(ongoingSave.deltas ?? {}),
                    terminal: deepClone(ongoingSave.terminal ?? null),
                    lastTurnStamp: null
                }
            }
        },
        system: {
            description: {
                value: `<p>Automatically inferred from <strong>${escapeHTML(spell.name)}</strong>. The target attempts an ongoing ${escapeHTML(ongoingSave.saveType)} save at the end of each of its turns.</p>`
            },
            duration: { value: -1, unit: "unlimited", expiry: null, sustained: false },
            rules: [],
            start: {
                value: game.time?.worldTime ?? 0,
                initiative: game.combat?.combatant?.initiative ?? null
            },
            tokenIcon: { show: true },
            traits: { value: [] }
        }
    };

    try {
        const [tracker] = await actor.createEmbeddedDocuments("Item", [source]);
        debug("ONGOING SAVE STATE CREATED:", {
            spell: spell.name,
            target: actor.name,
            saveType: ongoingSave.saveType,
            dc: castEntry.dc,
            trackedCondition: ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
            initialValue,
            traits: ongoingSave.traits ?? [],
            terminal: ongoingSave.terminal ?? null
        });
        return tracker ?? null;
    } catch (error) {
        try { await tracked.delete(); } catch (_) {}
        throw error;
    }
}

function getOngoingSaveTrackers(actor) {
    return actor?.items?.filter?.(item =>
        item.type === "effect"
        && !!item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave
    ) ?? [];
}

function getEndingCombatantContext(combat) {
    const combatant = combat?.combatant ?? null;
    if (!combatant) {
        return {
            combatant: null,
            actor: null,
            token: null,
            stamp: null
        };
    }

    const token = combatant.token
        ?? combat?.scene?.tokens?.get?.(combatant.tokenId)
        ?? canvas?.scene?.tokens?.get?.(combatant.tokenId)
        ?? null;

    return {
        combatant,
        actor: combatant.actor ?? token?.actor ?? null,
        token,
        stamp: `${combat?.id ?? "combat"}:${combat?.round ?? 0}:${combat?.turn ?? -1}:${combatant.id}`
    };
}

async function removeOngoingTrackerAndCondition(actor, tracker, condition = null) {
    const rule = tracker?.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave;
    const tracked = condition ?? (rule?.trackedConditionId ? actor.items.get(rule.trackedConditionId) : null);
    try { if (tracked?.parent) await tracked.delete(); } catch (_) {}
    try { if (tracker?.parent) await tracker.delete(); } catch (_) {}
}

async function resolveOngoingSaveTracker(actor, token, tracker, combat, turnContext) {
    const rule = tracker.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave;
    if (!rule) return;

    const condition = actor.items.get(rule.trackedConditionId);
    if (!condition) {
        try { await tracker.delete(); } catch (_) {}
        return;
    }

    const turnStamp = turnContext?.stamp
        ?? `${combat?.id ?? "combat"}:${combat?.round ?? 0}:${combat?.turn ?? -1}`;
    if (rule.lastTurnStamp === turnStamp) return;
    const statistic = actor.saves?.[rule.saveType];
    if (!statistic || typeof statistic.roll !== "function") {
        ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: could not roll ongoing ${rule.saveType} save.`);
        return;
    }

    let spell = null;
    if (rule.sourceSpell) {
        try { spell = await fromUuid(rule.sourceSpell); } catch (_) {}
    }

    let originSpell = null;
    if (rule.saveOriginSpell) {
        try { originSpell = await fromUuid(rule.saveOriginSpell); } catch (_) {}
    }
    if (!originSpell?.spellcasting?.statistic && spell?.spellcasting?.statistic) originSpell = spell;

    // v8.29 referenced-spell trackers only stored the compendium UUID. Recover
    // their caster from their existing source cast ID without guessing an actor.
    if (!originSpell?.spellcasting?.statistic && rule.sourceCastId) {
        const parentCastId = String(rule.sourceCastId).split(":manual-trigger:")[0];
        const castMessage = game.messages.get(parentCastId);
        const sourceCast = findCastById(parentCastId) ?? (castMessage ? detectSpellCard(castMessage) : null);
        if (sourceCast) originSpell = sourceCast.saveOriginSpell ?? resolveSpellAtCastRank(sourceCast);
    }
    if (!spell && originSpell?.uuid === rule.sourceSpell) spell = originSpell;

    let saveItem;
    try {
        saveItem = createContextualSaveItem(spell, originSpell, rule.saveItemRank ?? spell?.rank);
    } catch (error) {
        console.warn("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Ongoing save origin unavailable:", error);
        ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: ${rule.sourceSpellName ?? "Spell"} has no recoverable caster spell. Resolve this ongoing save manually.`);
        return;
    }
    await tracker.update({
        "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.ongoingSave.lastTurnStamp": turnStamp
    });

    const saveName = String(rule.saveType).replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase());
    const roll = await statistic.roll({
        dc: Number(rule.dc),
        token: token ?? undefined,
        item: saveItem,
        origin: originSpell.actor,
        label: `${rule.sourceSpellName ?? "Spell"} — Ongoing ${saveName} Save`,
        title: `${rule.sourceSpellName ?? "Spell"} — Ongoing ${saveName} Save`,
        extraRollOptions: [
            "pf2e-auto-ongoing-save",
            `pf2e-auto-ongoing-save:${normalizeSlug(rule.sourceSpellName ?? "spell")}`,
            ...(rule.traits ?? []).map(trait => `item:trait:${normalizeSlug(trait)}`)
        ],
        traits: [...(rule.traits ?? [])],
        skipDialog: false
    });
    if (!roll) return;

    const degree = normalizeDegree(roll.degreeOfSuccess ?? roll.options?.degreeOfSuccess);
    if (!degree) {
        ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: ongoing save degree could not be read.`);
        return;
    }

    const delta = Number(rule.deltas?.[degree] ?? 0);
    const before = getConditionValue(condition);
    const after = Math.max(0, before + delta);

    debug("ONGOING SAVE RESULT:", {
        spell: rule.sourceSpellName,
        target: actor.name,
        saveType: rule.saveType,
        dc: rule.dc,
        degree,
        before,
        delta,
        after,
        trackedCondition: rule.trackedCondition,
        traits: rule.traits ?? []
    });

    if (rule.terminal?.threshold === "unable-to-act" && after >= 3) {
        await applyCondition(actor, rule.terminal.condition, null);
        await removeOngoingTrackerAndCondition(actor, tracker, condition);
        debug("ONGOING SAVE TERMINAL CONDITION APPLIED:", {
            spell: rule.sourceSpellName,
            target: actor.name,
            condition: rule.terminal.condition
        });
        return;
    }

    if (after <= 0) {
        await removeOngoingTrackerAndCondition(actor, tracker, condition);
        debug("ONGOING SAVE STATE ENDED:", {
            spell: rule.sourceSpellName,
            target: actor.name,
            trackedCondition: rule.trackedCondition
        });
        return;
    }

    await setIndependentConditionValue(condition, after);
}

function queueOngoingSavesAtCombatProgression(combat, updateData, updateOptions, hookName) {
    if (!game.user.isGM) return;

    const direction = Number(updateOptions?.direction ?? 0);
    if (direction <= 0) {
        debug("ONGOING COMBAT PROGRESSION ignored (not forward):", {
            hook: hookName,
            combat: combat?.id ?? null,
            direction,
            round: combat?.round ?? null,
            turn: combat?.turn ?? null,
            updateData,
            updateOptions
        });
        return;
    }

    // Both hooks fire BEFORE the Combat document updates. At this moment
    // combat.combatant is still the creature whose turn is ending.
    // combatTurn handles ordinary next-turn movement. combatRound catches the
    // final combatant -> first combatant of the next round transition.
    const context = getEndingCombatantContext(combat);
    const { actor, token, combatant, stamp } = context;

    if (!actor || !combatant) {
        debug("ONGOING COMBAT PROGRESSION: no ending actor found", {
            hook: hookName,
            combat: combat?.id ?? null,
            direction,
            round: combat?.round ?? null,
            turn: combat?.turn ?? null,
            updateData
        });
        return;
    }

    queueManagedRecoveryAtTurnEnd(context);
    const trackers = [...getOngoingSaveTrackers(actor)];

    debug("ONGOING COMBAT END TURN:", {
        hook: hookName,
        combat: combat?.id ?? null,
        endingActor: actor.name,
        endingCombatantId: combatant.id,
        endingTokenId: combatant.tokenId ?? null,
        round: combat?.round ?? null,
        turn: combat?.turn ?? null,
        nextRound: updateData?.round ?? null,
        nextTurn: updateData?.turn ?? null,
        direction,
        trackerCount: trackers.length,
        turnStamp: stamp
    });

    if (!trackers.length) return;

    // Hooks are not awaited. Queue resolution so the normal combat update can
    // continue while retaining the actor/token/stamp captured before it moves.
    setTimeout(async () => {
        for (const tracker of trackers) {
            if (!actor.items.get(tracker.id)) continue;

            try {
                await resolveOngoingSaveTracker(actor, token, tracker, combat, context);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(
                    "PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Ongoing save resolution failed:",
                    error,
                    tracker
                );
                ui.notifications.error(`${tracker.name}: ongoing save resolution failed.`);
            }
        }
    }, 0);
}

// =================================================================
// PF2e DAMAGE ROLL SERIALIZATION
// =================================================================

function getPF2eDamageRollClass() {
    const DamageRoll = (CONFIG.Dice?.rolls ?? []).find(cls => cls?.name === "DamageRoll");
    if (!DamageRoll) throw new Error("PF2e DamageRoll class could not be found.");
    return DamageRoll;
}

function serializeDamageRoll(roll) {
    return JSON.stringify(roll.toJSON());
}

function restoreDamageRoll(rollJSON) {
    if (!rollJSON) return null;
    return getPF2eDamageRollClass().fromData(JSON.parse(rollJSON));
}

async function getDamageFromSpellItem(castEntry) {
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);

    const tableDamage = castEntry.automationProfile?.sharedDamageSpec;
    if (tableDamage) {
        const payload = buildTypedDamageFormula(tableDamage);
        if (!payload) throw new Error("The selected table row has no damage formula.");
        const formula = payload.trim().startsWith("{") ? payload : `{${payload}}`;
        const DamageRoll = getPF2eDamageRollClass();
        return {
            spell, roll: new DamageRoll(formula, getOutcomeDamageRollData(castEntry)),
            context: { options: getSpellDamageRollOptions(castEntry) }
        };
    }

    if (typeof spell.getDamage !== "function") {
        throw new Error(`${spell.name} does not expose spell.getDamage().`);
    }

    const result = await spell.getDamage({ skipDialog: true });
    if (!result) throw new Error(`PF2e returned no damage data for ${spell.name}.`);

    const roll = result.template?.damage?.roll;
    if (!roll) throw new Error(`PF2e did not produce a DamageRoll for ${spell.name}.`);

    debug("PF2e SPELL DAMAGE READ:", {
        spell: spell.name,
        castRank: castEntry.castRank,
        resolvedRank: spell.rank,
        formula: roll.formula,
        save: spell.system?.defense?.save,
        rawDamage: spell.system?.damage,
        heightening: spell.system?.heightening
    });

    return { spell, roll, context: result.context ?? null };
}

async function postSharedDamageMessage(castEntry, roll, { restored = false } = {}) {
    // The parent table card contains all exposure controls and formulas.
    // Keep the evaluated native roll in memory without separate damage cards.
    if (castEntry.tableParentCastId) return null;
    const damageMessage = await roll.toMessage({
        speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
        flavor:
            `<strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)}</strong> — Save Damage`
            + (restored ? `<br><small>Restored original damage roll</small>` : "")
    });

    castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId = damageMessage?.id ?? null;
    return damageMessage;
}

async function ensureSharedDamage(castEntry) {
    if (castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId && game.messages.get(castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId)) {
        return castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId;
    }

    if (castEntry.sharedDamageRolling) {
        while (castEntry.sharedDamageRolling) {
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 25));
        }
        return castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId;
    }

    castEntry.sharedDamageRolling = true;

    try {
        if (castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON) {
            const restoredRoll = restoreDamageRoll(castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON);
            if (!restoredRoll) throw new Error("Stored PF2e damage roll could not be reconstructed.");

            castEntry.sharedDamageRolled = true;
            castEntry.sharedDamageFormula = restoredRoll.formula;
            await postSharedDamageMessage(castEntry, restoredRoll, { restored: true });

            debug("RESTORED SAME SHARED DAMAGE ROLL:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                cycle: castEntry.cycle,
                formula: restoredRoll.formula,
                total: restoredRoll.total,
                damageMessageId: castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId
            });

            return castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId;
        }

        const prepared = await getDamageFromSpellItem(castEntry);
        const roll = prepared.roll;
        await roll.evaluate();

        castEntry.sharedDamageRollOptions = Array.from(prepared.context?.options ?? []);
        castEntry.sharedDamageFormula = roll.formula;
        castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON = serializeDamageRoll(roll);
        castEntry.sharedDamageRolled = true;

        await postSharedDamageMessage(castEntry, roll);

        debug("NEW SHARED PF2e DAMAGE ROLL:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            cycle: castEntry.cycle,
            castRank: castEntry.castRank,
            formula: roll.formula,
            total: roll.total,
            damageMessageId: castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId
        });

        return castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId;
    } finally {
        castEntry.sharedDamageRolling = false;
    }
}

async function getSharedDamageRoll(castEntry) {
    await ensureSharedDamage(castEntry);

    const messageRoll = castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId
        ? game.messages.get(castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId)?.rolls?.[0]
        : null;

    return messageRoll ?? restoreDamageRoll(castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON);
}

// =================================================================
// OUTCOME-DEFINED / CUSTOM DAMAGE
// =================================================================

function customDamageStateKey(key) {
    return String(key ?? "custom");
}

function getCustomDamageState(castEntry, key) {
    castEntry.customDamageStates ??= {};
    const normalized = customDamageStateKey(key);

    castEntry.customDamageStates[normalized] ??= {
        key: normalized,
        rolling: false,
        messageId: null,
        rollJSON: null,
        formula: null,
        total: null
    };

    return castEntry.customDamageStates[normalized];
}

function buildTypedDamageFormula(damageSpec) {
    if (damageSpec?.inlineFormula) return damageSpec.inlineFormula;
    if (!damageSpec?.formula || !damageSpec?.type) return null;
    return `(${damageSpec.formula})[${damageSpec.type}]`;
}

async function postCustomDamageMessage(castEntry, state, roll, label, { restored = false } = {}) {
    const damageMessage = await roll.toMessage({
        speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
        flavor:
            `<strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)}</strong> — ${escapeHTML(label)}`
            + (restored ? `<br><small>Restored original damage roll</small>` : "")
    });

    state.messageId = damageMessage?.id ?? null;
    return damageMessage;
}

async function ensureCustomDamage(castEntry, key, damageSpec, label = "Outcome Damage") {
    const state = getCustomDamageState(castEntry, key);

    if (state.messageId && game.messages.get(state.messageId)) {
        return state.messageId;
    }

    if (state.rolling) {
        while (state.rolling) {
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 25));
        }
        return state.messageId;
    }

    state.rolling = true;

    try {
        if (state.rollJSON) {
            const restored = restoreDamageRoll(state.rollJSON);
            if (!restored) throw new Error("Stored custom damage roll could not be restored.");

            await postCustomDamageMessage(castEntry, state, restored, label, { restored: true });

            debug("RESTORED SAME OUTCOME DAMAGE ROLL:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                key,
                formula: restored.formula,
                total: restored.total,
                damageMessageId: state.messageId
            });

            return state.messageId;
        }

        const typedFormula = buildTypedDamageFormula(damageSpec);
        if (!typedFormula) throw new Error("Outcome-defined damage is missing formula or type.");

        const DamageRoll = getPF2eDamageRollClass();
        const rollData = getOutcomeDamageRollData(castEntry);
        const roll = new DamageRoll(typedFormula, rollData);
        await roll.evaluate();

        state.rollJSON = serializeDamageRoll(roll);
        state.formula = roll.formula;
        state.total = roll.total;

        await postCustomDamageMessage(castEntry, state, roll, label);

        debug("NEW OUTCOME-DEFINED DAMAGE ROLL:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            key,
            formula: roll.formula,
            total: roll.total,
            damageMessageId: state.messageId
        });

        return state.messageId;
    } finally {
        state.rolling = false;
    }
}

async function getCustomDamageRoll(castEntry, key, damageSpec, label) {
    await ensureCustomDamage(castEntry, key, damageSpec, label);
    const state = getCustomDamageState(castEntry, key);

    const messageRoll = state.messageId
        ? game.messages.get(state.messageId)?.rolls?.[0]
        : null;

    return messageRoll ?? restoreDamageRoll(state.rollJSON);
}

async function getDamageRollForGroup(castEntry, group) {
    const damage = group.damage;

    if (!damage || damage.kind === "none") return null;

    if (damage.kind === "shared") {
        return await getSharedDamageRoll(castEntry);
    }

    if (damage.kind === "formula") {
        return await getCustomDamageRoll(
            castEntry,
            `degree:${group.degree}`,
            damage,
            `${readableDegree(group.degree)} Damage`
        );
    }

    return null;
}

// =================================================================
// RESULT GROUPS
// =================================================================

function getOutcome(castEntry, degree) {
    return castEntry.automationProfile?.outcomes?.[degree] ?? null;
}

function normalizeOutcomeDamage(outcome) {
    if (outcome?.damage) return deepClone(outcome.damage);

    // Backward compatibility with v8.0/basic-save profiles.
    const multiplier = Number(outcome?.damageMultiplier);
    if (Number.isFinite(multiplier)) {
        return multiplier > 0
            ? { kind: "shared", multiplier }
            : { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 };
    }

    return { kind: "none", multiplier: 0 };
}

function getOrCreateResultGroup(castEntry, degree) {
    castEntry.resultGroups ??= {};

    if (!castEntry.resultGroups[degree]) {
        const outcome = getOutcome(castEntry, degree);
        if (!outcome) throw new Error(`No inferred outcome for ${degree}.`);

        const damage = normalizeOutcomeDamage(outcome);

        castEntry.resultGroups[degree] = {
            degree,
            damage,
            multiplier: damage.kind === "shared" ? damage.multiplier : null,
            conditions: deepClone(outcome.conditions ?? []),
            linkedConditions: deepClone(outcome.linkedConditions ?? []),
            nativeEffects: deepClone(outcome.nativeEffects ?? []),
            managedEffects: deepClone(outcome.managedEffects ?? []),
            followUpSave: deepClone(outcome.followUpSave ?? null),
            recognizedConditions: deepClone(outcome.recognizedConditions ?? outcome.conditions ?? []),
            secondarySave: deepClone(outcome.secondarySave ?? null),
            ongoingSave: deepClone(outcome.ongoingSave ?? null),
            terminal: deepClone(outcome.terminal ?? null),
            manualRider: outcome.manualRider === true,
            manualRiderText: outcome.manualRiderText ?? null,
            rawText: outcome.rawText ?? "",
            conditionalDamageRules: deepClone(castEntry.automationProfile?.conditionalDamageRules ?? []),
            targetHealingRules: deepClone(castEntry.automationProfile?.targetHealingRules ?? []),
            targetDamageSubstitutionRules: deepClone(castEntry.automationProfile?.targetDamageSubstitutionRules ?? []),
            targets: []
        };
    }

    return castEntry.resultGroups[degree];
}

function getTargetKey(actor, token) {
    return token?.uuid ?? actor?.uuid ?? actor?.id;
}

function findTargetResult(castEntry, targetKey) {
    for (const [degree, group] of Object.entries(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {})) {
        const target = group.targets.find(candidate => candidate.key === targetKey);
        if (target) return { degree, group, target };
    }
    return null;
}

function removeTargetFromAllGroups(castEntry, targetKey) {
    const removed = [];

    for (const group of Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {})) {
        const matches = group.targets.filter(target => target.key === targetKey);
        removed.push(...matches);
        group.targets = group.targets.filter(target => target.key !== targetKey);
    }

    return removed;
}

function groupHasAutomatedConditions(group) {
    return (group.conditions?.length ?? 0) > 0
        || (group.linkedConditions?.length ?? 0) > 0
        || (group.nativeEffects?.length ?? 0) > 0
        || (group.managedEffects?.length ?? 0) > 0
        || !!group.ongoingSave;
}

function groupHasDamage(group) {
    return group.damage?.kind === "shared" && group.damage.multiplier > 0
        || group.damage?.kind === "formula";
}

function groupHasTargetHealing(group) {
    return (group?.targets ?? []).some(target =>
        target?.targetMode?.kind === "healing"
        && !target.applied
        && !target.manualCorrection
    );
}

function groupNeedsAutomatedAction(group) {
    return groupHasDamage(group)
        || groupHasConditionalDamage(group)
        || groupHasAutomatedConditions(group)
        || groupHasTargetHealing(group)
        || !!group.secondarySave
        || !!group.followUpSave
        || !!group.terminal;
}

function addTargetToResultGroup(
    group,
    actor,
    token,
    { manualCorrection = false, targetMode = null, spellImmunity = null } = {}
) {
    const key = getTargetKey(actor, token);
    if (!key) return null;

    const existing = group.targets.find(target => target.key === key);
    if (existing) return existing;

    const target = {
        key,
        actorName: actor.name,
        actorUUID: actor.uuid ?? "",
        tokenUUID: token?.uuid ?? "",
        degree: group.degree,

        applied: !!spellImmunity || manualCorrection || !(groupNeedsAutomatedAction(group) || targetMode?.kind === "healing"),
        damageApplied: false,
        healingApplied: false,
        targetMode: targetMode ? deepClone(targetMode) : null,
        spellImmunity: spellImmunity ? deepClone(spellImmunity) : null,
        effectsApplied: false,
        secondaryResolved: false,
        secondaryDegree: null,
        secondaryBranch: null,
        terminalResult: null,
        terminalApplied: false,
        conditionalDamageApplied: [],
        manualCorrection
    };

    group.targets.push(target);
    return target;
}

function prepareTargetForReroll(castEntry, actor, token) {
    const key = getTargetKey(actor, token);
    if (!key) return { manualCorrection: false };

    const removed = removeTargetFromAllGroups(castEntry, key);
    const casterPool = getCasterTemporaryHPState(castEntry);
    const somethingWasApplied = !!casterPool?.records[key] || removed.some(target =>
        target.damageApplied
        || target.effectsApplied
        || target.secondaryResolved
        || target.terminalApplied
    );

    if (somethingWasApplied) {
        if (casterPool) {
            casterPool.reviewRequired = true;
            casterPool.reviewReason = "Caster temporary HP require manual review after an applied save was rerolled.";
            persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry).catch(error => console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not save temporary HP review state:", error));
        }
        const castWide = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
        if (castWide) {
            castWide.reviewRequired = true;
            castWide.result = "A save was rerolled after application. Correct this cast manually; automatic backlash is paused.";
            persistCastBacklash(castEntry).catch(error => console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not save reroll review state:", error));
        }
        ui.notifications.warn(
            `${actor.name} rerolled after spell effects were already applied. `
            + `The result card was updated, but that target must be corrected manually.`
        );
    }

    debug("REROLL REPLACING RESULT:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        target: actor.name,
        oldDegree: removed[0]?.degree ?? null,
        somethingWasApplied
    });

    return { manualCorrection: somethingWasApplied };
}

// =================================================================
// MASTER CARD UI
// =================================================================

function damageLabelFromSpec(damage, resolvedFormula = null) {
    if (!damage || damage.kind === "none") return "No Damage";

    if (damage.kind === "formula") {
        // PF2e has already resolved rank expressions in this stored formula.
        // Use its display text, preserving dice and damage types; rendering
        // never rolls again or substitutes a rolled total for the dice.
        if (resolvedFormula) return String(resolvedFormula).trim();
        const formula = damage.formula ?? damage.inlineLabel ?? "Scaling";
        const type = damage.type ? ` ${damage.type}` : "";
        return `${formula}${type}`.trim();
    }

    const multiplier = damage.multiplier;
    if (multiplier === 0) return "No Damage";
    if (multiplier === 0.5) return "0.5× Damage";
    if (multiplier === 1) return "1× Damage";
    if (multiplier === 2) return "2× Damage";
    return `${multiplier}× Damage`;
}

function formatConditionSummary(effect) {
    if (effect.recipient === "caster") return `Caster: ${formatConditionSummary({ ...effect, recipient: "target" })}`;
    let label = effect.name ?? effect.condition;
    if (effect.value !== null && effect.value !== undefined) label += ` ${effect.value}`;
    if (effect.duration) label += ` for ${effect.duration.value} ${effect.duration.unit}`;
    return label;
}

function formatLinkedConditionSummary(effect) {
    const label = effect.name ?? effect.condition;
    const dependency = effect.whileConditionName ?? effect.whileCondition;
    return `${label} while ${dependency}`;
}

function formatNativeEffectSummary(effect) {
    return effect.label ?? effect.name ?? effect.uuid ?? "PF2e Spell Effect";
}

function buildSecondarySaveSummary(secondarySave, resolvedFormula = null) {
    if (!secondarySave) return null;

    const saveName = secondarySave.saveType.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase());
    const parts = [`Secondary ${saveName} Save`];

    const success = secondarySave.successBranch;
    if (success?.damage?.kind === "formula") {
        parts.push(`success: ${damageLabelFromSpec(success.damage, resolvedFormula)}`);
    }

    if (success?.conditions?.length) {
        parts.push(`success: ${success.conditions.map(formatConditionSummary).join(", ")}`);
    }

    if (success?.linkedConditions?.length) {
        parts.push(`success: ${success.linkedConditions.map(formatLinkedConditionSummary).join(", ")}`);
    }

    if (secondarySave.failureBranch?.terminal) {
        parts.push(`failure: ${secondarySave.failureBranch.terminal.label}`);
    }

    return parts.join(" • ");
}

function buildGroupDescriptor(group, castEntry = null) {
    const parts = [];

    if (group.secondarySave) {
        parts.push(buildSecondarySaveSummary(group.secondarySave,
            castEntry?.customDamageStates?.[`degree:${group.degree}:secondary:success`]?.formula));
    } else {
        const damageLabel = damageLabelFromSpec(group.damage,
            castEntry?.customDamageStates?.[`degree:${group.degree}`]?.formula);
        if (damageLabel !== "No Damage" || !group.terminal) parts.push(damageLabel);
    }

    if (group.conditions?.length) {
        parts.push(group.conditions.map(formatConditionSummary).join(", "));
    }

    if (group.linkedConditions?.length) {
        parts.push(group.linkedConditions.map(formatLinkedConditionSummary).join(", "));
    }

    if (group.nativeEffects?.length) {
        parts.push(group.nativeEffects.map(formatNativeEffectSummary).join(", "));
    }

    if (group.managedEffects?.length) {
        parts.push(group.managedEffects.map(effect => {
            const duration = effect.duration ? ` (${effect.duration.value} ${effect.duration.unit})` : "";
            const recovery = effect.recovery ? `; ${effect.recovery.timing === "turn-end" ? "turn-end" : "triggered"} ${effect.recovery.saveType} recovery save` : "";
            return `${effect.label}${duration}${recovery}`;
        }).join(", "));
    }
    if (group.followUpSave) parts.push(`then ${group.followUpSave.saveType} save${group.followUpSave.requiresHead ? " if it has a head" : ""}`);

    if (group.ongoingSave) {
        const saveName = group.ongoingSave.saveType.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase());
        parts.push(`ongoing ${saveName} at turn end`);
    }

    if (groupHasConditionalDamage(group)) {
        parts.push(`Conditional: ${group.conditionalDamageRules.map(conditionalDamageSummary).join(", ")}`);
    }

    if (group.terminal) {
        parts.push(group.terminal.label ?? "Terminal result");
    }

    if ((group.targets ?? []).some(target => target?.targetMode?.kind === "healing")) {
        parts.push("target-dependent healing");
    }

    if (group.manualRider) parts.push(group.manualRiderText ? `⚠ Manual: ${group.manualRiderText}` : "⚠ Manual rider");

    return parts.filter(Boolean).join(" • ");
}

function getGroupButtonLabel(group, castEntry = null) {
    if (group.followUpSave) return "Apply Damage + Second Save";
    if ((group.targets ?? []).some(target => target?.targetMode?.kind === "healing" && !target.applied)) {
        return "Apply Results";
    }

    if (group.secondarySave) {
        const saveName = group.secondarySave.saveType.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase());
        return `Roll ${saveName}`;
    }

    const hasDamage = groupHasDamage(group);
    const hasConditionalDamage = groupHasConditionalDamage(group);
    const hasEffects = groupHasAutomatedConditions(group);
    const hasTerminal = !!group.terminal;

    if (group.damage?.kind === "formula") {
        const formula = damageLabelFromSpec(group.damage,
            castEntry?.customDamageStates?.[`degree:${group.degree}`]?.formula);
        if (hasEffects && hasTerminal) return `Apply ${formula} + Effects + Dead`;
        if (hasEffects) return `Apply ${formula} + Effects`;
        if (hasTerminal) return `Apply ${formula} + Dead`;
        return `Apply ${formula}`;
    }

    const multiplier = group.damage?.multiplier ?? 0;
    if (hasConditionalDamage && !hasDamage && !hasEffects && !hasTerminal) return "Apply Conditional Damage";
