    if (hasConditionalDamage && !hasDamage && hasEffects && !hasTerminal) return "Apply Conditional Damage + Effects";
    if (hasConditionalDamage && !hasDamage && !hasEffects && hasTerminal) return "Apply Conditional Damage + Dead";
    if (hasDamage && hasConditionalDamage && hasEffects && hasTerminal) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Conditional + Effects + Dead`;
    if (hasDamage && hasConditionalDamage && hasEffects) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Conditional + Effects`;
    if (hasDamage && hasConditionalDamage && hasTerminal) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Conditional + Dead`;
    if (hasDamage && hasConditionalDamage) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Conditional`;
    if (hasDamage && hasEffects && hasTerminal) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Effects + Dead`;
    if (hasDamage && hasEffects) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Effects`;
    if (hasDamage && hasTerminal) return `Apply ${multiplier}× + Dead`;
    if (hasDamage) return `Apply ${multiplier}×`;
    if (hasEffects && hasTerminal) return "Apply Effects + Dead";
    if (hasEffects) return "Apply Effects";
    if (hasTerminal) return "Apply Dead";
    return group.manualRider ? "Manual Review" : "No Damage";
}

function formatTargetDisplay(target) {
    let name = escapeHTML(target.actorName);

    if (target.spellImmunity) {
        const label = escapeHTML(target.spellImmunity.label ?? target.spellImmunity.type ?? "Immune");
        return `${name} [Immune: ${label}] ✓`;
    }

    if (target.targetMode?.kind === "healing") {
        name += ` [Healing]`;
    }

    if (target.secondaryResolved && target.secondaryDegree) {
        const branch = target.secondaryBranch ? ` → ${target.secondaryBranch}` : "";
        name += ` [${escapeHTML(readableDegree(target.secondaryDegree))}${escapeHTML(branch)}]`;
    }

    if (target.followUpDegree) name += ` [Second save: ${escapeHTML(readableDegree(target.followUpDegree))}]`;
    if (target.followUpNote) name += ` [${escapeHTML(target.followUpNote)}]`;
    if (target.terminalResult) {
        const label = escapeHTML(target.terminalResult.label ?? "Terminal result");
        return target.terminalApplied
            ? `${name} ☠ ${label}`
            : `${name} ⚠ ${label}`;
    }

    if (target.manualCorrection) return `${name} ⚠`;
    if (target.applied) return `${name} ✓`;
    return name;
}

function getTargetExclusionReason(castEntry, target) {
    const recorded = getCastBacklashState(castEntry)?.initial?.[target.key]?.excluded;
    const note = /^Not affected:/i.test(target.followUpNote ?? "") ? target.followUpNote : null;
    return String(recorded ?? note ?? "").replace(/^Not affected:\s*/i, "").trim();
}

function buildExcludedResultRow(castEntry) {
    const targets = Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {}).flatMap(group => group.targets);
    const names = targets.flatMap(target => {
        const reason = getTargetExclusionReason(castEntry, target);
        return reason ? [`${escapeHTML(target.actorName)} [${escapeHTML(reason)}]`] : [];
    });
    if (!names.length) return "";

    return `
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto;gap:5px 8px;align-items:center;padding:4px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(128,128,128,0.25);">
            <div>
                <strong>Not affected</strong>
                <div style="margin-top:2px;font-size:0.9em;opacity:0.85;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">${names.join(", ")}</div>
            </div>
            <button type="button" disabled style="min-width:100px;height:25px;min-height:25px;padding:1px 6px;opacity:0.6;">No Action</button>
        </div>`;
}

function buildResultRow(castEntry, degree) {
    const original = castEntry.resultGroups?.[degree];
    if (!original) return "";
    // Partition only the display. The complete original group and persistent
    // application records remain authoritative for resolution and rerolls.
    const group = { ...original, targets: original.targets.filter(target => !getTargetExclusionReason(castEntry, target)) };
    if (group.targets.length === 0) return "";

    const pending = group.targets.filter(target => !target.applied);
    const names = group.targets.map(formatTargetDisplay).join(", ");
    const descriptor = escapeHTML(buildGroupDescriptor(group, castEntry));
    const actionable = groupNeedsAutomatedAction(group);

    let buttonHTML = "";

    if (pending.length > 0 && actionable) {
        const buttonClass = group.secondarySave
            ? "pf2e-auto-secondary-save"
            : "pf2e-auto-apply-master-damage";

        buttonHTML = `
            <button
                type="button"
                class="${buttonClass}"
                data-cast-id="${escapeHTML(castEntry.castId)}"
                data-degree="${escapeHTML(degree)}"
                style="min-width:100px;height:25px;min-height:25px;padding:1px 6px;"
            >${escapeHTML(getGroupButtonLabel(group, castEntry))}</button>`;
    } else {
        const text = group.manualRider && !actionable
            ? "Manual Review"
            : pending.length === 0 && actionable
                ? group.manualRider ? "Applied • GM Steps" : "✓ Resolved"
                : "No Action";

        buttonHTML = `
            <button type="button" disabled
                style="min-width:100px;height:25px;min-height:25px;padding:1px 6px;opacity:0.6;"
            >${escapeHTML(text)}</button>`;
    }

    return `
        <div style="
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto;
            gap:5px 8px;
            align-items:center;
            padding:4px 0;
            border-top:1px solid rgba(128,128,128,0.25);
        ">
            <div>
                <div>
                    <strong>${escapeHTML(readableDegree(degree))}</strong>
                    <span style="opacity:0.7;"> — ${descriptor}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="margin-top:2px;font-size:0.9em;opacity:0.85;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">
                    ${names}
                </div>
            </div>
            ${buttonHTML}
        </div>`;
}

function buildMasterResolutionCard(castEntry) {
    if (castEntry.automationProfile?.flow) return buildSpellFlowCard(castEntry);
    if (castEntry.automationProfile?.kind === "random-table-basic-save") return buildRandomTableResolutionCard(castEntry);
    const rows = [
        buildResultRow(castEntry, "criticalSuccess"),
        buildResultRow(castEntry, "success"),
        buildResultRow(castEntry, "failure"),
        buildResultRow(castEntry, "criticalFailure"),
        buildExcludedResultRow(castEntry)
    ].filter(Boolean).join("");

    const hasManualCorrection = Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {})
        .flatMap(group => group.targets)
        .some(target => target.manualCorrection || (target.terminalResult && !target.terminalApplied));

    const hasManualRider = Object.values(castEntry.resultGroups ?? {})
        .some(group => group.manualRider);

    const label = castEntry.automationProfile?.resolutionLabel ?? "Save Resolution";

    return `
        <div class="pf2e-auto-master-save-card" style="font-size:0.82em;line-height:1.15;padding:1px 0;">
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding-bottom:3px;">
                <strong>${escapeHTML(castEntry.spell.name)} — ${escapeHTML(label)}</strong>
                <span style="opacity:0.65;white-space:nowrap;">
                    ${castEntry.castRank ? `Rank ${castEntry.castRank} • ` : ""}Cycle ${castEntry.cycle}
                </span>
            </div>

            ${rows}
            ${buildCastBacklashControls(castEntry)}
            ${buildCasterTemporaryHPSummary(castEntry)}

            ${hasManualRider ? `
                <div style="border-top:1px solid rgba(128,128,128,0.25);padding-top:4px;margin-top:2px;font-size:0.9em;opacity:0.82;">
                    ⚠ Follow the listed GM steps for manual restrictions, choices, or unrecognized outcomes.
                </div>` : ""}

            ${hasManualCorrection ? `
                <div style="border-top:1px solid rgba(128,128,128,0.25);padding-top:4px;margin-top:2px;font-size:0.9em;opacity:0.82;">
                    ⚠ An unresolved terminal result or post-application reroll requires manual GM handling.
                </div>` : ""}
        </div>`;
}

function queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry) {
    if (castEntry.flowParentCastId) {
        const parent = findCastById(castEntry.flowParentCastId);
        if (!parent) throw new Error("The original spell resolution is no longer cached.");
        return queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(parent);
    }
    if (castEntry.tableParentCastId) {
        const parent = findCastById(castEntry.tableParentCastId);
        if (!parent) throw new Error("The table spell's original cast is no longer cached.");
        return queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(parent);
    }
    castEntry.resolutionQueue ??= Promise.resolve();

    const performUpdate = async () => {
        const content = buildMasterResolutionCard(castEntry);

        if (castEntry.resolutionMessageId) {
            const existing = game.messages.get(castEntry.resolutionMessageId);
            if (existing) {
                await existing.update({ content });
                return existing;
            }
            castEntry.resolutionMessageId = null;
        }

        const created = await ChatMessage.create({
            speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: castEntry.actor }),
            content
        });

        castEntry.resolutionMessageId = created?.id ?? null;

        debug("Master resolution card created:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            castId: castEntry.castId,
            cycle: castEntry.cycle,
            messageId: castEntry.resolutionMessageId
        });

        return created;
    };

    castEntry.resolutionQueue = castEntry.resolutionQueue.then(performUpdate, performUpdate);
    return castEntry.resolutionQueue;
}

// =================================================================
// RESET CYCLE
// =================================================================

function resetResolutionCycle(castEntry, reason = "reset") {
    castEntry.cycle = Number(castEntry.cycle ?? 1) + 1;
    if (castEntry.randomTableState) castEntry.randomTableState.exposures = [];

    castEntry.sharedDamageRolled = false;
    castEntry.sharedDamageRolling = false;
    castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId = null;
    castEntry.sharedDamageFormula = null;
    castEntry.sharedDamageRollOptions = [];
    castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON = null;
    castEntry.customDamageStates = {};

    castEntry.resolutionMessageId = null;
    castEntry.resolutionQueue = Promise.resolve();
    castEntry.resultGroups = {};
    castEntry.directResolvedCycle = null;
    castEntry.directResolution = null;
    castEntry.timestamp = Date.now();

    STATE.activeCastId = castEntry.castId;
    STATE.activeCastAt = Date.now();

    debug("RESET SPELL RESOLUTION CYCLE:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        castId: castEntry.castId,
        cycle: castEntry.cycle,
        reason
    });
}

// =================================================================
// PROCESS AUTOMATIC SAVE
// =================================================================

async function processAutomaticSave(message, targetActor, castEntry, degree) {
    const outcome = getOutcome(castEntry, degree);
    if (!outcome) throw new Error(`No automatic outcome for ${degree}.`);

    const spellImmunity = getSpellEffectImmunity(targetActor, castEntry);

    if (spellImmunity) {
        debug("SPELL EFFECT BLOCKED BY IMMUNITY:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            target: targetActor.name,
            degree,
            immunity: spellImmunity,
            reason: "PF2e prepared Immunity predicate matched the spell's effect traits"
        });
    }

    const damage = normalizeOutcomeDamage(outcome);
    const healingRule = findMatchingTargetHealingRule(
        targetActor,
        castEntry.automationProfile?.targetHealingRules ?? []
    );

    const targetMode = healingRule
        ? {
            kind: "healing",
            ruleId: healingRule.id,
            predicate: deepClone(healingRule.predicate),
            healing: deepClone(healingRule.healing),
            saveIndependent: true
        }
        : null;

    // An immunity to the spell effect blocks the entire result before any
    // damage/healing roll is prepared. Otherwise a healing branch uses its full
    // amount regardless of the irrelevant save degree.
    if (spellImmunity) {
        // No roll preparation and no target-mode application. The target is
        // recorded below as already resolved/immune for the master card.
    } else if (targetMode?.kind === "healing") {
        if (targetMode.healing?.kind === "shared") {
            await ensureSharedDamage(castEntry);
        } else if (targetMode.healing?.kind === "formula") {
            await ensureCustomDamage(
                castEntry,
                `target-healing:${targetMode.ruleId}`,
                targetMode.healing,
                `Target Healing — ${targetMode.predicate?.label ?? "Target"}`
            );
        }

        debug("TARGET MODE MATCH:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            target: targetActor.name,
            mode: "healing",
            predicate: targetMode.predicate,
            healing: targetMode.healing,
            ignoredSaveDegree: degree
        });
    } else if (damage.kind === "shared" && damage.multiplier > 0) {
        // Roll/store damage as soon as the first damaging target reaches this outcome.
        await ensureSharedDamage(castEntry);
    } else if (damage.kind === "formula") {
        await ensureCustomDamage(
            castEntry,
            `degree:${degree}`,
            damage,
            `${readableDegree(degree)} Damage`
        );
    }

    const token = getTokenDocumentFromSpeaker(message);
    const reroll = isRerollMessage(message);
    let manualCorrection = false;

    if (reroll) {
        manualCorrection = prepareTargetForReroll(castEntry, targetActor, token).manualCorrection;
    }

    const key = getTargetKey(targetActor, token);

    if (!reroll) {
        const previous = findTargetResult(castEntry, key);
        if (previous) {
            debug("Duplicate non-reroll save ignored:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                target: targetActor.name,
                existingDegree: previous.degree,
                attemptedDegree: degree,
                cycle: castEntry.cycle
            });
            return;
        }
    }

    const group = getOrCreateResultGroup(castEntry, degree);
    addTargetToResultGroup(group, targetActor, token, {
        manualCorrection,
        targetMode: spellImmunity ? null : targetMode,
        spellImmunity
    });

    await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry);

    debug(reroll ? "AUTOMATIC SAVE REROLL:" : "AUTOMATIC SAVE RESULT:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        automation: castEntry.automationProfile?.kind,
        target: targetActor.name,
        degree,
        damage: group.damage,
        conditions: group.conditions,
        linkedConditions: group.linkedConditions,
        nativeEffects: group.nativeEffects,
        secondarySave: group.secondarySave,
        ongoingSave: group.ongoingSave,
        targetMode: spellImmunity ? null : targetMode,
        spellImmunity,
        manualRider: group.manualRider,
        reroll,
        cycle: castEntry.cycle,
        resolutionMessageId: castEntry.resolutionMessageId
    });
}

// =================================================================
// EXPLICIT RULE EFFECTS
// =================================================================

async function processExplicitEffect(targetActor, spell, entry) {
    if (!entry.condition) return;

    if (entry.duration) {
        await applyTimedCondition(targetActor, spell, {
            ...entry,
            name: entry.condition
        });
    } else {
        await applyCondition(targetActor, entry.condition, entry.value);
    }
}

// =================================================================
// SAVE PROCESSOR
// =================================================================

async function processSave(message) {
    if (!game.user.isGM) return;
    if (STATE.processed.has(message.id)) return;

    // PF2e carries our internal-roll options onto rerolls, including an
    // Athletics check. Route a referenced reroll before the internal-save
    // filter and saving-throw-only matching, always to its original cast.
    const flowReference = getSpellFlowReference(message);
    if (isRerollMessage(message) && flowReference?.parentCastId) {
        const original = game.messages.get(flowReference.parentCastId);
        const cast = findCastById(flowReference.parentCastId) ?? (original ? cacheSpellCast(original) : null);
        const actor = getActorFromSpeaker(message);
        const degree = getDegree(message);
        if (!cast || !actor || !degree || !(await determineSpellAutomation(cast))?.flow) {
            ui.notifications.warn("The original spell exposure for this reroll is unavailable. Correct it manually.");return;
        }
        STATE.processed.add(message.id);
        await queueDamageApplication(`flow:${cast.castId}`, () => recordSpellFlowSave(cast, actor,
            getTokenDocumentFromSpeaker(message), degree, { message }));
        return;
    }

    const contextOptions = message.flags?.pf2e?.context?.options ?? [];
    if (Array.isArray(contextOptions) && (
        contextOptions.includes("pf2e-auto-secondary-save")
        || contextOptions.includes("pf2e-auto-ongoing-save")
        || contextOptions.includes("pf2e-auto-manual-trigger-save")
    )) return;

    const saveType = getSaveType(message);
    if (!saveType) return;

    const degree = getDegree(message);
    if (!degree) return;

    const targetActor = getActorFromSpeaker(message);
    if (!targetActor) return;

    const directSpell = await getSpellDirectlyFromSave(message);

    const tableReference = getSpellFlowReference(message) ?? message.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.randomTableExposure;
    let castEntry;
    if (isRerollMessage(message) && tableReference?.parentCastId) {
        // A second cast of the same item must not steal an earlier exposure's reroll.
        castEntry = findCastById(tableReference.parentCastId);
        if (!castEntry) {
            const original = game.messages.get(tableReference.parentCastId);
            if (original) castEntry = cacheSpellCast(original);
        }
        if (!castEntry) {
            ui.notifications.warn("The original table spell cast for this reroll is unavailable. Adjust its result manually.");
            return;
        }
    } else {
        castEntry = findCachedSpellEntry(message, directSpell);
        if (!castEntry) castEntry = recoverCastEntryFromChat(message, directSpell);
    }

    if (!castEntry) {
        debug("Save detected but no matching spell card could be recovered:", {
            target: targetActor.name,
            saveType,
            degree,
            directSpell: directSpell?.name ?? null
        });
        ui.notifications.warn("Spell save detected, but no matching spell card could be found in chat.");
        return;
    }

    const automation = await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);

    if (!automation) {
        debug(`${castEntry.spell.name}: no supported automatic or explicit rule.`);
        return;
    }

    STATE.processed.add(message.id);

    debug("Associated save:", {
        target: targetActor.name,
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        degree,
        reroll: isRerollMessage(message),
        castId: castEntry.castId,
        cycle: castEntry.cycle,
        automation: automation.kind
    });

    if (automation.kind === "interactive-spell") {
        await queueDamageApplication(`flow:${castEntry.castId}`, () => recordSpellFlowSave(castEntry, targetActor,
            getTokenDocumentFromSpeaker(message), degree, { message }));
        return;
    }

    if (automation.kind === "random-table-basic-save") {
        try { await processRandomTableSave(message, targetActor, castEntry, degree); }
        catch (error) {
            console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Random table resolution failed:", error);
            ui.notifications.error(`${castEntry.spell.name}: table resolution failed; check the console.`);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (automation.kind === "basic-save-damage" || automation.kind === "degree-save-damage") {
        try {
            await processAutomaticSave(message, targetActor, castEntry, degree);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(
                "PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Automatic save processing failed:",
                error
            );
            ui.notifications.error(`${castEntry.spell.name}: automatic save resolution failed.`);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (automation.source === "explicit") {
        if (isRerollMessage(message)) {
            ui.notifications.warn(
                `${castEntry.spell.name}: reroll detected, but explicit condition overrides are not automatically reversible yet.`
            );
            return;
        }

        for (const entry of automation.rule?.[degree] ?? []) {
            try {
                await processExplicitEffect(targetActor, castEntry.spell, entry);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Explicit effect failed:", entry, error);
            }
        }
    }
}

// =================================================================
// APPLY RESULT GROUPS / SECONDARY SAVES
// =================================================================

async function resolveGroupedTarget(target) {
    let token = null;

    if (target.tokenUUID) {
        try { token = await fromUuid(target.tokenUUID); } catch (_) {}
    }

    let actor = token?.actor ?? null;

    if (!actor && target.actorUUID) {
        try { actor = await fromUuid(target.actorUUID); } catch (_) {}
    }

    return { actor, token };
}

function getActorHPValue(actor) {
    const candidates = [
        actor?.hitPoints?.value,
        actor?.system?.attributes?.hp?.value
    ];

    for (const candidate of candidates) {
        const value = Number(candidate);
        if (Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
    }

    return null;
}

async function clearConditionCompletely(actor, slug) {
    if (!actor || !slug) return;

    let safety = 0;
    while (getExistingCondition(actor, slug) && safety < 12) {
        safety++;
        const before = getExistingCondition(actor, slug);
        const beforeValue = getConditionValue(before);

        try {
            if (typeof actor.decreaseCondition === "function") {
                await actor.decreaseCondition(slug);
            } else if (before?.delete && before.readonly !== true) {
                await before.delete();
            } else {
                break;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn(
                `PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not clear ${slug} from ${actor.name}:`,
                error
            );
            break;
        }

        const after = getExistingCondition(actor, slug);
        if (!after) break;

        // Guard against a granted/read-only condition that cannot be reduced
        // directly. Do not loop forever or delete an unknown granter.
        if (after.id === before?.id && getConditionValue(after) === beforeValue) {
            break;
        }
    }
}

async function applyDeadStatus(actor, { spellName = "Spell", reason = "Dies" } = {}) {
    if (!actor) return false;

    try {
        if (!actor.statuses?.has?.("dead")) {
            if (typeof actor.toggleStatusEffect !== "function") {
                throw new Error(`${actor.name} does not expose toggleStatusEffect().`);
            }

            await actor.toggleStatusEffect("dead", { active: true });
        }

        const deadApplied = actor.statuses?.has?.("dead") === true;

        // Never clear ordinary 0-HP conditions unless the Dead status was
        // positively confirmed first. This prevents a failed status toggle
        // from leaving a 0-HP creature with neither Dying nor Dead.
        if (!deadApplied) {
            throw new Error(
                `Foundry did not report the configured "dead" status on ${actor.name} after toggling it.`
            );
        }

        // Explicit death supersedes ordinary Dying/Unconscious bookkeeping.
        // Clear Dying first because it can itself grant/maintain Unconscious.
        await clearConditionCompletely(actor, "dying");
        await clearConditionCompletely(actor, "unconscious");

        ui.notifications.info(`${spellName}: ${actor.name} is Dead (${reason}).`);

        debug("DEAD STATUS APPLIED:", {
            spell: spellName,
            target: actor.name,
            reason,
            statuses: Array.from(actor.statuses ?? [])
        });

        return true;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not apply Dead status:", error);
        ui.notifications.error(
            `${spellName}: ${actor.name} should be Dead, but the Dead status could not be applied automatically.`
        );
        return false;
    }
}

async function applyTerminalResult(castEntry, target, actor, terminal) {
    if (!terminal) return false;

    target.terminalResult = deepClone(terminal);

    if (terminal.kind === "death") {
        const applied = await applyDeadStatus(actor, {
            spellName: castEntry.spell.name,
            reason: terminal.label ?? "Dies"
        });

        target.terminalApplied = applied;
        target.manualCorrection = !applied;
        return applied;
    }

    target.manualCorrection = true;
    ui.notifications.warn(
        `${castEntry.spell.name}: ${actor.name} reached terminal result "${terminal.label ?? terminal.kind}". `
        + `This terminal type is not automated.`
    );
    return false;
}

async function handlePostDamageTerminal(castEntry, target, actor, beforeHP, afterHP) {
    const terminal = castEntry.automationProfile?.postDamageTerminal;
    if (!terminal) return false;

    if (!Number.isFinite(beforeHP) || !Number.isFinite(afterHP)) return false;

    const threshold = Number(terminal.threshold ?? 0);

    // "Reduced to 0 by this spell" requires the target to cross the
    // threshold because of this particular damage application.
    if (terminal.requiresReductionBySpell && !(beforeHP > threshold && afterHP <= threshold)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (!terminal.requiresReductionBySpell && afterHP > threshold) {
        return false;
    }

    const applied = await applyTerminalResult(castEntry, target, actor, terminal);

    debug("POST-DAMAGE TERMINAL RESULT:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        target: actor.name,
        beforeHP,
        afterHP,
        terminal,
        terminalApplied: applied
    });

    return applied;
}

function getSpellFlowState(cast) {
    if (!cast.automationProfile?.flow) return null;
    if (!cast.spellFlowState) {
        const stored = game.messages.get(cast.castId)?.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.spellFlow;
        const duration = cast.automationProfile.flow.duration;
        const seconds = ({ rounds: 6, minutes: 60, hours: 3600 })[duration?.unit];
        cast.spellFlowState = stored ? deepClone(stored) : {
            version: 1, nextId: 0, nextBatch: 0, entries: [], damage: {}, turnImmunity: {},
            startedAt: Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0), expiresAt: seconds ? Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) + seconds * duration.value : null,
            ended: false, chainStop: null, reviewRequired: false
        };
    }
    if (cast.automationProfile.flow.mode === "chain") synchronizeSpellChainStop(cast.spellFlowState);
    return cast.spellFlowState;
}

function synchronizeSpellChainStop(state) {
    const stopIndex = state.entries.findIndex(entry => entry.degree === "criticalSuccess");
    state.chainStop = stopIndex >= 0 ? state.entries[stopIndex].id : null;
    for (let index = 0; index < state.entries.length; index++) state.entries[index].skipped = stopIndex >= 0 && index > stopIndex;
}

function spellFlowEntryChangedActor(entry) {
    return !!(entry.pendingApplication || entry.target?.damageApplied || entry.target?.effectsApplied || entry.target?.terminalApplied);
}

async function persistSpellFlow(cast) {
    const card = game.messages.get(cast.castId);
    if (!card) throw new Error("The original cast card is missing; this resolution cannot be saved.");
    await card.update({ "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.spellFlow": deepClone(getSpellFlowState(cast)) });
}

function spellFlowTurnStamp() {
    const combat = game.combat;
    return combat?.started ? `${combat.id}:${combat.round}:${combat.turn}` : null;
}

function spellFlowEnded(cast) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    return state.ended || (state.expiresAt != null && Number(game.time?.worldTime ?? 0) >= state.expiresAt);
}

function spellFlowPhaseItem(cast, phase) {
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(cast);
    if (!phase.traits?.length) return spell;
    const data = deepClone(spell.toObject());
    data.system.traits.value = [...new Set([...(spell.system?.traits?.value ?? []), ...phase.traits])];
    data.system.defense = { save: { statistic: phase.saveType, basic: false } };
    const item = new spell.constructor(data, { parent: spell.actor, parentItem: spell.parentItem ?? null });
    return createContextualSaveItem(item, cast.saveOriginSpell ?? spell, cast.castRank);
}

function spellFlowChild(cast, entry, actor, token) {
    cast.spellFlowChildren ??= new Map();
    if (cast.spellFlowChildren.has(entry.id)) return cast.spellFlowChildren.get(entry.id);
    const phase = cast.automationProfile.flow.phases[entry.phase];
    const child = createVirtualReferencedCast(cast, spellFlowPhaseItem(cast, phase), cast.castRank, entry.id);
    child.castId = `${cast.castId}:flow:${entry.id}`;
    child.flowParentCastId = cast.castId;
    child.flowEntryId = entry.id;
    child.resolvedSpell = child.spell;
    child.automationProfile = { kind: "degree-save-damage", source: "scoped-description-inference", spell: child.spell,
        outcomes: deepClone(phase.outcomes), conditionalDamageRules: [], targetHealingRules: [], targetDamageSubstitutionRules: [] };
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    for (const outcome of Object.values(child.automationProfile.outcomes)) for (const spec of outcome.managedEffects ?? []) {
        if (["movement", "recurring"].includes(cast.automationProfile.flow.mode)) {
            spec.flowAreaCastId = cast.castId;
            spec.areaExpiresAt = state.expiresAt;
        }
    }
    const group = getOrCreateResultGroup(child, entry.degree);
    const target = entry.target ?? addTargetToResultGroup(group, actor, token, { spellImmunity: getSpellEffectImmunity(actor, child) });
    group.targets = [target];entry.target = target;
    cast.spellFlowChildren.set(entry.id, child);
    return child;
}

async function prepareSpellFlowDamage(cast, child, entry) {
    const group = child.resultGroups[entry.degree];
    if (!groupHasDamage(group) || entry.target.spellImmunity) return;
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    let saved = state.damage[entry.batch];
    if (!saved) {
        const { roll, context } = await getDamageFromSpellItem(cast);
        await roll.evaluate();
        saved = state.damage[entry.batch] = { rollJSON: serializeDamageRoll(roll), formula: roll.formula,
            rollOptions: Array.from(context?.options ?? getSpellDamageRollOptions(cast)), messageId: null };
        await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    }
    if (!saved.messageId || !game.messages.get(saved.messageId)) {
        const roll = restoreDamageRoll(saved.rollJSON);
        if (!roll) throw new Error("The saved damage roll could not be restored.");
        const message = await roll.toMessage({ speaker: ChatMessage.getSpeaker({ actor: cast.actor }),
            flavor: `<strong>${escapeHTML(cast.spell.name)}</strong> — ${escapeHTML(cast.automationProfile.flow.phases[entry.phase].label)} Damage` });
        saved.messageId = message?.id ?? null;
        await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    }
    child.sharedDamageRolled = true;
    child.sharedDamageMessageId = saved.messageId;
    child.sharedDamageRollJSON = saved.rollJSON;
    child.sharedDamageFormula = saved.formula;
    child.sharedDamageRollOptions = saved.rollOptions;
}

async function recordSpellFlowSave(cast, actor, token, degree, { phase: phaseId = null, message = null, batch = null, statistic = null } = {}) {
    const flow = cast.automationProfile.flow;
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    const reference = message ? getSpellFlowReference(message) : null;
    const key = getTargetKey(actor, token);
    const reroll = message && isRerollMessage(message);
    if (message && state.entries.some(entry => entry.messageIds.includes(message.id))) return null;
    const turnStamp = spellFlowTurnStamp();
    if (!reroll && flow.turnImmunityOnCriticalSuccess && turnStamp && state.turnImmunity[key] === turnStamp) {
        ui.notifications.info(`${actor.name}: already immune to further checks from this area this turn.`);
        return null;
    }
    let entry = reroll ? (reference?.parentCastId === cast.castId ? state.entries.find(entry => entry.id === reference.entryId)
        : [...state.entries].reverse().find(entry => entry.key === key)) : null;
    if (reroll && !entry) throw new Error("The original exposure for this reroll is unavailable. Correct it manually.");
    phaseId ??= entry?.phase ?? (flow.mode === "phases" ? Object.keys(flow.phases).find(id => flow.phases[id].saveType === getSaveType(message))
        : flow.mode === "chain" ? "chain" : flow.mode === "movement" ? "exposure"
            : state.entries.some(row => row.key === key) ? "exposure" : "initial");
    if (!flow.phases[phaseId]) throw new Error("This saving throw does not identify a supported phase.");
    if (!reroll && (flow.mode === "chain" || flow.mode === "phases" || phaseId === "initial")) {
        const existing = state.entries.find(entry => entry.key === key && entry.phase === phaseId);
        if (existing) return existing;
    }
    if (!reroll && spellFlowEnded(cast)) throw new Error("This spell has ended; cast it again for new exposures.");
    if (reroll) {
        if (entry.actorUUID !== actor.uuid || (entry.tokenUUID && token?.uuid && entry.tokenUUID !== token.uuid)) {
            throw new Error("The reroll's creature does not match the original exposure.");
        }
        if (state.turnImmunitySource?.[key] === entry.id) {
            delete state.turnImmunity[key];delete state.turnImmunitySource[key];
        }
        const outcomeChanged = entry.degree !== degree;
        const stopChanged = (entry.degree === "criticalSuccess") !== (degree === "criticalSuccess");
        const affected = flow.mode === "chain" && stopChanged ? state.entries.slice(state.entries.indexOf(entry)) : [entry];
        const changedAfterApplication = outcomeChanged && affected.some(spellFlowEntryChangedActor);
        if (changedAfterApplication) {
            entry.reviewRequired = true;state.reviewRequired = true;
            entry.degree = degree;
            entry.target ??= {};entry.target.manualCorrection = true;
            for (const row of affected.filter(spellFlowEntryChangedActor)) {
                row.reviewRequired = true;row.target ??= {};row.target.manualCorrection = true;
            }
            ui.notifications.warn(`${actor.name}: this reroll changes already-applied results. Correct the marked spell effects manually.`);
        } else if (outcomeChanged) {
            entry.degree = degree;entry.target = null;entry.applied = false;entry.skipped = false;
            cast.spellFlowChildren?.delete(entry.id);
        }
    } else {
        entry = { id: `entry-${++state.nextId}`, phase: phaseId, key, actorUUID: actor.uuid, tokenUUID: token?.uuid ?? null,
            actorName: actor.name, degree, statistic: statistic ?? flow.phases[phaseId].saveType,
            batch: batch ?? (flow.mode === "chain" ? "chain" : phaseId === "initial" ? "initial" : `${phaseId}:${state.nextId}`),
            messageIds: [], target: null, applied: false, pendingApplication: false, skipped: false };
        state.entries.push(entry);
    }
    if (!entry.reviewRequired) {
        const child = spellFlowChild(cast, entry, actor, token);
        if (flow.mode !== "chain" && !groupNeedsAutomatedAction(child.resultGroups[entry.degree])) entry.applied = true;
        if (flow.turnImmunityOnCriticalSuccess && entry.degree === "criticalSuccess") {
            const stamp = spellFlowTurnStamp();
            if (stamp) { state.turnImmunity[key] = stamp;state.turnImmunitySource ??= {};state.turnImmunitySource[key] = entry.id; }
        }
    }
    if (message) {
        entry.messageIds.push(message.id);
        const options = message.flags?.pf2e?.context?.options ?? [];
        const preservedOptions = (Array.isArray(options) ? options : []).filter(option => !String(option).startsWith("pf2e-auto-spell-flow:"));
        await message.update({
            "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.spellFlow": { parentCastId: cast.castId, entryId: entry.id },
            "flags.pf2e.context.options": [...preservedOptions, `pf2e-auto-spell-flow:${cast.castId}:${entry.id}`]
        });
    }
    if (flow.mode === "chain") {
        synchronizeSpellChainStop(state);
        debug("CHAIN SAVE UPDATED:", { spell: cast.spell.name, castId: cast.castId, target: actor.name,
            degree: entry.degree, reroll: !!reroll, entryId: entry.id, stoppedAt: state.chainStop,
            skipped: state.entries.filter(row => row.skipped).map(row => row.actorName), reviewRequired: state.reviewRequired });
    }
    await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(cast);
    return entry;
}

async function applySpellFlowEntry(cast, entry) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    if (entry.applied || entry.skipped) return;
    if (spellFlowEnded(cast)) throw new Error("This spell has ended. Resolve any old pending result manually.");
    if (state.reviewRequired || entry.pendingApplication || entry.reviewRequired) throw new Error("Review the interrupted or rerolled spell result manually. Its application will not be repeated.");
    const { actor, token } = await resolveGroupedTarget(entry);
    if (!actor) throw new Error(`${entry.actorName}: target is unavailable.`);
    const child = spellFlowChild(cast, entry, actor, token);
    await prepareSpellFlowDamage(cast, child, entry);
    entry.pendingApplication = true;
    await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    try {
        await applyResultGroup(child, child.resultGroups[entry.degree], { disabled: false, innerHTML: "Apply" });
        entry.applied = true;entry.pendingApplication = false;
        if (cast.automationProfile.flow.turnImmunityOnCriticalSuccess && entry.degree === "criticalSuccess") {
            const stamp = spellFlowTurnStamp();if (stamp) state.turnImmunity[entry.key] = stamp;
        }
        await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    } catch (error) {
        entry.reviewRequired = true;
        await persistSpellFlow(cast);
        throw error;
    } finally { await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(cast); }
}

async function rollSpellFlowCheck(cast, actor, token, phaseId, statistic) {
    const phase = cast.automationProfile.flow.phases[phaseId];
    const item = spellFlowPhaseItem(cast, phase);
    const dc = getCastSpellDC(cast);
    let message = null;
    if (statistic === "athletics") {
        const skill = actor.getStatistic?.("athletics") ?? actor.skills?.athletics;
        if (typeof skill?.check?.roll !== "function") throw new Error(`${actor.name}: Athletics is unavailable.`);
        const roll = await skill.check.roll({ dc, item, origin: cast.actor, token: token ?? undefined,
            extraRollOptions: ["pf2e-auto-manual-trigger-save"], skipDialog: false,
            callback: (_roll, _outcome, created) => { message = created; } });
        if (!roll) return null;
        const degree = normalizeDegree(roll.degreeOfSuccess ?? roll.options?.degreeOfSuccess);
        if (!degree) throw new Error("Athletics result could not be read.");
        return { degree, message };
    }
    const result = await rollManualTriggerSave(actor, token, phase.saveType, dc, item,
        `${cast.spell.name} — ${phase.label}`, phase.traits, { originSpell: cast.saveOriginSpell ?? resolveSpellAtCastRank(cast),
            castRank: cast.castRank, onSaveMessage: created => { message = created; } });
    return { ...result, message };
}

async function resolveSpellFlowTargets(cast, phaseId, statistic = null) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    const flow = cast.automationProfile.flow;
    const targets = [...(game.user.targets ?? [])].map(target => target.document ?? target);
    if (!targets.length) { ui.notifications.warn("Target at least one creature first."); return; }
    if (spellFlowEnded(cast)) throw new Error("This spell has ended; cast it again for new exposures.");
    const batch = phaseId === "initial" ? "initial" : `${phaseId}:batch-${++state.nextBatch}`;
    await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    for (const token of targets) {
        const actor = token.actor;
        if (!actor) continue;
        const key = getTargetKey(actor, token);
        const stamp = spellFlowTurnStamp();
        if (flow.turnImmunityOnCriticalSuccess && stamp && state.turnImmunity[key] === stamp) {
            ui.notifications.info(`${actor.name}: already immune to further checks from this area this turn.`); continue;
        }
        let entry = (flow.mode === "phases" || phaseId === "initial") ? state.entries.find(row => row.key === key && row.phase === phaseId) : null;
        if (!entry) {
            const result = await rollSpellFlowCheck(cast, actor, token, phaseId, statistic);
            if (!result) continue;
            entry = await recordSpellFlowSave(cast, actor, token, result.degree, { phase: phaseId, batch, statistic, message: result.message });
        }
        if (entry) await applySpellFlowEntry(cast, entry);
    }
}

async function validateSpellChainTarget(cast, entry, previous = null) {
    const flow = cast.automationProfile.flow;
    const { token } = await resolveGroupedTarget(entry);
    const previousTarget = previous ? await resolveGroupedTarget(previous) : null;
    const origin = previousTarget?.token ?? cast.actor.token ?? cast.actor.getActiveTokens?.()?.[0]?.document;
    const from = origin?.object ?? origin;
    const to = token?.object ?? token;
    const limit = previous ? flow.hopDistance : flow.range;
    if (limit && typeof from?.distanceTo === "function" && to) {
        const distance = from.distanceTo(to);
        if (Number.isFinite(distance) && distance > limit) throw new Error(`${entry.actorName} is ${distance} feet away; this ${previous ? "jump" : "spell"} allows ${limit} feet.`);
    }
}

async function resolveSpellChain(cast) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    if (state.reviewRequired) throw new Error("This chain requires manual correction after an applied result changed.");
    if (state.ended) return;
    if (state.entries.some(entry => entry.pendingApplication || entry.reviewRequired)) throw new Error("This chain has an interrupted application. Review it manually before continuing.");
    // Validate the proposed path before any damage. Do not validate targets
    // beyond a critical success: the electricity never reaches them.
    for (let index = 0; index < state.entries.length; index++) {
        const entry = state.entries[index];
        if (!entry.applied) await validateSpellChainTarget(cast, entry, state.entries[index - 1]);
        if (entry.degree === "criticalSuccess") break;
    }
    let stopped = false;
    for (const entry of state.entries) {
        if (stopped) { entry.skipped = true;continue; }
        await applySpellFlowEntry(cast, entry);
        if (entry.degree === "criticalSuccess") { state.chainStop = entry.id;stopped = true; }
    }
    await persistSpellFlow(cast);
    await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(cast);
}

async function resolveNextSpellChainTarget(cast) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    if (state.ended || state.chainStop) throw new Error("The chain has ended.");
    if (state.reviewRequired || state.entries.some(entry => entry.pendingApplication || entry.reviewRequired)) {
        throw new Error("This chain requires manual correction before another target can be resolved.");
    }
    if (state.entries.some(entry => !entry.applied)) throw new Error("Resolve the existing chain order before selecting its next target.");
    const targets = [...(game.user.targets ?? [])];
    if (targets.length !== 1) throw new Error("Target exactly one creature for the next link in the chain.");
    const token = targets[0].document ?? targets[0];const actor = token.actor;
    if (!actor) return;
    if (state.entries.some(entry => entry.key === getTargetKey(actor, token))) throw new Error("This creature has already been targeted by this chain.");
    const provisional = { actorUUID: actor.uuid, tokenUUID: token.uuid, actorName: actor.name };
    await validateSpellChainTarget(cast, provisional, state.entries.at(-1));
    const result = await rollSpellFlowCheck(cast, actor, token, "chain");
    if (!result) return;
    await recordSpellFlowSave(cast, actor, token, result.degree, { phase: "chain", batch: "chain", message: result.message });
    await resolveSpellChain(cast);
}

async function reorderSpellChain(cast, id, direction) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    if (state.entries.some(entry => entry.applied || entry.pendingApplication)) throw new Error("Chain order is locked after resolution begins.");
    const index = state.entries.findIndex(entry => entry.id === id);const next = index + direction;
    if (index < 0 || next < 0 || next >= state.entries.length) return;
    [state.entries[index], state.entries[next]] = [state.entries[next], state.entries[index]];
    await persistSpellFlow(cast);await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(cast);
}

async function endSpellFlow(cast, selectedOnly = false) {
    const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    const actors = selectedOnly ? [...(game.user.targets ?? [])].map(token => token.actor ?? token.document?.actor)
        : (await Promise.all(state.entries.map(entry => resolveGroupedTarget(entry)))).map(row => row.actor);
    for (const actor of new Map(actors.filter(Boolean).map(actor => [actor.uuid, actor])).values()) {
        for (const effect of managedEffectsOnActor(actor)) if (effect.flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.spec.flowAreaCastId === cast.castId) {
            await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect);
        }
    }
    if (!selectedOnly) state.ended = true;
    await persistSpellFlow(cast);await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(cast);
}

function spellFlowButton(cast, action, label, extra = "", disabled = false) {
    return `<button type="button" class="pf2e-auto-flow" data-cast-id="${escapeHTML(cast.castId)}" data-flow-action="${action}" ${extra} ${disabled ? "disabled" : ""}>${escapeHTML(label)}</button>`;
}

function buildSpellFlowCard(cast) {
    const flow = cast.automationProfile.flow;const state = getSpellFlowState(cast);
    const ended = spellFlowEnded(cast);
    const rows = state.entries.map((entry, index) => {
        const phase = flow.phases[entry.phase];
        const outcome = phase.outcomes[entry.degree];
        const group = { ...outcome, targets: [], damage: normalizeOutcomeDamage(outcome) };
        const detail = buildGroupDescriptor(group, cast);
        const status = entry.pendingApplication || entry.reviewRequired ? entry.skipped ? "Past chain stop — review already-applied results" : "Review required"
            : entry.target?.spellImmunity ? `Immune: ${entry.target.spellImmunity.label ?? entry.target.spellImmunity.type}`
            : entry.skipped ? "Not reached — chain ended" : state.chainStop === entry.id ? "Chain ends here — no damage"
            : entry.applied ? "Applied ✓" : "Ready";
        const order = flow.mode === "chain" && !state.entries.some(row => row.applied || row.pendingApplication)
            ? spellFlowButton(cast, "up", "↑", `data-entry-id="${entry.id}"`, index === 0)
                + spellFlowButton(cast, "down", "↓", `data-entry-id="${entry.id}"`, index === state.entries.length - 1) : "";
        const apply = flow.mode !== "chain" && !entry.applied && !entry.pendingApplication && !entry.reviewRequired
            ? spellFlowButton(cast, "apply", "Apply Result", `data-entry-id="${entry.id}"`, state.reviewRequired) : "";
        return `<div style="border-top:1px solid #777;padding:5px 0;"><strong>${index + 1}. ${escapeHTML(entry.actorName)}</strong> — ${escapeHTML(phase.label)}<br>`
            + `${escapeHTML(readableDegree(entry.degree))} • ${escapeHTML(detail)}<br><small>${escapeHTML(status)}</small>${order}${apply}</div>`;
    }).join("");
    let controls = "";
    if (!ended) {
        if (flow.mode === "chain") controls = spellFlowButton(cast, "chain", "Resolve Displayed Chain", "", !state.entries.length || state.reviewRequired)
            + spellFlowButton(cast, "next", "Resolve Next Target", "", !!state.chainStop || state.reviewRequired);
        else if (flow.mode === "phases") for (const [id, phase] of Object.entries(flow.phases)) controls += spellFlowButton(cast, id, `${phase.label}: ${phase.area}-foot ${phase.saveType} Save`);
        else if (flow.mode === "movement") controls = spellFlowButton(cast, "reflex", "Resolve Reflex Exposure") + spellFlowButton(cast, "athletics", "Resolve Athletics Exposure") + spellFlowButton(cast, "leave", "Selected Creatures Left Area");
        else controls = spellFlowButton(cast, "initial", "Resolve Initial Area") + spellFlowButton(cast, "exposure", "Resolve End-Turn Exposure");
        if (flow.mode !== "phases") controls += spellFlowButton(cast, "end", flow.mode === "chain" ? "End Chain" : "End Area Spell");
    }
    return `<div class="pf2e-auto-master-save-card" style="font-size:0.85em;line-height:1.3;"><strong>${escapeHTML(cast.spell.name)} — ${flow.mode === "chain" ? "Ordered Chain" : "Spell Resolution"}</strong>`
        + `<p>${escapeHTML(flow.notes)}</p>${flow.mode === "chain" ? "<p>Use the arrows to correct the order of already-rolled saves before resolving. If distances cannot be measured from scene tokens, check range manually.</p>" : ""}`
        + `${rows}${state.reviewRequired ? "<p>⚠ Applied results changed; this cast requires manual correction.</p>" : ""}${ended ? "<p>Spell ended.</p>" : state.chainStop ? "<p>Chain stopped by a critical success.</p>" : ""}${controls}</div>`;
}

function getSpellDamageRollOptions(castEntry) {
    // Structured PF2e damage already gives us the exact context options.
    if ((castEntry.sharedDamageRollOptions?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
        return new Set(castEntry.sharedDamageRollOptions);
    }

    // Outcome-defined damage never passes through spell.getDamage(), so build
    // the safest equivalent context we can from the spell item itself.
    const spell = resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry);
    const options = new Set([
        "action:cast-a-spell",
        "self:action:slug:cast-a-spell"
    ]);

    for (const trait of spell.system?.traits?.value ?? []) {
        options.add(trait);
        options.add(`item:trait:${trait}`);
    }

    const tradition = spell.spellcasting?.tradition
        ?? spell.system?.traditions?.value?.[0]
        ?? null;

    if (tradition) {
        options.add(tradition);
        options.add(`item:trait:${tradition}`);
    }

    try {
        for (const option of spell.getRollOptions?.("item") ?? []) {
            options.add(option);
        }
    } catch (_) {}

    return options;
}


async function getHealingRollForTargetMode(castEntry, targetMode) {
    const healing = targetMode?.healing;
    if (!healing) return null;

    if (healing.kind === "shared") {
        return await getSharedDamageRoll(castEntry);
    }

    if (healing.kind === "formula") {
        return await getCustomDamageRoll(
            castEntry,
            `target-healing:${targetMode.ruleId}`,
            healing,
            `Target Healing — ${targetMode.predicate?.label ?? "Target"}`
        );
    }

    return null;
}

async function applyHealingRollToActor(actor, token, roll, castEntry) {
    if (!roll) return { applied: false, beforeHP: null, afterHP: null, amount: 0 };

    const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(roll.total ?? 0)));
    const beforeHP = getActorHPValue(actor);

    debug("Applying target-mode healing:", {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        target: actor.name,
        amount,
        formula: roll.formula,
        cycle: castEntry.cycle
    });

    // PF2e's applyDamage accepts a numeric negative value as healing. Use
    // skipIWR because this branch is explicitly HP recovery, not typed damage.
    await actor.applyDamage({
        damage: -amount,
        token: token ?? undefined,
        skipIWR: true,
        rollOptions: getSpellDamageRollOptions(castEntry),
        shieldBlockRequest: false
    });

    return {
        applied: true,
        beforeHP,
        afterHP: getActorHPValue(actor),
        amount
    };
}

function cloneEvaluatedDamageRoll(roll) {
    if (!roll) return null;
    return getPF2eDamageRollClass().fromData(roll.toJSON());
}

/**
 * Change only matching DamageInstance types on an evaluated cloned roll.
 * Updating both `type` and the serialized flavor ensures PF2e's later
 * `DamageRoll#alter()` cloning preserves the replacement.
 */
function substituteDamageTypeOnRoll(roll, fromType, toType) {
    if (!roll) return { roll, replacements: 0 };

    const from = normalizeDamageTypeAlias(fromType);
    const to = normalizeDamageTypeAlias(toType);
    let replacements = 0;

    for (const instance of roll.instances ?? []) {
        if (normalizeDamageTypeAlias(instance.type) !== from) continue;

        instance.type = to;

        if (instance.options) {
            const identifiers = String(instance.options.flavor ?? "")
                .split(",")
                .map(value => value.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);

            let changedFlavor = false;
            const replaced = identifiers.map(identifier => {
                if (normalizeDamageTypeAlias(identifier) !== from) return identifier;
                changedFlavor = true;
                return to;
            });

            if (!changedFlavor) replaced.unshift(to);
            instance.options.flavor = Array.from(new Set(replaced)).join(",");
        }

        replacements++;
    }

    return { roll, replacements };
}

function getTargetAdjustedDamageRoll(actor, roll, castEntry) {
    const rules = getMatchingTargetDamageSubstitutionRules(actor, castEntry);
    if (!rules.length) return { roll, substitutions: [] };

    let adjusted = cloneEvaluatedDamageRoll(roll);
    const substitutions = [];

    for (const rule of rules) {
        const result = substituteDamageTypeOnRoll(adjusted, rule.fromType, rule.toType);
        adjusted = result.roll;
        if (result.replacements <= 0) continue;

        substitutions.push({
            ruleId: rule.id,
            fromType: rule.fromType,
            toType: rule.toType,
            replacements: result.replacements,
            predicate: deepClone(rule.predicate)
        });
    }

    return { roll: adjusted, substitutions };
}

const DAMAGE_APPLICATION_QUEUES = new Map();
let damageReceiptSequence = 0;

async function queueDamageApplication(key, work) {
    const previous = DAMAGE_APPLICATION_QUEUES.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve();
    const next = previous.catch(() => {}).then(work);
    DAMAGE_APPLICATION_QUEUES.set(key, next);
    try { return await next; }
    finally { if (DAMAGE_APPLICATION_QUEUES.get(key) === next) DAMAGE_APPLICATION_QUEUES.delete(key); }
}

async function applyDamageRollToActor(actor, token, roll, multiplier, castEntry, debugLabel, captureReceipt = false) {
    return await queueDamageApplication(`damage:${actor.uuid}`, () =>
        applyNativeDamageRollToActor(actor, token, roll, multiplier, castEntry, debugLabel, captureReceipt));
}

async function applyNativeDamageRollToActor(actor, token, roll, multiplier, castEntry, debugLabel, captureReceipt) {
    if (!roll) return { applied: false, beforeHP: null, afterHP: null };

    const targetAdjusted = getTargetAdjustedDamageRoll(actor, roll, castEntry);
    const baseDamage = targetAdjusted.roll;
    const damage = multiplier === null || multiplier === undefined
        ? baseDamage
        : baseDamage.alter(multiplier, 0);

    if (targetAdjusted.substitutions.length) {
        debug("TARGET DAMAGE-TYPE SUBSTITUTION APPLIED:", {
            spell: castEntry.spell.name,
            target: actor.name,
            substitutions: targetAdjusted.substitutions,
            originalFormula: roll.formula,
            adjustedFormula: damage.formula,
            cycle: castEntry.cycle
        });
    }

    const rollOptions = getSpellDamageRollOptions(castEntry);
    const shieldBlockRequest = CONFIG.PF2E?.chatDamageButtonShieldToggle ?? false;
    const beforeHP = getActorHPValue(actor);

    debug(debugLabel, {
        spell: castEntry.spell.name,
        target: actor.name,
        multiplier,
        originalTotal: roll.total,
        formula: roll.formula,
        effectiveFormula: damage.formula,
        targetDamageSubstitutions: targetAdjusted.substitutions,
        cycle: castEntry.cycle
    });

    // PF2e's update option contains full post-IWR/post-hardness damage,
    // including temp HP, stamina and overkill. HP deltas alone lose that
    // information. A unique roll option identifies its native chat receipt.
    const marker = captureReceipt ? `pf2e-auto:application:${castEntry.castId}:${Date.now()}:${++damageReceiptSequence}` : null;
    const updates = [];
    let hook = null;
    if (marker) {
        rollOptions.add(marker);
        hook = Hooks.on("updateActor", (updated, changes, options, userId) => {
            if (updated.uuid === actor.uuid && (userId == null || userId === game.user.id)
                && Number.isFinite(options?.damageTaken) && !options.damageUndo) {
                updates.push(options.damageTaken);
            }
        });
    }
    try {
        await actor.applyDamage({ damage, token: token ?? undefined, skipIWR: false, rollOptions, shieldBlockRequest });
    } finally {
        if (hook != null) Hooks.off("updateActor", hook);
    }

    const receipt = marker ? game.messages.find(message =>
        message.flags?.pf2e?.context?.type === "damage-taken"
        && message.flags.pf2e.context.options?.includes(marker)) : null;
    const applied = receipt?.flags?.pf2e?.appliedDamage;
    const receiptConfirmed = !!receipt && (!applied || applied.uuid === actor.uuid);
    const damageTaken = !receiptConfirmed ? null
        : updates.length === 1 ? Math.max(0, updates[0])
        : updates.length === 0 && !(applied?.updates?.length) ? 0 : null;

    const afterHP = getActorHPValue(actor);

    return {
        applied: true,
        beforeHP,
        afterHP,
        receiptConfirmed,
        damageTaken,
        persistentIds: receiptConfirmed ? applied?.persistent ?? [] : []
    };
}

async function applyResultGroup(castEntry, group, button) {
    if (group._applyInFlight) return group._applyInFlight;
    const work = castEntry.automationProfile?.casterTemporaryHP
        ? queueDamageApplication(`caster-pool:${castEntry.actor.uuid}`, () => applyResultGroupOnce(castEntry, group, button))
        : applyResultGroupOnce(castEntry, group, button);
    group._applyInFlight = work;
    try { return await work; }
    finally { group._applyInFlight = null; }
}

async function applyResultGroupOnce(castEntry, group, button) {
    if (!refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(castEntry)) return;
    if (group.secondarySave) {
        return await resolveSecondarySaveGroup(castEntry, group, button);
    }

    const needsDamage = groupHasDamage(group);
    const hasConditions = groupHasAutomatedConditions(group);

    let roll = null;

    if (needsDamage) {
        roll = await getDamageRollForGroup(castEntry, group);
        if (!roll) {
            ui.notifications.error("The PF2e damage roll could not be restored.");
            return;
        }
    }

    const pending = group.targets.filter(target => !target.applied && !target.manualCorrection);
    if (pending.length === 0) return;

    button.disabled = true;
    const oldHTML = button.innerHTML;
    button.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>`;

    let appliedCount = 0;

    try {
        for (const target of pending) {
            const { actor, token } = await resolveGroupedTarget(target);

            if (!actor || typeof actor.applyDamage !== "function") {
                console.warn("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not resolve target:", target);
                continue;
            }

            const casterPool = getCasterTemporaryHPState(castEntry);
            if (casterPool) {
                const recorded = casterPool.records[target.key];
                if (recorded) {
                    target.applied = true;
                    target.damageApplied = recorded.damageTaken > 0;
                    if (recorded.excluded) target.followUpNote = `Not affected: ${recorded.excluded}`;
                    continue;
                }
                if (casterPool.reviewRequired || casterPool.pending || casterPool.pendingGrant) {
                    throw new Error("This cast has an interrupted or rerolled application. Review target damage and caster temporary HP manually; uncertain applications will not be repeated.");
                }
                if (castEntry.automationProfile.casterTemporaryHP.livingOnly && !isLivingAreaCreature(actor)) {
                    target.applied = true;
                    target.followUpNote = "Not affected: nonliving";
                    casterPool.records[target.key] = { actorUUID: actor.uuid, damageTaken: 0, excluded: "nonliving" };
                    await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
                    continue;
                }
            }

            const castWide = getCastBacklashState(castEntry);
            if (castWide?.initial[target.key]?.complete) {
                target.applied = true;
                continue;
            }
            target.primaryBeforeHP ??= getActorHPValue(actor);
            target.primaryWasDead ??= actor.isDead || actor.statuses?.has?.("dead") === true;
            const primaryBeforeHP = target.primaryBeforeHP;
            const primaryWasDead = target.primaryWasDead;
            if (castWide?.pendingInitial?.[target.key] && !target.primaryStarted) {
                throw new Error(`${actor.name}: an initial application was interrupted. Review its HP and death status manually; this cast will not repeat uncertain damage.`);
            }
            if (castWide) {
                const eligibility = backlashTargetEligibility(actor, castEntry.automationProfile.castBacklash);
                if (!Number.isFinite(eligibility.level) && eligibility.living) throw new Error(`${actor.name}: could not read creature level.`);
                if (!eligibility.eligible || primaryWasDead) {
                    target.applied = true;
                    target.followUpNote = "Not subject to the initial effect";
                    await recordCastInitialResult(castEntry, target, actor, primaryBeforeHP, primaryWasDead);
                    continue;
                }
            }
            const liveSpellImmunity = getSpellEffectImmunity(actor, castEntry);
            if (liveSpellImmunity) {
                target.spellImmunity = deepClone(liveSpellImmunity);
                target.targetMode = null;
                target.applied = true;
                await recordCastInitialResult(castEntry, target, actor, primaryBeforeHP, primaryWasDead);
                debug("SPELL EFFECT BLOCKED BY IMMUNITY AT APPLY:", {
                    spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                    target: actor.name,
                    degree: group.degree,
                    immunity: liveSpellImmunity
                });
                appliedCount++;
                continue;
            }

            if (target.targetMode?.kind === "healing") {
                const healingRoll = await getHealingRollForTargetMode(
                    castEntry,
                    target.targetMode
                );

                if (!healingRoll) {
                    console.warn("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Could not resolve target-mode healing roll:", target);
                    continue;
                }

                await applyHealingRollToActor(
                    actor,
                    token,
                    healingRoll,
                    castEntry
                );

                target.healingApplied = true;
                target.applied = true;
                appliedCount++;
                continue;
            }

            let damageResult = null;
            if (castWide && !target.primaryStarted) {
                castWide.pendingInitial ??= {};
                castWide.pendingInitial[target.key] = { beforeHP: primaryBeforeHP, wasDead: primaryWasDead, degree: target.degree };
                await persistCastBacklash(castEntry);
                target.primaryStarted = true;
            }

            if (needsDamage && !target.damageApplied) {
                const multiplier = group.damage.kind === "shared"
                    ? group.damage.multiplier
                    : null;

                if (casterPool) {
                    casterPool.pending = { key: target.key, actorName: actor.name, degree: group.degree };
                    await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
                }
                damageResult = await applyDamageRollToActor(
                    actor,
                    token,
                    roll,
                    multiplier,
                    castEntry,
                    "Applying automatic save damage:",
                    !!casterPool
                );

                target.damageApplied = true;
                if (casterPool) {
                    const taken = damageResult.damageTaken;
                    casterPool.records[target.key] = { actorUUID: actor.uuid, degree: group.degree, damageTaken: taken };
                    casterPool.pending = null;
                    if (!Number.isFinite(taken)) {
                        casterPool.reviewRequired = true;
                        casterPool.reviewReason = `${actor.name}: native damage could not be confirmed; review caster temporary HP manually.`;
                        await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
                        throw new Error(`${actor.name}: damage was applied, but the native damage receipt was ambiguous. Review caster temporary HP manually; this damage will not be repeated.`);
                    }
                    casterPool.peakDamage = Math.max(casterPool.peakDamage, taken);
                    await persistCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
                }
            }

            await applyConditionalDamageRules(
                castEntry,
                group,
                target,
                actor,
                token
            );

            const hasOrdinaryConditionEffects = (group.conditions?.length ?? 0) > 0
                || (group.linkedConditions?.length ?? 0) > 0
                || !!group.ongoingSave;
            const hasNativeEffects = (group.nativeEffects?.length ?? 0) > 0;

            if (hasOrdinaryConditionEffects && !target.effectsApplied) {
                if (group.ongoingSave) {
                    const trackedSlug = normalizeSlug(group.ongoingSave.trackedCondition);
                    const trackedEffect = (group.conditions ?? []).find(effect =>
                        normalizeSlug(effect.condition) === trackedSlug
                    ) ?? {
                        condition: group.ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
                        name: group.ongoingSave.trackedConditionName ?? group.ongoingSave.trackedCondition,
                        value: 1,
                        duration: null
                    };

                    const ordinaryConditions = (group.conditions ?? []).filter(effect =>
                        normalizeSlug(effect.condition) !== trackedSlug
                    );

                    await applyOutcomeConditions(
                        actor,
                        castEntry.spell,
                        ordinaryConditions,
                        group.linkedConditions
                    );

                    await createOngoingSaveTracker(
                        actor,
                        castEntry.spell,
                        castEntry,
                        group.ongoingSave,
                        trackedEffect
                    );
                } else {
                    await applyOutcomeConditions(
                        actor,
                        castEntry.spell,
                        group.conditions,
                        group.linkedConditions
                    );
                }
            }

            if (hasNativeEffects && !target.effectsApplied) {
                const nativeApplied = await applyNativeSpellEffects(
                    actor,
                    castEntry.spell,
                    castEntry,
                    group.nativeEffects
                );
                debug("NATIVE SPELL EFFECT APPLICATION SUMMARY:", {
                    spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                    target: actor.name,
                    degree: group.degree,
                    requested: group.nativeEffects.length,
                    applied: nativeApplied
                });
            }

            if (group.managedEffects?.length && !target.managedEffectsApplied) {
                const complete = await applyManagedSpellEffects(actor, castEntry, group.managedEffects,
                    `${castEntry.castId}:${castEntry.cycle}:${target.key}:${group.degree}`);
                if (!complete) continue;
                target.managedEffectsApplied = true;
            }
            if (hasOrdinaryConditionEffects || hasNativeEffects || target.managedEffectsApplied) {
                target.effectsApplied = true;
            }

            if (damageResult?.applied) {
                await handlePostDamageTerminal(
                    castEntry,
                    target,
                    actor,
                    damageResult.beforeHP,
                    damageResult.afterHP
                );
            }

            // An unconditional terminal result written directly in this
            // degree of success is separate from a post-damage 0-HP trigger.
            if (group.terminal && !target.terminalApplied) {
                await applyTerminalResult(castEntry, target, actor, group.terminal);
            }

            if (group.followUpSave && !await resolveAfterDamageSave(castEntry, group, target, actor, token)) continue;
            await recordCastInitialResult(castEntry, target, actor, primaryBeforeHP, primaryWasDead);
            target.applied = true;
            appliedCount++;
        }

        await updateCasterTemporaryHP(castEntry);
        await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry);

        await maybeApplyCastBacklash(castEntry);
        if (castEntry.automationProfile?.castBacklash) await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry);
        ui.notifications.info(
            `${castEntry.spell.name}: result applied to ${appliedCount} target${appliedCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}.`
        );
    } catch (error) {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.innerHTML = oldHTML;
        throw error;
    }
}

function classifySecondaryDegree(degree) {
    return degree === "success" || degree === "criticalSuccess"
        ? "success"
        : "failure";
}

async function applySecondaryBranch(castEntry, group, target, actor, token, branchName, branch) {
    if (!branch) {
        target.manualCorrection = true;
        target.applied = true;
        return;
    }

    if (branch.terminal) {
        await applyTerminalResult(castEntry, target, actor, branch.terminal);
        target.applied = true;
        return;
    }

    if (branch.damage?.kind === "formula") {
        const damageKey = `degree:${group.degree}:secondary:${branchName}`;
        const roll = await getCustomDamageRoll(
            castEntry,
            damageKey,
            branch.damage,
            `${readableDegree(group.degree)} — Secondary ${branchName} Damage`
        );

        const damageResult = await applyDamageRollToActor(
            actor,
            token,
            roll,
            null,
            castEntry,
            "Applying secondary-save damage:"
        );

        target.damageApplied = true;

        if (damageResult?.applied) {
            await handlePostDamageTerminal(
                castEntry,
                target,
                actor,
                damageResult.beforeHP,
                damageResult.afterHP
            );
        }
    }

    if ((branch.conditions?.length ?? 0) > 0 || (branch.linkedConditions?.length ?? 0) > 0) {
        await applyOutcomeConditions(
            actor,
            castEntry.spell,
            branch.conditions,
            branch.linkedConditions
        );
        target.effectsApplied = true;
    }

    target.applied = true;
}

async function resolveSecondarySaveGroup(castEntry, group, button) {
    const secondary = group.secondarySave;
    if (!secondary) return;

    const pending = group.targets.filter(target =>
        !target.applied
        && !target.manualCorrection
        && !target.secondaryResolved
    );

    if (pending.length === 0) return;

    button.disabled = true;
    const oldHTML = button.innerHTML;
    button.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>`;

    try {
        for (const target of pending) {
            const { actor, token } = await resolveGroupedTarget(target);
            if (!actor) continue;

            const statistic = actor.saves?.[secondary.saveType];
            if (!statistic || typeof statistic.roll !== "function") {
                target.manualCorrection = true;
                target.applied = true;
                ui.notifications.warn(
                    `${actor.name}: could not find native PF2e ${secondary.saveType} save statistic.`
                );
                continue;
            }

            const saveName = secondary.saveType.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase());

            const roll = await statistic.roll({
                dc: castEntry.dc,
                token: token ?? undefined,
                item: castEntry.spell,
                label: `${castEntry.spell.name} — Secondary ${saveName} Save`,
                title: `${castEntry.spell.name} — Secondary ${saveName} Save`,
                extraRollOptions: [
                    "pf2e-auto-secondary-save",
                    `pf2e-auto-secondary-save:${normalizeSlug(castEntry.spell.name)}`
                ],
                traits: secondary.traits ?? [],
                skipDialog: false
            });

            if (!roll) continue;

            const degree = normalizeDegree(roll.degreeOfSuccess ?? roll.options?.degreeOfSuccess);
            if (!degree) {
                target.manualCorrection = true;
                target.applied = true;
                ui.notifications.warn(
                    `${actor.name}: secondary save rolled, but its degree of success could not be read.`
                );
                continue;
            }

            const branchName = classifySecondaryDegree(degree);
            const branch = branchName === "success"
                ? secondary.successBranch
                : secondary.failureBranch;

            target.secondaryResolved = true;
            target.secondaryDegree = degree;
            target.secondaryBranch = branchName;

            debug("SECONDARY SAVE RESULT:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                target: actor.name,
                saveType: secondary.saveType,
                dc: castEntry.dc,
                degree,
                branch: branchName,
                branchData: branch
            });

            await applySecondaryBranch(
                castEntry,
                group,
                target,
                actor,
                token,
                branchName,
                branch
            );
        }

        await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry);
    } catch (error) {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.innerHTML = oldHTML;
        throw error;
    }
}

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_TURN_HOOK = Hooks.on(
    "combatTurn",
    (combat, updateData, updateOptions) => {
        queueOngoingSavesAtCombatProgression(
            combat,
            updateData,
            updateOptions,
            "combatTurn"
        );
    }
);

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_ROUND_HOOK = Hooks.on(
    "combatRound",
    (combat, updateData, updateOptions) => {
        queueOngoingSavesAtCombatProgression(
            combat,
            updateData,
            updateOptions,
            "combatRound"
        );
    }
);

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_ONGOING_ITEM_HOOK = Hooks.on(
    "deleteItem",
    item => {
        const actor = item.actor;
        if (!actor) return;

        const trackerRule = item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave;
        if (trackerRule?.trackedConditionId) {
            setTimeout(async () => {
                const tracked = actor.items.get(trackerRule.trackedConditionId);
                if (!tracked) return;
                try { await tracked.delete(); } catch (_) {}
            }, 0);
            return;
        }

        if (item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.independentCondition === true) {
            setTimeout(async () => {
                const tracker = getOngoingSaveTrackers(actor).find(effect =>
                    effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.ongoingSave?.trackedConditionId === item.id
                );
                if (!tracker) return;
                try { await tracker.delete(); } catch (_) {}
            }, 0);
        }
    }
);
