);

// =================================================================
// CHAT RENDER HOOK
// =================================================================

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_RENDER_HOOK = Hooks.on(
    "renderChatMessageHTML",
    (message, html) => {
        const root = html instanceof HTMLElement ? html : html?.[0];
        if (!root) return;

        if (game.user.isGM && !root.querySelector(".pf2e-auto-scroll-spell-card")) {
            const scroll = getScrollFromCard(message);
            if (scroll && scroll.isIdentified !== false) {
                const button = document.createElement("button");
                button.type = "button";
                button.className = "pf2e-auto-scroll-spell-card";
                button.textContent = "Show Spell Card";
                button.title = "Post the scroll's spell with the caster's spell DC. Does not consume the scroll.";
                button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                    event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
                    if (button.disabled) return;
                    button.disabled = true;
                    try { await showScrollSpellCard(message); }
                    catch (error) {
                        console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Scroll spell card failed:", error);
                        ui.notifications.error(`${scroll.name}: ${error.message}`);
                    } finally { button.disabled = false; }
                });
                (root.querySelector(".card-buttons") ?? root).appendChild(button);
            }
        }

        // Resolve the spell-card cast entry once before any direct-card automation
        // checks use it. Both no-save and manual-trigger spell paths depend on
        // this same cached cast object.
        let directCastEntry = findCastById(message.id);
        if (!directCastEntry) {
            directCastEntry = detectSpellCard(message);
            if (directCastEntry) {
                STATE.recentSpells.unshift(directCastEntry);
                pruneCastCache();
            }
        }

        if (game.user.isGM && directCastEntry && isPotentialRandomDamageTableSpell(directCastEntry)
            && !root.querySelector(".pf2e-auto-table-controls")) {
            const controls = document.createElement("div");
            controls.className = "pf2e-auto-table-controls";
            for (const [label, action] of [
                ["Table Result", () => setRandomTableResult(directCastEntry)],
                ["Resolve Exposure", () => resolveRandomTableTargets(directCastEntry)]
            ]) {
                const button = document.createElement("button");
                button.type = "button";
                button.textContent = label;
                button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                    event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
                    button.disabled = true;
                    try { activateSpellCard(message); await action(); }
                    catch (error) {
                        console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Table control failed:", error);
                        ui.notifications.error(`${directCastEntry.spell.name}: ${error.message}`);
                    } finally { button.disabled = false; }
                });
                controls.appendChild(button);
            }
            (root.querySelector(".card-buttons") ?? root.querySelector(".card-content") ?? root).appendChild(controls);
        }

        // Manual-trigger multi-save spells (layered crossing effects) also have
        // no native save button. Add a one-click crossing resolver that rolls
        // every component save before applying any component outcomes.
        if (directCastEntry && isPotentialManualMultiSaveCrossingSpell(directCastEntry)) {
            const existingCrossingButton = root.querySelector(".pf2e-auto-resolve-crossing-target");
            if (!existingCrossingButton) {
                const button = document.createElement("button");
                button.type = "button";
                button.className = "pf2e-auto-resolve-crossing-target";
                button.dataset.castId = directCastEntry.castId;
                button.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-rainbow"></i> Resolve Crossing';

                const host = root.querySelector(".card-buttons")
                    ?? root.querySelector(".card-content")
                    ?? root;
                host.appendChild(button);

                button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();

                    const castEntry = findCastById(button.dataset.castId);
                    if (!castEntry) {
                        ui.notifications.error("The original spell card is no longer cached.");
                        return;
                    }

                    const targets = Array.from(game.user.targets ?? []);
                    if (targets.length !== 1) {
                        ui.notifications.warn(`${castEntry.spell.name}: target exactly one creature before resolving a crossing.`);
                        return;
                    }

                    const targetToken = targets[0];
                    const targetActor = targetToken?.actor ?? targetToken?.document?.actor ?? null;
                    if (!targetActor) {
                        ui.notifications.error("Could not resolve the crossing creature's actor.");
                        return;
                    }

                    const oldHTML = button.innerHTML;
                    button.disabled = true;
                    button.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Resolving Crossing';

                    try {
                        activateSpellCard(message);
                        await resolveManualMultiSaveCrossing(castEntry, targetActor, targetToken?.document ?? null);
                    } catch (error) {
                        console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Manual crossing resolution failed:", error);
                        ui.notifications.error(`${castEntry.spell.name}: crossing resolution failed.`);
                    } finally {
                        button.disabled = false;
                        button.innerHTML = oldHTML;
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        // No-save level-threshold spells have no system save button, so add a
        // resolver directly to their spell card. It uses the GM's currently
        // targeted creature and resolves immediately from target level.
        if (directCastEntry && isPotentialNoSaveLevelThresholdSpell(directCastEntry)) {
            const existingButton = root.querySelector(".pf2e-auto-resolve-no-save-target");
            if (!existingButton) {
                const button = document.createElement("button");
                button.type = "button";
                button.className = "pf2e-auto-resolve-no-save-target";
                button.dataset.castId = directCastEntry.castId;
                button.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-bolt"></i> Resolve Target';

                const host = root.querySelector(".card-buttons")
                    ?? root.querySelector(".card-content")
                    ?? root;
                host.appendChild(button);

                button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();

                    const castEntry = findCastById(button.dataset.castId);
                    if (!castEntry) {
                        ui.notifications.error("The original spell card is no longer cached.");
                        return;
                    }

                    const targets = Array.from(game.user.targets ?? []);
                    if (targets.length !== 1) {
                        ui.notifications.warn(`${castEntry.spell.name}: target exactly one creature before resolving.`);
                        return;
                    }

                    const targetToken = targets[0];
                    const targetActor = targetToken?.actor ?? targetToken?.document?.actor ?? null;
                    if (!targetActor) {
                        ui.notifications.error("Could not resolve the targeted creature's actor.");
                        return;
                    }

                    const oldHTML = button.innerHTML;
                    button.disabled = true;
                    button.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Resolving';

                    try {
                        activateSpellCard(message);
                        const result = await resolveNoSaveTarget(castEntry, targetActor, targetToken?.document ?? null);
                        if (!result) {
                            button.disabled = false;
                            button.innerHTML = oldHTML;
                            return;
                        }
                        button.disabled = false;
                        button.innerHTML = oldHTML;
                    } catch (error) {
                        button.disabled = false;
                        button.innerHTML = oldHTML;
                        console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Direct no-save resolution failed:", error);
                        ui.notifications.error(`${castEntry.spell.name}: direct resolution failed.`);
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        for (const button of root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="spell-save"]')) {
            button.addEventListener(
                "click",
                () => activateSpellCard(message),
                { capture: true }
            );
        }

        for (const button of root.querySelectorAll(".pf2e-auto-apply-master-damage")) {
            button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();

                const castEntry = findCastById(button.dataset.castId);
                const degree = button.dataset.degree;

                if (!castEntry) {
                    ui.notifications.error("The original spell card is no longer cached.");
                    return;
                }

                const group = castEntry.resultGroups?.[degree];
                if (!group) {
                    ui.notifications.error("Could not locate this save group.");
                    return;
                }

                if (castEntry.tableParentCastId && castEntry.tableApplying) return;
                if (castEntry.tableParentCastId) castEntry.tableApplying = true;
                try {
                    await applyResultGroup(castEntry, group, button);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Result application failed:", error);
                    ui.notifications.error("Automatic spell result application failed.");
                } finally { if (castEntry.tableParentCastId) castEntry.tableApplying = false; }
            });
        }

        for (const button of root.querySelectorAll(".pf2e-auto-table-choose")) {
            if (!game.user.isGM) { button.hidden = true; continue; }
            button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
                if (!game.user.isGM) return;
                const parent = findCastById(button.dataset.castId);
                const exposure = findCastById(button.dataset.exposureId);
                if (!parent || !exposure) return;
                button.disabled = true;
                try {
                    await queueRandomTableWork(parent, async () => {
                        if (exposure.tablePending) await completeRandomTableExposure(parent, exposure);
                    });
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Table exposure failed:", error);
                    ui.notifications.error(`${parent.spell.name}: ${error.message}`);
                } finally { button.disabled = false; }
            });
        }

        for (const button of root.querySelectorAll(".pf2e-auto-secondary-save")) {
            button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();

                const castEntry = findCastById(button.dataset.castId);
                const degree = button.dataset.degree;

                if (!castEntry) {
                    ui.notifications.error("The original spell card is no longer cached.");
                    return;
                }

                const group = castEntry.resultGroups?.[degree];
                if (!group?.secondarySave) {
                    ui.notifications.error("Could not locate this secondary save.");
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    await resolveSecondarySaveGroup(castEntry, group, button);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Secondary save failed:", error);
                    ui.notifications.error("Automatic secondary save resolution failed.");
                }
            });
        }
    }
);

// =================================================================
// DELETE CHAT MESSAGE HOOK
// =================================================================

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_DELETE_HOOK = Hooks.on("deleteChatMessage", message => {
    if (!game.user.isGM) return;

    for (const castEntry of [...STATE.recentSpells]) {
        if (message.id === castEntry.castId) {
            STATE.recentSpells = STATE.recentSpells.filter(entry => entry !== castEntry);

            if (STATE.activeCastId === castEntry.castId) {
                STATE.activeCastId = null;
                STATE.activeCastAt = 0;
            }

            debug("Original spell card deleted; removed cast:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                castId: castEntry.castId
            });
            continue;
        }

        if (message.id === castEntry.resolutionMessageId) {
            resetResolutionCycle(castEntry, "master resolution card deleted");
            ui.notifications.info(
                `${castEntry.spell.name}: resolution cleared. The next save will roll fresh damage.`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (message.id === castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId) {
            const storedRoll = castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON
                ? restoreDamageRoll(castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON)
                : null;

            debug("Damage chat card deleted; EVALUATED ROLL RETAINED:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                cycle: castEntry.cycle,
                formula: storedRoll?.formula ?? castEntry.sharedDamageFormula,
                total: storedRoll?.total ?? null
            });

            castEntry.sharedDamageMessageId = null;
            castEntry.sharedDamageRolled = !!castEntry.sharedDamageRollJSON;

            ui.notifications.info(
                `${castEntry.spell.name}: damage card removed; the original damage result is still retained.`
            );
            continue;
        }

        for (const state of Object.values(castEntry.customDamageStates ?? {})) {
            if (message.id !== state.messageId) continue;

            const storedRoll = state.rollJSON ? restoreDamageRoll(state.rollJSON) : null;

            debug("Outcome damage chat card deleted; EVALUATED ROLL RETAINED:", {
                spell: castEntry.spell.name,
                cycle: castEntry.cycle,
                key: state.key,
                formula: storedRoll?.formula ?? state.formula,
                total: storedRoll?.total ?? state.total
            });

            state.messageId = null;

            ui.notifications.info(
                `${castEntry.spell.name}: outcome damage card removed; the original result is still retained.`
            );
        }
    }
});

// =================================================================
// MAIN CHAT CREATE HOOK
// =================================================================

globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_SPELL_CONDITIONS_HOOK = Hooks.on(
    "createChatMessage",
    async message => {
        try {
            const castEntry = cacheSpellCast(message);
            if (game.user.isGM && castEntry && isPotentialRandomDamageTableSpell(castEntry)) {
                const profile = await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);
                if (profile?.kind === "random-table-basic-save") await ensureRandomTableSelection(castEntry);
            }
            if (game.user.isGM && castEntry && potentialSpellFlow(resolveSpellAtCastRank(castEntry))) {
                const profile = await determineSpellAutomation(castEntry);
                if (profile?.flow) { getSpellFlowState(castEntry);await persistSpellFlow(castEntry);await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(castEntry); }
            }
            await processSave(message);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(
                "PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Unexpected error:",
                error,
                message
            );
        }
    }
);

// Rerunning this macro replaces these hooks, including their control handlers.
for (const hook of globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_MANAGED_HOOKS ?? []) Hooks.off(hook.name, hook.id);
globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_MANAGED_HOOKS = [];
function managedHook(name, callback) {
    globalThis.PF2E_AUTO_MANAGED_HOOKS.push({ name, id: Hooks.on(name, callback) });
}

function queueManagedReconcile(actor = null) {
    if (!managedAutomationGM()) return;
    const actors = actor ? [actor] : [
        ...(game.actors?.contents ?? [...(game.actors ?? [])]),
        ...(canvas.tokens?.placeables ?? []).map(token => token.actor).filter(Boolean)
    ];
    const unique = [...new Map(actors.map(a => [a.uuid, a])).values()];
    setTimeout(async () => {
        for (const target of unique) {
            try { await reconcileManagedEffects(target); }
            catch (error) { console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Effect expiry failed:", error); }
        }
    }, 0);
}
managedHook("updateWorldTime", () => queueManagedReconcile());
managedHook("updateCombat", () => queueManagedReconcile());
managedHook("updateItem", item => {
    if (item.type === "condition" || item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect) queueManagedReconcile(item.actor);
});
managedHook("deleteItem", item => {
    if (!managedAutomationGM() || !item.actor) return;
    if (item.type === "condition") queueManagedReconcile(item.actor);
    const state = item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
    if (state && !state.endedByMacro) {
        runManagedEffectJob(`${item.actor.uuid}:${item.id}:end`, () => finishManagedEffect(item.actor, item,
            { expired: item.isExpired || item.remainingDuration?.expired, alreadyDeleted: true }))
            .catch(error => console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Effect cleanup failed:", error));
    } else if (item.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedParent) queueManagedReconcile(item.actor);
});
managedHook("renderChatMessageHTML", (message, html) => {
    const root = html instanceof HTMLElement ? html : html?.[0];
    if (!root) return;
    if (game.user.isGM && !root.querySelector(".pf2e-auto-backlash-area")) {
        const cast = findCastById(message.id) ?? detectSpellCard(message);
        if (cast && inferCastBacklashRule(resolveSpellAtCastRank(cast), cast)) {
            if (!findCastById(cast.castId)) cacheSpellCast(message);
            const button = document.createElement("button");
            button.type = "button"; button.className = "pf2e-auto-backlash-area";
            button.dataset.castId = cast.castId; button.textContent = "Resolve Selected Area";
            button.title = "Target every living creature in the line, including creatures above the level limit. Rolls missing saves and resolves damage and any backlash.";
            (root.querySelector(".card-buttons") ?? root).appendChild(button);
        }
    }
    for (const button of root.querySelectorAll(".pf2e-auto-backlash-area")) {
        if (!game.user.isGM) { button.hidden = true; continue; }
        button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
            event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
            if (button.disabled) return;
            button.disabled = true;
            try {
                const original = game.messages.get(button.dataset.castId);
                const cast = findCastById(button.dataset.castId) ?? (original ? cacheSpellCast(original) : null);
                if (!cast) throw new Error("The original cast card is unavailable.");
                await resolveCastBacklashArea(cast);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Area resolution failed:", error);
                ui.notifications.error(error.message);
            } finally { button.disabled = false; }
        });
    }
    for (const button of root.querySelectorAll(".pf2e-auto-managed-control")) {
        if (!game.user.isGM) { button.hidden = true; continue; }
        button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
            event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
            if (button.disabled) return;
            button.disabled = true;
            try {
                const actor = await fromUuid(button.dataset.actorUuid);
                const effect = actor?.items.get(button.dataset.effectId);
                if (!effect) { button.textContent = "Effect ended"; return; }
                const state = effect.flags?.pf2eAutoSpellResolution?.managedSpellEffect;
                if (!state) return;
                const action = button.dataset.effectAction;
                if (action === "end") await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect);
                else if (action === "end-cast") await endManagedSustainedCast(actor, effect);
                else if (action === "hostility" && state.spec.endsOnHostility) await finishManagedEffect(actor, effect);
                else if (action === "phase" && state.spec.transition) {
                    if (state.spec.untilTargetNextTurn) await advanceTurnBoundSpellEffect(actor, effect);
                    else await advanceManagedPhase(actor, effect);
                }
                else if (action === "escape") await resolveSpellEscape(actor, effect);
                else if (action === "release") await releaseSpellCapture(actor, effect);
                else if (action === "save") await resolveManagedRecovery(actor, null, effect);
                else if (action === "environment" && state.spec.environment) {
                    const answer = await promptSpellDecision(state.sourceSpellName, `Is ${actor.name} ${state.spec.environment.question}?`,
                        [{ value: "no", label: "No" }, { value: "yes", label: "Yes" }]);
                    if (answer != null && answer !== false) {
                        const rules = deepClone(effect.system.rules);
                        const rule = rules.find(r => r.key === "RollOption" && r.option === state.environmentOption);
                        if (!rule) throw new Error("The environment rule is missing.");
                        rule.value = answer === "yes";
                        await effect.update({ "system.rules": rules, "flags.pf2eAutoSpellResolution.managedSpellEffect.environmentActive": answer === "yes" });
                        button.textContent = `Environment: ${answer === "yes" ? "Yes" : "No"} — Update`;
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Effect control failed:", error);
                ui.notifications.error(error.message);
            } finally { button.disabled = false; }
        });
    }
});
queueManagedReconcile();

managedHook("renderChatMessageHTML", (message, html) => {
    const root = html instanceof HTMLElement ? html : html?.[0];
    if (!root) return;
    if (game.user.isGM && !root.querySelector('.pf2e-auto-flow[data-flow-action="open"]')) {
        const cast = findCastById(message.id) ?? detectSpellCard(message);
        if (cast && potentialSpellFlow(resolveSpellAtCastRank(cast))) {
            const button = document.createElement("button");
            button.type = "button";button.className = "pf2e-auto-flow";button.dataset.castId = cast.castId;
            button.dataset.flowAction = "open";button.textContent = "Open Spell Resolution";
            (root.querySelector(".card-buttons") ?? root).appendChild(button);
        }
    }
    for (const button of root.querySelectorAll(".pf2e-auto-flow")) {
        if (!game.user.isGM) { button.hidden = true;continue; }
        if (button.dataset.flowBound) continue;
        button.dataset.flowBound = "true";
        button.addEventListener("click", async event => {
            event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
            if (button.disabled || button.dataset.flowBoundBusy) return;
            button.disabled = true;button.dataset.flowBoundBusy = "true";
            try {
                const original = game.messages.get(button.dataset.castId);
                const cast = findCastById(button.dataset.castId) ?? (original ? cacheSpellCast(original) : null);
                if (!cast || !refreshEmbeddedCastEntry(cast)) throw new Error("The original spell card is unavailable or has changed.");
                await determineSpellAutomation(cast);
                if (!cast.automationProfile?.flow) throw new Error("This spell has no supported staged resolution.");
                await queueDamageApplication(`flow:${cast.castId}`, async () => {
                    const action = button.dataset.flowAction;
                    if (action === "open") { getSpellFlowState(cast);await persistSpellFlow(cast);await queueMasterResolutionCardUpdate(cast); }
                    else if (action === "chain") await resolveSpellChain(cast);
                    else if (action === "next") await resolveNextSpellChainTarget(cast);
                    else if (action === "end") await endSpellFlow(cast);
                    else if (action === "leave") await endSpellFlow(cast, true);
                    else if (action === "up" || action === "down") await reorderSpellChain(cast, button.dataset.entryId, action === "up" ? -1 : 1);
                    else if (action === "apply") {
                        const entry = getSpellFlowState(cast).entries.find(row => row.id === button.dataset.entryId);
                        if (entry) await applySpellFlowEntry(cast, entry);
                    } else await resolveSpellFlowTargets(cast, ["reflex", "athletics"].includes(action) ? "exposure" : action,
                        action === "athletics" ? "athletics" : null);
                });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("PF2e Auto Spell Resolution | Staged resolution failed:", error);
                ui.notifications.error(error.message);
            } finally { button.disabled = false;delete button.dataset.flowBoundBusy; }
        });
    }
});
